Description

A Cell MO includes the cell ID and related radio frequency (RF) information. It is configured by network planning engineers based on site requirements.

Effective Scenario

Immediately

Relations Between MOs

  • Possible parents:

    eNodeBFunction

  • Possible children:

    BcchCfg[1~1];Cdma20001XrttNCell[0~32];Cdma20001XrttPreReg[0~1];Cdma2000BandClass[0~16];Cdma2000HrpdNCell[0~32];Cdma2000HrpdPreReg[0~1];Cdma2000HrpdRefCell[0~6];CellAcBar[1~1];CellAccess[1~1];CellAlgoExtSwitch[1~1];CellAlgoSwitch[1~1];CellAuxDuBind[0~6];CellAuxEnbBind[0~6];CellBackOff[1~1];CellBf[1~1];CellCeCfg[1~1];CellCeSchCfg[1~4];CellChPwrCfg[1~1];CellCounterParaGroup[1~1];CellCqiAdjAlgo[1~1];CellCsiRsParaCfg[1~1];CellCspcPara[1~1];CellDacqCfg[1~1];CellDlIcic[1~1];CellDlpcPdcch[1~1];CellDlpcPdsch[1~1];CellDlpcPhich[1~1];CellDlschAlgo[1~1];CellDss[1~1];CellDynAcBarAlgoPara[1~1];CellDynPowerSharing[1~1];CellEABAlgoPara[1~1];CellEicic[1~1];CellEmergencyArea[0~16];CellEmtcAlgo[1~1];CellFrameOffset[0~1];CellIfInfo[0~1];CellLaaCoexistPara[0~1];CellLaaFcsPara[0~2];CellLcsCfg[1~1];CellLowPower[1~1];CellLteFlexBw[0~1];CellLteFlexBwItfCfg[0~6];CellMbfcs[1~1];CellMBMSCfg[1~1];CellMLB[1~1];CellMlbAutoGroup[1~1];CellMlbUeSel[1~1];CellMMAlgo[1~1];CellMro[1~1];CellNprsCfg[0~2];CellOp[0~6];CellPcAlgo[1~1];CellPdcchAlgo[1~1];CellPrbValMlb[1~1];CellPrsCfg[0~1];CellPucchAlgo[1~1];CellRachAlgo[1~1];CellRachCECfg[3~3];CellRacThd[1~1];CellRbReserve[0~100];CellRelayAlgoPara[0~1];CellResel[1~1];CellReselCdma2000[0~1];CellReselGeran[0~1];CellReselToNr[0~1];CellReselUtran[0~1];CellRfShutdown[1~1];CellRicAlgo[1~1];CellSel[1~1];CellServiceDiffCfg[1~1];CellShortTtiAlgo[1~1];CellShutdown[1~1];CellSidelinkV2xCfg[1~1];CellSiMap[1~1];CellSimuLoad[0~1];CellSLV2xCarrCfg[0~2];CellSrlte[1~1];CellTtiBundlingAlgo[1~1];CellUlIcic[1~1];CellULIcicMcPara[1~1];CellUlMimoParaCfg[1~1];CellUlpcComm[1~1];CellUlschAlgo[1~1];CellULUnifiedOLC[1~1];CellUSParaCfg[1~1];CellVMS[1~1];CellWttxParaCfg[1~1];CeRachCfg[4~4];VolteAlgoConfig[1~1];CLZeroBufferzone[1~1];CSFallBackHo[1~1];DMIMOAlgo[1~1];EmtcSibConfig[5~5];eNBCellRsvdPara[1~1];EuCellAlmCfg[1~1];EuCellPriBBEqm[0~1];eUCellSectorEqm[0~12];EuSectorEqmGroup[0~8];EutranBlkNCell[0~16];EutranInterFreqNCell[0~256];EutranInterNFreq[0~16];EutranIntraFreqNCell[0~256];GeranInterfCfg[1~1];GeranNcell[0~128];GeranNfreqGroup[0~16];HighSpdAdaptionPara[1~1];InterFreqBlkCell[0~32];InterRatCellShutdown[1~1];InterVendorMlbAlgo[1~1];IntraFreqBlkCell[0~32];NbCellDynPwrShrPara[1~1];NbCellMultiPrbConfig[1~1];NCellDlRsrpMeasPara[1~1];NCellParaCfg[4~4];NCellSrsMeasPara[1~1];NrNFreq[0~16];NrNRelationship[0~256];PCCHCfg[1~1];PDSCHCfg[1~1];PHICHCfg[1~1];Prb[0~8];PUCCHCfg[1~1];PUSCHCfg[1~1];RACHCfg[1~1];RruJointCalParaCfg[1~1];SfnEdgeRruRelation[0~2];SpectrumCloud[0~1];SRSCfg[1~1];SuperCombCell[0~1];TimeAlignmentTimer[1~1];UeCooperationPara[1~1];UeTimerConst[1~1];UlCsAlgoPara[1~1];UlInterfSuppressCfg[1~1];ULZeroBufferZone[1~1];UpPTSInterfCfg[1~1];UserVpfmPara[1~1];UtranBlkNCell[0~16];UtranNCell[0~128];UtranNFreq[0~16];VoiceAmrControl[0~4];WtcpProxyAlgo[1~1];CellIntrfCtrlParam[1~1];EmtcCeSchCfg[4~4];SymbolPwrSaving[1~1];CellUacBar[0~8];ContigIntraBandCarr[1~1];CellMultiCarrUniSch[1~1];CellIntelAmcConfig[1~1];CellMbsfnSfEnhConfig[1~1];CellEnhCoverage[1~1];AbnRrcAccessCfg[0~1];CellAutoShutdown[1~1];CellEdtConfig[1~1];CellSmartPwrLock[1~1];CaGroupSCellCfg[0~576];CaMgtCfg[1~1];CellBfMimoParaCfg[1~1];CellCqiAdaptiveCfg[1~1];CellDlCompAlgo[1~1];CellDlpcPdschPa[1~1];CellDmrsCfg[0~1];CellDrxPara[1~1];CellDrxSpecialPara[1~1];CellHoParaCfg[1~1];CellMcPara[1~1];CellMimoParaCfg[1~1];CellMlbHo[1~1];CellOpHoCfg[0~6];CellPreallocGroup[0~9];CellQciPara[15~75];CellSrsAdaptiveCfg[1~1];CellUciOnPuschPara[1~1];CellUeMeasControlCfg[1~1];CellUlCompAlgo[1~1];CellUlIcAlgo[1~1];CellUlpcDedic[1~1];CePucchCfg[4~4];DistBasedHO[1~1];DrxParaGroup[4~50];eNBCellQciRsvdPara[9~9];GeranInterfArfcn[0~20];InterFreqHoGroup[1~20];InterRatHoCdmaHrpdGroup[1~10];InterRatHoCommGroup[1~20];InterRatHoGeranGroup[1~20];InterRatHoNrParamGrp[1~10];InterRatHoUtranGroup[1~20];IntraFreqHoGroup[1~20];LwaMgtCfg[1~1];NsaDcMgmtConfig[1~1];QCIEUTRANRELATION[0~16];QCIGERANRELATION[0~32];QCIUTRANRELATION[0~16];RlfTimerConstGroup[0~10];UeDiffConfig[0~288];UeSpecDrxParaGroup[0~252];CellDataComprConfig[1~1];NsaDcQciParamGroup[1~10]
  • Relation with the PUSCHCfg MO:
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, the number of RB resources corresponding to the value of UlBandWidth in a Cell MO must be greater than or equal to the value of HoppingOffset in the PUSCHCfg MO. If UlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N6(1.4M), the number of RB resources is 6. If UlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N15(3M), the number of RB resources is 15. If UlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N25(5M), the number of RB resources is 25. If UlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N50(10M), the number of RB resources is 50. If UlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N75(15M), the number of RB resources is 75. If UlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N100(20M), the number of RB resources is 100.
    • The HoppingMode parameter in the PUSCHCfg MO can be set to INTRA_AND_INTER_SUB_FRAME only if the HighSpeedFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to LOW_SPEED.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, and the TxRxMode parameter in this MO is set to 8T8R or 32T32R, the HoppingMode parameter in the PUSCHCfg MO must be set to INTER_SUB_FRAME.
  • Relation with the PHICHCfg MO:
    • If CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd is set to CFG, CustomizedDLBandWidth is set to a value in the range of 44 to 47 or 80 or 89 to 95 or 183 to 192, and DlPuncturedRbNum is set to a value other than 0 in a Cell MO, PhichResource in the associated PHICHCfg MO cannot be set to TWO.
  • Relation with the EutranIntraFreqNCell and EutranExternalCell MOs:
    • Before adding an inter-eNodeB EutranIntraFreqNCell, ensure that the associated EutranExternalCell MO has been configured, and the NbCellFlag of the server Cell of the inter-eNodeB intra-frequency neighboring cell must be same with the NbCellFlag of EutranExternalCell.
    • The carrier frequency calculated based on DlEarfcn in the EutranExternalCell MO that represents an inter-eNodeB EutranIntraFreqNCell must be the same as the carrier frequency calculated based on DlEarfcn in the local Cell MO.
  • Relation with the EutranIntraFreqNCell MO:
    • Before adding an intra-eNodeB EutranIntraFreqNCell for a serving cell, ensure that a Cell MO corresponding to this intra-eNodeB intra-frequency cell has been configured, and the parameter NbCellFlag must be set to the same value for the two cells.
    • A maximum of eight EutranIntraFreqNCell MOs with AttachCellSwitch set to ON can be configured under a Cell MO.
    • The CGI specified by parameters in an EutranBlkNCell MO cannot be the same with the CGI specified by parameters in an EutranIntraFreqNCell MO. The CGI consists of the MCC, MNC, eNodeB ID, and cell ID. Two CGIs are considered the same when the four items are the same. In the EutranBlkNCell MO, the CGI is specified by Mcc, Mnc, eNodeBId, and CellId. In the EutranIntraFreqNCell MO, the CGI is specified by Mcc, Mnc, eNodeBId, and CellId.
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, the carrier frequency calculated based on DlEarfcn in the Cell MO that represents an intra-eNodeB EutranIntraFreqNCell must be the same as the carrier frequency calculated based on DlEarfcn in the local Cell MO.
  • Relation with the EutranInterFreqNCell, EutranExternalCell, CnOperator, eNodeBFunction, and ENodeBSharingMode MOs:
    • Before adding an inter-eNodeB EutranInterFreqNCell MO, ensure that the associated EutranExternalCell MO has been configured.
  • Relation with the EutranInterFreqNCell and EutranExternalCell MOs:
    • Before adding an inter-eNodeB EutranInterFreqNCell MO, ensure that the associated EutranExternalCell MO has been configured, and the NbCellFlag of the server Cell of the inter-eNodeB EutranInterFreqNCell MO must be same with the NbCellFlag of EutranExternalCell.
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, and DlEarfcn in the local Cell MO belongs to an LTE FDD frequency band, the carrier frequency calculated based on DlEarfcn in the EutranExternalCell MO that represents an inter-eNodeB EutranInterFreqNCell MO must be different from the carrier frequency calculated based on DlEarfcn in the local Cell MO.
    • Before adding an inter-eNodeB inter-frequency neighboring cell, ensure that the associated EutranExternalCell MO has been configured, and the NbCellFlag of the server Cell of the inter-eNodeB EutranInterFreqNCell MO must be same with the NbCellFlag of EutranExternalCell.
  • Relation with the EutranInterFreqNCell MO:
    • Before adding an intra-eNodeB EutranInterFreqNCell MO for a serving cell, ensure that a Cell MO corresponding to this intra-eNodeB inter-frequency cell has been configured, and the parameter NbCellFlag must be set to the same value for the two cells.
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, and DlEarfcn in the local Cell MO belongs to an LTE FDD frequency band, the carrier frequency calculated based on DlEarfcn in the Cell MO that represents an intra-eNodeB EutranInterFreqNCell MO must be different from the carrier frequency calculated based on DlEarfcn in the local Cell MO.
    • The CGI specified by parameters in an EutranBlkNCell MO cannot be the same with the CGI specified by parameters in an EutranInterFreqNCell MO. The CGI consists of the MCC, MNC, eNodeB ID, and cell ID. Two CGIs are considered the same when the four items are the same. In the EutranBlkNCell MO, the CGI is specified by Mcc, Mnc, eNodeBId, and CellId. In the EutranInterFreqNCell MO, the CGI is specified by Mcc, Mnc, eNodeBId, and CellId.
  • Relation with the EutranInterFreqNCell and EutranInterNFreq MOs:
    • DlEarfcn in the Cell MO corresponding to an intra-eNodeB EutranInterFreqNCell MO must be the same as DlEarfcn in an EutranInterNFreq MO.
  • Relation with the CellRicAlgo MO:
    • If CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd is CELL_TDD Model and DlBandWidth is CELL_BW_N25, RicAlgoSwitch in the CellRicAlgo must be set OFF.
    • The DuctDLSubfrmShutoffSwitch parameter in the CellRicAlgo MO can be set to ON(On) only when the FddTddInd and SubframeAssignment parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD(TDD) and SA2(SA2) respectively for a cell and the SpecialSubframePatterns parameter in the Cell MO is set to SSP5(SSP5), SSP6(SSP6), SSP7(SSP7), or SSP9(SSP9) for this cell.
    • When the DUCT_DL_SUBFRM_SHUTOFF_OPT_SW option of the DuctDLSubfrmShutoffOptSw parameter in a CellRicAlgo MO is selected, the following parameters in the associated Cell MO must be set to the specific values: Set FddTddInd to CELL_TDD, set SubframeAssignment to SA2, and set SpecialSubframePatterns to SSP5, SSP6, SSP7, or SSP9.
    • If the DUCT_DL_SF_SCHED_SHUTOFF_SW option of the DuctDLSubfrmShutoffOptSw parameter in the CellRicAlgo MO is selected, then in the Cell MO, the FddTddInd parameter must be set to CELL_TDD, the SubframeAssignment parameter must be set to SA2, and the SpecialSubframePatterns parameter must be set to SSP5, SSP6, SSP7, or SSP9.
  • Relation with the CellRfShutdown MO:
    • If CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd is CELL_TDD Model and DlBandWidth is CELL_BW_N25, RfShutdownSwitch in the CellRfShutdown must be set OFF.
    • If the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50, CELL_BW_N75, or CELL_BW_N100, the TxRxMode parameter in this Cell MO is set to 2T2R, 2T4R, 4T4R, 2T8R, or 4T8R, the FddTddInd parameter in this Cell MO is set toCELL_FDD, and the MultiRatJointChnShutdownSw parameter in the CellRfShutdown MO under this Cell MO is set to ON, then the RfShutdownSwitch parameter in this CellRfShutdown MO must be set to ON.
    • If the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to SFN, and a CellAuxEnbBind, or CellAuxDuBind MO is configured under this Cell MO, then the MultiRatJointChnShutdownSw parameter in the CellRfShutdown MO under this Cell MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the TxRxMode parameter in a Cell MO is set to 8T8R, 32T32R, or 64T64R, the MultiRatJointChnShutdownSw parameter in the CellRfShutdown MO under this Cell MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the MultiRruCellMode parameter in a Cell MO is set to MPRU_AGGREGATION, the MultiRatJointChnShutdownSw parameter in the CellRfShutdown under this Cell MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch MO:
    • If CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd is CELL_TDD Model and DlBandWidth is CELL_BW_N25, UlJointReceptionSwitch under UplinkCompSwitch and BfAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set OFF.
    • If CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and MultiRruCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and MultiRruCellMode in a Cell MO is set to DIGITAL_COMBINATION, UlJointReceptionSwitch under UplinkCompSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be turned off.
    • The SFNPDCCHDCSSWITCH option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the SfnDlSchSwitch parameter is set to ADAPTIVE.
    • DlCompSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be set to On only if HighSpeedFlag in a Cell MO is set to LOW_SPEED.
    • If CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd is CELL_TDD Model and DlBandWidth is CELL_BW_N25, DlCompSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch must be set OFF.
    • PsicSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be set to On only if the FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the TxRxMode in a Cell MO is set to 1T2R or 2T2R.
    • If TxRxMode in a Cell MO is set to 2T8R, UlVmimoSwitch under UlSchSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be turned off.
    • If TxRxMode in a Cell MO is set to 2T8R, DlCompSwitch under DlCompSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be turned off.
    • In a Cell, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, then the AntCalibrationAlgoSwitch in CellAlgoSwitch could not turn on CrossPolaCalibrationSwitch.
    • AntCalibrationAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch could turn on CrossPolaCalibrationSwitch only if TxRxMode in a Cell MO is set to 4T4R.
    • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, if SmartIrcSwitch under PuschIrcAlgoSwitch is turned on, FddTddInd must be set to TDD.
    • In a CellAlgoSwitch, if EnhMIMOSwitch turn on TM9Switch or TM10Switch, DlCyclicPrefix must be set to NORMAL_CP.
    • In a Cell, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_TDD and in a CellAlgoSwitch, if EnhMIMOSwitch turn on TM9Switch, DlBandWidth must be set to CELL_BW_N50 or CELL_BW_N75 orCELL_BW_N100.
    • In a Cell, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_TDD and in a CellAlgoSwitch, if EnhMIMOSwitch turn on TM9Switch, SubframeAssignment must be set to SA1 or SA2 or SA5.
    • In a Cell, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_TDD and in a CellAlgoSwitch, and EnhMIMOSwitch turn on TM9Switch, then TxRxMode is 4T4R or TxRxMode is 8T8R or TxRxMode is 64T64R or TxRxMode is 32T32R, BfAlgoSwitch must be set to turn on BfSwitch.
    • In a CellAlgoSwitch, if EnhMIMOSwitch turn on TM9Switch, HighSpeedFlag must be set to LOW_SPEED or HIGH_SPEED.
    • In a Cell, only when HighSpeedFlag is set to LOW_SPEED, IntraBaseBandSwitch and InterBaseBandSwitch under SfnUplinkCompSwitch in CellAlgoSwitch can be configured to on.
    • If the TM9Switch or TM10Switch option of EnhMIMOSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under a Cell MO is selected, CrsPortNum in the Cell MO must be set to CRS_PORT_4 (valid only for FDD), CRS_PORT_2, or CRS_PORT_1.
    • In a Cell MO, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, then the SfnPdcchSdmaSwitch option of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected.
    • If TxRxMode in a Cell MO is set to 4T8R, UlVmimoSwitch under UlSchSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be turned off.
    • If TxRxMode in a Cell MO is set to 4T8R, DlCompSwitch under DlCompSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be turned off.
    • In a Cell MO, if the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD, the MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, and the MultiRruCellMode is set to CELL_COMBINATION, the ComCoverUlCompSwitch option can be selected for the SfnUplinkCompSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to InterDlCompCbfSwitch or InterDlCompDcsSwitch, the EicicSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be set to STATIC.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to InterDlCompCbfSwitch or InterDlCompDcsSwitch, the HighSpeedFlag parameter in the Cell MO must be set to LOW_SPEED.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to InterDlCompCbfSwitch, the BfSwitch option of BfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the InterDlCompCbfSwitch option of DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO must be set to CELL_COMBINATION.
    • If FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, EicicSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO associated with this Cell MO cannot be set to DYNAMIC.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to InterDlCompCbfSwitch, the DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO must be set to a value greater than CELL_BW_N25.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to InterDlCompCbfSwitch, the DlCyclicPrefix parameter in the Cell MO must be set to NORMAL_CP.
    • If the DlCyclicPrefix parameter in the Cell MO is set to EXTENDED_CP, the BfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be turned off.
    • If the DlCyclicPrefix parameter in the Cell MO is set to EXTENDED_CP, the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be turned off.
    • If HighSpeedFlag in a Cell MO is set to LOW_SPEED, SuperCombCellSwitch of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If SectorEqmNum in a Cell MO is set to smaller than 3, SuperCombCellSwitch of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • The SuperCombCellSwitch option of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only if FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, MultiRruCellMode in a Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD, BOOLEAN_TRUE, SFN, respectively.
    • If CellPIMInterMitigSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ON, FddTddInd in the Cell MO associated with this CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to CELL_FDD.
    • If CellPIMInterMitigSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ON and MultiRruCellFlag in the Cell MO associated with this CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, MultiRruCellMode in this Cell MO must be set to SFN.
    • When the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, if CellRadius in a Cell MO is set to a value greater than 100000 m, UlJointReceptionSwitch, UlJointReceptionPhaseIISwitch, UlJointReception3CellSwitch, and UlHetnetJointReceptionSwitch of UplinkCompSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO associated with this Cell MO must be deselected.
    • When the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, if CellRadius in a Cell MO is set to a value greater than 100000 m, MumimoPuschPsicSwitch of PsicSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO associated with this Cell MO must be deselected.

    • The AvoidPrachInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter can be selected when the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD.
    • The AvoidPrachInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter can be selected when the MultiRruCellFlag parameter is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE or when the MultiRruCellFlag parameter is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and the MultiRruCellMode parameter is set to CELL_COMBINATION.
    • The AvoidPrachInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter can be selected when the HighSpeedFlag parameter is set to LOW_SPEED.
    • The AvoidPrachInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter can be selected when the value of the PreambleFmt parameter is smaller than 4.

    • The AvoidPrachInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter can be selected when the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N50, CELL_BW_N75, or CELL_BW_N100.

    • In the Cell MO, if the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD and the UlCamcSw option is selected under the CamcSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, note the following: The HighSpeedFlag parameter can only be set to LOW_SPEED. (2) If the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 2T2R or 2T4R, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO must be set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to SFN or MPRU_AGGREGATION.     • The TDOA_SWITCH option of the LbsSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the MultiRruCellMode, and HighSpeedFlag parameters in the associated Cell MO are set to MPRU_AGGREGATION, and LOW_SPEED, respectively.

In other situations, the PucchMeasOptSwitch option cannot be selected.

    • In a Cell MO, when the NbCellFlag, MultiRruCellFlag, MultiRruCellMode parameters are set to TRUE, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and SFN or MPRU_AGGREGATION, respectively, the UlJointReceptionSwitch option of the UplinkCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.

(c) The MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to MPRU_AGGREGATION, the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 2T2R, the CrsPortNum parameter in the Cell MO is set to CRS_PORT_4, the OuterLoopPucchSwitch option of the UlPcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, and the InnerLoopPucchSwitch option of the UlPcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.

(b) If the CrsPortNum parameter in the Cell MO is CRS_PORT_4, the TxRxMode parameter in this MO must be set to 2T2R, 2T4R, 4T4R, 4T8R, 8T8R, or 32T32R.

    • For an LTE TDD cell, if the ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch option of the UlSuMimoAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO must not be set to CELL_BW_N6(1.4M), CELL_BW_N15(3M), or CELL_BW_N25(5M). The SU-MIMO feature takes effect only when the cell bandwidth is 10 MHz, 15 MHz, or 20 MHz.

    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the VoltePreemptionSwitch option of the RacAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.

The SU-MIMO feature takes effect only when the cell bandwidth is 10 MHz, 15 MHz, or 20 MHz.
    • The InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected if the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE.

    • If the TM9Pusch12Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under a Cell MO is selected, the following requirements must be met:
    • (1) The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO must be set to CELL_FDD.
    • (2) The New4TCodebookSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter must be deselected.
    • (3) The TxRxMode parameter must be set to 4T4R, 4T8R, or 32T32R.

    • The SmallBandOptSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be set to ON only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N6 or CELL_BW_N15 or CELL_BW_N25.
  • Relation with the CellUlschAlgo and PUSCHCfg MOs:
    • Let A be the number of RB resources corresponding to the value of UlBandWidth in a Cell MO, B the even number that HoppingOffset in the PUSCHCfg MO is rounded up to, and C the difference between A and B. When the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, if UlHoppingType in the CellUlschAlgo MO is set to HOPPING_TYPE1, C must be greater than A modulo 2. If UlHoppingType in the CellUlschAlgo MO is set to HOPPING_TYPE2 or HOPPING_TYPE2_RANDOM, and Nsb in the PUSCHCfg MO is greater than 1, the integer value of C divide Nsb in the PUSCHCfg MO must be greater than 0. If UlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N6(1.4M), the number of RB resources is 6. If UlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N15(3M), the number of RB resources is 15. If UlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N25(5M), the number of RB resources is 25. If UlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N50(10M), the number of RB resources is 50. If UlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N75(15M), the number of RB resources is 75. If UlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N100(20M), the number of RB resources is 100.
  • Relation with the Cell MO:
    • If FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, a maximum of six CellAuxEnbBind and CellAuxDuBind MOs can be configured under the Cell MO. If FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, a maximum of five CellAuxEnbBind and CellAuxDuBind MOs can be configured in total under the Cell MO.
    • In intra-eNodeB scenarios, the CGI of a blacklisted neighboring cell represented by an EutranBlkNCell MO cannot be the same as the CGI of the serving cell represented by the associated Cell MO. The CGI consists of the MCC, MNC, eNodeB ID, and cell ID. Two CGIs are considered the same when the four items are the same. The CGI of a blacklisted neighboring cell is specified by Mcc, Mnc, eNodeBId, and CellId in an EutranBlkNCell MO. The CGI of the serving cell is specified by Mcc and Mnc in an associated CnOperator MO, eNodeBId in the eNodeBFunction MO, and CellId in the Cell MO.
    • The CGI of the local cell must be different from that of any intra-eNodeB intra-frequency neighboring cell. A CGI is comprised of Mcc, Mnc, eNodeBId and CellId. Two CGIs are considered to be the same if they have an identical combination of the four parameters. The CGI of an intra-frequency neighboring cell is comprised of Mcc, Mnc, eNodeBId, and CellId in the EutranIntraFreqNCell MO. The CGI of the local cell is comprised of Mcc and Mnc in one associated CnOperator MO, eNodeBId in the associated eNodeBFunction MO, and CellId in the Cell MO.
    • The CGI of the local cell must be different from that of any intra-eNodeB inter-frequency neighboring cell. A CGI is comprised of Mcc, Mnc, eNodeBId and CellId. Two CGIs are considered to be the same if they have an identical combination of the four parameters. The CGI of an inter-frequency neighboring cell is comprised of Mcc, Mnc, eNodeBId, and CellId in the EutranInterFreqNCell MO. The CGI of the local cell is comprised of Mcc and Mnc in one associated CnOperator MO, eNodeBId in the associated eNodeBFunction MO, and CellId in the Cell MO.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, EuSectorEqmGroup, EuPrbSectorEqm, and EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MOs:
    • In a Cell MO, if MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, MultiRruCellMode is set to SFN, and the value of CellActiveState is CELL_ACTIVE: When NbCellFlag is set to FALSE, SectorEqmNum must be set to a value greater than or equal to the sum of the numbers of eUCellSectorEqm MOs and EuSectorEqmGroup MOs under this Cell MO; when NbCellFlag is set to TRUE, SectorEqmNum must be set to a value greater than or equal to the sum of the numbers of EuPrbSectorEqm MOs and EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MOs under all Prb MOs that are under this Cell MO.
    • When FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, MultiRruCellFlag and MultiRruCellMode in the Cell MO are set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and SFN, if NbCellFlag is set to FALSE, at most eight eUCellSectorEqm and EuSectorEqmGroup MOs can be configured under the Cell MO, if NbCellFlag is set to TRUE, at most sixteen EuPrbSectorEqm and EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MOs can be configured under the Cell MO.
    • In a Cell MO, if NbCellFlag is TRUE, eUCellSectorEqm and EuSectorEqmGroup cannot be configured under the Cell, if NbCellFlag is FALSE, EuPrbSectorEqm and EuPrbSectorEqmGroup cannot be configured under the Cell.
  • Relation with the CellAuxDuBind MO:
    • If MultiRruCellMode is not set to SFN, CellAuxDuBind with the same LocalCellId cannot be exist.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm MO:
    • One or two eUCellSectorEqm instances can be configured for a Cell MO instance in which the MultiRruCellFlag parameter is not configured and the CellActiveState, FddTddInd, NbCellFlag, and TxRxMode parameters are set to CELL_ACTIVE, CELL_FDD, FALSE, and 4T4R, respectively. When the settings of these parameters in the Cell MO instance differ from the preceding settings, only one eUCellSectorEqm instance can be configured.
    • In a Cell MO, if CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, and MultiRruCellMode is set to TWO_RRU_COMBINATION, two eUCellSectorEqm MOs must be configured under this Cell MO.
    • In a Cell MO, if CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, and MultiRruCellMode is set to DIGITAL_COMBINATION, the number of eUCellSectorEqm MOs configured under this Cell MO ranges from 2 to 8.
    • In the Cell MO, BaseBandEqmId must be set to the same value if CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, MultiRruCellFlag to BOOLEAN_TRUE, and MultiRruCellMode to CELL_COMBINATION for the cells under the Cell MO, and the following condition is met: UlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N25.
    • When CellActiveState and FddTddInd in a Cell MO are set to CELL_ACTIVE and CELL_TDD, respectively, a minimum of one and a maximum of seven eUCellSectorEqm MOs must be configured under the Cell MO if MultiRruCellFlag and MultiRruCellMode in the Cell MO are set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and SFN, respectively.
    • If FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, MultiRruCellMode is not set to SFN, SectorCpriCompression in eUCellSectorEqm can be set to NULL only.
    • If FddTddInd is set to CELL_TDD, MultiRruCellMode is not set to SFN or CELL_COMBINATION, SectorCpriCompression in eUCellSectorEqm can be set to NULL only.
    • If FddTddInd is set to CELL_TDD, MultiRruCellMode is set to SFN or CELL_COMBINATION, and DlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N25, SectorCpriCompression in eUCellSectorEqm can be set to NULL or NO_COMPRESSION.
    • When the value of the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, if the value of the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD, and the BaseBandEqmId in the eUCellSectorEqm MO or BaseBandEqmId parameter in the EuSectorEqmGroup MO corresponding to the Cell MO is configured, the SubframeAssignment parameter must be set to the same value for the cells for which the CellActiveState parameter in the corresponding Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE.
    • If FddTddInd in Cell is set to CELL_FDD, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, and MultiRruCellMode is set to SFN. If DlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N6 or CELL_BW_N15, SectorCpriCompression in eUCellSectorEqm in Cell can be set to NULL or NO_COMPRESSION. If DlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N25, SectorCpriCompression in eUCellSectorEqm in Cell can be set to NULL, NO_COMPRESSION or NORMAL_COMPRESSION. If DlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N50, CELL_BW_N75, CELL_BW_N100, SectorCpriCompression in eUCellSectorEqm in Cell can be set to NULL, NO_COMPRESSION, NORMAL_COMPRESSION or ENHANCED_COMPRESSION.
    • When the CellActiveState parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is not configured, and multiple eUCellSectorEqm MO instances are configured for this Cell MO, the ReferenceSignalPwr, BaseBandEqmId, SectorCpriCompression, AutoCfgFlag, and VisualCellId parameters in these eUCellSectorEqm MO instances must be set to the same values.
    • When the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to ADVANCED_BEAMFORMING, the FddTddInd and TxRxMode parameters in the Cell MO must be set to CELL_FDD and 4T4R, respectively.
    • When the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 or MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the DlCyclicPrefix and UlCyclicPrefix parameters in the associated Cell MO must be set to NORMAL_CP.
    • If the CellBeamMode parameter in an eUCellSectorEqm MO under a Cell MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1, the CellRadius parameter in this Cell MO cannot be set to a value greater than 15000.
    • When the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1, the FddTddInd parameter in Cell MO must be set to CELL_FDD.
    • When the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1, the TxRxMode parameter in the associated Cell MO must be set to 4T4R or 8T8R.
    • When the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 and the TxRxMode parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to 4T4R, the CrsPortNum and CrsPortMap parameters in the Cell MO must be set to CRS_PORT_4 and 4T4P_0321 or NOT_CFG, respectively.
    • If the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 or MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the associated Cell MO must be set to BOOLEAN_FALSE.
    • If the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 or MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the HighSpeedFlag parameter in the associated Cell MO must be set to LOW_SPEED.
    • When the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the DlBandWidth parameter in the associated Cell MO must not be set to CELL_BW_N6 or CELL_BW_N15.
    • When SectorCpriCompression in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to TRIPLEX_COMPRESSION_10M, FddTddInd in the associated Cell MO must be set to CELL_FDD, and DlBandWidth and UlBandWidth in this Cell MO must be set to CELL_BW_N50.
    • When the CellActiveState in Cell is set to CELL_ACTIVE and the CellBeamMode parameter in eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 and the TxRxMode parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to 8T8R, or the CellBeamMode parameter in eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the SectorEqmCombineGrpId in the eUCellSectorEqm MO must be invalid value.
    • When the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the FddTddInd and TxRxMode parameters in the Cell MO must be set to CELL_FDD and 32T32R, respectively, the CPRICompression parameter in the Cell MO must be set based on the setting of UlBandWidth in the Cell MO. (1) If the UlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N100 or CELL_BW_N75, the CPRICompression parameter must be set to NORMAL_COMPRESSION, ENHANCED_COMPRESSION, or 3_2_COMPRESSION. (2) If the UlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N50, the CPRICompression parameter must be set to NORMAL_COMPRESSION, ENHANCED_COMPRESSION, or TRIPLEX_COMPRESSION_10M. (3) If the UlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N25, the CPRICompression parameter must be set to NORMAL_COMPRESSION.
    • When the FddTddInd and TxRxMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and 32T32R, respectively, the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO must be set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2.
    • If the CellBeamMode parameter in an eUCellSectorEqm MO under a Cell MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the CellRadius parameter in the Cell MO cannot be set to a value greater than 77300 when the PreambleFmt parameter in this Cell MO is set to 1 or a value greater than 15000 when the PreambleFmt parameter in this Cell MO is set to a value other than 1.
    • If the CellBeamMode parameter in an eUCellSectorEqm MO under a Cell MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the CellRadius parameter in the Cell MO cannot be set to a value greater than 77300 when the PreambleFmt parameter in this Cell MO is set to 1 or a value greater than 15000 when the PreambleFmt parameter in this Cell MO is set to a value other than 1.
    • In a Cell MO, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, MultiRruCellMode is set to MPRU_AGGREGATION and CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be associated with one TX antenna, two TX antennas, or four TX antennas (the preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER), one RX antenna, two RX antennas, or four RX antennas.
    • In a Cell MO, if CellActiveState is CELL_ACTIVE and MultiRruCellMode is not set to CELL_COMBINATION, and MultiRruCellMode is not set to SFN, the total number of TX antennas and the total number of RX antennas are calculated as follows: If TxRxMode is set to 1T1R or 2T2R and WorkMode is set to UL_DL, the total number of TX antennas (The preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER) and that of RX antennas associated with the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be equal to the number of TX antennas and that of RX antennas specified by the value of TxRxMode, respectively. If TxRxMode is set to 1T1R or 2T2R and WorkMode is set to DL_ONLY, the total number of TX antennas (The preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER) associated with the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be equal to 0 or the number of TX antennas specified by the value of TxRxMode, and the total number of RX antennas associated with this SECTOREQM MO must be equal to the number of RX antennas specified by the value of TxRxMode.
    • In the Cell MO, if CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, TxRxMode is set to 8T8R, and CrsPortNum is set to CRS_PORT_2, CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm MO associated with this cell must be set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1.
    • If the SectorCpriCompression parameter in an eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to 3_2_COMPRESSION, then in the corresponding Cell MO, the FddTddInd parameter must be set to CELL_TDD and the UlBandWidth parameter must be set to CELL_BW_N75 or CELL_BW_N100.
    • If the value of CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd and TxRxMode in this Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and 8T8R, respectively, and CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to NORMAL, then CellRadius in this Cell MO must be set to a value less than or equal to 15000.
    • If the value of CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd and TxRxMode in this Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and 8T8R, respectively, and CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to NORMAL, then SectorEqmCombineGrpId in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be set to an invalid value.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm and EuSectorEqmGroup MOs:
    • In a Cell MO, if CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, and MultiRruCellMode is set to CELL_COMBINATION, the number of eUCellSectorEqm MOs configured under this Cell MO ranges from 1 to 12. If the EuSectorEqmGroup MO is configured under this Cell MO, the number of eUCellSectorEqm MO instances, and the number of EuSectorEqmGroup instances must be less than 13.
    • In a Cell MO, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_TDD and MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and MultiRruCellMode is set to MPRU_AGGREGATION, the number of EuSectorEqmGroup plus the number of different values of SectorEqmRef between eUCellSectorEqm and EuSectorEqmGroup must be less than 8.
    • In a Cell MO, if CellActiveState, NbCellFlag and MultiRruCellMode are set to CELL_ACTIVE, FALSE and MPRU_AGGREGATION, respectively, the eUCellSectorEqm MO or EuSectorEqmGroup MO under this Cell MO must have been configured.
    • In a Cell MO, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD and MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and MultiRruCellMode is set to MPRU_AGGREGATION, the number of EuSectorEqmGroup plus the number of different values of SectorEqmRef between eUCellSectorEqm and EuSectorEqmGroup must be less than 7.
    • When CellActiveState and FddTddInd in a Cell MO are set to CELL_ACTIVE and CELL_FDD, respectively, one to six eUCellSectorEqm and EuSectorEqmGroup MOs must be configured under the Cell MO if MultiRruCellFlag and MultiRruCellMode in the Cell MO are set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and SFN and NbCellFlag is set to FALSE, respectively.
    • When the value of the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, if the value of the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD, and the BaseBandEqmId in the eUCellSectorEqm MO or BaseBandEqmId parameter in the EuSectorEqmGroup MO corresponding to the Cell MO is configured, the SubframeAssignment parameter must be set to the same value for the cells for which the CellActiveState parameter in the corresponding Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE.
    • In a Cell MO, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, NbCellFlag is set to FALSE, CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE and MultiRruCellMode is set to SFN, the eUCellSectorEqm MO or EuSectorEqmGroup MO under this Cell MO must have been configured.
    • In a Cell MO, if the CellActiveState parameter is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, the MultiRruCellMode parameter is set to CELL_COMBINATION, and an EuSectorEqmGroup MO has been configured, then the sum of the EuSectorEqmGroup and eUCellSectorEqm MO instances configured in the Cell MO must be less than 7.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm and EuPrbSectorEqm MOs:
    • A maximum of eight eUCellSectorEqm or sixteen EuPrbSectorEqm can be configured for each Cell in which MultiRruCellMode is not set to MPRU_AGGREGATION or CELL_COMBINATION.
  • Relation with the EuSectorEqmGroup and EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MOs:
    • EuSectorEqmGroup MOs can be configured under a Cell MO only when FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, and MultiRruCellMode in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and MPRU_AGGREGATION or CELL_COMBINATION, respectively. EuSectorEqmGroup and EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MOs can be configured under a Cell MO only when FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, and MultiRruCellMode in the Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and MPRU_AGGREGATION or SFN, respectively.
  • Relation with the CellMLB MO:
    • In a Cell, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, then the DlDataMlbMode in CellMLB could only be set to NON_TRIGGERED.
  • Relation with the CellCspcPara MO:
    • If the CellCspcSwitch in the CellCspcPara MO is ON, HighSpeedFlag in the Cell MO cannot be set to HIGH_SPEED or ULTRA_HIGH_SPEED.
    • When the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, if CellRadius in a Cell MO is set to a value greater than 100000 m, CellCspcSwitch in the CellCspcPara MO under this Cell MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the CelleCspcSwitch in the CellCspcPara MO is ON, HighSpeedFlag in the Cell MO cannot be set to HIGH_SPEED, ULTRA_HIGH_SPEED, or EXTRA_HIGH_SPEED.
    • When the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, if CellRadius in a Cell MO is set to a value greater than 15000m, CelleCspcSwitch in the CellCspcPara MO under this Cell MO must be set to OFF.
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, CellCspcSwitch in the CellCspcPara must be set OFF.
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, CelleCspcSwitch in the CellCspcPara must be set OFF.
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, IntraEnbCspcSw in the CellCspcPara must be set OFF.
    • The CelleCspcSwitch parameter in the CellCspcPara MO can be set to ON only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the MultiCellShareMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to NORMAL.
  • Relation with the CellDss and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • When the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, if GLDynSpectrumShareSwitch under DynSpectrumShareSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is turned on, the frequency supporting the sum of RB quantities specified by HighFreqShareRbNum and LowFreqShareRbNum in the CellDss MO cannot exceed one third of the value of DlBandWidth in the Cell.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch and ENodeBAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • If GLDynSpectrumShareSwitch under DynSpectrumShareSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is turned on, DlIcicSwitch in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO and CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd in the Cell MO must be set to DlIcicSwitch_OFF_ENUM and NOT_CFG, respectively.
    • In the Cell MO, if the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD and the HighOrderVMIMOSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter is selected, the UlIcicFreqSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF.
    • If GLZeroBufferZoneSwitch under DynSpectrumShareSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is turned on, DlIcicSwitch in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO and CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd in the Cell MO must be set to DlIcicSwitch_OFF_ENUM and NOT_CFG, respectively.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the UL_ICS_SWITCH option under the UlIcSwitch parameter is selected, the UlIcicFreqSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF.
    • If FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the AvoidSpurEmissInterfSwitch option of AvoidInterfSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO associated with the Cell MO is selected, UlIcicFreqSwitch in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO associated with the Cell MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the MultiCellShareMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to SPLIT and the SsrdSchOptSwitch option of the SSRDAlgoSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch option of the UlSuMimoAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the CellDlschAlgo MO:
    • If FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, SubFrame0, SubFrame4, SubFrame5, SubFrame9 in MbsfnSfCfg in CellDlschAlgo cannot be configured to 1.
    • In the Cell MO, if the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD and the SubframeAssignment parameter is set to SA0 or SA3, the SubFrame0(SubFrame0), SubFrame1(SubFrame1), SubFrame2(SubFrame2), SubFrame3(SubFrame3), SubFrame4(SubFrame4), SubFrame5(SubFrame5), SubFrame6(SubFrame6), SubFrame7(SubFrame7), SubFrame8(SubFrame8), and SubFrame9(SubFrame9) options under the MbsfnSfCfg parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO cannot be selected.
    • In the Cell MO, if the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD and the SubframeAssignment parameter is set to SA1, the SubFrame0(SubFrame0), SubFrame1(SubFrame1), SubFrame2(SubFrame2), SubFrame3(SubFrame3), SubFrame5(SubFrame5), SubFrame6(SubFrame6), SubFrame7(SubFrame7), or SubFrame8(SubFrame8) option of the MbsfnSfCfg parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO cannot be selected.
    • In the Cell MO, if the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD and the SubframeAssignment parameter is set to SA2, the SubFrame0(SubFrame0), SubFrame1(SubFrame1), SubFrame2(SubFrame2), SubFrame5(SubFrame5), SubFrame6(SubFrame6), or SubFrame7(SubFrame7) option of the MbsfnSfCfg parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO cannot be selected.
    • In the Cell MO, if the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD and the SubframeAssignment parameter is set to SA5, the SubFrame0(SubFrame0), SubFrame1(SubFrame1), SubFrame2(SubFrame2), SubFrame5(SubFrame5), or SubFrame6(SubFrame6) option of the MbsfnSfCfg parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO cannot be selected.
    • If the UnCfgCsiRsUESchSwitch option of the CsiRsSfSchStrSwitch parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO is set to open, the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO must be set to CELL_TDD.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the RbgAllocStrategy parameter in the CELLDLSCHALGO MO cannot be set to TYPE1_FIRST.
    • The EdgeBeamThld parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO under a Cell MO can be set to a value other than 255 only when the FddTddInd parameter in this Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the TxRxMode parameter in this Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R.
    • The PilotOffRateIncreaseSw parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO can be set to NOT_BASED_ON_MEASUREMENT only when the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the DL_INTRA_JAMC_ALGO_SW option of the DlJointAMCAlgoSw parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO is selected, the HighSpeedFlag parameter in the Cell MO must be set to LOW_SPEED.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the DL_INTRA_JAMC_ALGO_SW option of the DlJointAMCAlgoSw parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO is selected, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO must be set to BOOLEAN_FALSE.
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the DL_INTRA_JAMC_ALGO_SW option of the DlJointAMCAlgoSw parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO can be selected only if the MultiCellShareMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to NORMAL.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch and CellUlschAlgo MOs:
    • UlHoppingType in a CellUlschAlgo is not set to HOPPING_OFF, SfnUplinkCompSwitch in CellAlgoSwitch can only be configured to off.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the UlCamcSw option is selected under the CamcSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the UlHoppingType parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO can only be set to HOPPING_OFF.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the UlInterEnbCamcSw option is selected under the CamcSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the UlHoppingType parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO can only be set to HOPPING_OFF.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the UL_ICS_SWITCH option under the UlIcSwitch parameter is selected, the UlHoppingType parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO must be set to HOPPING_OFF.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch, eUCellSectorEqm, EuSectorEqmGroup, CellAuxEnbBind, and CellAuxDuBind MOs:
    • Assume that: The FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, and MultiRruCellMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and SFN, respectively, the AsfnFastAttSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, and the CellAuxDuBind, and CellAuxEnbBind MOs are not configured. Then: The TM9Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when either of the following conditions is met: (a) The SfnDlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to JOINT. (b) The sum of the number of eUCellSectorEqm MO instances and the number of EuSectorEqmGroup MO instances is 2 and the SfnDlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ADAPTIVE.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch, eUCellSectorEqm, and EuSectorEqmGroup MOs:
    • Assume that: The FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, and MultiRruCellMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and MPRU_AGGREGATION, respectively, and the AsfnFastAttSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. Then: The TM9Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when either of the following conditions is met: (a) The SfnDlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to JOINT. (b) The sum of the number of eUCellSectorEqm MO instances and the number of EuSectorEqmGroup MO instances is 2 and the SfnDlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ADAPTIVE.
  • Relation with the RACHCfg MO:
    • When the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD: If the value of the PreambleFmt parameter is Type0 and the value of the SubframeAssignment parameter is SA0, the value of the PrachConfigIndex parameter must range from 0 to 8. If the value of the PreambleFmt parameter is Type0 and the value of the SubframeAssignment parameter is SA1, the value of the PrachConfigIndex parameter must range from 0 to 7. If the value of the PreambleFmt parameter is Type0 and the value of the SubframeAssignment parameter is SA2, the value of the PrachConfigIndex parameter must range from 0 to 4 or be 6. If the value of the PreambleFmt parameter is Type0 and the value of the SubframeAssignment parameter is SA5, the value of the PrachConfigIndex parameter must range from 0 to 1 or must be 3. If the value of the PreambleFmt parameter is Type1, the SubframeAssignment parameter cannot be set to SA2 or SA5. If the value of the PreambleFmt parameter is Type1 and the value of the SubframeAssignment parameter is SA0, the value of the PrachConfigIndex parameter must range from 20 to 25. If the value of the PreambleFmt parameter is Type1 and the value of the SubframeAssignment parameter is SA1, the value of the PrachConfigIndex parameter must range from 20 to 25. If the value of the PreambleFmt parameter is Type2, the SubframeAssignment parameter cannot be set to SA2 or SA5. If the value of the PreambleFmt parameter is Type2 and the value of the SubframeAssignment parameter is SA0, the value of the PrachConfigIndex parameter must range from 30 to 34. If the value of the PreambleFmt parameter is Type2 and the value of the SubframeAssignment parameter is SA1, the value of the PrachConfigIndex parameter must range from 30 to 34.
    • When the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD and the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE: If the value of the PreambleFmt parameter is Type3 and the value of the SubframeAssignment parameter is SA0, the value of the PrachConfigIndex parameter must range from 40 to 44. If the value of the PreambleFmt parameter is Type4, the value of the HighSpeedFlag parameter must be LOW_SPEED. If the value of the PreambleFmt parameter is Type4 and the value of the SubframeAssignment parameter is SA0, the value of the PrachConfigIndex parameter must range from 48 to 53. If the value of the PreambleFmt parameter is Type4 and the value of the SubframeAssignment parameter is SA1, the value of the PrachConfigIndex parameter must range from 48 to 53. If the value of the PreambleFmt parameter is Type4 and the value of the SubframeAssignment parameter is SA2, the value of the PrachConfigIndex parameter must range from 48 to 53. If the value of the PreambleFmt parameter is Type4 and the value of the SubframeAssignment parameter is SA5, the value of the PrachConfigIndex parameter must be 48, 49, or 51.
    • In a Cell MO, if the value of the CellActiveState parameter is CELL_ACTIVE, the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_FDD, and the NbCellFlag parameter is set a value other than FALSE, then:
    • When the PreambleFmt parameter is set to Type0, the PrachConfigIndex parameter in the RACHCfg MO must be set to 15 or a value in the range of 0 to 8.
    • When the PreambleFmt parameter is set to Type1, the PrachConfigIndex parameter in the RACHCfg MO must be set to 31 or a value in the range of 16 to 24.
    • When the PreambleFmt parameter is set to Type2, the PrachConfigIndex parameter in the RACHCfg MO must be set to 47 or a value in the range of 32 to 37.
    • When the PreambleFmt parameter is set to Type3, the PrachConfigIndex parameter in the RACHCfg MO must be set to 63 or a value in the range of 48 to 53.
    • Assume that the values of the CellActiveState and NbCellFlag parameters in the Cell MO for a cell are CELL_ACTIVE and FALSE, respectively, and the value of the PrachFreqOffsetStrategy parameter in the RACHCfg MO for this cell is MANUAL. If the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_FDD, or if the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD and the PreambleFmt parameter is set to a value other than 4, the PrachFreqOffset in the RACHCfg MO cannot be set to a value that causes the frequency-domain position of the PRACH to exceed the system bandwidth.
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the value of the NbRsrpFirstThreshold parameter in the RACHCfg MO associated with the Cell MO must be greater than or equal to the NbRsrpSecondThreshold parameter value.
    • The PreambInitRcvTargetPwr parameter in the RACHCfg MO can be set to one of the following values only if the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to TRUE: DBM_130(-130dBm), DBM_128(-128dBm), DBM_126(-126dBm), DBM_124(-124dBm), DBM_122(-122dBm), DBM_88(-88dBm), DBM_86(-86dBm), DBM_84(-84dBm), DBM_82(-82dBm), and DBM_80(-80dBm).
  • Relation with the EuSectorEqmGroup MO:
    • When the value of the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, if the value of the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD, and the BaseBandEqmId in the eUCellSectorEqm MO or BaseBandEqmId parameter in the EuSectorEqmGroup MO corresponding to the Cell MO is configured, the SubframeAssignment parameter must be set to the same value for the cells for which the CellActiveState parameter in the corresponding Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE.
    • Consider that FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, and MultiRruCellMode in a Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and SFN, respectively, SectorGroupCpriCompression in EuSectorEqmGroup MOs under this Cell MO must be set based on the setting of DlBandWidth in the Cell MO. (1) If DlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N6 or CELL_BW_N15, SectorGroupCpriCompression must be set to NULL or NO_COMPRESSION. (2) If DlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N25, SectorGroupCpriCompression must be set to NULL, NO_COMPRESSION, or NORMAL_COMPRESSION. (3) If DlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N50, CELL_BW_N75, or CELL_BW_N100, SectorGroupCpriCompression must be set to NULL, NO_COMPRESSION, NORMAL_COMPRESSION, or ENHANCED_COMPRESSION.
    • When SectorGroupLinkCompression in the EuSectorEqmGroup MO is set to a value other than NULL, DlBandWidth must be set to CELL_BW_N75 or CELL_BW_N100.
    • When the SectorGroupCpriCompression parameter in the EuSectorEqmGroup MO is set to QUADPLEX_COMPRESSION, the DlBandWidth parameter in this Cell MO must be set to CELL_BW_N75 or CELL_BW_N100.
    • If FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, MultiRruCellMode, and CellActiveState in a Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD, BOOLEAN_TRUE, CELL_COMBINATION, and CELL_ACTIVE, respectively, and the EuSectorEqmGroup MO is configured under this Cell MO, the TxRxMode in this Cell MO are set to 2T2R.
    • In a Cell MO, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, MultiRruCellMode is set to MPRU_AGGREGATION or SFN, and CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each EuSectorEqmGroup MO under this Cell MO must be associated with one TX antenna (The preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER) or two TX antennas, one RX antenna or two RX antennas.
    • If WorkMode and MultiRruCellFlag in a Cell MO are set to LAA and BOOLEAN_TRUE, respectively, MultiRruCellMode in this Cell MO must be set to MPRU_AGGREGATION. In addition, only one EuSectorEqmGroup MO can be configured for this Cell MO and this EuSectorEqmGroup MO can contain a maximum of four pieces of cell sector equipment.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch and CellRicAlgo MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the InterDlCompCbfSwitch option of the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the MuteUpPTSSymNum parameter in the CellRicAlgo MO must be set to 0.
  • Relation with the CellCsiRsParaCfg and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd and MultiRruCellFlag parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and BOOLEAN_TRUE respectively and the CsiRsSwitch parameter in the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO is set to FIXED_CFG or ADAPTIVE_CFG, the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to CELL_COMBINATION, SFN, or MPRU_AGGREGATION.
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the same MO is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE, if TM9Switch(TM9Switch) under the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter is selected in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the CsiRsSwitch parameter in the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO is not set to NOT_CFG, and the CsiRsPortNum parameter in the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO is set to CSI_RS_PORT_8, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to 8T8R or 32T32R or 64T64R.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm and PDSCHCfg MOs:
    • In a Cell MO, if CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and MultiRruCellMode is set to MPRU_AGGREGATION, the number of valid values of ReferenceSignalPwr in eUCellSectorEqm must be not more than that of ReferenceSignalPwr in PDSCHCfg.
    • When the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 and the TxRxMode parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to 4T4R, the SINGLE_CA_PWR_SHARE_SW_FOR_4T option of the TxChnPowerCfgSw parameter in the PDSCHCfg MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the SfnEdgeRruRelation MO:
    • A maximum of two SfnEdgeRruRelation MO with the same Cell MO.
  • Relation with the SfnEdgeRruRelation, eUCellSectorEqm, and eNodeBFunction MOs:
    • If eNodeBId in the SfnEdgeRruRelation MO is equal to eNodeBId in the eNodeBFunction MO, the Cell MO corresponding to NeighborCellId must already exist and be other cells, and eUCellSectorEqm MO belong to this cell corresponding to NeighborSectorEqmId should exist.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch, PIMCellGroup, and ENodeBAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • If CellPIMInterMitigSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ON and CellActiveState in the Cell MO associated with this CellAlgoSwitch MO is CELL_ACTIVE, LocalCellId in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must have been set in a PIMCellGroup MO. This constraint applies only when the PIM_ADP_CELL_SEL_SWITCH option of the PimSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is deselected.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell and CellUlschAlgo MOs:
    • The EnhancedVmimoSwitch parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO can be set to ON only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 4T4R, 8T8R, 32T32R, or 64T64R, and no SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and a SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, the MmUlHeavyLoadOptSwitch option of the VmimoOptAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • For a Cell with the FddTddInd parameter set to CELL_TDD, the HighOrderVMIMOSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 32T32R or 64T64R and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, or when the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 4T4R or 8T8R.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, the UlVmimoSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and no SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, the BfSwitch option of the BfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
    • The DL_TIME_DIM_EXT_SCH_SWITCH option of the DlSchExtSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the FddTddInd and TxRxMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD(TDD) and 64T64R or 32T32R respectively and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is not configured and the MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the NoSrsSccBfSwitch option of the NoSrsSccBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, either of the following conditions must be met: 1. The TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R. 2. The TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 32T32R or 64T64R, and no SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO.
    • The SplitSdmaSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, the MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and no SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO.
  • Relation with the CellPdcchAlgo MO:
    • If CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd in a Cell MO is set to CFG, the EpdcchFunctionSwitch option of EpdcchAlgoSwitch in the CellPdcchAlgo MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
    • If the MASSIVE_MIMO_PDCCH_MEAS_SW option of the SplitBeamPdcchSdmaSw parameter in the CellPdcchAlgo MO is selected, the CrsPortNum parameter in the Cell MO cannot be set to CRS_PORT_1.
    • The MM_HEAVY_LOAD_ECFI_ADAPT_ON option of the PdcchSymNumSwitch parameter in the CellPdcchAlgo MO can be selected only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R.
    • For an LTE TDD cell, if the MASSIVE_MIMO_PDCCH_BF_SW option of the PdcchBeamformingSwitch parameter in the CellPdcchAlgo MO is selected, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to 64T64R or 32T32R.
    • For an LTE TDD cell, the MASSIVE_MIMO_PDCCH_BF_SW option of the PdcchBeamformingSwitch parameter in the CellPdcchAlgo MO and the MASSIVE_MIMO_PDCCH_SDMA_SW option of the SplitBeamPdcchSdmaSw parameter in the CellPdcchAlgo MO must not be selected at the same time.
    • If the DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50 or CELL_BW_N75 or CELL_BW_N100, the EpdcchFunctionSwitch option of the EpdcchAlgoSwitch parameter only can be selected.
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the EpdcchFunctionSwitch option of the EpdcchAlgoSwitch in the CellPdcchAlgo MO must be deselected.
    • If the MASSIVE_MIMO_PDCCH_SDMA_SW option of the SplitBeamPdcchSdmaSw parameter in the CellPdcchAlgo MO is selected and the PdcchSdmaWeightType parameter in the CellPdcchAlgo MO is set to POWER_SHARING_AMONG_3_BEAMS, POWER_SHARING_AMONG_4_BEAMS, or POWER_SHARING_AMONG_5_BEAMS, the CrsPortNum parameter in the Cell MO cannot be set to CRS_PORT_1.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the PdcchPowerEnhancedSwitch parameter in the CellPdcchAlgo MO under this Cell MO must not be set to ON_EXPANDED_RANGE or ON_BASIC_RANGE.
  • Relation with the SRSCfg and CellUSParaCfg MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_FDD, Options of SfCtrlAsSCell and SfCtrlAsInterNCell of the UsAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellUSParaCfg MO can be selected only when the SrsSubframeCfg parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to SC3 and the SrsCfgInd parameter is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, or the SrsCfgInd parameter is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE.
  • Relation with the CellSimuLoad MO:
    • If TxRxMode in a Cell MO is set to 1T1R or 1T2R, SimuLoadTransMode in a CellSimuLoad MO under this Cell MO must be set to TM1.
    • Consider that MultiRruCellMode in a Cell MO is not set to CELL_COMBINATION or MPRU_AGGREGATION. If CrsPortNum in the Cell MO is set to CRS_PORT_1, SimuLoadTransMode in a CellSimuLoad MO under this Cell MO cannot be set to TM4.
    • If FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and MultiRruCellMode in the same Cell MO is set to CELL_COMBINATION or MPRU_AGGREGATION and the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of EmimoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, SimuLoadTransMode in the CellSimuLoad MO cannot be set to TM4 or TM9.
  • Relation with the CellSimuLoad and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • Consider that MultiRruCellMode in a Cell MO is not set to CELL_COMBINATION or MPRU_AGGREGATION. If the TM9Switch option of EnhMIMOSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO is deselected, SimuLoadTransMode in a CellSimuLoad MO under this Cell MO cannot be set to TM9.
  • Relation with the CellSimuLoad and SimuloadPmiTemplate MOs:
    • Consider that SimuLoadTransMode in a CellSimuLoad MO is set to TM9. If TxRxMode in the corresponding Cell MO is set to 2T2R, 2T4R, or 2T8R, CellSimuloadPmi in a SimuloadPmiTemplate MO under this CellSimuLoad MO must be set to a value in the range of 0 to 3.
    • If SimuLoadTransMode in a CellSimuLoad MO is set to TM4 and CrsPortNum in the related Cell MO is set to CRS_PORT_2, CellSimuloadPmi in associated SimuloadPmiTemplate MOs must be set to a value ranging from 0 to 3.
  • Relation with the CellPdcchAlgo and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • In a TDD cell, if the MSG5Attempt option of HLNetAccSigAttempt in the CellPdcchAlgo MO is selected, HighSpeedFlag in the corresponding Cell MO must be set to LOW_SPEED, the PuschDtxSchOptSwitch option of UlSchSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected, and PDCCHAggLvlAdaptStrage in the CellPdcchAlgo MO must be set to STRATEGYBASEDONCAPACITY.
  • Relation with the EutranBlkNCell MO:
    • In intra-eNodeB scenarios, before an intra-eNodeB cell is configured as a blacklisted neighboring cell (represented by the EutranBlkNCell MO) of the serving cell, the Cell MO representing the intra-eNodeB cell must be configured first.
    • In intra-eNodeB scenarios, the downlink EARFCN and physical cell ID of a blacklisted neighboring cell represented by the EutranBlkNCell MO cannot be the same as values of DlEarfcn and PhyCellId in the Cell MO representing the serving cell at the same time.
    • A maximum of 16 EutranBlkNCell MOs can be configured for a cell.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch and NCellSrsMeasPara MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the UL_ICS_SWITCH option under the UlIcSwitch parameter is selected, the SrsAutoNCellMeasSwitch parameter in the NCellSrsMeasPara MO must be set to ON.
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to TRUE and the UlJointReceptionSwitch option of the UplinkCompSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the NCellDmrsMeasSwitch option of the NCellMeasSwitch parameter in the associated NCellSrsMeasPara MO must be selected.
  • Relation with the CLZeroBufferzone and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • When the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, if the AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch option of AvoidInterfSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the value of ClSharedFreqEndRb1 in the CLZeroBufferzone MO must be less than the maximum number of RBs corresponding to the bandwidth represented by UlBandWidth in the associated Cell MO. The UlBandWidth parameter can be set to CELL_BW_N6, CELL_BW_N15, CELL_BW_N25, CELL_BW_N50, CELL_BW_N75, or CELL_BW_N100, corresponding to the maximum number of RBs 6, 15, 25, 50, 75, or 100, respectively.
    • When the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, if the AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch option of AvoidInterfSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the value of ClSharedFreqEndRb2 in the CLZeroBufferzone MO must be less than the maximum number of RBs corresponding to the bandwidth represented by UlBandWidth in the associated Cell MO. The UlBandWidth parameter can be set to CELL_BW_N6, CELL_BW_N15, CELL_BW_N25, CELL_BW_N50, CELL_BW_N75, or CELL_BW_N100, corresponding to the maximum number of RBs 6, 15, 25, 50, 75, or 100, respectively.
  • Relation with the CellRacThd MO:
    • If CellCapacityMode in the CellRacThd MO is set to SMALLCAPACITY, FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, and MultiRruCellMode in the related Cell MO must be set to CELL_FDD, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and SFN or MPRU_AGGREGATION, respectively.
  • Relation with the DdCellGroup MO:
    • Cell MOs corresponding to LocalCellId1 and LocalCellId2 in a DdCellGroup MO must have been configured. LocalCellId1 and LocalCellId2 must be set to different values.
    • One Cell MO can be configured under only one DdCellGroup MO.
    • When the value of FreqBand in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId1 in a DdCellGroup MO is equal to the value of FreqBand in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId2 in the DdCellGroup MO, the difference between values of DlEarfcn in the two Cell MOs and the difference between values of UlEarfcn in the two Cell MOs must simultaneously be one of the following values under related conditions: (1) 12 when the value of DdBandwidth is BW_2DOT6M. (2) 18 when the value of DdBandwidth is BW_4M. (3) 27 when the value of DdBandwidth is BW_5DOT7M. (4) 33 when the value of DdBandwidth is BW_7DOT3M. (5) 36 when the value of DdBandwidth is BW_7DOT6M. (6) 51 when the value of DdBandwidth is BW_10DOT8M. (7) 57 when the value of DdBandwidth is BW_12DOT2M. (8) 60 when the value of DdBandwidth is BW_12DOT5M. (9) 81 when the value of DdBandwidth is BW_17DOT1M. (10) 84 when the value of DdBandwidth is BW_17DOT4M. (11) 126 when the value of DdBandwidth is BW_27DOT6M. (12) 135 when the value of DdBandwidth is BW_28DOT5M. (13) 114 when the value of DdBandwidth is BW_23DOT9M. (14) 36 when the value of DdBandwidth is BW_8DOT6M. (15) 39 when the value of DdBandwidth is BW_8DOT9M. (16) 66 when the value of DdBandwidth is BW_14DOT1M. (17) 69 when the value of DdBandwidth is BW_14DOT4M. (18) 87 when the value of DdBandwidth is BW_18DOT7M. (19) 90 when the value of DdBandwidth is BW_19M.
    • When the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, and the value of FreqBand in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId1 in a DdCellGroup MO is equal to the value of FreqBand in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId2 in the DdCellGroup MO, the following formulas are met: (1) Value of DlBandWidth in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId1 x 1/2 + Center frequency spacing + Value of DlBandWidth in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId2 x 1/2 = Value of DdBandwidth in the DdCellGroup MO(2) Value of UlBandWidth in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId1 x 1/2 + Center frequency spacing + Value of UlBandWidth in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId2 x 1/2 = Value of DdBandwidth in the DdCellGroup MO
    • The value of DlCyclicPrefix and the value of UlCyclicPrefix in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId1 in a DdCellGroup MO must be equal to the value of DlCyclicPrefix and the value of UlCyclicPrefix in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId2 in the DdCellGroup MO, respectively.
    • FddTddInd, NbCellFlag in the Cell MOs corresponding to LocalCellId1 and LocalCellId2 in a DdCellGroup MO must be set to CELL_FDD, FALSE respectively.
    • If CustomizedDLBandWidth in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId1 or LocalCellId2 in a DdCellGroup MO is set to a value in the ranges of 44~47, 88~95, 139~145, or 183~192, DlPuncturedRbNum must be set to a zero.
    • If MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE in the Cell MO instances for the cells specified by LocalCellId1 and LocalCellId2 in a DdCellGroup MO instance, MultiRruCellMode can be set only to SFN in the Cell MO instances.
    • When the following conditions are met: (1) DdBandwidth in a DdCellGroup MO is set toBW_4M, BW_7DOT3M, or BW_12DOT2M; (2) In the Cell MO that corresponds to LocalCellId2 and whose value of DlEarfcn is smaller than the value of DlEarfcn in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId1, DlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N15(3M); (3) the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, PhyCellId in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId2 must be set to a value in any of the following ranges: 0~5, 9~12, 18~20, 26~27, 30~31, 35, 38~40, 45~48, 53~57, 61~65, 70~72, 78~80, 83, 87, 90~92, 98~100, 105~109, 113~117, 122~125, 130~132, 135, 139~140, 143~144, 150~152, 158~161, 165~170, 174~177, 182~185, 191~192, 195~196, 200, 203~204, 210~213, 218~222, 226~230, 234~237, 243~245, 248, 252, 255~256, 260, 263~265, 270~274, 278~282, 286~290, 295~297, 300, 304~305, 308, 312, 315~317, 323~326, 330~334, 338~342, 347~350, 356~357, 360, 364~365, 368~369, 375~378, 383~386, 390~395, 399~402, 408~410, 416~417, 420~421, 425, 428~430, 435~438, 443~447, 451~455, 460~462, 468~470, 473, 477, 480~482, 488~490, 495~499, and 503.
    • When the following conditions are met: (1) DdBandwidth in a DdCellGroup MO is set toBW_4M, BW_7DOT3M, or BW_12DOT2M; (2) In the Cell MO that corresponds to LocalCellId1 and whose value of DlEarfcn is smaller than the value of DlEarfcn in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId2, DlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N15(3M); (3) the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, PhyCellId in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId1 must be set to a value in any of the following ranges: 0~5, 9~12, 18~20, 26~27, 30~31, 35, 38~40, 45~48, 53~57, 61~65, 70~72, 78~80, 83, 87, 90~92, 98~100, 105~109, 113~117, 122~125, 130~132, 135, 139~140, 143~144, 150~152, 158~161, 165~170, 174~177, 182~185, 191~192, 195~196, 200, 203~204, 210~213, 218~222, 226~230, 234~237, 243~245, 248, 252, 255~256, 260, 263~265, 270~274, 278~282, 286~290, 295~297, 300, 304~305, 308, 312, 315~317, 323~326, 330~334, 338~342, 347~350, 356~357, 360, 364~365, 368~369, 375~378, 383~386, 390~395, 399~402, 408~410, 416~417, 420~421, 425, 428~430, 435~438, 443~447, 451~455, 460~462, 468~470, 473, 477, 480~482, 488~490, 495~499, and 503.

    • When the following conditions are met: (1) DdBandwidth in a DdCellGroup MO is set to BW_2DOT6M, BW_4M, BW_5DOT7M, BW_7DOT3M, BW_7DOT6M, BW_10DOT8M, BW_12DOT2M, BW_12DOT5M, BW_17DOT1M, BW_17DOT4M, BW_8DOT6M, BW_8DOT9M, BW_14DOT1M, BW_18DOT7M, BW_19M, or if BW_27DOT6M and UlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50; (2) Between Cell MOs corresponding to LocalCellId1 and LocalCellId2 in the DdCellGroup MO, CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd in the Cell MO is set to CFG. CustomizedULBandWidth in the Cell MO meeting the second condition cannot be set to a value in any of the following ranges: 44~47, 89~95, 139~145, 183~192.
    • When the following conditions are met: (1) NbCellFlag in the Cell MO that corresponds to LocalCellId1 and LocalCellId2 are set to FALSE; (2) DdBandwidth in a DdCellGroup MO is set to BW_8DOT6M or BW_18DOT7M; (3) In the Cell MO that corresponds to LocalCellId2 and whose value of DlEarfcn is smaller than the value of DlEarfcn in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId1, DlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N25(5M). PhyCellId in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId2 must be set to a value in any of the following ranges: 0~7, 16~19, 31~32, 50~53, 63~68, 77~82, 92~94, 113~114, 125~129, 138~144, 154~157, 169, 175, 188~191, 200~206, 215~219, 230~232, 250~252, 263~267, 276~282, 292~294, 307, 313, 325~329, 338~344, 353~357, 368~369, 375, 388~390, 400~405, 414~419, 430~432, 450~451, 463~467, 476~482, 491~494.
    • When the following conditions are met: (1) NbCellFlag in the Cell MO that corresponds to LocalCellId1 and LocalCellId2 are set to FALSE; (2) DdBandwidth in a DdCellGroup MO is set to BW_8DOT6M or BW_18DOT7M; (3) In the Cell MO that corresponds to LocalCellId1 and whose value of DlEarfcn is smaller than the value of DlEarfcn in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId2, DlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N25(5M). PhyCellId in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId1 must be set to a value in any of the following ranges: 0~7, 16~19, 31~32, 50~53, 63~68, 77~82, 92~94, 113~114, 125~129, 138~144, 154~157, 169, 175, 188~191, 200~206, 215~219, 230~232, 250~252, 263~267, 276~282, 292~294, 307, 313, 325~329, 338~344, 353~357, 368~369, 375, 388~390, 400~405, 414~419, 430~432, 450~451, 463~467, 476~482, 491~494.
    • When the following conditions are met: (1) DdBandwidth in a DdCellGroup MO is set to BW_8DOT6M or BW_8DOT9M; (2) In the Cell MO that corresponds to LocalCellId2 and whose value of DlEarfcn is larger than the value of DlEarfcn in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId1, DlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N25(5M). PhyCellId in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId2 must be set to a value in any of the following ranges: 6~12, 22~24, 37, 56~58, 69~73, 83~87, 98~99, 119, 131~134, 144~149, 160~162, 194~196, 206~211, 221~224, 236~237, 256~257, 269~272, 282~287, 298~299, 331~334, 344~349, 359~362, 374, 394~395, 406~410, 420~424, 436~437, 456, 469~472, 482~487, 497~499.
    • When the following conditions are met: (1) DdBandwidth in a DdCellGroup MO is set to BW_8DOT6M or BW_8DOT9M; (2) In the Cell MO that corresponds to LocalCellId2 and whose value of DlEarfcn is larger than the value of DlEarfcn in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId1, DlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N25(5M).
