PhyCellId in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId2 must be set to a value in any of the following ranges: 6~12, 22~24, 37, 56~58, 69~73, 83~87, 98~99, 119, 131~134, 144~149, 160~162, 194~196, 206~211, 221~224, 236~237, 256~257, 269~272, 282~287, 298~299, 331~334, 344~349, 359~362, 374, 394~395, 406~410, 420~424, 436~437, 456, 469~472, 482~487, 497~499.
    • When the following conditions are met: (1) NbCellFlag in the Cell MO that corresponds to LocalCellId1 and LocalCellId2 are set to FALSE; (2) DdBandwidth in a DdCellGroup MO is set to BW_18DOT7M; (3) In the Cell MO that corresponds to LocalCellId2 and whose value of DlEarfcn is larger than the value of DlEarfcn in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId1, DlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N25(5M). PhyCellId in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId2 must be set to a value in any of the following ranges: 5~12, 21~24, 36~37, 55~58, 68~73, 82~87, 97~99, 118~119, 130~134, 143~149, 159~162, 174, 180, 193~196, 205~211, 220~224, 235~237, 255~257, 268~272, 281~287, 297~299, 312, 318, 330~334, 343~349, 358~374, 380, 393~395, 405~410, 419~424, 435~437, 455~456, 468~472, 481~487, 496~499.
    • When the following conditions are met: (1) NbCellFlag in the Cell MO that corresponds to LocalCellId1 and LocalCellId2 are set to FALSE; (2) DdBandwidth in a DdCellGroup MO is set to BW_18DOT7M; (3) In the Cell MO that corresponds to LocalCellId1 and whose value of DlEarfcn is larger than the value of DlEarfcn in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId2, DlBandWidth is set to CELL_BW_N25(5M). PhyCellId in the Cell MO corresponding to LocalCellId1 must be set to a value in any of the following ranges: 5~12, 21~24, 36~37, 55~58, 68~73, 82~87, 97~99, 118~119, 130~134, 143~149, 159~162, 174, 180, 193~196, 205~211, 220~224, 235~237, 255~257, 268~272, 281~287, 297~299, 312, 318, 330~334, 343~349, 358~374, 380, 393~395, 405~410, 419~424, 435~437, 455~456, 468~472, 481~487, 496~499.
    • If the CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CFG, the CustomizedDLBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO corresponding to the LocalCellId1 or LocalCellId2 parameter in the DdCellGroup MO cannot be set to 74, 75, 87, or 175-178.
    • If DdBandwidth in a DdCellGroup MO is set to BW_28DOT5M, BW_27DOT6M, or BW_23DOT9M, DlBandWidth and UlBandWidth in only one of the Cell MOs in which the LocalCellId parameters are set to the same values as the LocalCellId1 and LocalCellId2 parameters in the DdCellGroup MO can be set to CELL_BW_N100.
    • If DdBandwidth in a DdCellGroup MO is set to BW_14DOT1M, BW_14DOT4M, BW_18DOT7M, or BW_19M, DlBandWidth and UlBandWidth in only one of the Cell MOs in which the LocalCellId parameters are set to the same values as the LocalCellId1 and LocalCellId2 parameters in the DdCellGroup MO can be set to CELL_BW_N25.
    • In a Cell MO, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, TxRxMode is set to 8T8R, and MultiCellShareMode is set to SPLIT, the cell must not be in the cell group indicated by the DdCellGroup MO.
  • Relation with the DdCellGroup and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • The GLDynSpectrumShareSwitch option of DynSpectrumShareSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected when the cell corresponding to LocalCellId1 or LocalCellId2 in a DdCellGroup MO meets the following requirements: (1) DdBandwidth is set to BW_5DOT7M; (2) DdBandwidth is set to BW_7DOT3M; (3) DdBandwidth is set to BW_7DOT6M; (4) DdBandwidth is set to BW_8DOT6M; (5) DdBandwidth is set to BW_8DOT9M; (6) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_4M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15; (7) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_12DOT2M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15; (8) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_17DOT1M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15; (9) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_27DOT6M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50; (10) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_28DOT5M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50. (11) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_14DOT1M, BW_18DOT7M or BW_19M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25.
    • The GLZeroBufferZoneSwitch option of DynSpectrumShareSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected when the cell corresponding to LocalCellId1 or LocalCellId2 in a DdCellGroup MO meets the following requirements: (1) DdBandwidth is set to BW_5DOT7M; (2) DdBandwidth is set to BW_7DOT3M; (3) DdBandwidth is set to BW_7DOT6M; (4) DdBandwidth is set to BW_8DOT6M; (5) DdBandwidth is set to BW_8DOT9M; (6) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_4M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15; (7) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_12DOT2M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15; (8) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_17DOT1M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15; (9) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_27DOT6M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50; (10) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_28DOT5M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50; (11) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_14DOT1M, BW_18DOT7M or BW_19M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25.
    • The AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch option of AvoidInterfSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected when the cell corresponding to LocalCellId1 or LocalCellId2 in a DdCellGroup MO meets the following requirements: (1) DdBandwidth is set to BW_5DOT7M; (2) DdBandwidth is set to BW_7DOT3M; (3) DdBandwidth is set to BW_7DOT6M; (4) DdBandwidth is set to BW_8DOT6M; (5) DdBandwidth is set to BW_8DOT9M; (6) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_4M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15; (7) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_12DOT2M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15; (8) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_17DOT1M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15; (9) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_27DOT6M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50; (10) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_28DOT5M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50; (11) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_14DOT1M, BW_18DOT7M or BW_19M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25.
  • Relation with the DdCellGroup and CellPdcchAlgo MOs:
    • The EpdcchFunctionSwitch option of EpdcchAlgoSwitch in the CellPdcchAlgo MO must be deselected when the cell corresponding to LocalCellId1 or LocalCellId2 in a DdCellGroup MO meets the following requirements: (1) DdBandwidth is set to BW_5DOT7M; (2) DdBandwidth is set to BW_7DOT3M; (3) DdBandwidth is set to BW_7DOT6M; (4) DdBandwidth is set to BW_8DOT6M; (5) DdBandwidth is set to BW_8DOT9M; (6) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_4M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15; (7) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_12DOT2M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15; (8) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_17DOT1M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15; (9) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_27DOT6M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50; (10) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_28DOT5M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50; (11) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_14DOT1M, BW_18DOT7M or BW_19M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch and CellAlgoExtSwitch MOs:
    • When 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch of EmimoSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO or Tm9FourLayerPairSwitch of WttxMuMimoSwitch in a CellAlgoExtSwitch MO is selected, FddTddInd in the associated Cell MO must be set to CELL_FDD.
    • When Virtual4T4RSwitch or 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch of EmimoSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO or Tm9FourLayerPairSwitch of WttxMuMimoSwitch in a CellAlgoExtSwitch MO is selected, DlCyclicPrefix and UlCyclicPrefix in the associated Cell MO must be set to NORMAL_CP.
    • When New4TCodebookSwitch of EmimoSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch of EmimoSwitch in the same CellAlgoSwitch MO and Tm9FourLayerPairSwitch of WttxMuMimoSwitch in a CellAlgoExtSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When HighSpeedFlag in a Cell MO is set to HIGH_SPEED, ULTRA_HIGH_SPEED, or EXTRA_HIGH_SPEED, 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch of EmimoSwitch in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO and Tm9FourLayerPairSwitch of WttxMuMimoSwitch in a CellAlgoExtSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N6 or CELL_BW_N15, the 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO and Tm9FourLayerPairSwitch of WttxMuMimoSwitch in a CellAlgoExtSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch of EmimoSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO or Tm9FourLayerPairSwitch of WttxMuMimoSwitch in a CellAlgoExtSwitch MO is selected, TxRxMode in the associated Cell MO must be set to 4T4R or 4T8R, or 32T32R and the CrsPortNum parameter must be set to CRS_PORT_4.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch and EuSectorEqmGroup MOs:
    • The TxRxMode parameter in a Cell MO must be set to 2T2R or 4T4R when the following conditions are met: The Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the MultiRruCellFlag and MultiRruCellMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and MPRU_AGGREGATION, respectively. If the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 4T4R, only one EuSectorEqmGroup MO instance can be configured for this Cell MO.
    • If MultiRruCellFlag in an Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and MultiRruCellMode is set to MPRU_AGGREGATION, only one set of cell sector equipment can be configured in each EuSectorEqmGroup MO for the cell when the DMIMOAlgoSwitch option under DMIMOAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
  • Relation with the SsrdCellGroup MO:
    • When the primary cell is in the active state, the UlCyclicPrefix, DlCyclicPrefix, FreqBand, DlEarfcn, DlBandWidth, SubframeAssignment, SpecialSubframePatterns, and CPRICompression parameters related to the PrimaryLocalCellID parameter must be set to the same values as the soft split cell specified by the SsrdLocalCellID parameter, and the cells corresponding to the PrimaryLocalCellID and SsrdLocalCellID parameters are configured under the same sector equipment specified by the SECTOREQM.
    • When the primary cell is in the active state, the UlCyclicPrefix, DlCyclicPrefix, FreqBand, DlEarfcn, DlBandWidth, SubframeAssignment, SpecialSubframePatterns, and CPRICompression parameters related to the PrimaryLocalCellID parameter must be set to the same values as the soft split cell specified by the SsrdLocalCellID parameter, and the cells corresponding to the PrimaryLocalCellID and SsrdLocalCellID parameters are configured under the same sector equipment specified by the SECTOREQM.
    • When an SsrdCellGroup MO is to be added, the cells specified by the PrimaryLocalCellID must have been configured in the Cell MO and the MultiCellShareMode parameter for these cells must be set to SPLIT.
    • When an SsrdCellGroup MO is to be added, the cells specified by the SsrdLocalCellID parameters must have been configured in the Cell MO and the MultiCellShareMode parameter for these cells must be set to SPLIT.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the MultiCellShareMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to SPLIT, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to a value other than 32T32R or 64T64R, and the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, then the PrimaryLocalCellID or SsrdLocalCellID parameter in the SsrdCellGroup MO with the same value as the LocalCellId parameter in the Cell MO must be configured.
    • If the PrimaryLocalCellID parameter in the SsrdCellGroup MO corresponds to an activated cell, the CellActiveState parameter for the cell specified by the SsrdLocalCellID parameter must be set to CELL_ACTIVE(Active).
    • When a cell corresponding to a split cell is to be activated, the UlCyclicPrefix, DlCyclicPrefix, FreqBand, DlEarfcn, DlBandWidth, SubframeAssignment, SpecialSubframePatterns, and CPRICompression parameters related to the SsrdLocalCellID parameter must be set to the same values as the cell specified by the PrimaryLocalCellID parameter, and the cells corresponding to the SsrdLocalCellID and the PrimaryLocalCellID parameter are configured under the same SECTOREQM MO.
    • When a cell corresponding to a split cell is to be activated, the UlCyclicPrefix, DlCyclicPrefix, FreqBand, DlEarfcn, DlBandWidth, SubframeAssignment, SpecialSubframePatterns, and CPRICompression parameters related to the SsrdLocalCellID parameter must be set to the same values as the cell specified by the PrimaryLocalCellID parameter, and the cells corresponding to the SsrdLocalCellID and the PrimaryLocalCellID parameter are configured under the same SECTOREQM MO.
  • Relation with the CellMimoParaCfg MO:
    • If CellRadius in a Cell MO is set to a value greater than 100000 m and TxRxMode in the same Cell MO is set to 4T4R or 4T8R, MimoAdaptiveSwitch in the associated CellMimoParaCfg MO must be set to OL_ADAPTIVE or NO_ADAPTIVE. If MimoAdaptiveSwitch in the CellMimoParaCfg MO is set to NO_ADAPTIVE, FixedMimoMode in the CellMimoParaCfg MO must be set to TM2 or TM3.
    • The 8T_TM9_MU-MIMO_SW option of the MimoSwitch parameter in a CellMimoParaCfg MO can be selected only when the FddTddInd and TxRxMode parameters in the associated Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and 8T8R, respectively.
    • LocalCellId in a CellMimoParaCfg MO must have been set in the associated Cell MO.
  • Relation with the PIMCellGroup MO:
    • If PimMitigationMode in a PIMCellGroup MO instance is set to BASED_ON_JOINT_SCH, CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd cannot be set to CFG for the cells specified by Cell1Id and Cell2Id in the PIMCellGroup MO instance.
  • Relation with the SRSCfg and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • The SrvBasedSRSAdjAlgo option of the SrsCfgPolicySwitch parameter in the SRSCfg MO can be selected only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and no SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO.
    • The SrsInterfAvoidOptSwitch parameter in the SRSCfg MO can be set to a value other than OFF only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and no SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO.
    • The WTTxSRSIntrfAvoidanceSw option of the SrsCfgPolicySwitch parameter in the SRSCfg MO can be selected only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and no SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO is set to NOT_CFG, then the TddSrsCfgMode parameter in the SRSCfg MO must be set to ACCESS_ENHANCED.
  • Relation with the SRSCfg MO:
    • The TddSrsCfgMode parameter in the SRSCfg MO must be set to ACCESS_ENHANCED(Access Enhanced) when the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R.
    • If FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, SrsSubframeCfg in the associated SRSCfg MO must be smaller than SC14.
    • If the values of the CellActiveState, FddTddInd, and DlBandWidth parameters in the Cell MO for a cell are CELL_ACTIVE, CELL_TDD, and CELL_BW_N6 or CELL_BW_N15, respectively, the TddSrsCfgMode parameter in the SRSCfg MO for this cell must be set to ACCESS_FIRST or EXPERIENCE_FIRST.
    • When the SrsResOptSwitch parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to ON(On), the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to 2T2R .
  • Relation with the CellBf MO:
    • The HighOrderMubfMaxLayer parameter in the CellBf MO can be set to EIGHT_LAYERS, TWELVE_LAYERS, or SIXTEEN_LAYERS only when the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R.
    • When the FddTddInd and TxRxMode parameters in a Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and 16T16R, respectively, the HighOrderMubfMaxLayer parameter in the CellBf MO can be set to TWO_LAYERS, FOUR_LAYERS, or EIGHT_LAYERS.
    • The HighOrderMubfMaxLayer parameter in the CellBf MO can be set to SIX_LAYERS, TEN_LAYERS, FOURTEEN_LAYERS, EIGHTEEN_LAYERS, TWENTY_LAYERS, TWENTYTWO_LAYERS, or TWENTYFOUR_LAYERS only if the FddTddInd and TxRxMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and 64T64R, respectively.
    • The HighOrderMubfMaxLayer parameter in the CellBf MO can be set to TWO_LAYERS, FOUR_LAYERS, SIX_LAYERS, EIGHT_LAYERS, TEN_LAYERS, TWELVE_LAYERS, FOURTEEN_LAYERS, and SIXTEEN_LAYERS only if the FddTddInd and TxRxMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and 32T32R respectively.
    • The LARGE_DELAY_SPREAD_UE_IDENT_SW option of the UeIdentificationSwitch parameter in the CellBf MO can be selected only when the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 32T32R or 64T64R.
    • For a Cell MO with the TxRxMode parameter set to 64T64R or 32T32R and the FddTddInd parameter set to CELL_TDD: If the MovingUeMuBfScheme parameter in the CellBf MO is set to BIG_EVENT_PROTECT_SCHEME, SPLIT_SDMA_SCHEME, or HIGH_SPEED_OPT_SCHEME, the SRS_LMT_AND_ABN_UE_IDENT_SW option of the UeIdentificationSwitch parameter in the CellBf MO must be selected. If the MovingUeMuBfScheme parameter in the CellBf MO is set to HEAVY_LOAD_SCHEME or LIGHT_LOAD_SCHEME, the SRS_LMT_AND_ABN_UE_IDENT_SW option of the UeIdentificationSwitch parameter in the CellBf MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch and CellDlschAlgo MOs:
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, the MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the BfDetectRank1AdjSwitch option of the DlRankDetectSwitch parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO must not be selected at the same time.
    • The MaxMimoRankPara parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO can be set to SW_MAX_SM_RANK_8(Rank8) only when the HighSpeedFlag, MultiCellShareMode, MultiRruCellFlag, TxRxMode, and FddTddInd parameters in the Cell MO are set to LOW_SPEED(Low speed cell flag), NORMAL, BOOLEAN_FALSE(False), 8T8R or 32T32R or 64T64R, and CELL_TDD(TDD) respectively and the TM9Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
  • Relation with the HighSpdAdaptionPara and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • The HighspdVectorUpdateSwitch parameter in a HighSpdAdaptionPara MO takes effect only when the option ProcSwitchBasedOnUserSpeed of HighSpeedSchOptSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected and the FddTddInd parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and HighSpeedFlag parameter in a Cell is not set to LOW_SPEED and an option of the InterfAvoidMode parameter in the HighSpdAdaptionPara MO associated with this Cell MO is selected, the ProcSwitchBasedOnUserSpeed option of the HighSpeedSchOptSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and an option of the InterfAvoidMode parameter in the HighSpdAdaptionPara MO associated with this Cell MO is selected, the InterfHighSpeedSW option of the HighSpeedSchOptSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and HighSpeedFlag parameter in a Cell is not set to LOW_SPEED and the HSCellSleepInterfAvoidSwitch option of the InterfAvoidMode parameter in the HighSpdAdaptionPara MO associated with this Cell MO is selected, the ProcSwitchBasedOnUserSpeed option of the HighSpeedSchOptSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
  • Relation with the EutranIntraFreqNCell and Prb MOs:
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE and an intra-eNodeB cell is to be added in an EutranIntraFreqNCell MO for this Cell MO, Prb MO whose AnchorCarrierFlag is TRUE must be configured first for the two cells.
  • Relation with the EutranInterFreqNCell and Prb MOs:
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE and an intra-eNodeB cell is to be added in an EutranInterFreqNCell MO for this Cell MO, Prb MO whose AnchorCarrierFlag is TRUE must be configured first for the two cells.
  • Relation with the CellUSParaCfg and SRSCfg MOs:
    • When the UsSrsOptSwitch option of the UsAlgoExSwitch parameter in a CellUSParaCfg MO is selected, the TxRxMode parameter in the associated Cell MO must not be set to 1T1R or 2T2R, the UlBandWidth parameter in this Cell MO must be set to CELL_BW_N100, and the TddSrsCfgMode parameter in a SRSCfg MO must be set to ACCESS_ENHANCED.
  • Relation with the CellBf and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is not set to 64T64R or 32T32R, or a SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, the MovingUeMuBfScheme parameter in the CellBf MO must be set to MOVING_MUBF_SCHEME_OFF.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, and the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is not set to 64T64R or 32T32R, or a SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, the NonAntSelUEMubfPairMode parameter in the CellBf MO must be set to SINGLE_STREAM_PAIR.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, and the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is not set to 64T64R or 32T32R, or a SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, the AntSelUEMubfPairMode parameter in the CellBf MO must be set to SINGLE_STREAM_PAIR.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and no SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, the BfWeightOptDegreeFactor parameter in the CellBf MO must be set to a value ranging from 0 to 255. If the preceding conditions are not met, the BfWeightOptDegreeFactor parameter in the CellBf MO must be set to 0.
    • If the ACK_FEEDBACK_IN_MUX_MODE_SW option of the DualStreamBfAlgoSw parameter in the CellBf MO is selected, the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO must be set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and the SectorSplitCell MO must not be configured for the Cell MO.
  • Relation with the CellUSParaCfg MO:
    • The JTUserCoCellPowerSwitch option of the UsAlgoExSwitch parameter in a CellUSParaCfg MO can be selected only when the following condition is met: The FddTddInd and MultiRruCellFlag parameters in the associated Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and BOOLEAN_FALSE, respectively, for a common cell.
    • The UsCellCrsOptSwitch option of the UsAlgoExSwitch parameter in the CellUSParaCfg MO can be selected only when the TxRxMode, FddTddInd, and MultiRruCellFlag parameters in the Cell MO are set to 8T8R, CELL_TDD(TDD), and BOOLEAN_FALSE(False), respectively.
    • In the Cell, if the UlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N50, the value range of UlUsVoipRsvEndRb in the CellUSParaCfg is from 0 to 49
    • In the Cell, if the UlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N100, the value range of UlUsVoipRsvEndRb in the CellUSParaCfg is from 0 to 99
    • In the Cell, if the UlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N6, the value range of UlUsVoipRsvEndRb in the CellUSParaCfg is from 0 to 5
    • In the Cell, if the UlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N15, the value range of UlUsVoipRsvEndRb in the CellUSParaCfg is from 0 to 14
    • The UsSrsGuaranteeSwitch parameter in the CellUSParaCfg MO can be set to 8_USERS_GUARANTEE(8_USERS_GUARANTEE), 16_USERS_GUARANTEE(16_USERS_GUARANTEE), or 24_USERS_GUARANTEE(24_USERS_GUARANTEE) only when the FddTddInd, DlBandWidth, and TxRxMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD(TDD), CELL_BW_N100(20M), and 64T64R or 32T32R respectively.
    • In the Cell, if the UlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N25, the value range of UlUsVoipRsvEndRb in the CellUSParaCfg is from 0 to 24
    • In the Cell, if the UlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N75, the value range of UlUsVoipRsvEndRb in the CellUSParaCfg is from 0 to 74
    • If the value of the UsUeDlAdaptPwrBoostThld parameter in the CellUSParaCfg MO is greater than 0, the MultiCellShareMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to NORMAL.
  • Relation with the CellOp and ENodeBSharingMode MOs:
    • When the ENodeBSharingMode parameter of an ENodeBSharingMode MO is set to SEPARATED_FREQ_SPEC, for each LocalCellId parameter in the associated Cell MO, a maximum of two CellOp MO instances can exist and a maximum of one CellOp MO instance with CellReservedForOp set to CELL_NOT_RESERVED_FOR_OP can exist.
    • If the ENodeBSharingMode parameter in the ENodeBSharingMode MO is set to INDEPENDENT, a maximum of one CellOp MO with the same LocalCellId as that in a Cell MO can be configured.
    • If the ENodeBSharingMode parameter in the ENodeBSharingMode MO is set to SEPARATED_FREQ, a maximum of one CellOp MO with the same LocalCellId as that in a Cell MO can be configured.
    • If ENodeBSharingMode in ENodeBSharingMode is set to SHARED_FREQ or HYBRID_SHARED, a maximum of six CellOp MO with the same LocalCellId as that in a Cell MO can be configured.
  • Relation with the CellOp, CnOperatorTa, and ENodeBSharingMode MOs:
    • If ENodeBSharingMode in the ENodeBSharingMode MO is set to SEPARATED_FREQ_SPEC and CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, CnOperatorId in the CnOperatorTa MO corresponding to TrackingAreaId in each CellOp MO must be unique.
    • If ENodeBSharingMode in the ENodeBSharingMode MO is set to SHARED_FREQ or HYBRID_SHARED and CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, CnOperatorId in the CnOperatorTa MO corresponding to TrackingAreaId in each CellOp MO must be unique.
  • Relation with the CellOp, CnOperatorTa, ENodeBSharingMode, and CnOperator MOs:
    • If ENodeBSharingMode in the ENodeBSharingMode MO is set to SEPARATED_FREQ_SPEC and CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, Tac in the CnOperatorTa MO corresponding to TrackingAreaId in each CellOp MO must have the same value.
    • When the ENodeBSharingMode parameter of an ENodeBSharingMode MO is set to SEPARATED_FREQ_SPEC, for each LocalCellId parameter in the associated Cell MO, a maximum of one CellOp MO instance can exist if: the CnOperatorType parameter in a CnOperator MO associated by the TrackingAreaId parameter in the CellOp MO is set to CNOPERATOR_PRIMARY, and the CellReservedForOp parameter of the CellOp MO is set to CELL_NOT_RESERVED_FOR_OP.
    • When the ENodeBSharingMode parameter of an ENodeBSharingMode MO is set to SEPARATED_FREQ_SPEC, for each LocalCellId parameter in the associated Cell MO, if there are two CellOp MO instances, there must be one CellOp MO instance with the CnOperatorType parameter in a CnOperator MO associated by the TrackingAreaId parameter in the CellOp MO is set to CNOPERATOR_PRIMARY, and the CellReservedForOp parameter in the CellOp MO is set to CELL_RESERVED_FOR_OP.
    • If ENodeBSharingMode in the ENodeBSharingMode MO is set to SHARED_FREQ or HYBRID_SHARED and CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, Tac in the CnOperatorTa MO corresponding to TrackingAreaId in each CellOp MO must have the same value.
  • Relation with the CellPdcchAlgo and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • The MASSIVE_MIMO_PDCCH_SDMA_SW and MASSIVE_MIMO_PDCCH_MEAS_SW options option of the SplitBeamPdcchSdmaSw parameter in the CellPdcchAlgo MO can be selected only when the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is not configured.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, the PdcchSymNumSwitch parameter in the CellPdcchAlgo MO cannot be set to MM_HEAVY_LOAD_ECFI_ADAPT_ON.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, the HEAVY_LOAD_BIG_PKT_SCH_OPT_SW and LOW_AGG_PREFERENTIAL_SCH_SW option of the PdcchOptimizationSwitch parameter in the associated CellPdcchAlgo MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the EuSectorEqmGroup and UlInterfSuppressCfg MOs:
    • The UlIntrfSuppressionValue parameter in the UlInterfSuppressCfg MO can be set to a non-zero value only for a cell with the EuSectorEqmGroup MO configured.
  • Relation with the Prb, EuPrbSectorEqm, and EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MOs:
    • When CellActiveState and FddTddInd in a Cell MO are set to CELL_ACTIVE and CELL_FDD, respectively, one to six EuPrbSectorEqm and EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MOs must be configured under each Prb of the Cell if MultiRruCellFlag, MultiRruCellMode and NbCellFlag in the Cell MO are set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, TRUE and SFN, respectively.
  • Relation with the Prb and EuPrbSectorEqm MOs:
    • When the CellActiveState parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is not configured, and multiple EuPrbSectorEqm MO instances are configured for one Prb of this Cell MO, the ReferenceSignalPwr, BasebandEqmId, AutoConfigFlag parameters in these EuPrbSectorEqm MO instances must be set to the same values.
    • One or two EuPrbSectorEqm instances must be configured for a Prb MO instance under the Cell in which the MultiRruCellFlag parameter is not configured and the CellActiveState, FddTddInd, and TxRxMode parameters are set to CELL_ACTIVE, CELL_FDD, and 4T4R, respectively. When the settings of these parameters in the Cell MO instance differ from the preceding settings, only one EuPrbSectorEqm instance must be configured.
    • In a Cell MO, if NbCellFlag is FALSE, Prb and EuPrbSectorEqm cannot be configured under the Cell.
  • Relation with the CellAuxEnbBind MO:
    • If the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell object is not set to SFN, the CellAuxEnbBind object with the same LocalCellId parameter of the Cell object cannot exist.
  • Relation with the CellEmtcAlgo MO:
    • When the FddTddInd and TxRxMode parameters in a Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and 16T16R, respectively, the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO must be deselected.
    • Under the same cell, when the NbCellFlag, MultiRruCellFlag, and MultiRruCellMode parameters in a Cell MO are set to FALSE, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and TWO_RRU_COMBINATION, respectively, the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO must be deselected.
    • Under the same cell, when the FddTddInd, TxRxMode and NbCellFlag parameters in a Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD, 64T64R or 32T32R and FALSE, respectively, the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO must be deselected.
    • EMTC_SWITCH of EmtcAlgoSwitch in a CellEmtcAlgo MO must be deselected when CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd in the associated Cell MO is set to CFG and CustomizedDLBandWidth in the associated Cell MO is set to a value in the range of 44 to 47.
    • If the CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CFG, the PRACH_FREQ_HOPPING_SW, PUSCH_FREQ_HOPPING_SW, and MPDCCH_PDSCH_FREQ_HOPPING_SW options of the EmtcFreqHoppingSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO associated through LocalCellId must not be selected.
    • If the FddTddInd, HighSpeedFlag, and NbCellFlag parameters in a Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD, ULTRA_HIGH_SPEED or EXTRA_HIGH_SPEED, and FALSE, respectively, the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO under this Cell MO cannot be selected.
    • If the FddTddInd, HighSpeedFlag, and NbCellFlag parameters in a Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD, HIGH_SPEED, and FALSE, respectively, the EMTC_ONLY_CELL_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO under this Cell MO cannot be selected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the HighSpeedFlag parameter in this Cell MO is set to a value other than LOW_SPEED, the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO under this Cell MO cannot be selected.
    • If the FddTddInd and NbCellFlag parameters in a Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and FALSE, respectively, and the HighSpeedFlag parameter in this Cell MO is set to a value other than LOW_SPEED, then the PUSCH_FREQ_HOPPING_SW option of the EmtcFreqHoppingSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO under this Cell MO cannot be selected.
    • If the FddTddInd and NbCellFlag parameters in a Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and FALSE, respectively, and the HighSpeedFlag parameter in this Cell MO is set to a value other than LOW_SPEED, then the MPDCCH_PDSCH_FREQ_HOPPING_SW option of the EmtcFreqHoppingSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO under this Cell MO cannot be selected.
    • Under the same cell, when the value of the CellRadius parameter in a Cell MO is greater than or equal to 100000 m and the NbCellFlag parameter in this Cell MO is set to FALSE, the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO must be deselected.
    • Under the same cell, when the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD(TDD) and the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellEmtcAlgo MO is selected, the value of the PreambleFmt parameter in the associated Cell MO must be smaller than 3.
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD(TDD), and the UlBandWidth parameter or DlBandWidth parameter in the same MO is set to CELL_BW_N6(1.4M), CELL_BW_N15(3M), or CELL_BW_N25(5M), the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellEmtcAlgo MO must be deselected.
    • Under the same cell, when the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to FALSE and the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellEmtcAlgo MO is selected, the values of the UlCyclicPrefix and DlCyclicPrefix parameters in the Cell must be the same. That is, the two parameters are set to NORMAL_CP or EXTENDED_CP at the same time.
    • Under the same cell, when the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to FALSE and the IDLE_EDRX_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellEmtcAlgo MO is selected, the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO must be selected.
    • Under the same cell, when the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellEmtcAlgo MO must be deselected.
    • Under the same cell, when the EMTC_ONLY_CELL_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO is selected, the FddTddInd and UlBandWidth or DlBandWidth parameters in the Cell MO must be set to CELL_FDD(FDD) and CELL_BW_N6(1.4M), CELL_BW_N15(3M), or CELL_BW_N25(5M), respectively.
    • Under the same cell, the EMTC_ONLY_CELL_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO must be selected when the following conditions are true: The NbCellFlag, FddTddInd, and UlBandWidth or DlBandWidth parameters in the Cell MO are set to FALSE, CELL_FDD(FDD), and CELL_BW_N6(1.4M) or CELL_BW_N15(3M), respectively; the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO is selected.
    • Under the same cell, the MPDCCH_MORE_AVAIL_NB_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellEmtcAlgo MO can be selected only when the DlBandWidth or UlBandWidth parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50(10M), CELL_BW_N75(15M) or CELL_BW_N100(20M).
    • For an FDD cell, the EmtcDlFreqHoppingInterval parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO associated through LocalCellId can be set only to INTERVAL1, INTERVAL2, INTERVAL4, and INTERVAL8.
    • For an FDD cell, the EmtcUlFreqHoppingInterval parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO associated through LocalCellId can be set only to INTERVAL1, INTERVAL2, INTERVAL4, and INTERVAL8.
    • If the PRACH_FREQ_HOPPING_SW, PUSCH_FREQ_HOPPING_SW, or MPDCCH_PDSCH_FREQ_HOPPING_SW option of the EmtcFreqHoppingSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO is selected, the MpdcchPdschHoppingNbCnt parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO cannot be set to NB4 for cells with 3 MHz bandwidth.
    • If the MPDCCH_PDSCH_FREQ_HOPPING_SW option of the EmtcFreqHoppingSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO is selected, the following conditions must be true: The value of the MpdcchPdschFreqHoppingOfs parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO must not exceed 2 for cells with 3 MHz bandwidth. The value of the MpdcchPdschFreqHoppingOfs parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO must not exceed 4 for cells with 5 MHz bandwidth. The value of the MpdcchPdschFreqHoppingOfs parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO must not exceed 8 for cells with 10 MHz bandwidth. The value of the MpdcchPdschFreqHoppingOfs parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO must not exceed 12 for cells with 15 MHz bandwidth.
    • If the PUSCH_FREQ_HOPPING_SW option of the EmtcFreqHoppingSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO is selected, the following conditions must be true: The value of the PuschFreqHoppingOffset parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO must not exceed 2 for cells with 3 MHz bandwidth. The value of the PuschFreqHoppingOffset parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO must not exceed 4 for cells with 5 MHz bandwidth. The value of the PuschFreqHoppingOffset parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO must not exceed 8 for cells with 10 MHz bandwidth. The value of the PuschFreqHoppingOffset parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO must not exceed 12 for cells with 15 MHz bandwidth.
    • The PRACH_FREQ_HOPPING_SW, PUSCH_FREQ_HOPPING_SW, or MPDCCH_PDSCH_FREQ_HOPPING_SW option of the EmtcFreqHoppingSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO associated through LocalCellId cannot be selected for cells with 1.4 MHz bandwidth.
    • For a TDD cell, the EmtcDlFreqHoppingInterval parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO associated through LocalCellId can be set only to INTERVAL1, INTERVAL5, INTERVAL10, and INTERVAL20.
    • For a TDD cell, the EmtcUlFreqHoppingInterval parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO associated through LocalCellId can be set only to INTERVAL1, INTERVAL5, INTERVAL10, and INTERVAL20.
    • If the EXCEED_RADIUS_CRA_DETECTION_SW option of EmtcRachAlgoSwitch in the CellEmtcAlgo MO under a Cell MO is selected, the HighSpeedFlag parameter in the Cell MO must be set to LOW_SPEED.
    • If the EXCEED_RADIUS_CRA_DETECTION_SW option of EmtcRachAlgoSwitch in the CellEmtcAlgo MO under a Cell MO is selected, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO must be set to FALSE.
    • If the EXCEED_RADIUS_CFRA_OPT_SW option of EmtcRachAlgoSwitch in the CellEmtcAlgo MO under a Cell MO is selected, HighSpeedFlag in the Cell MO must be set to LOW_SPEED.
    • If the EXCEED_RADIUS_CFRA_OPT_SW option of EmtcRachAlgoSwitch in the CellEmtcAlgo MO under a Cell MO is selected, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO must be set to FALSE.
  • Relation with the CellRbReserve MO:
    • When the RbRsvMode parameter in a CellRbReserve MO is set to LNR_SPECTRUM_SHR_RB_RESERVED, the sum of the values of the RbRsvStartIndex and RbRsvEndIndex parameters in this MO should be equal to the RB quantity indicated by the value of the UlBandWidth parameter in the corresponding Cell MO minus 1.
    • If the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50 and the RbRsvMode parameter in a CellRbReserve MO under this Cell MO is set to LNR_SPECTRUM_SHR_RB_RESERVED, the RbRsvStartIndex parameter in this CellRbReserve MO must be set to a value less than or equal to 10.
    • If the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75 and the RbRsvMode parameter in a CellRbReserve MO under this Cell MO is set to LNR_SPECTRUM_SHR_RB_RESERVED, the RbRsvStartIndex parameter in this CellRbReserve MO must be set to a value less than or equal to 7.
    • If the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100 and the RbRsvMode parameter in a CellRbReserve MO under this Cell MO is set to LNR_SPECTRUM_SHR_RB_RESERVED, the RbRsvStartIndex parameter in this CellRbReserve MO must be set to a value less than or equal to 12.
    • If RbRsvMode in a CellRbReserve MO instance is set to RB_MASKING, UlBandWidth in the Cell MO instance associated with the CellRbReserve MO instance through the LocalCellId parameter cannot be set to CELL_BW_N6(1.4M) or CELL_BW_N15(3M).
    • If RbRsvMode in a CellRbReserve MO instance is set to RB_MASKING_WITH_SRS, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO instance associated with the CellRbReserve MO instance through the LocalCellId parameter cannot be set to CELL_BW_N6(1.4M). If RbRsvMode in a CellRbReserve MO instance is set to RB_MASKING_WITH_SRS, UlBandWidth in the Cell MO instance associated with the CellRbReserve MO instance through the LocalCellId parameter cannot be set to CELL_BW_N6(1.4M).
    • If RbRsvMode in a CellRbReserve MO instance is set to RB_MASKING_WITH_SRS, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO instance associated with the CellRbReserve MO instance through the LocalCellId parameter cannot be set to CELL_BW_N15(3M). If RbRsvMode in a CellRbReserve MO instance is set to RB_MASKING_WITH_SRS, UlBandWidth in the Cell MO instance associated with the CellRbReserve MO instance through the LocalCellId parameter cannot be set to CELL_BW_N15(3M).
    • In a Cell MO, if CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N6(1.4M), the RbRsvStartIndex parameter in a CellRbReserve MO associated with this Cell MO must be set to a value in the range of 0 to 5. When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15(3M), the RbRsvStartIndex parameter in a CellRbReserve MO associated with this Cell MO must be set to a value in the range of 0 to 14. When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25(5M), the RbRsvStartIndex parameter in a CellRbReserve MO associated with this Cell MO must be set to a value in the range of 0 to 24. When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50(10M), the RbRsvStartIndex parameter in a CellRbReserve MO associated with this Cell MO must be set to a value in the range of 0 to 49. When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75(15M), the RbRsvStartIndex parameter in a CellRbReserve MO associated with this Cell MO must be set to a value in the range of 0 to 74. When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100(20M), the RbRsvStartIndex parameter in a CellRbReserve MO associated with this Cell MO must be set to a value in the range of 0 to 99.
    • If the value of CellActiveState in the Cell MO associated with a CellRbReserve MO is CELL_ACTIVE, NbCellFlag in the Cell MO identified by LocalCellId must be set to FALSE.
    • When the CellActiveState parameter in a Cell MO associated with the CellRbReserve MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO associated through LocalCellId cannot be set to CELL_BW_N6(1.4M). When the CellActiveState parameter in a Cell MO associated with the CellRbReserve MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the UlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO associated through LocalCellId cannot be set to CELL_BW_N6(1.4M).
    • If the RbRsvMode parameter in a CellRbReserve MO under a Cell MO is set to COVERAGE_ENH_RB_RESERVE, the RbRsvEndIndex parameter in this CellRbReserve MO must be set as follows: When the UlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25, the RbRsvEndIndex parameter in the CellRbReserve MO must be set to a value in the range of 0 to 24; when the UlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50, the RbRsvEndIndex parameter in the CellRbReserve MO must be set to a value in the range of 0 to 49; when the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75, the RbRsvEndIndex parameter in the CellRbReserve MO must be set to a value in the range of 0 to 74; when the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100, the RbRsvEndIndex parameter in the CellRbReserve MO must be set to a value in the range of 0 to 99.
    • When the RbRsvMode parameter in a CellRbReserve MO is set to NB_DEPLOYMENT(NB_DEPLOYMENT), and the CellActiveState in the Cell MO associated with the CellRbReserve MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, then the UlCyclicPrefix parameter in the Cell MO must be set to NORMAL_CP(Normal) and the DlCyclicPrefix parameter in the Cell MO must be set to NORMAL_CP(Normal).
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD(TDD), the RbRsvMode parameter in the CellRbReserve MO must be set to RB_MASKING(RB_MASKING) or MCPTT_RB_RESERVED(MCPTT_RB_RESERVED).
    • When the RbRsvMode parameter in the CellRbReserve MOs for the same cell is set to NB_RESERVED or NB_DEPLOYMENT, and CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, values of the RbRsvStartIndex and RbRsvEndIndex parameters in the CellRbReserve MOs must be the same.
    • When FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD(FDD) and RbRsvMode in the CellRbReserve MO is set to RB_MASKING_WITH_SRS, RbRsvType in this CellRbReserve MO must not be set to DOWNLINK_MODE.
    • If the RbRsvMode of CellRbReserve is set to MCPTT_RB_RESERVED, the value of UlBandWidth of the Cell can be set to CELL_BW_N25, CELL_BW_N50, CELL_BW_N75 or CELL_BW_N100. And the WorkMode of the Cell should be set UL_DL.
    • If the RbRsvMode of CellRbReserve is set to MCPTT_RB_RESERVED and the UlBandWidth of Cell is set to CELL_BW_N25, the RB number between the value of the RbRsvEndIndex and RbRsvStartIndex parameters in the CellRbReserve MO must be 5 or 10.
    • If the RbRsvMode of CellRbReserve is set to MCPTT_RB_RESERVED and the UlBandWidth of Cell is set to CELL_BW_N50, the RB number between the value of the RbRsvEndIndex and RbRsvStartIndex parameters in the CellRbReserve MO must be 5, 10, 15, 20, 25.
    • If the RbRsvMode of CellRbReserve is set to MCPTT_RB_RESERVED and the UlBandWidth of Cell is set to CELL_BW_N75, the RB number between the value of the RbRsvEndIndex and RbRsvStartIndex parameters in the CellRbReserve MO must be 5, 10, 15, 20, 25, 30, 35.
    • If the RbRsvMode of CellRbReserve is set to MCPTT_RB_RESERVED and the UlBandWidth of Cell is set to CELL_BW_N100, the RB number between the value of the RbRsvEndIndex and RbRsvStartIndex parameters in the CellRbReserve MO must be 5, 10, 15, 20, 25, 30, 35, 40, 45, 50.
    • If the RbRsvMode of CellRbReserve is set to MCPTT_RB_RESERVED, the RbRsvStartIndex and RbRsvEndIndex of CellRbReserve must be smaller than or equal to the value of UlBandWidth parameter minus 1.
    • If the RbRsvMode parameter in a CellRbReserve MO is set to COVERAGE_ENH_RB_RESERVED, the UlBandWidth parameter in the corresponding Cell MO cannot be set to CELL_BW_N6 or CELL_BW_N15. In addition, the number of CellRbReserve MOs (under this Cell MO) in which the RbRsvMode parameter is set to COVERAGE_ENH_RB_RESERVED cannot exceed one.
  • Relation with the CellRbReserve and SpectrumCloud MOs:
    • If the RbRsvMode parameter in a CellRbReserve MO under a Cell MO is set to LNR_SPECTRUM_SHR_RB_RESERVED, the FddTddInd parameter in this Cell MO must be set to CELL_FDD, the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO must be configured and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter must be set to LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR or LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch and CellCspcPara MOs:
    • If the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected and the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the CelleCspcSwitch parameter in the CellCspcPara MO must be set to OFF.
    • FDDHomNetDlCompSwitch and FDDHetNetDlCompSwitch of DlCompSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set off if TxRxMode in the associated Cell MO is set to 1T1R or 1T2R, EicicSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to DYNAMIC, CelleCspcSwitch in the associated CellCspcPara MO is set to ON, or Virtual4T4RSwitch of EmimoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set on.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell and SectorSplitGroup MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and a SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, the PATTERN_ADJ_SWITCH and POWER_ADJ_SWITCH options of the SectorSplitSwitch parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R, 32T32R, or 8T8R, and the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the number of SectorSplitCell MOs associated to the SectorSplitGroup MO for the corresponding Cell MO must be 2, 3, or 4.
    • If the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, a SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, and the MM_INTELLIGENT_BEAM_SHAPING_SW option of the SectorSplitSwitch parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO associated with the Cell MO is selected, three or four FDD cells must be configured in the SectorSplitCell MOs associated with the SectorSplitGroup corresponding to the Cell.
    • For all Cell MOs associated with the SectorSplitCell MOs in the same SectorSplitGroup MO, if the FddTddInd parameter in one of these Cell MOs is set to CELL_TDD and the TxRxMode parameter in the same Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, the SubframeAssignment and SpecialSubframePatterns parameters in these Cell MOs must be set to the same values.
    • For all Cell MOs associated with the SectorSplitCell MOs in the same SectorSplitGroup MO, if the FddTddInd parameter in one of these Cell MOs is set to CELL_TDD and the TxRxMode parameter in the same Cell MO is set to 8T8R and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO is set to MODE_3, the SubframeAssignment and SpecialSubframePatterns parameters in these Cell MOs must be set to the same values.
    • The BeamDeploymentMode parameter in a SectorSplitGroup MO must be set to MODE_3 when the parameters in a Cell MO corresponding to a SectorSplitCell MO under this SectorSplitGroup MO are set as follows: The FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_FDD, the TxRxMode parameter is set to 8T8R, and the MultiCellShareMode parameter is set to NORMAL.
    • If the FddTddInd and TxRxMode parameters in a Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and 32T32R, respectively, and the cell specified by this Cell MO is configured in a SectorSplitCell MO, then the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO where the SectorSplitCell MO is included cannot be set to NOT_CFG. If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the cell specified by this Cell MO is configured in a SectorSplitCell MO, then the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO where the SectorSplitCell MO is included must be set to NOT_CFG or MODE_3. In addition, if the TxRxMode parameter in this Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the BeamTemplateId parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO must be set to 0.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO is set to MODE_3, then in Cell MO: the CrsPortNum parameter must be set to CRS_PORT_2 or CRS_PORT_4, the DlCyclicPrefix and UlCyclicPrefix parameters must be set to NORMAL_CP, the SubframeAssignment parameter must be set to SA1 or SA2, the SpecialSubframePatterns parameter must be set to SSP5, SSP6, or SSP7, the DlBandWidth parameter must be set to CELL_BW_N50, CELL_BW_N75, or CELL_BW_N100, and the UlBandWidth parameter must be set to CELL_BW_N50, CELL_BW_N75, or CELL_BW_N100.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO is set to MODE_3, then in the Cell MO: the HighSpeedFlag parameter must be set to LOW_SPEED, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter must be set to BOOLEAN_FALSE, and the CPRICompression parameter must be set to NO_COMPRESSION or NORMAL_COMPRESSION.
    • If the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO is set to MODE_3, then the BeamTemplateId parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO must be set to 0.
    • If the Cell MO is added in a SectorSplitCell MO under a SectorSplitGroup MO, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in this SectorSplitGroup MO is set to MODE_3, then the CellBeamIndex parameter in the SectorSplitCell MO must be set to 0 or 1.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and PDSCHCfg MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the EmfPowerLimitSwitch parameter in the PDSCHCfg MO under this Cell MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, and the SpctShrMode parameter in this SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_PWR_DYN_SHR_WITH_SPCT, then the CellPowerLimit parameter in the PDSCHCfg MO under this Cell MO must be set to 0.
  • Relation with the CaGroup, CaGroupCell, and eNodeBFunction MOs:
    • If CaGroupTypeInd in a CaGroup MO is set to TDD, FddTddInd in Cell MO associated with a CaGroupCell MO under this CaGroup MO must be CELL_TDD, SubframeAssignment must be SA1 or SA2.
    • If CaGroupTypeInd in a CaGroup MO is set to TDD, the Cell MOs associated with the CaGroupCell MO under this CaGroup MO must also work in CELL_TDD mode.
    • If CaGroupTypeInd in a CaGroup MO is set to FDDTDD, FddTddInd in Cell MO associated with a CaGroupCell MO under this CaGroup MO must be CELL_TDD, SubframeAssignment must be SA1 or SA2.
    • If CaGroupTypeInd in a CaGroup MO is set to FDD, the Cell MOs associated with the CaGroupCell MO under this CaGroup MO must also work in CELL_FDD mode.
  • Relation with the CellEicic and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • In a Cell, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_TDD, and SubframeAssignment is set to SA1, and EicicSwitch in CellAlgoSwitch is set to STATIC, then the AbsPattern in CellEicic could not have subframe 3 and subframe 8.
    • If EicicAdaptiveSwitch in the CellEicic MO is set to ON, FddTddInd in the associated Cell MO and EicicSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to CELL_FDD and DYNAMIC, respectively.
  • Relation with the CellEicic, CellAlgoSwitch, and CellMBMSCfg MOs:
    • The AbsPattern parameter in the CellEicic MO that is related to the Cell MO can be configured only with subframe 0 and subframe 5 when the following conditions are met: In a Cell MO, the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD, and the SubframeAssignment parameter is set to SA1 or SA2. In the CellMBMSCfg MO that is related to the Cell MO, the MBMSSwitch parameter is set to 1. In the CellAlgoSwitch MO that is related to the Cell MO, the EicicSwitch parameter is set to STATIC.
  • Relation with the CellDlschAlgo and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • In the Cell MO, if the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD and the DlVoIPLoadBasedSchSwitch option of the DlEnhancedVoipSchSw parameter is selected, the SpsSchSwitch option of the DlSchSwitch parameter must be selected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the DL_INTRA_JAMC_ALGO_SW option of the DlJointAMCAlgoSw parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO is selected, the IntraDlCompSwitch option of the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the DL_INTRA_JAMC_ALGO_SW option of the DlJointAMCAlgoSw parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO is selected, the InterDlCompDcsSwitch and InterDlCompCbfSwitch options of the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the DL_INTRA_JAMC_ALGO_SW option of the DlJointAMCAlgoSw parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO is selected, the InterCellDmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the DL_INTRA_JAMC_ALGO_SW option of the DlJointAMCAlgoSw parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO is selected, the DmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the CellOp MO:
    • If CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, one or more CellOp MOs must have been configured for the Cell MO.
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the OpResourceGroupIndex parameter in a CellOp MO under this Cell MO must be set to 255.
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the CellReservedForOp parameter in the CellOp MOs under this Cell MO cannot be set to CELL_DYNAMIC_RESERVED_FOR_OP.
  • Relation with the IntraFreqBlkCell MO:
    • PhyCellId of the local Cell MO cannot be within the intra-frequency blacklist cell range specified by PhyCellId and PhyCellIdRange in an IntraFreqBlkCell MO.
  • Relation with the CellOp, ENodeBSharingMode, and CnOperator MOs:
    • If ENodeBSharingMode in the ENodeBSharingMode MO is set to SHARED_FREQ and CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, one of the CellOp MOs with the same local cell ID as that specified by LocalCellId in the Cell MO must meet the following requirement: CnOperatorType is set to CNOPERATOR_PRIMARY in the CnOperator MO corresponding to the CnOperatorTa MO with the same tracking area ID as that specified by TrackingAreaId in the CellOp MO.
  • Relation with the EutranBlkNCell and EutranExternalCell MOs:
    • In inter-eNodeB scenarios, values of DlEarfcn and PhyCellId in the EutranExternalCell MO associated with an EutranBlkNCell MO cannot be the same as values of DlEarfcn and PhyCellId in the corresponding Cell MO.
  • Relation with the UtranBlkNCell, UtranNCell, and UtranExternalCell MOs:
    • Values of UtranDlArfcn and PScrambCode in the UtranExternalCell MO associated with the UtranBlkNCell MO configured for the serving cell cannot be the same as values of UtranDlArfcn and PScrambCode in the UtranExternalCell MO associated with the UtranNCell MO configured for the serving cell at the same time.
  • Relation with the UtranBlkNCell MO:
    • A maximum of 16 UtranBlkNCell MOs can be configured for a cell.
  • Relation with the EutranInterNFreq MO:
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, DlEarfcn in a Cell MO must be different from that in each associated EutranInterNFreq MO.
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, MeasBandWidth in an EutranInterNFreq MO associated with this Cell MO must be set to MBW6(1.4M).
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, the QRxLevMin parameter in the EutranInterNFreq MOs whose LocalCellId is corresponding to this Cell MO must be set to a value in the range of -70 to -22.
  • Relation with the EutranExternalCell, EutranInterFreqNCell, and EutranInterNFreq MOs:
    • DlEarfcn in the EutranExternalCell MO corresponding to an inter-eNodeB EutranInterFreqNCell MO must be the same as DlEarfcn in an EutranInterNFreq MO.
    • In inter-eNodeB scenarios, the value of DlEarfcn in the EutranExternalCell MO associated with an EutranInterFreqNCell MO of a Cell MO must be the same as one of the values of DlEarfcn in the EutranInterNFreq MOs associated with this Cell MO and the values of DlFreqOffset and DlFreqOffset must be the same.
  • Relation with the CellPdcchAlgo and PHICHCfg MOs:
    • When the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, if PhichDuration in the PHICHCfg MO is set to NORMAL, and PdcchSymNumSwitch in the CellPdcchAlgo MO is set to OFF, InitPdcchSymNum in the CellPdcchAlgo MO cannot be set to 1 if the cell bandwidth is 1.4 MHz.
    • When the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, if PhichDuration in the PHICHCfg MO is set to NORMAL, InitPdcchSymNum in the CellPdcchAlgo MO cannot be set to 4 if the cell bandwidth is not 1.4 MHz.
    • When the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, if PhichDuration in the PHICHCfg MO is set to EXTENDED, InitPdcchSymNum in the CellPdcchAlgo MO can be set to 3 or 4 if the cell bandwidth is 1.4 MHz.
    • When the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, if PhichDuration in the PHICHCfg MO is set to EXTENDED, InitPdcchSymNum in the CellPdcchAlgo MO must be set to 3 when the cell bandwidth is not 1.4 MHz.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch and RACHCfg MOs:
    • When NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, if FddTddInd in this Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the PucchSwitch option of PucchAlgoSwitch in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, and PrachFreqOffsetStrategy in the RACHCfg MO is not set to FULLY_AUTOMATIC, PrachFreqOffset in the RACHCfg MO must be properly set so that the PRACH RBs and PUCCH RBs do not overlap in the frequency domain.
  • Relation with the ULZeroBufferZone MO:
    • When the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, in the ULZeroBufferZone MO, the ULSharedFreqEndRB parameter must be set to a value smaller than Resource Block of the UlBandWidth parameter.
    • The UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in a ULZeroBufferZone MO can be selected only when the FddTddInd parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD.
    • When the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in the ULZeroBufferZone MO is selected, the UlBandWidth parameter in the associated Cell MO must be set to CELL_BW_N25, CELL_BW_N50, CELL_BW_N75, or CELL_BW_N100.
    • The UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in a ULZeroBufferZone MO under the Cell MO can be selected only when the NbCellFlag parameter in this Cell MO is set to FALSE.
    • If the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in a ULZeroBufferZone MO is selected, the HighSpeedFlag parameter in the Cell MO must not be set to HIGH_SPEED, ULTRA_HIGH_SPEED, or EXTRA_HIGH_SPEED.
  • Relation with the CellLaaFcsPara and CellLaaCoexistPara MOs:
    • If WorkMode in a Cell MO is set to LAA, a maximum of one CellLaaCoexistPara MO and four CellLaaFcsPara MOs can be configured under this Cell MO.
    • If WorkMode in a Cell MO is set not to LAA, CellLaaCoexistPara MO or CellLaaFcsPara MO cannot be configured under this Cell MO.
  • Relation with the EutranInterNFreq and Prb MOs:
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE and an EutranInterNFreq MO is to be added for this Cell MO, a Prb MO whose AnchorCarrierFlag is TRUE must be added first for this Cell MO.
    • If the NbCellFlag in the Cell MO is set to TRUE, the DlEarfcn in the EutranInterNFreq MO for this local cell cannot be the same as the DlEarfcn in the Prb whose AnchorCarrierFlag is set to TRUE when the LteDlRbIndex is set to an invalid value, or the downlink frequency point calculated based on the DlEarfcn and DlBandWidth in the Cell MO corresponding to LteDlRbIndex.
  • Relation with the EutranIntraFreqNCell, EutranExternalCell, and Prb MOs:
    • In inter-eNodeB scenarios, if NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE and an EutranIntraFreqNCell MO is to be added for this Cell MO, a Prb MO whose AnchorCarrierFlag is TRUE must be configured first for this Cell MO.
  • Relation with the EutranInterFreqNCell, EutranExternalCell, and Prb MOs:
    • In inter-eNodeB scenarios, if NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE and an EutranInterFreqNCell MO is to be added for this Cell MO, a Prb MO whose AnchorCarrierFlag is TRUE must be configured first for this Cell MO.
  • Relation with the NbCellDynPwrShrPara and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • When the DlPrbUsageThld parameter in an NbCellDynPwrShrPara MO is set to a non-zero value, the NbCellFlag parameter in the associated Cell MO must be set to TRUE, and the COVERAGE_EXTENSION_SWITCH option of the NbCellAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be selected.
  • Relation with the NbCellDynPwrShrPara, Prb, and CellEmtcAlgo MOs:
    • The DlPrbUsageThld parameter in an NbCellDynPwrShrPara MO can be set to a non-zero value only if the following conditions are met: The NbCellFlag parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to TRUE, the DeployMode parameter in the Prb MO under the Cell MO is set to IN_BAND(IN_BAND), the AnchorCarrierFlag parameter is set to TRUE, the bandwidth of the LTE cell associated by the LteCellId parameter in this Prb MO is higher than 5 MHz, and the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO of the LTE cell is deselected.
  • Relation with the PDSCHCfg MO:
    • The CellPowerLimit parameter in the PDSCHCfg MO must be set to 0 if the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE.
    • The CellPowerLimit parameter in the PDSCHCfg MO must be set to 0 if the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE.
    • If MultiRruCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE or FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, TxPowerOffsetAnt0, TxPowerOffsetAnt1, TxPowerOffsetAnt2, and TxPowerOffsetAnt3 in the associated PDSCHCfg MO must be set to 255.
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the CrsPowerBoostingAmplitude parameter in the PDSCHCfg MO can only be set to DB_0. When the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, the CrsPowerBoostingAmplitude parameter in the PDSCHCfg MO can only be set to DB_0. When the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, the CrsPowerBoostingAmplitude parameter in the PDSCHCfg MO can only be set to DB_0.
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the PdschPowerBoostingSwitch parameter in the PDSCHCfg MO under this Cell MO can be set to ON only if the TxRxMode parameter in this Cell MO is set to 8T8R or 4T4R.
    • If the NbCellFlag is set to TRUE or the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the PdschPowerBoostingSwitch parameter in the PDSCHCfg MO can only be set to OFF.
    • If LogicalPortSwapSwitch in the PDSCHCfg MO is set to SWAP_MODE_1, FddTddInd in the Cell MO must be set to CELL_FDD, TxRxMode in the Cell MO must be set to 4T4R, 4T8R, or 32T32R, and CrsPortNum in the Cell MO must be set to CRS_PORT_4.
  • Relation with the EutranIntraFreqNCell, EutranExternalCell, CellAlgoSwitch, and SfnEdgeRruRelation MOs:
    • PhyCellId in the EutranExternalCell MO associated with an EutranIntraFreqNCell MO (representing an inter-eNodeB intra-frequency neighboring cell of the local cell) can be the same as PhyCellId in the Cell MO representing the local cell if both conditions are met: (1) The SuperCombCellSwitch option of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected in the local cell; (2) All values of parameters Mcc, Mnc, eNodeBId, and CellId in the EutranIntraFreqNCell MO are the same with values of parameters Mcc, Mnc, EnodeBId, and NeighborCellId in at least one SfnEdgeRruRelation MO configured for the local cell. If either condition is not met, PhyCellId in the EutranExternalCell MO cannot be the same as PhyCellId in the Cell MO.
  • Relation with the EutranIntraFreqNCell, CellAlgoSwitch, and SfnEdgeRruRelation MOs:
    • PhyCellId in the Cell MO representing the local cell can be the same as PhyCellId in the Cell MO associated with the EutranIntraFreqNCell MO (representing an intra-eNodeB inter-frequency neighboring cell of the local cell) if both conditions are met: (1) The SuperCombCellSwitch option of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected in the local cell; (2) All values of parameters Mcc, Mnc, eNodeBId, and CellId in the EutranIntraFreqNCell MO are the same with values of parameters Mcc, Mnc, EnodeBId, and NeighborCellId in at least one SfnEdgeRruRelation MO configured for the local cell. If either condition is not met, values of PhyCellId in the two Cell MOs cannot be the same.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • If the values of the CellActiveState, FddTddInd, and TxRxMode parameters in a Cell MO are CELL_ACTIVE, CELL_FDD, and 32T32R, respectively, the value of CellBeamMode parameter in the corresponding eUCellSectorEqm MO is MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, and the SectorSplitCell or SectorSplitCellExt MO associated with the cell exists, then the PreambleFmt parameter in this Cell MO must be set to 0 or 1.
    • If the CellActiveState parameter in the current Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, only one SectorSplitCell MO must be configured.
    • If the CellActiveState parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, and the total number of SectorSplitCell MOs in the SectorSplitGroup MO to which the Cell MO belongs is greater than one, then the BaseBandEqmId parameter must be set to the same value in the eUCellSectorEqm MOs under the Cell MOs corresponding to all the SectorSplitCell MOs under the SectorSplitGroup MO to which the Cell MO belongs.
    • When the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, and the number of SectorSplitCell MO instances in the SectorSplitGroup MO associated with the Cell MO is less than or equal to two, the BaseBandEqmId parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must not be set to 255.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitGroup and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • If the values of the CellActiveState, FddTddInd, and TxRxMode parameters in the Cell MO for a cell are CELL_ACTIVE, CELL_TDD, and 64T64R, respectively, and the number of SectorSplitCell MOs under the SectorSplitGroup MO including this cell is greater than or equal to 2 and less than or equal to 4, the PreambleFmt parameter in this Cell MO must be set to 0.
    • Under a SectorSplitGroup MO, the values of the DlEarfcn, DlBandWidth, UlEarfcnCfgInd, UlEarfcn, UlBandWidth, TxRxMode, CrsPortNum, and CPRICompression parameters in the Cell MO instances corresponding to SectorSplitCell MO instances must be the same.
    • The MassiveMimoEmfPowerLimit parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO can be set to a non-zero value only if the TxRxMode and FddTddInd parameters in the Cell MO are set to 32T32R and CELL_FDD, respectively.
    • In the Cell MOs corresponding to the SectorSplitCell MOs under the SectorSplitGroup MO, if TxRxMode and FddTddInd are set to 8T8R and CELL_FDD respectively, and MultiCellShareMode is set to SPLIT or CrsPortNum is set to CRS_PORT_4, the values of CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd, CustomizedULBandWidth, and CustomizedDLBandWidth must be the same in these Cell MOs.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, there is a SectorSplitCell MO that corresponds to this Cell MO, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO that includes this SectorSplitCell MO is set to MODE_3, then the following parameters in the Cell MO must be set as follows: CrsPortNum must be set to CRS_PORT_4, CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd must be set to NOT_CFG, NbCellFlag must be set to FALSE, TxRxMode must be set to 8T8R.
  • Relation with the CaGroupCell MO:
    • When the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, if CellRadius in a Cell MO representing a cell is set to a value greater than 100000 m, this cell cannot be added to a CaGroupCell MO.
    • UlCyclicPrefix or DlCyclicPrefix in the Cell MOs associated with a CaGroupCell MO must be set to the same value.
  • Relation with the EutranBlkNCell and EutranInterNFreq MOs:
    • In intra-eNodeB scenarios, the value of DlEarfcn in the Cell MO under which an EutranBlkNCell MO is configured must be the same as the value of either DlEarfcn in this Cell MO or DlEarfcn in the related EutranInterNFreq MO.
  • Relation with the CellPrsCfg MO:
    • When the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, if FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the value of PrsBandwidth cannot exceed the value of DlBandWidth.
  • Relation with the PCCHCfg MO:
    • When the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, if DlBandWidth in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N6, CELL_BW_N15, or CELL_BW_N25, ENHNCD_HIGH_PRI_PAGING_SWITCH and ENHNCD_CSFB_PAGING_SWITCH of EnhPagingSwitch in the associated PCCHCfg MO must be set off.
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, values of the DefaultPagingCycleForNb and NbForNbIoT parameters in the associated PCCHCfg MO cannot be changed and are always rf256(256 radio frames) and ONE_64TH_T(ONE_64TH_T), respectively.
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, values of the DefaultPagingCycle, Nb, PagingSentNum, and MaxPagingRecordsNum parameters in the associated PCCHCfg MO cannot be changed and are always rf128(128 radio frames), ONET, 1, and 16, respectively.
    • If the PagingStrategy parameter in the PCCHCfg MO is set to DIFFERENTIATION_BY_NB_COV_LVL, the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO must be set to TRUE.
  • Relation with the SRSCfg and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • For an LTE TDD cell, if the ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch option of the UlSuMimoAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the TddSrsCfgMode parameter in the SRSCfg MO must be set to EXPERIENCE_ENHANCED(Experience Enhanced) or ACCESS_ENHANCED(Access Enhanced). If the TddSrsCfgMode parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to EXPERIENCE_ENHANCED(Experience Enhanced) or ACCESS_ENHANCED(Access Enhanced), the ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch option of the UlSuMimoAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected. The SU-MIMO feature takes effect only when the TddSrsCfgMode parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to EXPERIENCE_ENHANCED(Experience Enhanced) or ACCESS_ENHANCED(Access Enhanced).
    • The InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the following conditions are true: In the Cell MO, the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_FDD; in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the SrsSubframeRecfSwitch option of the SrsAlgoSwitch parameter is deselected; in the SRSCfg MO, the FddSrsCfgMode, SrsSubframeCfg, and AnSrsSimuTrans parameters are set to DEFAULTMODE, SC0, and BOOLEAN_TRUE, respectively.
  • Relation with the UlCsAlgoPara MO:
    • If CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd in a Cell MO is set to CFG and CustomizedULBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to a value less than 50, UL_COORD_RES_ALLOC_SWITCH of UlCsSw in the associated UlCsAlgoPara MO must be set off.
    • If CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd in a Cell MO is set to CFG and CustomizedULBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to a value less than 50, UL_COORD_RES_ALLOC_SWITCH of UlCsSw in the associated UlCsAlgoPara MO must be set off.
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the UL_COORD_RES_ALLOC_SWITCH option of the UlCsSw in the UlCsAlgoPara must be deselected.
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the UL_COORD_PC_SWITCH option of the UlCsSw in the UlCsAlgoPara must be deselected.
    • UL_COORD_RES_ALLOC_SWITCH of UlCsSw in a UlCsAlgoPara MO can be set on only if HighSpeedFlag in the associated Cell MO is set to LOW_SPEED(Low speed cell flag).
    • If CellRadius in a Cell MO is set to a value greater than 15000, UL_COORD_RES_ALLOC_SWITCH of UlCsSw in the associated UlCsAlgoPara MO must be set off.
    • UL_COORD_PC_SWITCH of UlCsSw in a UlCsAlgoPara MO can be set on only if HighSpeedFlag in the associated Cell MO is set to LOW_SPEED(Low speed cell flag).
    • If CellRadius in a Cell MO is set to a value greater than 15000, UL_COORD_PC_SWITCH of UlCsSw in the associated UlCsAlgoPara MO must be set off.
    • When the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, if UlBandWidth in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N6(1.4M) or CELL_BW_N15(3M), UL_COORD_RES_ALLOC_SWITCH of UlCsSw in the associated UlCsAlgoPara MO must be set off.
    • If FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, UL_COORD_RES_ALLOC_SWITCH of UlCsSw in the associated UlCsAlgoPara MO must be set off.
    • If FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, UL_COORD_PC_SWITCH of UlCsSw in the associated UlCsAlgoPara MO must be set off.
  • Relation with the SimuloadPmiTemplate, CellAlgoSwitch, eUCellSectorEqm, PDSCHCfg, and CellAlgoExtSwitch MOs:
    • The SINGLE_CA_PWR_SHARE_SW_FOR_4T option of TxChnPowerCfgSw in the PDSCHCfg MO for a cell must be deselected if any of the following condition is met: TxRxMode in the Cell MO is set to a value other than 4T4R, FddTddInd in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, CrsPortNum in the Cell MO is set to a value other than CRS_PORT_4, CellSimuloadPmi in the SimuloadPmiTemplate MO is set to 4, 5, 6, or 7, the 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch, Virtual4T4Rswitch, or New4TcodebookSwitch option of EmimoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the Dl256QamAccessorySwitch option of Dl256QamAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, or CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to ADVANCED_BEAMFORMING, or Tm9FourLayerPairSwitch of WttxMuMimoSwitch in a CellAlgoExtSwitch MO is selected.
  • Relation with the CellRbReserve and CellUlschAlgo MOs:
    • If RbRsvMode in a CellRbReserve MO under a Cell MO is set to NB_DEPLOYMENT and the value of CellActiveState in this Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, UlHoppingType in the CellUlschAlgo MO under this Cell MO must be set to HOPPING_OFF.
    • When RbRsvMode in a CellRbReserve MO is set to RB_MASKING_WITH_SRS and CellActiveState in an associated Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, UlHoppingType in a CellUlschAlgo MO under the Cell MO must be set to HOPPING_OFF.
  • Relation with the Prb MO:
    • When the DeployMode parameter in a Prb MO is set to IN_BAND(IN_BAND), the values of the PhyCellId parameters in the two Cell MOs associated by the LocalCellId and LteCellId parameters must be the same.
    • If DeployMode in the Prb MO is set to IN_BAND, FddTddInd and NbCellFlag in the Cell MO identified by LteCellId in the Prb MO must be set to CELL_FDD and FALSE, respectively.
    • When the DeployMode parameter in a Prb MO is set to IN_BAND, the cell associated by the LteCellId parameter is activated, and the CrsPortNum and TxRxMode parameters in the Cell MO associated by the LteCellId parameter are set to CRS_PORT_4 and 4T4R, respectively, the CrsPortNum and TxRxMode parameters in another Cell MO associated by the LocalCellId parameter must be set to CRS_PORT_2 and 4T4R, respectively. If the CrsPortNum and TxRxMode parameters in the Cell MO associated by the LteCellId parameter are set to CRS_PORT_4 and 2T2R, respectively, the CrsPortNum and TxRxMode parameters in another Cell MO associated by the LocalCellId parameter must be set to CRS_PORT_2 and 2T2R, respectively. If the CrsPortNum and TxRxMode parameters in the Cell MO associated by the LteCellId parameter are set to CRS_PORT_4 and 2T4R, respectively, the CrsPortNum and TxRxMode parameters in another Cell MO associated by the LocalCellId parameter must be set to CRS_PORT_2 and 2T4R, respectively. In other scenarios, the values of the CrsPortNum and TxRxMode parameters must be equal to their counterparts in the two cells associated by the LocalCellId and LteCellId parameters.
    • If the DeployMode parameter in a Prb MO is set to IN_BAND, the following conditions must be met when you activate the corresponding NB-IoT cell: The system bandwidth of the LTE cell identified by the LteCellId parameter in this Prb MO must be greater than or equal to 3 MHz.
