In addition, if the AnchorCarrierFlag parameter in the Prb MO is set to TRUE and the LteDlRbIndex parameter in this MO is set to an invalid value, the DlFreqOffset parameter in this MO must be set as follows: If the LTE cell bandwidth is 3 MHz, 5 MHz, or 15 MHz, DlFreqOffset can only be set only to NEG_2 or POS_1; if the LTE cell bandwidth is 10 MHz or 20 MHz, DlFreqOffset can only be set to NEG_1 or POS_0.
    • If NbCellFlag, MultiRruCellFlag, and MultiRruCellMode in a Cell MO are set to TRUE, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and MPRU_AGGREGATION, respectively, a maximum of eight Prb MOs can be configured under this Cell MO.
    • When the DeployMode parameter in the Prb MO is set to IN_BAND(IN_BAND), the value of the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MOs must be the same for the two cells indicated by the LocalCellId and LteCellId parameters, if the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE(BOOLEAN_TRUE), the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell MOs must be the same for the two cells indicated by the LocalCellId and LteCellId parameters, and value of the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell MOs must be set to SFN(SFN) or MPRU_AGGREGATION.
    • If the AnchorCarrierFlag, DeployMode, and LteDlRbIndex parameters in a Prb MO are set to TRUE, IN_BAND, and a valid value, respectively, then the setting of the LteDlRbIndex parameter must meet the following requirements:
    • If the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N15(3M) for the LTE cell, the LteDlRbIndex parameter can only be set to 2 or 12.
    • If the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N25(5M) for the LTE cell, the LteDlRbIndex parameter can only be set to 2, 7, 17, or 22.
    • If the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N50(10M) for the LTE cell, the LteDlRbIndex parameter can only be set to 4, 9, 14, 19, 30, 35, 40, or 45.
    • If the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N75(15M) for the LTE cell, the LteDlRbIndex parameter can only be set to 2, 7, 12, 17, 22, 27, 32, 42, 47, 52, 57, 62, 67, or 72.
    • If the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N100(20M) for the LTE cell, the LteDlRbIndex parameter can only be set to 4, 9, 14, 19, 24, 29, 34, 39, 44, 55, 60, 65, 70, 75, 80, 85, 90, or 95.
    • If NbCellFlag, MultiRruCellFlag, and MultiRruCellMode in a Cell MO are set to TRUE, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and SFN, respectively, a maximum of two Prb MOs can be configured under this Cell MO.
    • For any Cell MO served by an LMPT, a maximum of two Prb MOs can be configured.
    • When the DeployMode parameter in the Prb MO is set to IN_BAND(IN_BAND), the WorkMode parameter in the Cell MO for the LTE cell indicated by the LteCellId parameter must be set to UL_DL(Uplink and downlink).
    • NbCellFlag in the Cell MO identified by LocalCellId in the Prb MO must be set to TRUE.
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE and the CellActiveState parameter is set to CELL_ACTIVE, there must be only one Prb MO instance with the AnchorCarrierFlag parameter set to TRUE for this Cell MO.
    • When the AnchorCarrierFlag and DeployMode parameters in a Prb MO are set to TRUE and GUARD_BAND, respectively: For PRBs with the same LocalCellId in the Prb MO, if the AnchorCarrierFlag and DeployMode parameters are set to FALSE and IN_BAND, respectively, the DlEarfcn and DlBandWidth parameters of the LTE cell associated by the LteCellId parameter must be the same as the LteDlEarfcn and LteBandWidth parameters in the Prb MO instance with AnchorCarrierFlag set to TRUE.
    • When the AnchorCarrierFlag and DeployMode parameters in a Prb MO are set to TRUE and IN_BAND, respectively: For another PRB with the same LocalCellId, if the AnchorCarrierFlag and DeployMode parameters are set to FALSE and GUARD_BAND, respectively, the LteDlEarfcn and LteBandWidth parameters must be the same as the DlEarfcn and DlBandWidth parameters for the LTE cell associated by the LteCellId parameter in the Prb MO with AnchorCarrierFlag set to TRUE.
    • When the AnchorCarrierFlag and DeployMode parameters in a Prb MO are set to FALSE and IN_BAND, respectively: For PRBs with the same LocalCellId in the Prb MO, if the AnchorCarrierFlag and DeployMode parameters are set to TRUE and GUARD_BAND, respectively, the DlEarfcn and DlBandWidth parameters of the LTE cell associated by the LteCellId parameter must be the same as the LteDlEarfcn and LteBandWidth parameters in the Prb MO with AnchorCarrierFlag set to TRUE.
    • When the AnchorCarrierFlag and DeployMode parameters in a Prb MO are set to FALSE and GUARD_BAND, respectively: For PRBs with the same LocalCellId in the Prb MO, if the AnchorCarrierFlag and DeployMode parameters are set to TRUE and IN_BAND, respectively, the DlEarfcn and DlBandWidth parameters of the LTE cell associated by the LteCellId parameter must be the same as the LteDlEarfcn and LteBandWidth parameters in the Prb MO with AnchorCarrierFlag set to TRUE.
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, there must be only one Prb MO instance with the AnchorCarrierFlag parameter set to TRUE for this Cell MO.
    • The COVERAGE_LEVEL_0 option of the PrachCoverageLevelType parameter in a Prb MO can be selected and the CellActiveState parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE only when all the following conditions are true: The NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO associated by the LocalCellId parameter in this Prb MO is set to TRUE(TRUE) and the COVERAGE_LEVEL_0 option of the CoverageLevelType parameter is selected.
    • The COVERAGE_LEVEL_1 option of the PrachCoverageLevelType parameter in a Prb MO can be selected and the CellActiveState parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE only when all the following conditions are true: The NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO associated by the LocalCellId parameter in this Prb MO is set to TRUE(TRUE) and the COVERAGE_LEVEL_1 option of the CoverageLevelType parameter is selected.
    • The COVERAGE_LEVEL_2 option of the PrachCoverageLevelType parameter in a Prb MO can be selected and the CellActiveState parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE only when all the following conditions are true: The NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO associated by the LocalCellId parameter in this Prb MO is set to TRUE(TRUE) and the COVERAGE_LEVEL_2 option of the CoverageLevelType parameter is selected.
    • When the DeployMode and WorkMode parameters in a Prb MO are set to GUARD_BAND and UL_DL (or DL_ONLY), respectively, the following condition must be met when you activate the NB-IoT cell identified by the LocalCellId parameter in the Prb MO:
    • The 180 kHz frequency range with the frequency calculated based on the DlEarfcn and DlFreqOffset parameter settings as the center frequency must be within the LTE guard band calculated based on the LteDlEarfcn and LteBandWidth parameter settings.
    • A maximum of eight Prb MO instances can be configured for a Cell MO.
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE and CellActiveState is CELL_ACTIVE for this Cell MO, a maximum of four Prb MOs can be configured for the cell and DeployMode in the Prb MOs must be set to STAND_ALONE.
    • If DlBandWidth in the LTE Cell MO associated with LteCellId in the Prb MO is set to CELL_BW_N25 and CellActiveState in the corresponding NB-IoT Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, the number of Prb MO instances (under the NB-IoT Cell MO) with DeployMode set to IN_BAND and WorkMode set to UL_DL or DL_ONLY cannot exceed four.
    • If UlBandWidth in the LTE Cell MO associated with LteCellId in the Prb MO is set to CELL_BW_N25 and CellActiveState in the corresponding NB-IoT Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, the number of Prb MO instances (under the NB-IoT Cell MO) with DeployMode set to IN_BAND and WorkMode set to UL_DL or UL_ONLY cannot exceed four.
    • In the Cell MO, if NbCellFlag is set to TRUE and CellActiveState is CELL_ACTIVE, a maximum of two Prb MO instances can be configured for the cell and DeployMode in the Prb MO instances must be set to GUARD_BAND.
    • If DlBandWidth in the LTE Cell MO associated with LteCellId in the Prb MO is set to CELL_BW_N15 and CellActiveState in the corresponding NB-IoT Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, the number of Prb MO instances (under the NB-IoT Cell MO) with DeployMode set to IN_BAND cannot exceed two.
    • If there are more than two Prb instances under a Cell MO instance, WorkMode can be set to UL_ONLY or DL_ONLY in the Prb instances. In addition, the WorkMode parameter settings in all the Prb instances under the Cell MO cannot include both UL_ONLY and DL_ONLY, and the number of PRB MOs with WorkMode set to UL_DL must be greater than or equal to 2.
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the FreqBand parameter in a Prb MO under this Cell MO must be set to 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 8, 11, 12, 13, 17, 18, 19, 20, 21, 25, 26, 28, 31, 66, 70, 71, 72, 73, 74, 85, 111, or 222. If the DeployMode parameter in this Prb MO is set to IN_BAND, the FreqBand parameter in this Prb MO must be set to the same value as the FreqBand parameter in the Cell MO of the LTE cell identified by the LteCellId parameter.
    • If the DeployMode parameter in a Prb MO is set to STAND_ALONE or GUARD_BAND, or the DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO of the cell identified by the LteCellId parameter in this Prb MO is set to CELL_BW_N6, then the LteDlRbIndex and LteUlRbIndex parameter in this Prb MO must be set to an invalid value.
    • If the DeployMode parameter in a Prb MO is set to IN_BAND and the LteDlRbIndex parameter in this Prb MO is set to a valid value, the setting of the DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO of the LTE cell identified by the LteCellId parameter in this Prb MO and the setting of the LteDlRbIndex parameter in the Prb MO must meet the following requirements:
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N15, the LteDlRbIndex parameter must be set to a value in the range of 0 to 14.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N25, the LteDlRbIndex parameter must be set to a value in the range of 0 to 24.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N50, the LteDlRbIndex parameter must be set to a value in the range of 0 to 49.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N75, the LteDlRbIndex parameter must be set to a value in the range of 0 to 74.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N100, the LteDlRbIndex parameter must be set to a value in the range of 0 to 99.
    • If the DeployMode and UlEarfcnCfgInd parameters in a Prb MO are set to IN_BAND and CFG, respectively, and the LteUlRbIndex parameter in this Prb MO is set to a valid value, the setting of the DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO of the LTE cell identified by the LteCellId parameter in this Prb MO and the setting of the LteUlRbIndex parameter in the Prb MO must meet the following requirements:
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N15, the LteUlRbIndex parameter must be set to a value in the range of 0 to 14.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N25, the LteUlRbIndex parameter must be set to a value in the range of 0 to 24.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N50, the LteUlRbIndex parameter must be set to a value in the range of 0 to 49.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N75, the LteUlRbIndex parameter must be set to a value in the range of 0 to 74.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N100, the LteUlRbIndex parameter must be set to a value in the range of 0 to 99.
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the parameter settings in the Prb MOs under this Cell MO must meet the following requirements: If the LteDlRbIndex parameter in a Prb MO is set to a valid value, the DL EARFCN corresponding to the LteDlRbIndex parameter in this Prb MO must be different from the setting of the DlEarfcn parameter in any other Prb MO; if the LteDlRbIndex parameter is also set to a valid value in the latter Prb MO, the LteDlRbIndex parameter must be set to different values in the two Prb MOs. The UL EARFCNs of the PRBs must be different from one another. Specifically, if UlEarfcnCfgInd and LteUlRbIndex in a Prb MO are set to CFG and a valid value, respectively, the UL EARFCN of the PRB is calculated based on LteUlRbIndex in this Prb MO. If UlEarfcnCfgInd in a Prb MO is not set to CFG, then: (1) If LteDlRbIndex in this Prb MO is set to an invalid value, the UL EARFCN is automatically calculated based on DlEarfcn and DlFreqOffset in this Prb MO given that the system is symmetrical in the uplink and downlink. (2) If LteDlRbIndex in this Prb MO is set to a valid value, the UL EARFCN is calculated based on LteDlRbIndex in this Prb MO and DlEarfcn and DlBandWidth in the Cell MO of the LTE cell identified by LteCellId given that the system is symmetrical in the uplink and downlink.
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the parameter settings in the Prb MOs (under this Cell MO) with the same LocalCellId setting must meet the following requirements: The setting of DlEarfcn in any Prb MO with LteDlRbIndex set to an invalid value must be different from the setting of DlEarfcn parameter in any other Prb MO with LteDlRbIndex also set to an invalid value, or different from the DL EARFCN corresponding to LteDlRbIndex in any other Prb MO with LteDlRbIndex set to a valid value. The UL EARFCNs of the PRBs must be different from one another. Specifically, if UlEarfcnCfgInd and LteUlRbIndex in a Prb MO are set to CFG and an invalid value, respectively, the UL EARFCN of the PRB is specified by UlEarfcn in this Prb MO. If UlEarfcnCfgInd in a Prb MO is not set to CFG, then: (1) If LteDlRbIndex in this Prb MO is set to an invalid value, the UL EARFCN is automatically calculated based on DlEarfcn and DlFreqOffset in this Prb MO given that the system is symmetrical in the uplink and downlink. (2) If LteDlRbIndex in this Prb MO is set to a valid value, the UL EARFCN is automatically calculated based on LteDlRbIndex in this Prb MO and DlEarfcn and DlBandWidth in the Cell MO of the LTE cell identified by LteCellId given that the system is symmetrical in the uplink and downlink.
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the parameter settings in the Prb MOs under this Cell MO must meet the following requirements: If the LteDlRbIndex parameter in a Prb MO is set to a valid value, the DL EARFCN corresponding to the LteDlRbIndex parameter in this Prb MO must be different from the setting of the DlEarfcn parameter in any other Prb MO; if the LteDlRbIndex parameter is also set to a valid value in the latter Prb MO, the LteDlRbIndex parameter must be set to different values in the two Prb MOs. The UL EARFCNs of the PRBs must be different from one another. Specifically, if UlEarfcnCfgInd and LteUlRbIndex in a Prb MO are set to CFG and a valid value, respectively, the UL EARFCN of the PRB is calculated based on LteUlRbIndex in this Prb MO. If UlEarfcnCfgInd in a Prb MO is not set to CFG, then: (1) If LteDlRbIndex in this Prb MO is set to an invalid value, the UL EARFCN is automatically calculated based on DlEarfcn and DlFreqOffset in this Prb MO given that the system is symmetrical in the uplink and downlink. (2) If LteDlRbIndex in this Prb MO is set to a valid value, the UL EARFCN is calculated based on LteDlRbIndex in this Prb MO and DlEarfcn and DlBandWidth in the Cell MO of the LTE cell identified by LteCellId given that the system is symmetrical in the uplink and downlink.
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the parameter settings in the Prb MOs (under this Cell MO) with the same LocalCellId setting must meet the following requirements: The setting of DlEarfcn in any Prb MO with LteDlRbIndex set to an invalid value must be different from the setting of DlEarfcn parameter in any other Prb MO with LteDlRbIndex also set to an invalid value, or different from the DL EARFCN corresponding to LteDlRbIndex in any other Prb MO with LteDlRbIndex set to a valid value. The UL EARFCNs of the PRBs must be different from one another. Specifically, if UlEarfcnCfgInd and LteUlRbIndex in a Prb MO are set to CFG and an invalid value, respectively, the UL EARFCN of the PRB is specified by UlEarfcn in this Prb MO. If UlEarfcnCfgInd in a Prb MO is not set to CFG, then: (1) If LteDlRbIndex in this Prb MO is set to an invalid value, the UL EARFCN is automatically calculated based on DlEarfcn and DlFreqOffset in this Prb MO given that the system is symmetrical in the uplink and downlink. (2) If LteDlRbIndex in this Prb MO is set to a valid value, the UL EARFCN is automatically calculated based on LteDlRbIndex in this Prb MO and DlEarfcn and DlBandWidth in the Cell MO of the LTE cell identified by LteCellId given that the system is symmetrical in the uplink and downlink.
    • In a Prb MO, if the DeployMode parameter is set to IN_BAND, the AnchorCarrierFlag parameter is set to FALSE, and the LteDlRbIndex parameter is set to a valid value, then the LteDlRbIndex parameter cannot be set to the following value depending on the setting of the DlBandWidth in the Cell MO for the LTE cell identified by the LteCellId parameter in the Prb MO: 7 if the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N15, 12 if the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N25, or 37 if the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N75.
    • If CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd in the LTE Cell MO associated with LteCellId in the Prb MO is set to CFG, DlBandWidth in the LTE Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50, and CellActiveState in the corresponding NB-IoT Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, then the number of Prb MO instances (under the NB-IoT Cell MO) with DeployMode set to IN_BAND and WorkMode set to UL_DL or DL_ONLY cannot exceed four.
    • If CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd in the LTE Cell MO associated with LteCellId in the Prb MO is set to CFG, UlBandWidth in the LTE Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50, and CellActiveState in the corresponding NB-IoT Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, then the number of Prb MO instances (under the NB-IoT Cell MO) with DeployMode set to IN_BAND and WorkMode set to UL_DL or UL_ONLY cannot exceed four.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch and CellRbReserve MOs:
    • When the RbRsvMode parameter in a CellRbReserve MO is set to NB_DEPLOYMENT(NB_DEPLOYMENT), and the CellActiveState in the Cell MO associated with the CellRbReserve MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the DynAdjVoltSwitch option of the DynAdjVoltSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected for the cell.
    • When the RbRsvMode parameter in a CellRbReserve MO is set to NB_DEPLOYMENT(NB_DEPLOYMENT), and the CellActiveState in the Cell MO associated with the CellRbReserve MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the SuperCombCellSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected for the cell.
  • Relation with the CellRbReserve and InterRatCellShutdown MOs:
    • When the RbRsvMode parameter in a CellRbReserve MO is set to NB_DEPLOYMENT(NB_DEPLOYMENT), and the CellActiveState in the Cell MO associated with the CellRbReserve MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the ForceShutdownSwitch parameter in the InterRatCellShutdown MO must be set to OFF(Off) for the cell.
  • Relation with the InterRatCellShutdown MO:
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, ForceShutdownSwitch in the InterRatCellShutdown must be set OFF.
  • Relation with the CellShutdown MO:
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the CellShutdownSwitch parameter in the CellShutdown MO under this Cell MO must be set to OFF or ON.
    • If the CellShutdownSwitch parameter in the CellShutdown MO under a Cell MO is set to a value other than OFF and at least one of the UlPrbThd and DlPrbThd parameters in the CellShutdown MO is set to 0, then the WorkMode parameter in the Cell MO cannot be set to DL_ONLY or LAA.
  • Relation with the EuSectorEqmGroup, CellAuxEnbBind, CellAuxDuBind, eUCellSectorEqm, EuPrbSectorEqm, and EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MOs:
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE(TRUE), the eUCellSectorEqm, EuSectorEqmGroup, CellAuxDuBind and CellAuxEnbBind MOs could not be configured; When the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell is set to FALSE, the EuPrbSectorEqm, EuPrbSectorEqmGroup could not be configured.
  • Relation with the CellDss MO:
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the ReMuteSwitch in the CellDss MO must be deselected.
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the FlexSpecSchSwitch option of the SpecShrPfmOptSwitch in the CellDss MO must be deselected.
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the UlSchInterfAvdSwitch option of the SpecShrPfmOptSwitch in the CellDss MO must be deselected.
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the HoZoneUserSchSwitch option of the SpecShrPfmOptSwitch in the CellDss MO must be deselected.
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the UlShrSpecSchSwitch option of the SpecShrPfmOptSwitch in the CellDss MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the CellEmtcAlgo and CellDss MOs:
    • Under the same cell, when the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to FALSE and the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellEmtcAlgo MO is selected, the ReMuteSwitch parameter in a CellDss MO must not be set to ON.
  • Relation with the CellEmtcAlgo and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • Under the same cell, when the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to FALSE and the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellEmtcAlgo MO is selected, the MBSFNShutDownSwitch option of the DlSchSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch, CellAlgoSwitch, and CellAlgoExtSwitch MOs:
    • When the MultiRruCellFlag and MultiRruCellMode parameters in a Cell MO is set to TRUE and MPRU_AGGREGATION or SFN, respectively, and the SfnDlSchSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ADAPTIVE, the 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in this CellAlgoSwitch MO and Tm9FourLayerPairSwitch of WttxMuMimoSwitch in a CellAlgoExtSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the RruJointCalParaCfg MO:
    • When the NbCellFlag and TxRxMode parameters in a Cell MO are set to TRUE and 4T4R, respectively, the TxChnCalSwitch parameter in the RruJointCalParaCfg under the Cell MO must be set to OFF.
    • The UlCyclicPrefix and DlCyclicPrefix parameters in a Cell MO must be set to NORMAL_CP when the AauPassivePortCalibPeriod parameter in the RruJointCalParaCfg MO is not set to 0.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and PHICHCfg MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50, and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the PhichResource parameter in the associated PHICHCfg MO cannot be set to TWO.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the PhichDuration parameter in the associated PHICHCfg MO must be set to NORMAL.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • When the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 and the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, or the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the FDDHomNetDlCompSwitch option of the DlCompSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 and the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, or the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the FDDHetNetDlCompSwitch option of the DlCompSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 and the TxRxMode parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to 8T8R, or if the CellBeamMode parameter is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the UlVmimoSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 and the TxRxMode parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to 4T4R, the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When the CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 and the TxRxMode in the Cell is set to 8T8R, or when the CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, UL_HIGH_ORDER_COMP_FOR_2R under the UlHighOrderCompSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch must be turned off.
    • In a Cell MO, if the CellActiveState parameter is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, the MultiRruCellMode parameter is set to CELL_COMBINATION, and more than six eUCellSectorEqm MO instances are configured, then the DmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must not be selected.
    • In a Cell MO, if the CellActiveState parameter is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, the MultiRruCellMode parameter is set to CELL_COMBINATION, the HighSpeedFlag parameter is set to LOW_SPEED, and more than six eUCellSectorEqm MO instances are configured, then neither the SfnDlSchSwitch parameter nor the SfnUlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be set to ADAPTIVE.
    • If the value of CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd and TxRxMode in this Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and 8T8R, respectively, and CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to NORMAL, then the Dl256QamAccessorySwitch option of Dl256QamAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
    • If the value of CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd and TxRxMode in this Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and 8T8R, respectively, and CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to NORMAL, then the AdptCellEdgePwrAllocSw option of CellDlCoverEnhanceSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
    • If the value of CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd and TxRxMode in this Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and 8T8R, respectively, and CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to NORMAL, then the EDlMaxTXPwrSwitch option of DlPcAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
    • If the value of CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd and TxRxMode in this Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and 8T8R, respectively, and CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to NORMAL, then the 8T4PortTM9EnhanceSwitch option of EnhMIMOSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
    • If the value of CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd and TxRxMode in this Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and 8T8R, respectively, and CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to NORMAL, then PwrDeratSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the value of CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd and TxRxMode in this Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and 8T8R, respectively, and CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to NORMAL, then the UL_HIGH_ORDER_COMP_FOR_2R option of UlHighOrderCompSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be deselected and the UlVmimoSwitch option of UlSchSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must also be deselected.
    • If the value of the CellActiveState parameter in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, the FddTddInd and TxRxMode parameters in this Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and 8T8R, respectively, and the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to NORMAL, then the FDDHomNetDlCompSwitch and FDDHetNetDlCompSwitch options of the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the CellLteFlexBw MO:
    • When the CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CFG, the LTE_FB_CFG_SWITCH option of the LteFlexBwSwitch parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO must be deselected.
    • When the LTE_FB_CFG_SWITCH option of the LteFlexBwSwitch parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is selected, the UlBandWidth and DlBandWidth parameters in the associated Cell MO must be set to CELL_BW_N25, CELL_BW_N50, CELL_BW_N75, or CELL_BW_N100.
    • The LTE_FB_CFG_SWITCH option of the LteFlexBwSwitch parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO can be selected only if the FddTddInd parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD.
    • The LTE_FB_CFG_SWITCH option of the LteFlexBwSwitch parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO cannot be selected if the HighSpeedFlag parameter in a Cell MO is not set to LOW_SPEED.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50 and the value of the DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is smaller than the value of the DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the DlCustStartPrbIndex and DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter values must be within the range of [0, 21] and [28, 49], respectively.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50 and the value of the DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is greater than the value of the DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter value must be within the range of [0, 21], or the DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter value must be within the range of [28, 49].
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75 and the value of the DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is smaller than the value of the DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the DlCustStartPrbIndex and DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter values must be within the range of [0, 33] and [41, 74], respectively.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75 and the value of the DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is greater than the value of the DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter value must be within the range of [0, 33], or the DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter value must be within the range of [41, 74].
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100 and the value of the DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is smaller than the value of the DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the DlCustStartPrbIndex and DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter values must be within the range of [0, 46] and [53, 99], respectively.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100 and the value of the DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is greater than the value of the DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter value must be within the range of [0, 46], or the DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter value must be within the range of [53, 99].
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50 and the value of the DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is smaller than the value of the DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the following condition must be met: DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter value ≥ (DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter value + 50 x 0.75 - 1).
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75 and the value of the DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is smaller than the value of the DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the following condition must be met: DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter value ≥ (DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter value + 75 x 0.75 - 1).
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100 and the value of the DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is smaller than the value of the DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the following condition must be met: DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter value ≥ (DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter value + 100 x 0.75 - 1).
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50 and the value of the DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is greater than the value of the DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the following condition must be met: DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter value ≥ (DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter value - 50 x 0.25 + 1).
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75 and the value of the DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is greater than the value of the DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the following condition must be met: DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter value ≥ (DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter value - 75 x 0.25 + 1).
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100 and the value of the DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is greater than the value of the DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the following condition must be met: DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter value ≥ (DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter value - 100 x 0.25 + 1).
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50 and the value of the UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is smaller than the value of the UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the following condition must be met: UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter value ≥ (UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter value + 50 x 0.75 - 1).
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75 and the value of the UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is smaller than the value of the UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the following condition must be met: UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter value ≥ (UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter value + 75 x 0.75 - 1).
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100 and the value of the UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is smaller than the value of the UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the following condition must be met: UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter value ≥ (UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter value + 100 x 0.75 - 1).
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50 and the value of the UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is greater than the value of the UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the following condition must be met: UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter value ≥ (UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter value - 50 x 0.25 + 1).
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75 and the value of the UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is greater than the value of the UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the following condition must be met: UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter value ≥ (UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter value - 75 x 0.25 + 1).
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100 and the value of the UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is greater than the value of the UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the following condition must be met: UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter value ≥ (UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter value - 100 x 0.25 + 1).
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50, the values of the UlCustStartPrbIndex and UlCustEndPrbIndex parameters in a CellLteFlexBw MO must be within the range of [0, 49].
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75, the values of the UlCustStartPrbIndex and UlCustEndPrbIndex parameters in a CellLteFlexBw MO must be within the range of [0, 74].
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100, the values of the UlCustStartPrbIndex and UlCustEndPrbIndex parameters in a CellLteFlexBw MO must be within the range of [0, 99].
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50, the values of the DlCustStartPrbIndex and DlCustEndPrbIndex parameters in a CellLteFlexBw MO must be within the range of [0, 49].
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75, the values of the DlCustStartPrbIndex and DlCustEndPrbIndex parameters in a CellLteFlexBw MO must be within the range of [0, 74].
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100, the values of the DlCustStartPrbIndex and DlCustEndPrbIndex parameters in a CellLteFlexBw MO must be within the range of [0, 99].
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the number of CellLteFlexBw MO must be equal to 0.
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50 and the value of the UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is smaller than the value of the UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the UlCustStartPrbIndex and UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter values must be within the range of [0, 21] and [28, 49], respectively.
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50 and the value of the UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is greater than the value of the UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter value must be within the range of [0, 21], or the UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter value must be within the range of [28, 49].
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75 and the value of the UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is smaller than the value of the UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the UlCustStartPrbIndex and UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter values must be within the range of [0, 33] and [41, 74], respectively.
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75 and the value of the UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is greater than the value of the UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter value must be within the range of [0, 33], or the UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter value must be within the range of [41, 74].
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100 and the value of the UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is smaller than the value of the UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the UlCustStartPrbIndex and UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter values must be within the range of [0, 46] and [53, 99], respectively.
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100 and the value of the UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is greater than the value of the UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter value must be within the range of [0, 46], or the UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter value must be within the range of [53, 99].
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25, the values of the DlCustStartPrbIndex and DlCustEndPrbIndex parameters in a CellLteFlexBw MO must be within the range of [0, 24].
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25, the values of the UlCustStartPrbIndex and UlCustEndPrbIndex parameters in a CellLteFlexBw MO must be within the range of [0, 24].
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25 and the value of the DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is smaller than the value of the DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the DlCustStartPrbIndex and DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter values must be within the range of [0, 8] and [16, 24], respectively.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25 and the value of the DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is greater than the value of the DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter value must be within the range of [0, 8], or the DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter value must be within the range of [16, 24].
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25 and the value of the UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is smaller than the value of the UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the UlCustStartPrbIndex and UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter values must be within the range of [0, 8] and [16, 24], respectively.
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25 and the value of the UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is greater than the value of the UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter value must be within the range of [0, 8], or the UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter value must be within the range of [16, 24].
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25 and the value of the DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is smaller than the value of the DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the following condition must be met: DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter value ≥ (DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter value + 25 x 0.75 - 1).
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25 and the value of the DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is greater than the value of the DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the following condition must be met: DlCustEndPrbIndex parameter value ≥ (DlCustStartPrbIndex parameter value - 25 x 0.25 + 1).
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25 and the value of the UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is smaller than the value of the UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the following condition must be met: UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter value ≥ (UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter value + 25 x 0.75 - 1).
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25 and the value of the UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is greater than the value of the UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter in the same MO, the following condition must be met: UlCustEndPrbIndex parameter value ≥ (UlCustStartPrbIndex parameter value - 25 x 0.25 + 1).
  • Relation with the CellLteFlexBwItfCfg MO:
    • A maximum of six CellLteFlexBwItfCfg MO instances can be configured for a cell.
  • Relation with the CellLteFlexBw and CellLteFlexBwItfCfg MOs:
    • When the LTE_FB_CFG_SWITCH option of the LteFlexBwSwitch parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is selected, the downlink GSM frequencies indicated by the GsmCarrierFreq and HighItfGsmArfcn parameters in a CellLteFlexBwItfCfg MO must meet the frequency range constraints of the following parameters: DlEarfcn, FreqBand, and DlBandWidth in the associated Cell MO and DlCustStartPrbIndex and DlCustEndPrbIndex in the CellLteFlexBw MO.
    • When the LTE_FB_CFG_SWITCH option of the LteFlexBwSwitch parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO is selected, the uplink GSM frequencies indicated by the GsmCarrierFreq and HighItfGsmArfcn parameters in a CellLteFlexBwItfCfg MO must meet the frequency range constraints of the following parameters: UlEarfcn, FreqBand, and UlBandWidth in the associated Cell MO and UlCustStartPrbIndex and UlCustEndPrbIndex in the CellLteFlexBw MO.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, BfAnt, and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • If the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and no SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, a BfAnt MO must be associated with the WeightNO parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO bound to the cell, and the FreqBand parameter in the Cell MO must be set to the same value as the Band parameter.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell, eUCellSectorEqm, and BfAnt MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and no SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, a BfAnt MO must be associated with the WeightNO parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO bound to the cell, and the CoverageScenario parameter in the BfAnt MO must not be set to NONE.
  • Relation with the CellPdcchAlgo, eUCellSectorEqm, and BfAnt MOs:
    • When the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE(Active) and the MASSIVE_MIMO_PDCCH_SDMA_SW option of the SplitBeamPdcchSdmaSw parameter in the CellPdcchAlgo MO is selected, the BfAnt MO associated with the WeightNO parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO bound to the cell must exist, and the CoverageScenario parameter in the BfAnt MO must be set to SCENARIO_2(HBW 65 Degree, VBW 8 Degree), SCENARIO_3(HBW 45 Degree, VBW 8 Degree), SCENARIO_4(HBW 25 Degree, VBW 8 Degree), SCENARIO_6(HBW 65 Degree, VBW 17 Degree), SCENARIO_7(HBW 45 Degree, VBW 17 Degree), SCENARIO_8(HBW 25 Degree, VBW 17 Degree), SCENARIO_10(HBW 65 Degree, VBW 35 Degree), SCENARIO_11(HBW 45 Degree, VBW 35 Degree), or SCENARIO_12(HBW 25 Degree, VBW 35 Degree).
  • Relation with the CellEmtcAlgo and CellLteFlexBw MOs:
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25(5M), while EMTC_SWITCH of EmtcAlgoSwitch in a CellEmtcAlgo MO associated with the Cell MO is selected and LTE_FB_CFG_SWITCH of LteFlexBwSwitch in the CellLteFlexBw MO associated with the Cell MO is also selected, DlCustStartPrbIndex must be set to 0 or 255, and DlCustEndPrbIndex must be set to 24 or 255.
  • Relation with the CellEmtcAlgo and DdCellGroup MOs:
    • The EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO cannot be selected when any of the following conditions is met:
    • The DdBandwidth parameter in the DdCellGroup MO is set to BW_5DOT7M(5.7M), BW_7DOT3M(7.3M), BW_7DOT6M(7.6M), BW_8DOT6M(8.6M), or BW_8DOT9M(8.9M).
    • The DdBandwidth parameter in the DdCellGroup MO is set to BW_4M(4M) and the DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO for the cell identified by the LocalCellId1 or LocalCellId2 parameter in this DdCellGroup MO is set to CELL_BW_N15(3M).
    • The DdBandwidth parameter in the DdCellGroup MO is set to BW_12DOT2M(12.2M) and the DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO for the cell identified by the LocalCellId1 or LocalCellId2 parameter in this DdCellGroup MO is set to CELL_BW_N15(3M).
    • The DdBandwidth parameter in the DdCellGroup MO is set to BW_27DOT6M(27.6M) and the DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO for the cell identified by the LocalCellId1 or LocalCellId2 parameter in this DdCellGroup MO is set to CELL_BW_N50(10M).
    • The DdBandwidth parameter in the DdCellGroup MO is set to BW_14DOT1M(14.1M) and the DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO for the cell identified by the LocalCellId1 or LocalCellId2 parameter in this DdCellGroup MO is set to CELL_BW_N25(5M).
    • The DdBandwidth parameter in the DdCellGroup MO is set to BW_18DOT7M(18.7M) and the DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO for the cell identified by the LocalCellId1 or LocalCellId2 parameter in this DdCellGroup MO is set to CELL_BW_N25(5M).
  • Relation with the ENodeBSharingMode, CellOp, CnOperator, and CnOperatorTa MOs:
    • When the ENodeBSharingMode parameter in an ENodeBSharingMode MO is set to SEPARATED_FREQ_SPEC and the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO corresponding to the CellOp MO is set to TRUE, the CnOperatorType parameter in the CnOperator MO corresponding to this CellOp MO must be set to CNOPERATOR_PRIMARY.
    • When the ENodeBSharingMode parameter in an ENodeBSharingMode MO is set to SHARED_FREQ or SEPARATED_FREQ or HYBRID_SHARED, the total number of CnOperator MO instances associated with the CellOp MO under the Cell with NbCellFlag set to TRUE must not exceed four.
  • Relation with the CellUlschAlgo MO:
    • The HighSpeedSdmaSwitch option of the VmimoOptAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO cannot be selected when the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD or the HighSpeedFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to LOW_SPEED(Low speed cell flag).
    • The HighLoadUlSchOptSwitch parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO under a Cell MO can be set to ON only if the MultiRruCellFlag and MultiRruCellMode parameters in this Cell MO are set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and MPRU_AGGREGATION, respectively.
    • If the HighSpeedFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to LOW_SPEED, the IntvlOfUlSpsWithSkipping parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO can be set to a value other than MS0.
    • If the UlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to a value other than CELL_BW_N6 and CELL_BW_N15, the IntvlOfUlSpsWithSkipping parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO can be set to a value other than MS0.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, the IntvlOfUlSpsWithSkipping parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO can only be set to MS0.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the SubframeAssignment parameter in the Cell MO is set to SA2, the IntvlOfUlSpsWithSkipping parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO can be set to a value other than MS0.
    • If the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell is 8T8R or 32T32R or 64T64R, and EstimatedTcpAckPacketCount in the CellUlschAlgo is not 0, then the UlHeavyloadOptSchSwitch parameter in the CellUlschAlgo is ON.
    • The MmVmimoUePairingOptSw option of the VmimoOptAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO can be selected only if the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 32T32R or 64T64R, the EstimatedTcpAckPacketCount parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO is set to a non-zero value, the UlHeavyloadOptSchSwitch parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO is set to ON, and the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud MO:
    • The SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in a SpectrumCloud MO can be set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY only when the FreqBand parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to 2, 3, 5, 8, or 9.
    • When the HighSpeedFlag parameter in the Cell MO is not set to LOW_SPEED, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter cannot be set to LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR.
    • If SpectrumCloudSwitch in the SpectrumCloud MO under a Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR, then MultiRruCellFlag in the Cell MO must be set to BOOLEAN_FALSE (alternatively, MultiRruCellFlag and MultiRruCellMode in the Cell MO must be set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and MPRU_AGGREGATION, respectively.)
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the UlBandWidth parameter in the same Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100, CELL_BW_N75, or CELL_BW_N50, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter can be set to LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR.
    • When the WorkMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to UL_DL, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud can be set to LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR or LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR.
    • When the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud is set to LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR or LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the UlCyclicPrefix parameter in the Cell MO must be set to NORMAL_CP.
    • If FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and SpectrumCloudSwitch in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, then MultiRruCellFlag in this Cell MO must be set to BOOLEAN_FALSE, or MultiRruCellFlag and MultiRruCellMode in this Cell MO must be set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and MPRU_AGGREGATION, respectively.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, then the DlBandWidth, UlBandWidth, UlCyclicPrefix, DlCyclicPrefix, and CellRadius parameters in the Cell MO must be set to CELL_BW_N50, CELL_BW_N75 or CELL_BW_N100, CELL_BW_N50, CELL_BW_N75 or CELL_BW_N100, NORMAL_CP, NORMAL_CP, a value less than or equal to 100000 m, respectively.
    • When the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR or LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR and the FddTddInd parameter in this Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the LteNrSpectrumShrCellGrpId parameter can be referenced by only one cell.
    • If the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under a Cell MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING and the UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW option of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is selected, the TxRxMode parameter in this Cell MO must be set to 1T1R, 1T2R, 2T2R, 2T4R, 4T4R, or 32T32R.
    • If the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under a Cell MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING and the UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW option of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is deselected, the TxRxMode parameter in this Cell MO must be set to 1T1R, 1T2R, 2T2R, 2T4R, or 4T4R.
    • If the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under a Cell is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, the UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW option of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is selected, and the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 32T32R, then the UlBandWidth and DlBandWidth parameters in the Cell MO must be set to CELL_BW_N75 or CELL_BW_N100.
    • When the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in a SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR or UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, and the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in the same MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW, the UlBandWidth and DlBandWidth parameters in the associated Cell MO must be set to CELL_BW_N15, CELL_BW_N25, CELL_BW_N50, CELL_BW_N75, or CELL_BW_N100.
    • When the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR, UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR, or LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, and the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, the CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd parameter in the associated Cell MO must not be set to CFG.
    • When the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR, UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING or LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR, the FddTddInd parameter in the associated Cell MO must be set to CELL_FDD, and the NbCellFlag parameter in this Cell MO must not be set to TRUE.
    • When the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in a SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY or UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING and the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch is not set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW, the UlBandWidth and DlBandWidth parameters in the associated Cell MO must be set to CELL_BW_N25, CELL_BW_N50, CELL_BW_N75, or CELL_BW_N100.
    • When the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in a SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING or UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, the FddTddInd parameter in the associated Cell MO must be set to CELL_FDD, and the NbCellFlag parameter in this Cell MO must not be set to TRUE.
    • When the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, and the FddTddInd parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the NbCellFlag parameter in this Cell MO must not be set to TRUE.
    • When the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in a SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM or DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR, the TxRxMode parameter in the associated Cell MO must be set to 1T1R, 1T2R, 2T2R, 2T4R, or 4T4R.
    • If the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING and the UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW option of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in the same MO is selected, or if the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM or DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR, then the FreqBand parameter in the Cell MO must be set to 19, 25, 26, or a value in the range of 1 to 6 or 8 to 10.
    • When the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in a SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR, UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM or UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the associated Cell MO must not be set to MPRU_AGGREGATION.
    • If the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING and the UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW option of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in the same MO is deselected, the FreqBand parameter in the Cell MO must be set to 19, 25, 26, 30, or a value in the range of 1 to 6 or 8 to 10.
    • When the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in a SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, the TxRxMode parameter in the associated Cell MO must be set to 1T1R, 1T2R, 2T2R, 2T4R, 4T4R or 32T32R.
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO corresponding to the LocalCellId parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to CELL_TDD, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO can be set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR only if the UlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100 and the DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100, or the UlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50 and the DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR or LTE_NR_COORD_SPECTRUM_SHR, the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell MO must not be set to SFN, CELL_COMBINATION, TWO_RRU_COMBINATION, or DIGITAL_COMBINATION for this cell.
    • For all Cell MOs with the same LteNrSpectrumShrCellGrpId parameter value and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR in the SpectrumCloud MO, if the FddTddInd parameter in these Cell MOs is set to CELL_TDD and the CellActiveState parameter in these Cell MOs is set to CELL_ACTIVE, then the SubframeAssignment and SpecialSubframePatterns parameters in these Cell MOs must be set to the same values.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO must be set to CELL_TDD for all cells that have the same value of the LteNrSpectrumShrCellGrpId parameter and the same value of SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter as this cell.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the HighSpeedFlag parameter in the Cell MO must be set to LOW_SPEED for all cells that have the same value of the LteNrSpectrumShrCellGrpId parameter as this cell.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO corresponding to the LocalCellId parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to CELL_TDD, only when the SubframeAssignment in this Cell MO is set to SA2 and SpecialSubframePatterns parameters in this Cell MO is set to SSP7, SSP6 or SSP5, respectively, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO can be set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR.
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO corresponding to the LocalCellId parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to CELL_TDD, and UlBandWidth and DlBandWidth parameters are set to CELL_BW_N100 or UlBandWidth and DlBandWidth parameters are set to CELL_BW_N75 or UlBandWidth and DlBandWidth parameters are set to CELL_BW_N50, or when the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO corresponding to the LocalCellId parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to CELL_FDD, and UlBandWidth and DlBandWidth parameters are set to CELL_BW_N100 or UlBandWidth and DlBandWidth parameters are set to CELL_BW_N75 or UlBandWidth and DlBandWidth parameters are set to CELL_BW_N50 or UlBandWidth and DlBandWidth parameters are set to CELL_BW_N25, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in this SpectrumCloud MO can be set to LTE_NR_COORD_SPECTRUM_SHR.
  • Relation with the GsmInterfArfcnCfg and SpectrumCloud MOs:
    • When the FreqBand parameter in a Cell MO is set to 2 and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in a SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, the GsmArfcn parameter in the GsmInterfArfcnCfg MO corresponding to the SpectrumCloud MO under the Cell MO must be within the range of 512 to 810.
    • When the FreqBand parameter in a Cell MO is set to 3 and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in a SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, the GsmArfcn parameter in the GsmInterfArfcnCfg MO corresponding to the SpectrumCloud MO under the Cell MO must be within the range of 512 to 885.
    • When the FreqBand parameter in a Cell MO is set to 5 and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in a SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, the GsmArfcn parameter in the GsmInterfArfcnCfg MO corresponding to the SpectrumCloud MO under the Cell MO must be within the range of 128 to 251.
    • When the FreqBand parameter in a Cell MO is set to 8 and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in a SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, the GsmArfcn parameter in the GsmInterfArfcnCfg MO corresponding to the SpectrumCloud MO under the Cell MO must be within the range of 0 to 124 or 975 to 1023.
    • When the FreqBand parameter in a Cell MO is set to 9 and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in a SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, the GsmArfcn parameter in the GsmInterfArfcnCfg MO corresponding to the SpectrumCloud MO under the Cell MO must be within the range of 711 to 885.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameters in a Cell MO are set to CELL_BW_N25 and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in a SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, the GsmArfcn parameter in the GsmInterfArfcnCfg MO corresponding to the SpectrumCloud MO under the Cell MO must be set to a value indicating a frequency within 1.1 MHz from the LTE start/end frequency.
    • If the DlBandWidth parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_BW_N50, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, and the GL_SPCT_CONCURRENCY_PH2_SW option of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is selected, the GsmArfcn parameter in the GsmInterfArfcnCfg MO associated with the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO must be set to a value indicating a frequency within 2.8 MHz from the LTE start/end frequency.
    • If the DlBandWidth parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_BW_N75, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, and the GL_SPCT_CONCURRENCY_PH2_SW option of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is selected, the GsmArfcn parameter in the GsmInterfArfcnCfg MO associated with the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO must be set to a value indicating a frequency within 3.8 MHz from the LTE start/end frequency.
    • If the DlBandWidth parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_BW_N100, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, and the GL_SPCT_CONCURRENCY_PH2_SW option of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is selected, the GsmArfcn parameter in the GsmInterfArfcnCfg MO associated with the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO must be set to a value indicating a frequency within 4.8 MHz from the LTE start/end frequency.
    • If the DlBandWidth parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_BW_N50, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, and the GL_SPCT_CONCURRENCY_PH2_SW option of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is deselected, the GsmArfcn parameter in the GsmInterfArfcnCfg MO associated with the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO must be set to a value indicating a frequency within 2.1 MHz from the LTE start/end frequency.
    • If the DlBandWidth parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_BW_N75, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, and the GL_SPCT_CONCURRENCY_PH2_SW option of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is deselected, the GsmArfcn parameter in the GsmInterfArfcnCfg MO associated with the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO must be set to a value indicating a frequency within 2.5 MHz from the LTE start/end frequency.
    • If the DlBandWidth parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_BW_N100, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, and the GL_SPCT_CONCURRENCY_PH2_SW option of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is deselected, the GsmArfcn parameter in the GsmInterfArfcnCfg MO associated with the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO must be set to a value indicating a frequency within 3.1 MHz from the LTE start/end frequency.
  • Relation with the CellMMAlgo, eUCellSectorEqm, and BfAnt MOs:
    • When the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE(Active) and the BeamGainLimitSwitch parameter in the CellMMAlgo MO is set to ON(On), the BfAnt MO associated with the WeightNO parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO bound to the cell must exist, and the CoverageScenario parameter in the BfAnt MO must not be set to SCENARIO_3(HBW 45 Degree, VBW 8 Degree), SCENARIO_4(HBW 25 Degree, VBW 8 Degree), SCENARIO_8(HBW 25 Degree, VBW 17 Degree), SCENARIO_9(HBW 15 Degree, VBW 17 Degree), or SCENARIO_13(HBW 15 Degree, VBW 35 Degree).
  • Relation with the CellSidelinkV2xCfg MO:
    • When the SIDELINK_V2X_SWITCH option of the SidelinkV2xCfgSwitch parameter in a CellSidelinkV2xCfg MO is selected, the NbCellFlag parameter in the associated Cell MO must be set to FALSE.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch, CellBfMimoParaCfg, and CellBf MOs:
    • The TurboBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only if the FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, TxRxMode, and MultiCellShareMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD, BOOLEAN_FALSE, 8T8R, and NORMAL respectively, the BfSwitch option of the BfAlgoSwitch parameter and MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are selected, the FreqSelSwitch option of the DlSchSwitch parameter and InterCellDmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are deselected, the HighOrderMubfMaxLayer parameter in the CellBf MO is set to FOUR_LAYERS, and the BfMimoAdaptiveSwitch parameter in the CellBfMimoParaCfg MO is set to MIMO_BF_ADAPTIVE.
  • Relation with the ENodeBAlgoSwitch and SRSCfg MOs:
    • If the MultiCellShareMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to SPLIT and the SsrdSchOptSwitch option of the SSRDAlgoSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the TddSrsCfgMode parameter in the SRSCfg MO must be set to EXPERIENCE_ENHANCED or ACCESS_ENHANCED.
  • Relation with the ENodeBAlgoSwitch and CellRicAlgo MOs:
    • If the MultiCellShareMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to SPLIT and the SsrdSchOptSwitch option of the SSRDAlgoSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the MuteUpPTSSymNum parameter in the CellRicAlgo MO must be set to 0.
  • Relation with the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO:
    • If the MultiCellShareMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to SPLIT and the SsrdSchOptSwitch option of the SSRDAlgoSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the PreambleFmt parameter in the Cell MO must not be set to 4.
    • If the MultiCellShareMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to SPLIT and the SsrdSchOptSwitch option of the SSRDAlgoSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the SpecialSubframePatterns parameter in the Cell MO must not be set to SSP0(SSP0), SSP1(SSP1), SSP2(SSP2), SSP3(SSP3), or SSP4(SSP4).
  • Relation with the SRSCfg, CellRicAlgo, and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • The TDOA_SWITCH option of the LbsSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD(TDD), the TddSrsCfgMode parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to EXPERIENCE_ENHANCED or ACCESS_ENHANCED, and the MuteUpPTSSymNum parameter in the CellRicAlgo MO is set to 0.
    • The InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the following conditions are true: The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD; the TddSrsCfgMode parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to ACCESS_ENHANCED; and the MuteUpPTSSymNum parameter in the CellRicAlgo MO is set to 0.
  • Relation with the NCellSrsMeasPara MO:
    • The NCellSrsTimeMeasSwitch option of the NCellMeasSwitch parameter in the NCellSrsMeasPara MO must be deselected if the DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25
    • The NCellDmrsMeasSwitch option of the NCellMeasSwitch parameter in an NCellSrsMeasPara MO can be selected only when the HighSpeedFlag parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to LOW_SPEED.
  • Relation with the PDSCHCfg and EuSectorEqmGroup MOs:
    • The EuSectorEqmGroup MO cannot be configured when the following conditions are true: The SINGLE_CA_PWR_SHARE_SW_FOR_4T option of the TxChnPowerCfgSw parameter in a PDSCHCfg MO is selected, and the MultiRruCellFlag, MultiRruCellMode, and CellActiveState parameters in a Cell MO are set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, SFN, and CELL_ACTIVE, respectively.
  • Relation with the HighSpdAdaptionPara MO:
    • When the DlBandWidth parameters in a Cell MO are set to CELL_BW_N25, respectively, the HighSpeedOverlapBandInd parameter in the HighSpdAdaptionPara MO must be set to OVERLAP_BAND_HIGH_5M or OVERLAP_BAND_LOW_5M.
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and any option of the InterfAvoidMode parameter in a HighSpdAdaptionPara MO is selected, the DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO must not be set to CELL_BW_N6 or CELL_BW_N15.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameters in a Cell MO are set to CELL_BW_N50, respectively, the HighSpeedOverlapBandInd parameter in the HighSpdAdaptionPara MO must be set to OVERLAP_BAND_HIGH_5M, OVERLAP_BAND_LOW_5M, OVERLAP_BAND_HIGH_10M, or OVERLAP_BAND_LOW_10M.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameters in a Cell MO are set to CELL_BW_N75, respectively, the HighSpeedOverlapBandInd parameter in the HighSpdAdaptionPara MO must be set to OVERLAP_BAND_HIGH_5M, OVERLAP_BAND_LOW_5M, OVERLAP_BAND_HIGH_10M, OVERLAP_BAND_LOW_10M, OVERLAP_BAND_HIGH_15M, or OVERLAP_BAND_LOW_15M.
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the RbBasedAbsoluteAvoidInd option of the InterfAvoidMode parameter in the HighSpdAdaptionPara MO can be selected only if the RbBasedInterfAvoid or UlRbBasedInterfAvoid option of the InterfAvoidMode parameter in the HighSpdAdaptionPara MO is selected.
  • Relation with the CellChPwrCfg MO:
    • When the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 32T32R and the FddTddInd parameter in the same MO is set to CELL_FDD, or when the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R and the FddTddInd parameter in the same MO is set to CELL_TDD, the PdcchPowerIncreaseOffset parameter in the CellChPwrCfg MO can be set to a non-zero value.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch and CellBf MOs:
    • The MovingUeMuBfScheme parameter in the CellBf MO can be set to HIGH_SPEED_OPT_SCHEME, BIG_EVENT_PROTECT_SCHEME, HEAVY_LOAD_SCHEME, or LIGHT_LOAD_SCHEME only when the FddTddInd and TxRxMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and 64T64R or 32T32R respectively, and the SplitSdmaSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected.
    • The AdaptMubfStartThld parameter in the CellBf MO can be set to a value ranging from 0 to 100 only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE, the InterCellDmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, the BfSwitch option of the BfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch, SectorSplitCell, and CellCsiRsParaCfg MOs:
    • The TM9HybridPrecodingSwitch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TM9Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, no SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, and the CsiRsSwitch parameter in the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO is set to FIXED_CFG or ADAPTIVE_CFG.
  • Relation with the CellDynPowerSharing, CspcAlgoPara, CellCspcPara, CellAlgoSwitch, and PHICHCfg MOs:
    • The LTE_DYN_POWER_SHARING_SW and LTE_EXTREME_POWER_SHARING_SW options of the DynamicPowerSharingSwitch parameter in the CellDynPowerSharing MO can be selected only when the following conditions are met: The GLPwrShare parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the CspcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CspcAlgoPara MO, and the CellCspcSwitch and CelleCspcSwitch parameters in the CellCspcPara MO are set to OFF, the PhichInnerLoopPcSwitch option of the DlPcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, the HighSpeedFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to LOW_SPEED, and the PhichDuration parameter in the PHICHCfg MO is not set to EXTENDED.
  • Relation with the CellDynPowerSharing MO:
    • The LTE_DYN_POWER_SHARING_SW option of the DynamicPowerSharingSwitch parameter in the CellDynPowerSharing MO can be selected when the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell MO is not set to MPRU_AGGREGATION, TWO_RRU_COMBINATION, or DIGITAL_COMBINATION.
    • The UMTS_LTE_DYN_POWER_SHARING_SW and LTE_EXTREME_POWER_SHARING_SW options of the DynamicPowerSharingSwitch parameter in the CellDynPowerSharing MO under a Cell MO can be selected only if the FddTddInd parameter in this Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD.
    • If the LTE_EXTREME_POWER_SHARING_SW option of the DynamicPowerSharingSwitch parameter in the CellDynPowerSharing MO under a Cell MO is selected, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in this Cell MO cannot be set to BOOLEAN_TRUE.
    • The LTE_NR_DYN_POWER_SHARING_SW option of the DynamicPowerSharingSwitch parameter in the CellDynPowerSharing MO can be selected only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the LTE_NR_DYN_POWER_SHARING_SW option of the DynamicPowerSharingSwitch parameter in the CellDynPowerSharing MO is selected, then the LTE_DYN_POWER_SHARING_SW option of the DynamicPowerSharingSwitch parameter in the CellDynPowerSharing MO must be selected.
    • The LTE_NR_DYN_POWER_SHARING_SW option of the DynamicPowerSharingSwitch parameter in the CellDynPowerSharing MO can be selected only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R.
  • Relation with the CellDynPowerSharing, CellUSParaCfg, and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the LTE_DYN_POWER_SHARING_SW option of the DynamicPowerSharingSwitch parameter in the CellDynPowerSharing MO is selected, the MultiCellShareMode and SubframeAssignment parameters in the Cell MO must be set to NORMAL and SA2 respectively, and both the USPwrSharePaSwitch option of the UsAlgoExSwitch parameter in the CellUSParaCfg MO and InterCellDmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the CellDynPowerSharing and CellEmtcAlgo MOs:
    • The LTE_DYN_POWER_SHARING_SW and LTE_EXTREME_POWER_SHARING_SW options of the DynamicPowerSharingSwitch parameter in the CellDynPowerSharing MO can be selected only when the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is not set to TRUE, the EMTC_ONLY_CELL_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO is deselected, and the DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is not set to CELL_BW_N6(1.4M) or CELL_BW_N15(3M).
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch and CellCsiRsParaCfg MOs:
    • If the SfnJtSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected and the FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, and MultiRruCellMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and MPRU_AGGREGATION respectively, the SfnUlSchSwitch and SfnDlSchSwitch parameters in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to JOINT, and the CsiRsPortNum parameter in the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO must be set to CSI_RS_PORT_4.
  • Relation with the HighSpdAdaptionPara and CellUlschAlgo MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd and HighSpeedFlag parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and LOW_SPEED respectively, the UlRbBasedInterfAvoid option of the InterfAvoidMode parameter in the HighSpdAdaptionPara MO is selected, and the RbBasedAbsoluteAvoidInd option is deselected, the UlDelaySchStrategy parameter in the CELLULSCHALGO MO must be set to VOIP_AND_DATA_DELAYSCH.
  • Relation with the EuPrbSectorEqm and Prb MOs:
    • In a Cell MO, when NbCellFlag is TRUE, CellActiveState is CELL_ACTIVE, and the number of Prb MOs under the Cell is more than one, EuPrbSectorEqm MOs under the Cell with the same SectorEqmId must use the same AutoConfigFlag.
    • In a Cell MO, when NbCellFlag is TRUE, CellActiveState is CELL_ACTIVE, and the number of Prb MOs under the Cell is more than one, the number of EuPrbSectorEqm MOs under the Cell with the same SectorEqmId must be equal to the number of Prb MOs.
  • Relation with the CellRbReserve and Prb MOs:
    • Assume that the RbRsvMode and RbRsvType parameters in a CellRbReserve MO are set to NB_DEPLOYMENT and DOWNLINK_MODE respectively, the AnchorCarrierFlag and DeployMode parameters in a Prb MO are set to TRUE and IN_BAND respectively, and the LteCellId parameter in this Prb MO is set to the same value as CellId of the cell corresponding to the CellRbReserve MO. If the LteDlRbIndex parameter in this Prb MO is set to an invalid value, the frequency value calculated based on the values of the DlEarfcn and DlFreqOffset parameters in this Prb MO must be the same as the RB center frequency value calculated based on the value of the RbRsvStartIndex parameter in the CellRbReserve MO. In this situation, if the CellActiveState parameter value is CELL_ACTIVE for the cell corresponding to the CellRbReserve MO, the RbRsvStartIndex parameter must be set as follows:
    • If the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N15(3M) for the cell, the RbRsvStartIndex parameter can only be set to 2 or 12.
    • If the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N25(5M) for the cell, the RbRsvStartIndex parameter can only be set to 2, 7, 17, or 22.
    • If the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N50(10M) for the cell, the RbRsvStartIndex parameter can only be set to 4, 9, 14, 19, 30, 35, 40, or 45.
    • If the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N75(15M) for the cell, the value of the RbRsvStartIndex parameter must be 2, 7, 12, 17, 22, 27, 32, 42, 47, 52, 57, 62, 67, or 72.
    • If the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N100(20M) for the cell, the RbRsvStartIndex parameter can only be set to 4, 9, 14, 19, 24, 29, 34, 39, 44, 55, 60, 65, 70, 75, 80, 85, 90, or 95.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and CellEmtcAlgo MOs:
    • When the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO is selected, and the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in this SpectrumCloud MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, the CoCovGsmSharingCarrierPwr parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO must be set to 65535.
