    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
    • Under the same cell, when the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25(5M) and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR or UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO must not be selected.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch and CellBfMimoParaCfg MOs:
    • The BfMimoAdaptiveSwitch parameter in the CellBfMimoParacfg MO must be set to MIMO_BF_ADAPTIVE when the SfnJtSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, and MultiRruCellMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and MPRU_AGGREGATION respectively.
    • The TM9_DL_4LAYER_BOOST_SW option of the Tm9Dl4LayerMimoEnhSw parameter in the CellBfMimoParaCfg MO can be selected only when the following conditions are true: In the Cell MO, FddTddInd is set to CELL_TDD, and MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE but MultiRruCellMode is not set to MPRU_AGGREGATION; in the CellBfMimoParaCfg MO, BfMimoAdaptiveSwitch is set to MIMO_BF_ADAPTIVE; in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the TM9Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter is selected.
  • Relation with the EuPrbSectorEqm and EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MOs:
    • In a Cell MO, when NbCellFlag is TRUE and CellActiveState is CELL_ACTIVE, if MultiRruCellFlag is BOOLEAN_FALSE, EuPrbSectorEqm of the Cell must be configured, if MultiRruCellFlag is BOOLEAN_TRUE and MultiRruCellMode is SFN, EuPrbSectorEqm or EuPrbSectorEqmGroup of the Cell must be configured, if MultiRruCellFlag is BOOLEAN_TRUE and MultiRruCellMode is MPRU_AGGREGATION, EuPrbSectorEqmGroup of the Cell must be configured.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, SectorSplitCell, and SectorSplitGroup MOs:
    • If the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, and the CrsPortNum parameter in the Cell MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to CRS_PORT_4, the number of SectorSplitCell MO instances under the SectorSplitGroup MO must not exceed 8.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell MO:
    • When a Cell MO is associated with a SectorSplitCell MO, if the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_FDD, the TxRxMode parameter must be set to 32T32R or 8T8R; if the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter must be set to 8T8R or 64T64R or 32T32R. In addition, this Cell MO must belong to only one SectorSplitGroup MO.
    • In the Cell MO, if the FddTddInd is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode is set to 8T8R and the corresponding SectorSplitCell is configured, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO is set to NOT_CFG, the HighSpeedFlag must be set to LOW_SPEED, the MultiRruCellFlag must be set to BOOLEAN_FALSE and the CPRICompression must be set to NO_COMPRESSION or NORMAL_COMPRESSION.
    • If the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the FddTddInd parameter in this Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, and the MultiCellShareMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to SPLIT, the cell must be configured with a unique SectorSplitCell MO.
    • If the FddTddInd and MultiCellShareMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and SPLIT, respectively, the CellBeamIndex parameter in the SectorSplitCell MO must be set to 0 or 1.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, then the MultiCellShareMode parameter in the Cell MO cannot be set to SPLIT.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO is set to NOT_CFG, then in Cell MO: the CrsPortNum parameter must be set to CRS_PORT_2, the DlCyclicPrefix and UlCyclicPrefix parameters must be set to NORMAL_CP, the SubframeAssignment parameter must be set to SA1 or SA2, the SpecialSubframePatterns parameter must be set to SSP5, SSP6, or SSP7, the DlBandWidth parameter must be set to CELL_BW_N50, CELL_BW_N75, or CELL_BW_N100, and the UlBandWidth parameter must be set to CELL_BW_N50, CELL_BW_N75, or CELL_BW_N100.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, then the MultiCellShareMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to SPLIT.
    • When the EuCellStandbyMode parameter in the Cell MO for a cell is set to STANDBY, the cell cannot be configured in any SectorSplitCell MO.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch, Prb, and EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MOs:
    • In the Cell MO, if the NbCellFlag, MultiRruCellFlag, and MultiRruCellMode parameters are set to TRUE, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and MPRU_AGGREGATION, respectively, the TxRxMode parameter can be se to 4T4R only when the following conditions are met: The number of EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MO instances under this Cell MO does not exceed 1; the DeployMode parameter in the Prb MO under this Cell MO is set to IN_BAND; the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO associated through LteCellId is selected.
  • Relation with the CellRbReserve and CellEmtcAlgo MOs:
    • The EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO must be deselected when the following conditions are true: The RbRsvMode parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to NB_DEPLOYMENT; the DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO associated by LocalCellId is set to CELL_BW_N15(3M); the CellActiveState parameter in this Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE.
    • Under the same cell, when FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD(FDD) or CELL_TDD(TDD) and RbRsvMode in the CellRbReserve MO is set to RB_MASKING(RB_MASKING), EMTC_SWITCH of EmtcAlgoSwitch in the CellEmtcAlgo MO under the Cell MO must be deselected.
    • When FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD(FDD) and RbRsvMode in the CellRbReserve MO is set to RB_MASKING_WITH_SRS, EMTC_SWITCH of EmtcAlgoSwitch in the CellEmtcAlgo MO under the Cell MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the CellBfMimoParaCfg, CellAlgoSwitch, CellBf, and ENodeBAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • The HEAVYLOADTRANSMODEADAPTBOOSTSW option of the BfMimoAlgoOptSwitch parameter in the CellBfMimoParaCfg MO can be selected only when the following conditions are met:
    • The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD.
    • The BfMimoAdaptiveSwitch parameter in the CellBfMimoParaCfg MO is set to MIMO_BF_ADAPTIVE.
    • The BfSwitch option of the BfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • The SsrdSchOptSwitch option of the SSRDAlgoSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is selected or the MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • The WaitPairingLayerThd parameter in the CellBf MO is set to 0.
    • The MultiLayerThdSwitchToTM7 parameter in the CellBf MO is set to 0.
    • The TurboBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch, CellMMAlgo, and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • The MU_PAIRING_MCS_EXACT_SWITCH option of the MMAlgoOptSwitch parameter in the CellMMAlgo MO can be selected only when the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is not configured and the MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
  • Relation with the EuPrbSectorEqm MO:
    • If the NbCellFlag, MultiRruCellFlag and MultiRruCellMode parameters in a Cell MO are set to TRUE, BOOLEAN_TRUE and MPRU_AGGREGATION, respectively, the number of EuPrbSectorEqm MO must be equal to 0.
  • Relation with the Tm9DedicatedCarrier MO:
    • The CrsPortMap parameter in the Cell MO must be set to NOT_CFG when the following conditions are true: In the Cell MO, the FddTddInd, TxRxMode, and CrsPortNum parameters are set to CELL_FDD, 4T4R, and CRS_PORT_1, respectively; there is a Tm9DedicatedCarrier MO instance with Tm9CcDlEarfcn the same as DlEarfcn in the Cell MO.
    • The CrsPortMap parameter in the Cell MO must be set to NOT_CFG when the following conditions are true: In the Cell MO, the FddTddInd, TxRxMode, and CrsPortNum parameters are set to CELL_FDD, 4T8R, and CRS_PORT_1, respectively; there is a Tm9DedicatedCarrier MO instance with Tm9CcDlEarfcn the same as DlEarfcn in the Cell MO.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, SectorSplitCell, SectorSplitGroup, and CellCspcPara MOs:
    • The IntraEnbCspcSw parameter in the CellCspcPara MO under a Cell MO must be set to OFF when the following conditions are met: The CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under the Cell MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, and the number of SectorSplitCell MO instances under the SectorSplitGroup MO associated with the Cell MO is not greater than 2.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, SectorSplitCell, and SRSCfg MOs:
    • The FddSrsCfgMode parameter in the SRSCfg MO under a Cell MO must be set to DEFAULTMODE when the following conditions are met: The Cell MO is associated with an SectorSplitGroup MO, the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, and the number of SectorSplitCell MO instances in the SectorSplitGroup MO is less than or equal to two.
  • Relation with the CellPcAlgo MO:
    • The SrsPcStrategy parameter in the CellPcAlgo MO must not be set to SRSPC_SINR_ADAPTIVE if the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE.
  • Relation with the CellPcAlgo, NCellSrsMeasPara, and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • The SrsPcStrategy parameter in the CellPcAlgo MO can be set to SRSPC_SINR_ADAPTIVE only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, no SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, and the NCellSrsTimeMeasSwitch option of the NCellMeasSwitch parameter in the NCellSrsMeasPara MO is selected.
  • Relation with the CellShortTtiAlgo MO:
    • When the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_FDD, the NbCellFlag parameter is set to FALSE, the DlCyclicPrefix and UlCyclicPrefix parameters in the Cell MO are set to NORMAL_CP, the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO can be selected.
    • When the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_FDD, the NbCellFlag parameter is set to FALSE, the DlCyclicPrefix and UlCyclicPrefix parameters in the Cell MO are set to NORMAL_CP, the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO can be selected.
    • When the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE, the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO can be selected.
    • When the HighSpeedFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to LOW_SPEED, the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO can be selected.
    • When the CellRadius parameter in the Cell MO is set to a value less than or equal to 10000, the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO can be selected.
    • When the DlBandWidth and UlBandWidth parameters in the Cell MO are set to values greater than CELL_BW_N25, the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO can be selected.
    • When the DlBandWidth and UlBandWidth parameters in the Cell MO are set to values greater than CELL_BW_N25, the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO can be selected.
    • When the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO is selected and the UlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50, the value of the UlSrbgCntReservedForStti parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO must be smaller than 12.
    • When the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO is selected and the UlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75, the value of the UlSrbgCntReservedForStti parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO must be smaller than 18.
    • When the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO is selected and the UlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100, the value of the UlSrbgCntReservedForStti parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO must be smaller than 25.
    • When the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO is selected and the DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50, the value of the DlSrbgCntReservedForStti parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO must be smaller than 9.
    • When the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO is selected and the DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75, the value of the DlSrbgCntReservedForStti parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO must be smaller than 7.
    • When the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO is selected and the DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100, the value of the DlSrbgCntReservedForStti parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO must be smaller than 9.
    • The SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellShortTtiAlgo MO can be selected only if the TxRxMode parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to 1T1R, 1T2R, 2T2R, 2T4R or 4T4R.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud, CellLteFlexBw, CellAlgoSwitch, and CellShortTtiAlgo MOs:
    • When the CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to NOT_CFG, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to OFF, the LTE_FB_CFG_SWITCH option of the LteFlexBwSwitch parameter in the CellLteFlexBw MO is deselected, and the GLDynSpectrumShareSwitch and GLZeroBufferZoneSwitch options of the DynSpectrumShareSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are deselected, the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO can be selected.
  • Relation with the ULZeroBufferZone and ULSpectrumShrConfig MOs:
    • Under the same cell, if the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in a ULZeroBufferZone MO is selected, the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO is set to UMTS_CORE_SPECTRUM_1, UMTS_CORE_SPECTRUM_2, UMTS_CORE_SPECTRUM_3, or UMTS_CORE_SPECTRUM_4, and the LtePrbStartIndex parameter value is 0 or the LtePrbEndIndex parameter value is equal to the number of RBs corresponding to the UlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO minus 1, the difference between the LtePrbEndIndex and LtePrbStartIndex parameter values must be greater than or equal to 13.
    • Under the same cell, if the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in a ULZeroBufferZone MO is selected, the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO is set to UMTS_CORE_SPECTRUM_1, UMTS_CORE_SPECTRUM_2, UMTS_CORE_SPECTRUM_3, UMTS_CORE_SPECTRUM_4, or UMTS_LTE_SHARED_SPECTRUM, the LtePrbStartIndex and LtePrbEndIndex parameter values must be smaller than the number of RBs corresponding to the UlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO.
  • Relation with the GlobalProcSwitch MO:
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, PreambleFmt is smaller than 4 and HighSpeedFlag is set to ULTRA_HIGH_SPEED and HighSpeedR14Switch is selected, the following requirements must be met during the setting of CellRadius and HsCellRootSequenceIndex:
    • 1 m <= CellRadius <= 1199 m: the value range of HsCellRootSequenceIndex is 24~351,356~381,386~405,408~419,422~435,438~461,466~493,498~503,506~509,512~513,516~519,522~529,532~545,548~819;
    • 1199 m < CellRadius <= 1649 m: the value range of HsCellRootSequenceIndex is 30~329,332~351,356~381,386~405,408~419,422~435,438~459,466~473,476~493,498~503,506~509,512~513,516~519,522~529,532~545,548~549,552~815;
    • 1649 m < CellRadius <= 2098 m: the value range of HsCellRootSequenceIndex is 36~329,334~349,356~381,386~393,396~405,408~419,422~435,438~445,448~459,466~473,476~493,498~503,506~509,512~513,516~519,522~529,532~545,548~549,552~809;
    • 2098 m < CellRadius <= 2698 m: the value range of HsCellRootSequenceIndex is 42~329,334~349,356~381,386~391,396~405,408~419,422~435,438~445,448~449,452~459,466~473,476~493,498~503,506~509,512~513,516~519,522~525,528~529,532~537,540~545,548~549,552~803;
    • 2698 m < CellRadius <= 3598 m: the value range of HsCellRootSequenceIndex is 52~329,334~349,356~363,368~369, 372~381,386~391,396~401,404~405,408~411,414~419,422~435,438~445,448~449,452~453,456~459,466~471,476~479,482~493,498~503,506~509,512~513,516~519,522~525,528~529,532~537,540~545,548~549,552~795;
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, PreambleFmt is smaller than 4 and HighSpeedFlag is set to ULTRA_HIGH_SPEED and HighSpeedR14Switch is selected, the following requirements must be met during the setting of CellRadius and HsCellRootSequenceIndex:
    • 3598 m < CellRadius <= 4497 m: the value range of HsCellRootSequenceIndex is 64~273,276~329,334~335,338~349,356~363,368~369,372~381,386~391,396~401,408~411,414~419,424~435,438~445,456~459,466~471,476~479,482~493,498~503,506~509,512~513,516~519,522~525,528~529,532~537,540~545,548~549,552~555,558~789;

    • 5547 m < CellRadius <= 6896 m: the value range of HsCellRootSequenceIndex is 90~273,276~279,282~307,310~327,334~335,338~343,346~349,356~363,368~369,372~381,396~399,408~411,414~419,426~431,434~435,438~445,456~457,466~469,476~477,482~489,492~493,500~503,506~509,512~513,516~519,522~525,532~533,536~537,540~545,548~549,552~555,558~573,576~595,598~765;

    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, PreambleFmt is smaller than 4 and HighSpeedFlag is set to ULTRA_HIGH_SPEED and HighSpeedR14Switch is selected, the following requirements must be met during the setting of CellRadius and HsCellRootSequenceIndex:
    • 8695 m < CellRadius <= 10794 m: the value range of HsCellRootSequenceIndex is 136~273,276~279,282~307,310~313,316~327,338~343,348~349,356~357,360~361,374~375,378~379,396~397,408~409,428~431,438~445,466~467,476~477,484~485,492~493,502~503,506~509,512~513,516~519,522~525,532~533,536~537,542~545,552~553,558~559,562~565,568~573,576~583,586~595,598~601,604~611,614~617,620~621,624~729;
    • 10794 m < CellRadius <= 13343 m: the value range of HsCellRootSequenceIndex is 168~273,276~277,282~303,306~307,310~313,316~317,320~327,340~343,360~361,374~375,396~397,408~409,438~441,466~467,484~485,502~503,506~509,512~513,518~519,536~537,544~545,552~553,562~565,568~573,576~583,586~591,594~595,598~601,604~611,614~617,620~621,624~627,630~707;
    • 13343 m < CellRadius <= 15892 m: the value range of HsCellRootSequenceIndex is 168~169,174~177,180~181,186~187,192~193,196~197,202~273,276~277,282~291,296~303,306~307,310~313,316~317,320~327,466~467,562~565,568~573,576~581,586~591,594~595,600~601,604~611,614~617,620~621,624~627,630~659,662~663,676~683,686~687,700~701;
    • 15892 m < CellRadius <= 18590 m: the value range of HsCellRootSequenceIndex is 204~205,208~221,226~229,232~239,242~245,250~255,258~259,262~265,268~269,272~273,276~277,282~287,290~291,298~301,306~307,310~313,316~317,320~321,324~327,564~565,568~569,572~573,578~581,586~591,604~611,614~617,620~621,626~627,630~639,642~643,646~649,652~653,656~659;
  • Relation with the GlobalProcSwitch and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • In a Cell MO, if HighSpeedFlag is set to ULTRA_HIGH_SPEED and HighSpeedR14Switch is selected, MaksIdxSwitch must be deselected.
    • In a Cell MO, if HighSpeedFlag is set to ULTRA_HIGH_SPEED and HighSpeedR14Switch is selected, RachAdjSwitch must be deselected.
  • Relation with the GlobalProcSwitch and ENodeBAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • In a Cell MO, if HighSpeedFlag is set to ULTRA_HIGH_SPEED and HighSpeedR14Switch is selected, PrachTimeStagSwitch must be deselected.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • When the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, and SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR, or LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the SrsSubframeRecfSwitch option of the SrsAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch must be selected.
    • When the PucchFlexCfgSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO cannot be set to LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR, or LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, then the EicicSwitch, SfnAlgoSwitch, FeicicSwitch parameters must be set to OFF in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the LowSpeedInterFreqHoSwitch and InterFreqDirectHoSwitch parameters in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be set to OFF and the LowSpeedBlindRedirect option of the HighSpeedSchOptSwitch parameter in this CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the SpsSchSwitch option of the DlSchSwitch and UlSchSwitch parameters in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
    • When one or more options of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch are selected, and the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO cannot be set to LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR or LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR.
    • When the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR, UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR, or LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, and the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, the GLDynSpectrumShareSwitch and GLZeroBufferZoneSwitch options of the DynSpectrumShareSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR or LTE_NR_COORD_SPECTRUM_SHR, the DmimoJTSwitch and InterCellDmimoJTSwitch options of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must not be set to ON for this cell.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR or LTE_NR_COORD_SPECTRUM_SHR, none of the options of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected for this cell.
  • Relation with the RACHCfg and GlobalProcSwitch MOs:
    • If FddTddInd and HighSpeedFlag in a Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and ULTRA_HIGH_SPEED, respectively, the HighSpeedR14Switch option of ProtocolCompatibilitySw in the GlobalProcSwitch MO is selected, and PrachConfigIndexCfgInd in the RACHCfg MO under this Cell MO is set to CFG, then PrachConfigIndex in the RACHCfg MO must be set as follows depending on the setting of PreambleFmt in the Cell MO: If PreambleFmt is set to 0, PrachConfigIndex must be set to 3; if PreambleFmt is set to 1, PrachConfigIndex must be set to 23; if PreambleFmt is set to 2, PrachConfigIndex must be set to 30; if PreambleFmt is set to 3, PrachConfigIndex must be set to 40.
    • If FddTddInd and HighSpeedFlag in a Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and ULTRA_HIGH_SPEED, respectively, the HighSpeedR14Switch option of ProtocolCompatibilitySw in the GlobalProcSwitch MO is selected, and PrachConfigIndexCfgInd in the RACHCfg MO under this Cell MO is set to CFG, then PrachConfigIndex in the RACHCfg MO must be set as follows depending on the setting of PreambleFmt in the Cell MO: If PreambleFmt is set to 0, PrachConfigIndex must be set to 3; if PreambleFmt is set to 1, PrachConfigIndex must be set to 19; if PreambleFmt is set to 2, PrachConfigIndex must be set to 32; if PreambleFmt is set to 3, PrachConfigIndex must be set to 48.
  • Relation with the CellAuxDuBind and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • In a Cell MO, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, MultiRruCellMode is set to SFN and CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, and the CellAuxDuBind MO is configured for the cell, the FDDHomNetDlCompSwitch and FDDHetNetDlCompSwitch option of the DlCompSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the CellBf and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • The BfUePowerLimitSwitch parameter in the CellBf MO can be set to ON only if the BFModeUserPwrSwitch option of the DlPcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the cell is activated, and the PdschSplitBeamConfigIndex parameter in the CellBf MO is set to CONFIG_INDEX4, then the SplitSdmaSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected.
    • The SRS_LMT_AND_ABN_UE_IDENT_SW option of the UeIdentificationSwitch parameter in the CellBf MO can be selected only if both of the following conditions are met: 1. The DmimoJTSwitch, DmimoSchAttriOptSwitch, and InterCellDmimoJTSwitch options of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are deselected. 2. The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD. The TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 32T32R or 64T64R. The MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE.
  • Relation with the CaMgtCfg and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • If the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, no SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, and the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the CaPreallocationSccUeCount parameter in the CaMgtCfg MO can be set to a non-zero value.
  • Relation with the UlInterfSuppressCfg MO:
    • The ChnCalOptSwitch parameter in the UlInterfSuppressCfg MO can be set to ON only if the FddTddInd and TxRxMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and 64T64R or 32T32R respectively.
    • The UlIntrfDetectionTrigThld parameter in the UlInterfSuppressCfg MO can be set to a value other than 127 when all of the following conditions are met: The TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 1T2R, 2T2R, 2T4R, or 4T4R, the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to NORMAL, the HighSpeedFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to LOW_SPEED, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE, the InBandRelayDeNbSwitch, InBandRelayReNbSwitch, and OutOfBandRelaySwitch options of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are deselected, the UlHoppingType parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO is set to HOPPING_OFF, and the CellRadius parameter in the Cell MO is set to a value smaller than or equal to 15000 m.
    • The UL_JOINT_INTRF_SUPPRESS_SW option of the UlJointIntrfManagementSw parameter in a UlInterfSuppressCfg MO can be selected only when the UlIntrfDetectionTrigThld parameter in the UlInterfSuppressCfg MO is not set to 127 and the UlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N6, CELL_BW_N15, CELL_BW_N25, or CELL_BW_N50.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell, CellAlgoSwitch, and UlInterfSuppressCfg MOs:
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and no SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, the REMOTE_INTRF_BF_ENH_SW option of the RemoteIntrfDlEnhSwitch parameter in the UlInterfSuppressCfg MO can be selected only if the UlInterfSuppressionSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
  • Relation with the CellSiMap, eUCellSectorEqm, and CellEmtcAlgo MOs:
    • The ForbidCellSiSwitch option of the SiSwitch parameter in the CellSiMap MO can be selected only when the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, or when all the following conditions are met: the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 1T1R, 1T2R, 2T2R, 2T4R, or 4T4R, or 8T8R, or 32T32R, or 64T64R, the WorkMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to UL_DL, the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to FALSE, and the EMTC_ONLY_CELL_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO under the Cell MO is deselected.
  • Relation with the CellSiMap, SectorSplitCell, and SectorSplitGroup MOs:
    • If the value of CellActiveState in the Cell MO for a cell is CELL_ACTIVE and this cell is added to a sector split group specified by a SectorSplitGroup MO, the setting of the ForbidCellSiSwitch option of SiSwitch in the CellSiMap MOs for all cells corresponding to the SectorSplitCell MOs under the SectorSplitGroup MO must be the same.
  • Relation with the ULZeroBufferZone and SpectrumCloud MOs:
    • If the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in an ULZeroBufferZone MO is selected, and the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO must not be set to LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR or LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR.
  • Relation with the CellCspcPara and SpectrumCloud MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR or LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the CellCspcSwitch parameter in a CellCspcPara MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch and SpectrumCloud MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected.
    • If the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the GLPwrShare parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the PagingInterfRandSwitch option of the DlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected.
  • Relation with the ContigIntraBandCarr and SpectrumCloud MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of ContigIntraBandCarrSw in the ContigIntraBandCarr MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the CellDynPowerSharing and SpectrumCloud MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the LTE_DYN_POWER_SHARING_SW and UMTS_LTE_DYN_POWER_SHARING_SW option of the DynamicPowerSharingSwitch parameter in the CellDynPowerSharing MO cannot be selected.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoExtSwitch and SpectrumCloud MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the UlCoverageEnhancementSw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the NbCellMultiPrbConfig, Prb, SpectrumCloud, and CellRbReserve MOs:
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the NbRfCarrierStrategy parameter in the NbCellMultiPrbConfig under the same cell is set to SHARE, and under the LTE cell associated by the LteCellId of the Prb, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, the RbRsvType parameter in a CellRbReserve MO is set to UPLINK_MODE, and the RbRsvMode parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to NB_RESERVED or NB_DEPLOYMENT, then if shared frequencies of ARFCNs specified by GsmArfcn and LTE RBs do not overlap, the RbRsvStartIndex and RbRsvEndIndex parameters in the CellRbReserve MO must be set in such a way that the reserved RBs are 280 kHz away from the center frequency of shared ARFCNs.
  • Relation with the NbCellMultiPrbConfig, Prb, CellAlgoSwitch, CellRbReserve, and CellDss MOs:
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the NbRfCarrierStrategy parameter in the NbCellMultiPrbConfig under the same cell is set to SHARE, and under the LTE cell associated by the LteCellId of the Prb, the DynSpectrumShareSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to GLDynSpectrumShareSwitch switch on, the RbRsvType parameter in a CellRbReserve MO is set to UPLINK_MODE, and the RbRsvMode parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to NB_RESERVED or NB_DEPLOYMENT, then the RbRsvStartIndex and RbRsvEndIndex parameters in the CellRbReserve MO must be set in such a way that the reserved RBs are 2 RBs away from the shared RBs.
  • Relation with the LteNrSpctShrCellGrp and SpectrumCloud MOs:
    • If the UlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50, the sum of the values of the LteGuaranteePuschRbNum and NrGuaranteePuschRbNum parameters in the LteNrSpctShrCellGrp MO must be less than or equal to 50. If the UlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75, the sum of the values of the LteGuaranteePuschRbNum and NrGuaranteePuschRbNum parameters in the LteNrSpctShrCellGrp MO must be less than or equal to 75. If the UlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100, the sum of the values of the LteGuaranteePuschRbNum and NrGuaranteePuschRbNum parameters in the LteNrSpctShrCellGrp MO must be less than or equal to 100.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitGroup, eUCellSectorEqm, and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • The TM4WeightScheme parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO that includes a cell specified by a Cell MO can be set to COVERAGE_MODE_1 when all of the following conditions are met: The value of the CellActiveState parameter in this Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the number of SectorSplitCell MO instances under the SectorSplitGroup MO is four, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO is set to MODE_2.
  • Relation with the CellCsiRsParaCfg, CellAlgoSwitch, eUCellSectorEqm, SectorSplitGroup, and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • If the CsiRsAdaptBeamCount parameter in the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO is not set to ADAPTIVE_BEAM_DISABLED and cell is active, the following requirements must be met:
    • The JOINT_SCHEDULING_SWITCH option of the IntelligentBeamSchSwitch parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO corresponding to the Cell MO must be selected, the 8T4PortTM9EnhanceSwitch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected, or the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under the Cell MO must be set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2 and the number of SectorSplitCell MO instances under the SectorSplitGroup MO corresponding to the Cell MO is less than or equal to 2 and the TM9Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
  • Relation with the ENodeBAlgoSwitch, eUCellSectorEqm, SectorSplitCell, SRSCfg, CellSrsAdaptiveCfg, and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • When the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, and FddTddInd in this Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, and the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under related Cell MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, and related SectorSplitGroup MO includes one or two SectorSplitCell MO instances, and the MM_SRS_ALLOC_OPT_SWITCH option of the UlResManageOptSw parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is selected, all of the following conditions must be met: (a) The SrsCfgInd parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE. (b) The FddSrsCfgMode parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to DEFAULTMODE. (c) The SrsPeriodAdaptive parameter in the CellSrsAdaptiveCfg MO is set to ON. (d) The SrsSubframeRecfSwitch option of the SrsAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
  • Relation with the CellEmtcAlgo and CellAlgoExtSwitch MOs:
    • If the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO under a Cell MO is selected and the CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd parameter in this Cell MO is set to CFG, the LteInBandInterFreqDeploySw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO under this Cell MO cannot be set to ON.
    • If the FddTddInd and DlBandWidth parameters in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and CELL_BW_N25, respectively, and the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO under this Cell MO is selected, the LteInBandInterFreqDeploySw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO under this Cell MO cannot be set to ON.
    • The LteInBandInterFreqDeploySw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO under a Cell MO cannot be set to ON when the following conditions are met: The FddTddInd parameter in this Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO is selected, and the MPDCCH_PDSCH_FREQ_HOPPING_SW or PUSCH_FREQ_HOPPING_SW option of the EmtcFreqHoppingSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO is selected.
  • Relation with the CellShortTtiAlgo and CellAlgoExtSwitch MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the LteInBandInterFreqDeploySw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO under this Cell MO is set to ON, the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO under this Cell MO cannot be selected.
  • Relation with the DdCellGroup and CellAlgoExtSwitch MOs:
    • The LteInBandInterFreqDeploySw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MOs under the Cell MOs identified by the LocalCellId1 and LocalCellId2 parameters in the DdCellGroup MO cannot be set to ON.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and CellMBMSCfg MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the MBMSSwitch parameter in the associated CellMBMSCfg MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and CellUlschAlgo MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the IntvlOfUlSpsWithSkipping parameter in the associated CellUlschAlgo MO must be set to MS0.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the AdaptHarqSwitch parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO under this Cell MO must be set to ADAPTIVE_HARQ_SW_ON or ADAPTIVE_HARQ_SW_SEMI_ON.
    • When the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR, UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR, or LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, and the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, the UlHoppingType parameter in the associated CellUlschAlgo MO must be set to HOPPING_OFF.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and CellRfShutdown MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the RfShutdownSwitch parameter in the associated CellRfShutdown MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the RfShutdownSwitch parameter in the CellRfShutdown MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud, CellAlgoSwitch, and ContigIntraBandCarr MOs:
    • The PCFICH_POWER_OPT_SWITCH and PDCCH_POWER_OPT_SWITCH options of the NonStandardBwAlgoSw parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only if the CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CFG or the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to ON or CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw in the ContigIntraBandCarr is selected.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and CellShortTtiAlgo MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and NCellSrsMeasPara MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the SrsAutoNCellMeasSwitch parameter in the NCellSrsMeasPara MO under this Cell MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and CellRbReserve MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the RbRsvMode parameter in the CellRbReserve MO under this Cell MO cannot be set to NB_RESERVED, NB_DEPLOYMENT, RB_MASKING, RB_MASKING_WITH_SRS or MCPTT_RB_RESERVED.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and UlCsAlgoPara MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the UL_COORD_RES_ALLOC_SWITCH and UL_COORD_PC_SWITCH options of the UlCsSw parameter in the UlCsAlgoPara MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and RACHCfg MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the PrachFreqOffsetStrategy parameter in the RACHCfg MO under this Cell MO must be set to FULLY_AUTOMATIC.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and SRSCfg MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the FddSrsCfgMode parameter in the SRSCfg MO under this Cell MO must be set to DEFAULTMODE, the SrsCfgInd parameter must be set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, and the SrsSubframeCfg parameter must be set to SC3, SC7, SC14, or SC0.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and CellLowPower MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the LowPwrSwitch parameter in the CellLowPower MO under this Cell MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR or LTE_NR_COORD_SPECTRUM_SHR, the LowPwrSwitch parameter in the CellLowPower MO cannot be set to ON for this cell.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and CellPdcchAlgo MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the EpdcchFunctionSwitch option of the EpdcchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellPdcchAlgo MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the CellMimoParaCfg and CellDlpcPdschPa MOs:
    • The HighOrderMimoPairingStrat parameter in the CellMimoParaCfg MO under a Cell MO can be set to a value other than DEFAULT only if the TxRxMode and FddTddInd parameters in the Cell MO are set to 32T32R and CELL_FDD, respectively, and both the EpreOffsetAdjustmentThld and TM9RankCorrectThld parameters in the CellDlpcPdschPa MO under the Cell MO are set to 255.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch, NbCellMultiPrbConfig, and Prb MOs:
    • If AnchorCarrierFlag is set to FALSE and DeployMode is set to GUARD_BAND or IN_BAND in some Prb MOs under a Cell MO for an NB-IoT cell meeting specific conditions, the COVERAGE_LEVEL_0 option of PrachCoverageLevelType must be selected in at least one of these Prb MOs. The specific conditions are as follows: The value of CellActiveState in the Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, the MULTI_CARRIER_ENH_SWITCH option of NbCellAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under the Cell MO is selected, the NPRACH_PRB_SELECTION_SW option of MultiCarrierOptSwitch in the NbCellMultiPrbConfig MO under the Cell MO is selected, and AnchorCarrierFlag and DeployMode in a Prb MO under the Cell MO are set to TRUE and STAND_ALONE, respectively.
    • If AnchorCarrierFlag is set to FALSE and DeployMode is set to STAND_ALONE in some Prb MOs under a Cell MO for an NB-IoT cell meeting specific conditions, the COVERAGE_LEVEL_0 option of PrachCoverageLevelType MOs must be selected in at least one of these Prb MOs. The specific conditions are as follows: The value of CellActiveState in the Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, the MULTI_CARRIER_ENH_SWITCH option of NbCellAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under the Cell MO is selected, the NPRACH_PRB_SELECTION_SW option of MultiCarrierOptSwitch in the NbCellMultiPrbConfig MO under the Cell MO is selected, and AnchorCarrierFlag and DeployMode in a Prb MO under the Cell MO are set to TRUE and GUARD_BAND or IN_BAND, respectively.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch, SpectrumCloud, and ENodeBAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, and the SymbolShutdownSwitch option of the PowerSaveSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is selected, then the MBSFNShutDownSwitch option of the DlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, then the IntraBandInterfMonitorSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and a SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, the MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO is set to NOT_CFG, then in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the following options must be deselected: QUICK_CHN_CAL_SWITCH option of the EnhChnCalSwitch parameter, IntraDlCompSwitch, InterDlCompDcsSwitch, and InterDlCompCbfSwitch options of the DlCompSwitch parameter, TurboBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter, AntSelEnhanceBfSwitch option of the BfAlgoSwitch parameter, InterCellDmimoJTSwitch and DmimoJTSwitch options of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter, UlCamcSw option of the CamcSwitch parameter, and UlVmimoSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter, and HighOrderVMIMOSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 32T32R or 64T64R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, then the DynAdjVoltSwitch option of the DynAdjVoltSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, then the UlInterfSuppressionSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell and UlInterfSuppressCfg MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, then the InterferenceRbRatioThld parameter in the UlInterfSuppressCfg MO must be set to 100.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, then the RMT_INF_AVOID_SCH_SW option of the RemoteInfULEnhanceSw parameter in the UlInterfSuppressCfg MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, then the REMOTE_INTRF_BF_ENH_SW option of the RemoteIntrfDlEnhSwitch parameter in the UlInterfSuppressCfg MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, then the ChnCalOptSwitch parameter in the UlInterfSuppressCfg MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell and CellBfMimoParaCfg MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, then the BfMimoAdaptiveSwitch parameter in the CellBfMimoParaCfg MO must be set to NO_ADAPTIVE or MIMO_BF_ADAPTIVE.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, then the FixedBfMimoMode parameter in the CellBfMimoParaCfg MO cannot be set to TM7, TM8, or TM9w/oPMI.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and LteNrSpctShrCellGrp MOs:
    • If MultiRruCellFlag and MultiRruCellMode in a Cell MO are set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and MPRU_AGGREGATION, respectively, and SpectrumCloudSwitch in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR or LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, then LteNrCoCarrCoCpriDataSw in the LteNrSpctShrCellGrp MO corresponding to the SpectrumCloud MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and EuSectorEqmGroup MOs:
    • If SpectrumCloudSwitch in the SpectrumCloud MO under a Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR, and MultiRruCellFlag and MultiRruCellMode in this Cell MO are set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and MPRU_AGGREGATION, respectively, then only one EuSectorEqmGroup MO can be configured under this Cell MO.
    • If FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, and MultiRruCellMode in a Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and MPRU_AGGREGATION, respectively, and SpectrumCloudSwitch in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, then only one EuSectorEqmGroup MO can be configured under this Cell MO.
  • Relation with the ENodeBSharingMode MO:
    • If ENodeBSharingMode in the ENodeBSharingMode MO is set to HYBRID_SHARED, CnOpSharingGroupId in a Cell MO must be set to a valid value.
  • Relation with the EutranInterFreqNCell, CellOp, CnOperator, CnOperatorTa, CnOpSharingGroup, and ENodeBSharingMode MOs:
    • A CellOp MO must be configured for each EutranInterFreqNCell MO that corresponds to an intra-eNodeB inter-frequency neighboring cell. If ENodeBSharingMode in the ENodeBSharingMode MO is set to HYBRID_SHARED, Mcc and Mnc in such an EutranInterFreqNCell MO must be set to the same values as Mcc and Mnc, respectively, in the CnOperator MO referenced by PrimaryOperatorId in the CnOpSharingGroup MO for the intra-eNodeB inter-frequency neighboring cell.
  • Relation with the EutranIntraFreqNCell, CellOp, CnOperator, CnOperatorTa, CnOpSharingGroup, and ENodeBSharingMode MOs:
    • A CellOp MO must be configured for each EutranIntraFreqNCell MO that corresponds to an intra-eNodeB intra-frequency neighboring cell. If ENodeBSharingMode in the ENodeBSharingMode MO is set to HYBRID_SHARED, Mcc and Mnc in such an EutranIntraFreqNCell MO must be set to the same values as Mcc and Mnc, respectively, in the CnOperator MO referenced by PrimaryOperatorId in the CnOpSharingGroup MO for the intra-eNodeB intra-frequency neighboring cell.
  • Relation with the CellOp, CnOperatorTa, CnOpSharingGroup, and ENodeBSharingMode MOs:
    • If ENodeBSharingMode in the ENodeBSharingMode MO is set to HYBRID_SHARED, CnOperatorId in the CnOperatorTa corresponding to TrackingAreaId in the CellOp MO under the Cell MO must be configured in PrimaryOperatorId or SecondaryOperatorList in the CnOpSharingGroup associated with the Cell MO.
  • Relation with the ENodeBSharingMode, CellOp, CnOperatorTa, and CnOpSharingGroup MOs:
    • If ENodeBSharingMode in the ENodeBSharingMode MO is set to HYBRID_SHARED and CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, CnOperatorId in a CnOperatorTa MO associated with the CellOp MO under the Cell MO must be set to the same value as PrimaryOperatorId in the CnOpSharingGroup MO associated with the Cell MO.
  • Relation with the CellOp, CnOperatorTa, and CnOperator MOs:
    • When the PlmnMode parameter in a CnOperator MO corresponding to the CnOperatorId parameter in the CnOperatorTa MO associated with the TrackingAreaId parameter in the CellOp MO is set to IOPS_PLMN, the CellReservedForOp parameter must be set to CELL_RESERVED_FOR_OP.
    • The sum of the values of OpDlRbUsedRatio in the CellOp MO instances excluding those meeting the following condition cannot be greater than 100: TrackingAreaId in the CellOp MO instances is referenced by the CnOperatorTa MO instances in which CnOperatorId is referenced by the CnOperator MO instances whose PlmnMode is set to IOPS_PLMN.
    • The sum of the values of OpUlRbUsedRatio in the CellOp MO instances excluding those meeting the following condition cannot be greater than 100: TrackingAreaId in the CellOp MO instances is referenced by the CnOperatorTa MO instances in which CnOperatorId is referenced by the CnOperator MO instances whose PlmnMode is set to IOPS_PLMN.
    • The sum of the values of OpUeNumRatio in the CellOp MO instances excluding those meeting the following condition cannot be greater than 100: TrackingAreaId in the CellOp MO instances is referenced by the CnOperatorTa MO instances in which CnOperatorId is referenced by the CnOperator MO instances whose PlmnMode is set to IOPS_PLMN.
  • Relation with the SECTOREQM and eUCellSectorEqm MOs:
    • In a Cell MO, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_TDD and CellActiveState is CELL_ACTIVE and MultiRruCellMode is not set to CELL_COMBINATION or MPRU_AGGREGATION, the CrsPortNum value must be smaller than or equal to the number of TX antennas (The preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER) specified by SECTOREQMANTENNA in the associated SECTOREQM MOs corresponding to SectorEqmId.
    • When the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and the HighSpeedFlag parameter in the Cell MO is not set to LOW_SPEED, if the TxRxMode and FddTddInd parameters in the Cell MO are set to 8T8R and CELL_TDD respectively, the SECTOREQM MO can correspond to only one RRU.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm and SECTOREQM MOs:
    • In a Cell MO, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_TDD, MultiRruCellMode is not set to MPRU_AGGREGATION, and the value of CellActiveState is CELL_ACTIVE, the eUCellSectorEqm MOs associated with this Cell MO must have been configured, and SECTOREQM MOs associated with the eUCellSectorEqm MOs must have been configured. In a Cell MO, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, NbCellFlag is set to FALSE, MultiRruCellMode is not set to MPRU_AGGREGATION or SFN, and the value of CellActiveState is CELL_ACTIVE, the eUCellSectorEqm MOs associated with this Cell MO must have been configured, and SECTOREQM MOs associated the eUCellSectorEqm MOs must have been configured.
    • When the CellActiveState parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and the MultiRruCellMode parameter is not set to CELL_COMBINATION or SFN, if the TxRxMode and FddTddInd parameters are set to 4T4R and CELL_TDD, respectively, or the TxRxMode parameter is set to 8T8R, and the ANTCFGMODE parameter in the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is not set to BEAM, the total numbers of TX antennas and RX antennas in the sector equipment configured using the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each eUCellSectorEqm MO instance of the Cell MO must be the same as the numbers in the value of the TxRxMode parameter. (The TX antennas refer to those whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER.)
    • In a Cell MO, if CellActiveState is CELL_ACTIVE and TxRxMode is set to 1T2R, the total number of TX antennas and the total number of RX antennas are calculated as follows: If WorkMode is set to UL_DL, the total number of TX antennas (The preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER) associated with the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be 1 or 2, and the total number of RX antennas associated with this SECTOREQM MO must be 2. If WorkMode is set to DL_ONLY, the total number of TX antennas (The preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER) associated with the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be 1 or 2, and the total number of RX antennas associated with this SECTOREQM MO must be 2 or 0.
    • The SECTOREQM MO referenced by the eUCellSectorEqm MO under a Cell MO must be configured with two or four TX antennas (The preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER) and four RX antennas when the following conditions are met: (1) TxRxMode in this Cell MO is set to 2T4R. (2) CellActiveState in this Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE. (3) MultiRruCellFlag in this Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and MultiRruCellMode in this Cell MO is not set to SFN. Alternatively, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE.
    • If CellActiveState, FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, and MultiRruCellMode in a Cell MO are set to CELL_ACTIVE, CELL_TDD, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and SFN, respectively, sector equipment corresponding to the SECTOREQM associated with the eUCellSectorEqm under this Cell MO must be RRUs.
    • In a Cell MO, if CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE and MultiRruCellMode is set to MPRU_AGGREGATION, the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must have been configured.
    • In a Cell MO, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_TDD, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, MultiRruCellMode is set to MPRU_AGGREGATION and CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be associated with two TX antennas (The preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER) and two RX antennas.
    • In a Cell MO, if CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, MultiRruCellMode is set to CELL_COMBINATION, HighSpeedFlag is not set to ULTRA_HIGH_SPEED, HighSpeedFlag is not set to HIGH_SPEED, and TxRxMode is set to 8T8R, the SECTOREQMMO corresponding to each eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be associated with an MO defining a unique RF module.
    • In a Cell MO, if CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, and MultiRruCellMode is set to CELL_COMBINATION and HighSpeedFlag is not set to ULTRA_HIGH_SPEED or HIGH_SPEED, or MultiRruCellMode is set to SFN, the total number of TX antennas and that of RX antennas associated with the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be equal to or less then the number of TX antennas (The preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER) and that of RX antennas specified by the value of TxRxMode, respectively.
    • Assume that in the Cell MO, FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, MultiRruCellMode is set to SFN, CellActiveState is CELL_ACTIVE, and TxRxMode is set to 2T4R, the total numbers of both TX and RX antennas specified in the SECTOREQM MO associated with the eUCellSectorEqm MO in the Cell MO must be 1 and 1, 1 and 2, 2 and 2, or 2 and 4. The preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER. Assume that in the Cell MO, FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, MultiRruCellMode is set to SFN, CellActiveState is CELL_ACTIVE, and TxRxMode is set to 2T2R, the total numbers of both TX and RX antennas specified in the SECTOREQM MO associated with the eUCellSectorEqm MO in the Cell MO must be 1 and 1, 1 and 2, or 2 and 2. The preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER. Assume that in the Cell MO, FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, MultiRruCellMode is set to SFN, CellActiveState is CELL_ACTIVE, and the TxRxMode parameter is set to 1T2R, the total numbers of both TX and RX antennas specified in the SECTOREQM MO associated with the eUCellSectorEqm MO in the Cell MO must be 1 and 1, or 1 and 2. The preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER.
    • The sector represented by a SECTOREQM MO of the corresponding eUCellSectorEqm MO associated with a Cell MO can be configured with four TX antennas and four RX antennas, two TX antennas and four RX antennas, or two TX antennas and two RX antennas if FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, MultiRruCellMode, CrsPortNum, TxRxMode, and CellActiveState in this Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD, BOOLEAN_TRUE, SFN, CRS_PORT_4, 4T4R, and CELL_ACTIVE, respectively, and ANTCFGMODE is set to ANTENNAPORT.
    • If FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, MultiRruCellMode, TxRxMode, CrsPortNum, and CellActiveState in a Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD, BOOLEAN_TRUE, SFN, 4T4R, CRS_PORT_2, and CELL_ACTIVE, respectively, sector equipment specified in the SECTOREQM MO associated with the eUCellSectorEqm of the Cell MO must be configured with four TX antennas and four RX antennas, two TX antennas and four RX antennas, two TX antennas and two RX antennas, one TX antennas and two RX antennas, or one TX antenna and one RX antenna.
    • In the Cell MO, if the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, the MultiRruCellMode parameter is set to SFN, and the CellActiveState parameter is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be associated with two, four or eight TX antennas and two, four or eight RX antennas (the TX antennas refer to the antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER.)
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the ANTCFGMODE parameter is set to BEAM(BEAM) in the SectorEqm MO specified by the SectorEqmId parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO mapping this Cell MO, the TxRxMode parameter in all Cell MOs mapping the SectorEqm MO must be set to the same value.
    • When the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD(TDD), and the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE(Active), the ANTCFGMODE, BEAMSHAPE, BEAMLAYERSPLIT, and BEAMAZIMUTHOFFSET parameters in the Sectoreqm MO specified by the SectorEqmId parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO must be set to BEAM(BEAM), SEC_120DEG(SEC_120DEG), None(None), and None(None), respectively.
    • If CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, each antenna associated with the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be unique.
    • When the CellBeamMode parameter in an eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 and the DlBandWidth parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N6 or CELL_BW_N15, the ANTCFGMODE parameter in the associated SECTOREQM MO must be set to BEAM.
    • In the Cell MO, if CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, TxRxMode is set to 8T8R, and MultiCellShareMode is set to SPLIT, CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm MO associated with this cell must be set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1. In addition, if ANTCFGMODE in the corresponding SECTOREQM MO is set to BEAM in this case, BEAMLAYERSPLIT must be set to None.
    • In a Cell MO, if the CellActiveState parameter is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, the MultiRruCellMode parameter is set to CELL_COMBINATION, and more than six eUCellSectorEqm MO instances are configured, then the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each eUCellSectorEqm MO in the Cell MO must be associated with a unique RF module.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, SECTOREQM, and RRU MOs:
    • In a Cell MO, if NbCellFlag is set FALSE, CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE and FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be associated with an RRU MO supporting LTE_FDD.
    • In a Cell MO, if CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE and FddTddInd is set to CELL_TDD, the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be associated with an RRU MO supporting LTE_TDD.
    • In a Cell MO, if CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, and MultiRruCellMode is set to DIGITAL_COMBINATION, the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be associated with an RRU MO with RT set to PRRU.
    • Consider that the value of CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and MultiRruCellMode is set to DIGITAL_COMBINATION. If RT is set to PRRU in at least one RRU MO used by the SECTOREQM associated with the eUCellSectorEqm MO under the Cell MO, RT in all RRU MOs must be set to PRRU.
    • If CellActiveState and TxRxMode in a Cell MO are set to CELL_ACTIVE and 2T8R, respectively, the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be associated with two RRU MOs. Specifically, the sector equipment corresponding to the SECTOREQM MO must use two transmit channels (The preceding transmit channels corresponding to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER in the SECTOREQM MO) and four receive channels of one RRU MO and four receive channels of the other RRU MO.
    • If CellActiveState and TxRxMode in a Cell MO are set to CELL_ACTIVE and 4T8R, respectively, the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be associated with two RRU MOs. Specifically, the sector equipment corresponding to the SECTOREQM MO must use two transmit channels (The preceding transmit channels corresponding to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER in the SECTOREQM MO) and four receive channels of each RRU MO.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, SECTOREQM, and RFU MOs:
    • When the CellActiveState parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_FDD, and the NbCellFlag is set to FALSE, the SECTOREQM MO associated with the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be configured with the antenna in the RFU MO supporting only LTE FDD.
    • In a Cell MO, if CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE and FddTddInd is set to CELL_TDD, the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be associated with an RFU MO supporting LTE_TDD.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, BASEBANDEQM, BBP, and MDU MOs:
    • When the NbCellFlag is set to FALSE and the value of CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE: (1) If FddTddInd in this Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and BaseBandEqmId in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO has been set, TYPE in all BBP MOs corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO must be set to UBBP or LBBP, and WM in all BBP MOs must be set to FDD, FDD_NBIOT, FDD_NBIOT_ENHANCE or FDD_NBIOT_CUSTOM; (2) If FddTddInd in this Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and BaseBandEqmId in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO has been set, TYPE in all BBP MOs corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO must be set to UBBP or UBBP-W or LBBP, and WM in all BBP MOs cannot be set to FDD, FDD_NBIOT, FDD_NBIOT_ENHANCE and FDD_NBIOT_CUSTOM.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm and BASEBANDEQM MOs:
    • If CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, BASEBANDEQMTYPE in all BASEBANDEQM MOs corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be set to ULDL.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, SECTOREQM, RRU, and RRUCHAIN MOs:
    • In a Cell MO, if CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, and MultiRruCellMode is set to DIGITAL_COMBINATION, and the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is associated with an RRU MO with RT set to PRRU, all RRU MOs must be configured on the same RRU chain.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, BBP, and MDU MOs:
    • When the NbCellFlag is set to FALSE and the value of CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE: (1) If FddTddInd in this Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and BaseBandEqmId in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO has been not set, TYPE in at least one BBP MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO must be set to UBBP or LBBP, and WM in the BBP MO must be set to FDD, FDD_NBIOT, FDD_NBIOT_ENHANCE or FDD_NBIOT_CUSTOM; (2) If FddTddInd in this Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and BaseBandEqmId in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO has been not set, TYPE in at least one BBP MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO must be set to UBBP or UBBP-W or LBBP, and WM in the BBP MO cannot be set to FDD, FDD_NBIOT, NBIOT, FDD_NBIOT_ENHANCE and FDD_NBIOT_CUSTOM.
  • Relation with the EuSectorEqmGroup and SECTOREQM MOs:
    • In a Cell MO, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_TDD, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, MultiRruCellMode is set to MPRU_AGGREGATION or CELL_COMBINATION and CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each EuSectorEqmGroup MO under this Cell MO must be associated with two TX antennas (The preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER) and two RX antennas.
    • If the value of CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, and MultiRruCellMode in the Cell MO is set to MPRU_AGGREGATION or SFN or CELL_COMBINATION, respectively, EuSectorEqmGroup MOs cannot be configured under this Cell MO after the cell is activated. If the EuSectorEqmGroup MO has been configured under this Cell MO before the cell is activated, the SECTOREQM MO associated with this EuSectorEqmGroup MO must already exist.
    • The sector represented by a SECTOREQM MO under the EuSectorEqmGroup MO associated with a Cell MO can be configured with four TX antennas and four RX antennas, two TX antennas and four RX antennas, or two TX antennas and two RX antennas if FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, MultiRruCellMode, CrsPortNum, TxRxMode, and CellActiveState in this Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD, BOOLEAN_TRUE, SFN, CRS_PORT_4, 4T4R, and CELL_ACTIVE, respectively, and ANTCFGMODE is set to ANTENNAPORT.
    • The total numbers of TX antennas and RX antennas in at least one piece of sector equipment configured using the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each EuSectorEqmGroup MO for a Cell MO must be both 4 when the following conditions are met: The Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the MultiRruCellFlag, MultiRruCellMode, CellActiveState, and TxRxMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, MPRU_AGGREGATION, CELL_ACTIVE, and 4T4R, respectively.
  • Relation with the SECTOREQM and EuSectorEqmGroup MOs:
    • In a Cell MO, if CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, SectorEqmRef in each EuSectorEqmGroup MO under this Cell MO must be unique.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, EuPrbSectorEqm, SECTOREQM, RRU, and RFU MOs:
    • In a Cell MO, if CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE and MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE, a maximum of two RRU MOs or RFU MOs can be associated with a SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm or EuPrbSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, EuPrbSectorEqm, and SECTOREQM MOs:
    • In a Cell MO, CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE, and two RRU or RFU are associated with a SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm or EuPrbSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO. If FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, TxRxMode must be set to 2T2R, 2T4R, 4T4R, 2T8R, 4T8R, or 8T8R. If FddTddInd is set to CELL_TDD, TxRxMode must be set to 2T2R or 8T8R.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, SECTOR, and SECTOREQM MOs:
    • If CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and FddTddInd is CELL_TDD Model, each SECTOR MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be configured and unique.
    • If CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd is CELL_TDD Model and multiple SECTOREQM MOs are associated with the SECTOR MO for this Cell MO, this Cell MO can reference only one SECTOREQM MO.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, CellAlgoSwitch, and SECTOREQM MOs:
    • Among all cells which use a same SECTOREQM, the number of cells which CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and GLPwrShare in the CellAlgoSwitch is set to ON must be less than 2.
    • For a Cell MO in which the value of the CellActiveState parameter is CELL_ACTIVE, if the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in this Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, the MultiRruCellMode parameter in this Cell MO is set to SFN or CELL_COMBINATION, and the Virtual4T4Rswitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, then the SectorEqmCombineGrpId parameter in each eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell must not be set to 255, and the number of TX antennas of the sector equipment configured in each SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each eUCellSectorEqm MO must be 2. In addition, if the Cell::FddTddInd parameter in this Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the number of RX antennas of the sector equipment configured in each SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each eUCellSectorEqm MO must be 2 or 4; if the Cell::FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the number of RX antennas of the sector equipment configured in each SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each eUCellSectorEqm MO must be 2.
  • Relation with the EuSectorEqmGroup, BASEBANDEQM, and EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MOs:
    • If CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, BaseBandEqmId in the EuSectorEqmGroup MO or BasebandEqmId in the EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MO under this Cell MO is not 255, the BASEBANDEQM MO corresponding to BaseBandEqmId or BasebandEqmId must have been configured.
  • Relation with the EuSectorEqmGroup, BASEBANDEQM, and BBP MOs:
    • When the value of CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE: (1) If FddTddInd in this Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and BaseBandEqmId in a EuSectorEqmGroup MO under this Cell MO has been set, TYPE in all BBP MOs corresponding to the EuSectorEqmGroup MO must be set to UBBP or LBBP, and WM in all BBP MOs must be set to FDD or FDD_NBIOT or FDD_NBIOT_ENHANCE or FDD_NBIOT_CUSTOM; (2) If FddTddInd in this Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and BaseBandEqmId in a EuSectorEqmGroup MO under this Cell MO has been set, TYPE in all BBP MOs corresponding to the EuSectorEqmGroup MO must be set to UBBP or UBBP-W or LBBP, and WM in all BBP MOs cannot be set to FDD, FDD_NBIOT, FDD_NBIOT_ENHANCE, FDD_NBIOT_CUSTOM and NBIOT.
    • When CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, BaseBandEqmId in the EuSectorEqmGroup MO under the Cell MO has been set, and TYPE is set to UBBP or UBBP-W in at least one BBP MO corresponding to the EuSectorEqmGroup MO, BBWS in the BBP MO must be set to LTE_FDD or LTE_TDD if FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD or CELL_TDD, respectively.
  • Relation with the EuSectorEqmGroup, SECTOREQM, RRU, RRUCHAIN, and BBP MOs:
    • When the CellActiveState and CPRICompression parameters in a Cell MO are set to CELL_ACTIVE and NO_COMPRESSION, respectively, and the SectorGroupCpriCompression parameter in the EuSectorEqmGroup MO under the Cell MO is set to NULL, the CPRIEX parameter in the BBP MO connected to the RRU MO used by the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the EuSectorEqmGroup MO under the Cell MO must be set to OFF.
    • When the CellActiveState in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and the SectorGroupCpriCompression in the EuSectorEqmGroup MO under the Cell MO is set to NO_COMPRESSION, the CPRIEX parameter in the BBP MO connected to the RRU MO used by the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the EuSectorEqmGroup MO under the Cell MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, SECTOREQM, RRU, RRUCHAIN, and BBP MOs:
    • When the CellActiveState and CPRICompression parameters in a Cell MO are set to CELL_ACTIVE and NO_COMPRESSION, respectively, and the SectorCpriCompression parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under the Cell MO is set to NULL, the CPRIEX parameter in the BBP MO connected to the RRU MO used by the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO under the Cell MO must be set to OFF.
    • When the CellActiveState in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and the SectorCpriCompression in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under the Cell MO is set to NO_COMPRESSION, the CPRIEX parameter in the BBP MO connected to the RRU MO used by the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO under the Cell MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the EQUIPMENT MO:
    • In a Cell MO, if SpecialSubframePatterns is set to SSP9 and CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, PROTOCOL in the EQUIPMENT MO must be TDL_IR.
  • Relation with the MPT MO:
    • If the number of MPT MOs with TYPE being set to UMPT is 0, the number of Cell MOs cannot exceed 18.
  • Relation with the EuSectorEqmGroup, SECTOREQM, RRUCHAIN, and RHUB MOs:
    • In the Cell MO, if CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE and MultiRruCellMode is set to MPRU_AGGREGATION or SFN or CELL_COMBINATION, the maximum of RRUs specified in the SECTOREQM MO under the EuSectorEqmGroup MO is 32, and these RRUs must be on the same BBI CPRI Port, and their connected RHUB number on the chain cannot be more than 4.
  • Relation with the EuCellPriBBEqm and BASEBANDEQM MOs:
