    • If CellActiveState in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the BASEBANDEQM MO corresponding to PriBaseBandEqmId in the EuCellPriBBEqm MO has been configured.
  • Relation with the EuCellPriBBEqm, BASEBANDEQM, BBP, and MDU MOs:
    • In the Cell MO, if CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE and FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, the baseband processing units corresponding to the PriBaseBandEqmId parameters correlated to the EuCellPriBBEqm in the BASEBANDEQM MO must be LBBPs and the related WM parameters must be set to FDD, FDD_ATG, FDD_NBIOT, FDD_NBIOT_ENHANCE or FDD_NBIOT_CUSTOM, or must be UBBPs and the related BBWS parameters must be set to LTE_FDD.
    • In the Cell MO, if CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE and FddTddInd is set to CELL_TDD, the baseband processing units corresponding to the PriBaseBandEqmId parameters correlated to the EuCellPriBBEqm in the BASEBANDEQM MO must be LBBPs and the related WM parameters cannot be set to FDD, FDD_ATG, FDD_NBIOT_ENHANCE or FDD_NBIOT_CUSTOM, or must be UBBPs and the related BBWS parameters cannot be set to LTE_FDD.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, BASEBANDEQM, and BBP MOs:
    • When the NbCellFlag is set to FALSE, CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, BaseBandEqmId in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under the Cell MO has been set, and TYPE is set to UBBP or UBBP-W in at least one BBP MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO, BBWS in the BBP MO must be set to LTE_FDD or LTE_TDD if FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD or CELL_TDD, respectively.
    • For a Cell MO in which the values of CellActiveState, FddTddInd, NbCellFlag, and MultiRruCellFlag are CELL_ACTIVE, CELL_FDD, FALSE, and BOOLEAN_FALSE, respectively, UlBandWidth or DlBandWidth is set to a value larger than or equal to CELL_BW_N75, and TxRxMode is set to a value larger than 2T2R, if the eUCellSectorEqm MOs corresponding to this Cell MO are bound to BASEBANDEQM MOs, and WM is set to SPEC_MODE1 and only the LTE_FDD option or only the LTE_FDD and NBIOT options of BBWS are selected in the BBP MOs corresponding to these BASEBANDEQM MOs, then CPRICompression cannot be set to NO_COMPRESSION.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm and BBP MOs:
    • BBWS is set to LTE_FDD in at least one BBP MO when all the following conditions are met: (1) NbCellFlag is set to FALSE and CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE; (2) FddTddInd in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD; (3) BaseBandEqmId in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under the Cell MO has not been set; (4) the number of BBP MOs with TYPE set to LBBP is zero; (5) the number of BBP MOs with TYPE set to UBBP is not zero.
    • BBWS is set to LTE_TDD in at least one BBP MO when all the following conditions are met: (1) CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE; (2) FddTddInd in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD; (3) BaseBandEqmId in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under the Cell MO has not been set; (4) the number of BBP MOs with TYPE set to LBBP is zero; (5) the number of BBP MOs with TYPE set to UBBP or UBBP-W is not zero.
  • Relation with the EuSectorEqmGroup and BBP MOs:
    • BBWS is set to LTE_FDD in at least one BBP MO when all the following conditions are met: (1) CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE; (2) FddTddInd in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD; (3) BaseBandEqmId in the EuSectorEqmGroup MO under the Cell MO has not been set; (4) the number of BBP MOs with TYPE set to LBBP is zero; (5) the number of BBP MOs with TYPE set to UBBP is not zero.
    • BBWS is set to LTE_TDD in at least one BBP MO when all the following conditions are met: (1) CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE; (2) FddTddInd in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD; (3) BaseBandEqmId in the EuSectorEqmGroup MO under the Cell MO has not been set; (4) the number of BBP MOs with TYPE set to LBBP is zero; (5) the number of BBP MOs with TYPE set to UBBP or UBBP-W is not zero.
  • Relation with the MPT and GlobalProcSwitch MOs:
    • In MPT MO, if the num of UMPT is 1, and the number of LMPT is 1, and PreferSigExtend in a GlobalProcSwitch MO is set to ON, a maximum of 36 Cell MOs can be configured.
  • Relation with the CellRelayAlgoPara MO:
    • The value of the UlRelaySubframeRatio parameter in the CellRelayAlgoPara MO can be a value from 1/10 to 4/10 when the FddTddInd and SubframeAssignment parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and SA1, respectively.
    • The value of the UlRelaySubframeRatio parameter in the CellRelayAlgoPara MO can be a value from 1/10 to 2/10 when the FddTddInd and SubframeAssignment parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and SA2, respectively.
    • The value of the UlRelaySubframeRatio parameter in the CellRelayAlgoPara MO is 1/10 when the FddTddInd and SubframeAssignment parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and SA5, respectively.
    • If the FddTddInd and SubframeAssignment parameters in a Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and SA1, respectively, and the OutOfBandRelaySwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the value range of the DlRelaySubframeRatio parameter in the CellRelayAlgoPara MO is [1/10, 6/10].
    • If the FddTddInd and SubframeAssignment parameters in a Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and SA2, respectively, and the OutOfBandRelaySwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the value range of the DlRelaySubframeRatio parameter in the CellRelayAlgoPara MO is [1/10, 8/10].
    • If the FddTddInd and SubframeAssignment parameters in a Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and SA5, respectively, and the OutOfBandRelaySwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the value range of the DlRelaySubframeRatio parameter in the CellRelayAlgoPara MO is [1/10, 9/10].
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch and CellRelayAlgoPara MOs:
    • If any of the OutOfBandRelaySwitch, InBandRelayDeNbSwitch, or InBandRelayReNbSwitch options of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, there must be a CellRelayAlgoPara MO instance under the Cell MO. The CellRelayAlgoPara MO instance under the Cell MO can be deleted only if the OutOfBandRelaySwitch, InBandRelayDeNbSwitch, and InBandRelayReNbSwitch options are deselected.
    • If the InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the value of the DlRelaySubframeRatio parameter in the CellRelayAlgoPara MO must be 1_10, 2_10, or 3_10.
    • If the InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the value of the DlRelaySubframeRatio parameter in the CellRelayAlgoPara MO must be 3_40, 6_40, 9_40, 12_40, 15_40, 18_40, 21_40, 24_40, or 10_10.
    • If the InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the value of the DlRelaySubframeRatio parameter in the CellRelayAlgoPara MO must be 1_10, 2_10, or 3_10.
    • If the InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the value of the DlRelaySubframeRatio parameter in the CellRelayAlgoPara MO must be 3_40, 6_40, 9_40, 12_40, 15_40, 18_40, 21_40, 24_40, or 10_10.
    • If the InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the value of the InBandRelayConCnt parameter in the CellRelayAlgoPara MO must be NON_CON.
    • If the InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the value of the InBandRelayConCnt parameter in the CellRelayAlgoPara MO must be NON_CON.
  • Relation with the MDU MO:
    • When CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, and TYPE is set to UMDU in at least one MDU MO, if NbCellFlag is set to FALSE and FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD or CELL_TDD, BBWS in the MDU MO must contain LTE_FDD or LTE_TDD, if NbCellFlag is set to TRUE, BBWS in the MDU MO must contain NBIOT.
    • If the number of MDU MOs with TYPE being set to UMDU is 1, the number of Cell MOs cannot exceed 12.
    • If the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and the TYPE parameter in the MDU MO is set to MDUC, the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO must be set to CELL_FDD.
    • If the CellActiveState and MultiRruCellFlag parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_ACTIVE and BOOLEAN_TRUE, respectively, and the TYPE parameter in the MDU MO is set to MDUC, then the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to SFN.
    • If the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and the TYPE parameter in the MDU MO is set to MDUC, the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO must be set to FALSE.
    • If the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and the TYPE parameter in the MDU MO is set to MDUC, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to 1T1R, 1T2R, or 2T2R.
    • If the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and the TYPE parameter in the MDU MO is set to MDUC, the HighSpeedFlag parameter in the Cell MO must be set to LOW_SPEED.
    • If the number of MDU MOs with TYPE being set to MDUC is 1, the number of Cell MOs cannot exceed 3.
    • When the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and there is an MDU MO instance in which the TYPE parameter is set to MDUC on the eNodeB, the LTE_FDD option of the BBWS parameter must be selected in the MDU MO instance if the FddTddInd and NbCellFlag parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and FALSE, respectively.
    • If the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and the TYPE parameter in the MDU MO is set to MDUC, the CellRadius in the Cell MO representing the cell cannot be set to a value greater than 10000 m.
  • Relation with the EuSectorEqmGroup, BASEBANDEQM, and MDU MOs:
    • When CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, BaseBandEqmId in the EuSectorEqmGroup MO under the Cell MO has been set, and TYPE is set to UMDU in at least one MDU MO corresponding to the EuSectorEqmGroup MO, BBWS in the MDU MO must be set to LTE_FDD or LTE_TDD if FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD or CELL_TDD, respectively.
  • Relation with the EuSectorEqmGroup and MDU MOs:
    • BBWS is set to LTE_FDD in at least one BBP or MDU MO when all the following conditions are met: (1) CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE; (2) FddTddInd in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD; (3) BaseBandEqmId in the EuSectorEqmGroup MO under the Cell MO has not been set; (4) the number of MDU MOs with TYPE set to UMDU is not zero.
    • BBWS is set to LTE_TDD in at least one BBP or MDU MO when all the following conditions are met: (1) CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE; (2) FddTddInd in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD; (3) BaseBandEqmId in the EuSectorEqmGroup MO under the Cell MO has not been set; (4) the number of MDU MOs with TYPE set to UMDU is not zero.
  • Relation with the CellFrameOffset, eUCellSectorEqm, SECTOREQM, and RRU MOs:
    • If the CellActiveState, FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, and MultiRruCellMode parameters are set to CELL_ACTIVE, CELL_TDD, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and CELL_COMBINATION respectively in a Cell MO, the FreqBand parameter is not set to 34 or 39 in a Cell MO, and no CellFrameOffset MO is configured under this Cell MO, then the values of the RS parameter in RRU MOs associated with this Cell MO must be the same.
  • Relation with the eNodeBFunction, eUCellSectorEqm, SfnAuxResBind, EuSectorEqmGroup, SfnAuxResGrpBind, EuPrbSectorEqm, MPT, Node, eNodeBResModeAlgo, and EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MOs:
    • The sum of the numbers of the following MO instances must meet a certain requirement: Cell MO instances in which MultiRruCellFlag and EuCellStandbyMode are set to BOOLEAN_FALSE and ACTIVE, respectively; Cell MO instances in which MultiRruCellFlag and MultiRruCellMode are set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and TWO_RRU_COMBINATION (or DIGITAL_COMBINATION), respectively; EuSectorEqmGroup and EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MO instances corresponding to the Cell MO instances in which MultiRruCellFlag and MultiRruCellMode are set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and MPRU_AGGREGATION, respectively; eUCellSectorEqm and eUPrbSectorEqm MO instances of other cells; SfnAuxResBind and SfnAuxResGrpBind MO instances. The requirement varies as follows: (1) The sum is less than or equal to 216 if UMPT boards are configured in the MPT MO and the conditions listed in (2) to (9) are not met. (2) The sum is less than or equal to 144 if one UMPT board is configured in the MPT MO, the BRDSPEC of the UMPT is UMPTe or UMPTg, and the conditions listed in (3) to (9) are not met. (3) The sum is less than or equal to 72 if UMPT boards are configured in the MPT MO, WM in the NODE MO is set to CONCURRENT, and the conditions listed in (4) to (9) are not met. (4) The sum is less than or equal to 72 if one UMPT is configured in the MPT MO and the BRDSPEC of the UMPT is UMPTga. (5) The sum is less than or equal to 36 if one UMPT is configured in the MPT MO and the BRDSPEC of the UMPT is UMPTa, UMPTb, or UMPTa/b. (6) The sum is less than or equal to 36 if two UMPT boards are configured in the MPT MO, the BRDSPEC of both UMPT boards is UMPTa, UMPTb, or UMPTa/b, and eNBCellNumCapabilityMode in the eNodeBResModeAlgo MO is set to NORMAL_MODE. (7) The sum is less than or equal to 72 if two UMPT boards are configured in the MPT MO, the BRDSPEC of both UMPT boards is UMPTga, and eNBCellNumCapabilityMode in the eNodeBResModeAlgo MO is set to NORMAL_MODE. (8) The sum is less than or equal to 144 if two UMPT boards are configured in the MPT MO, the BRDSPEC of both UMPT boards is UMPTga, and eNBCellNumCapabilityMode in the eNodeBResModeAlgo MO is set to ENHANCED_MODE. (9) The sum is less than or equal to 18 if no UMPT board is configured in the MPT MO.
  • Relation with the eNodeBFunction, SfnAuxResGrpBind, SfnAuxResBind, eNodeBResModeAlgo, Node, and MPT MOs:
    • The sum of the number of Cell MO instances in which EuCellStandbyMode is set to ACTIVE and the number of SfnAuxResBind and SfnAuxResGrpBind MO instances in which both Main eNodeB ID and Main eNodeB Local cell ID are set to different values must meet one of the following requirements: (1) The sum is less than or equal to 216 if UMPT boards are configured in the MPT MO and the conditions listed in (2) to (9) are not met. (2) The sum is less than or equal to 144 if one UMPT board is configured in the MPT MO, the BRDSPEC of the UMPT is UMPTe or UMPTg, and the conditions listed in (3) to (9) are not met. (3) The sum is less than or equal to 72 if UMPT boards are configured in the MPT MO, WM in the NODE MO is set to CONCURRENT, and the conditions listed in (4) to (9) are not met. (4) The sum is less than or equal to 72 if one UMPT is configured in the MPT MO and the BRDSPEC of the UMPT is UMPTga. (5) The sum is less than or equal to 36 if one UMPT is configured in the MPT MO and the BRDSPEC of the UMPT is UMPTa, UMPTb, or UMPTa/b. (6) The sum is less than or equal to 36 if two UMPT boards are configured in the MPT MO, the BRDSPEC of both UMPT boards is UMPTa, UMPTb, or UMPTa/b, and eNBCellNumCapabilityMode in the eNodeBResModeAlgo MO is set to NORMAL_MODE. (7) The sum is less than or equal to 72 if two UMPT boards are configured in the MPT MO, the BRDSPEC of both UMPT boards is UMPTga, and eNBCellNumCapabilityMode in the eNodeBResModeAlgo MO is set to NORMAL_MODE. (8) The sum is less than or equal to 144 if two UMPT boards are configured in the MPT MO, the BRDSPEC of both UMPT boards is UMPTga, and eNBCellNumCapabilityMode in the eNodeBResModeAlgo MO is set to ENHANCED_MODE. (9) The sum is less than or equal to 18 if no UMPT board is configured in the MPT MO.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, BASEBANDEQM, BBP, and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • In the same eNodeB, the number of Cell MOs related to which the UlJointReceptionSwitch option of UplinkCompSwitch is selected in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot exceed 3 if the following conditions are met: CellActiveState of a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the eUCellSectorEqm MO of the corresponding Cell MO is bound to a BASEBANDEQM MO, and BRDSPEC of the BBP MO related to the BASEBANDEQM MO is set to WD22LBBPC.
  • Relation with the Node MO:
    • If the LTEMODE parameter is set to LTE_TDD, the FddTddInd parameter of a cell cannot be set to CELL_FDD.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, CellAlgoSwitch, BASEBANDEQM, and BBP MOs:
    • The DlAfcSwitch option of the HighSpeedSchOptSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be set to ON for a cell only if the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the TYPE parameter is set to UBBP in the BBP MO that has the same cabinet number, subrack number, and slot number as the BBP specified in the BASEBANDEQM MO.
    • The DlAfcSwitch option of HighSpeedSchOptSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected for a cell only if the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the TYPE parameter is set to UBBP or LBBP in the BBP MO that has the same cabinet number, subrack number, and slot number as the BBP specified in the BASEBANDEQM MO.
  • Relation with the EuSectorEqmGroup, CellAlgoSwitch, and SECTOREQM MOs:
    • The total numbers of TX antennas and RX antennas in the sector equipment configured using the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each EuSectorEqmGroup MO for a Cell MO must be both 4 or 2 (the TX antennas refer to those whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER) when the following conditions are met: The Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the MultiRruCellFlag, MultiRruCellMode, CellActiveState, and TxRxMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, MPRU_AGGREGATION, CELL_ACTIVE, and 4T4R, respectively.
    • The SectorEqmGrpCombineId parameter in the EuSectorEqmGroup MO must not be 255 and the total numbers of TX antennas and RX antennas in the sector equipment configured using the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each EuSectorEqmGroup MO for a Cell MO must be both 2 (the TX antennas refer to those whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER) when the following conditions are met: The CellActiveState, MultiRruCellFlag, MultiRruCellMode, and TxRxMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_ACTIVE, BOOLEAN_TRUE, MPRU_AGGREGATION, and 2T2R, respectively, and the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • When the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO associated with a Cell MO is selected and the CellActiveState, MultiRruCellFlag, and MultiRruCellMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_ACTIVE or CELL_COMBINATION, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and SFN, respectively, the SectorEqmGrpCombineId parameter in each EuSectorEqmGroup MO under the Cell MO must not be 255, and the total numbers of TX antennas and RX antennas configured in all the SECTOREQM MOs corresponding to each EuSectorEqmGroup MO must be both 2.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch, SECTOREQM, and eUCellSectorEqm MOs:
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and ANTCFGMODE in a SECTOREQM MO referenced by a Cell is set to BEAM and BEAMAZIMUTHOFFSET is set to None, or the CellBeamMode parameter in eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 and the ANTCFGMODE parameter in SECTOREQM MO is set to ANTENNAPORT, the DynAdjVoltSwitch option of DynAdjVoltSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected.
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is not set to CELL_TDD and the ANTCFGMODE parameter in the SECTOREQM MO referenced by the Cell MO is set to BEAM(BEAM), or the CellBeamMode parameter in eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 and the ANTCFGMODE parameter in SECTOREQM MO is set to ANTENNAPORT, the PwrDeratSwitch parameter in theCellAlgoSwitch MO must not be set to ON(On).
  • Relation with the CellRfShutdown, SECTOREQM, and eUCellSectorEqm MOs:
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell is not set to 32T32R and the ANTCFGMODE parameter in the SECTOREQM MO referenced by the Cell MO is set to BEAM(BEAM), the RfShutdownSwitch parameter in theCellRfShutdown MO must not be set to ON(On).
  • Relation with the SECTOREQM MO:
    • When ANTCFGMODE and BEAMLAYERSPLIT in a SECTOREQM referenced by a Cell MO are set to BEAM and INNER_LAYER (or OUTER_LAYER), respectively, FddTddInd and TxRxMode in the Cell MO must be set to CELL_FDD and 4T4R, respectively.
  • Relation with the CellLowPower, SECTOREQM, and eUCellSectorEqm MOs:
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the ANTCFGMODE parameter in the SECTOREQM MO referenced by the Cell MO is set to BEAM(BEAM), the LowPwrSwitch parameter in the CellLowPower MO must not be set to ON(On).
  • Relation with the EutranInterFreqNCell, EutranIntraFreqNCell, and MDU MOs:
    • A maximum of 384 EutranInterFreqNCell and EutranIntraFreqNCell MOs can be configured in total for a Cell MO in the eNodeB when equipped with UMPT board or MDU type is MDUC or UMDU.
  • Relation with the EutranInterFreqNCell and EutranIntraFreqNCell MOs:
    • A maximum of 256 EutranInterFreqNCell and EutranIntraFreqNCell MOs can be configured in total for a Cell MO in the eNodeB when equipped with LMPT board.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch, EuSectorEqmGroup, and SECTOREQM MOs:
    • If the MultiRruCellFlag and MultiRruCellMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and MPRU_AGGREGATION respectively and the InterCellDmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO associated with the cell is selected, no more than one EuSectorEqmGroup MO can be configured for this Cell MO, and no more than one SECTOREQM MO can be configured for the EuSectorEqmGroup MO (if configured).
  • Relation with the EuPrbSectorEqm and SECTOREQM MOs:
    • In a Cell MO, if CellActiveState is CELL_ACTIVE and TxRxMode is set to 1T2R, the total number of TX antennas and the total number of RX antennas are calculated as follows: If WorkMode is set to UL_DL, the total number of TX antennas (The preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER) associated with the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the EuPrbSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be 1 or 2, and the total number of RX antennas associated with this SECTOREQM MO must be 2. If WorkMode is set to DL_ONLY, the total number of TX antennas (The preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER) associated with the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the EuPrbSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be 1 or 2, and the total number of RX antennas associated with this SECTOREQM MO must be 2 or 0.
    • The SECTOREQM MO referenced by the EuPrbSectorEqm MO under a Cell MO must be configured with two or four TX antennas (The preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER) and four RX antennas when the following conditions are met: (1) TxRxMode in this Cell MO is set to 2T4R. (2) CellActiveState in this Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE. (3) MultiRruCellFlag in this Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and MultiRruCellMode in this Cell MO is not set to SFN. Alternatively, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE.
    • Assume that in the Cell MO, FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, MultiRruCellMode is set to SFN, CellActiveState is CELL_ACTIVE, and TxRxMode is set to 2T4R, the total numbers of both TX and RX antennas specified in the SECTOREQM MO associated with the EuPrbSectorEqm MO in the Cell MO must be 1 and 1, 1 and 2, 2 and 2, or 2 and 4. The preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER. Assume that in the Cell MO, FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, MultiRruCellMode is set to SFN, CellActiveState is CELL_ACTIVE, and TxRxMode is set to 2T2R, the total numbers of both TX and RX antennas specified in the SECTOREQM MO associated with the EuPrbSectorEqm MO in the Cell MO must be 1 and 1, 1 and 2, or 2 and 2. The preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER. Assume that in the Cell MO, the FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, MultiRruCellMode is set to SFN, CellActiveState is CELL_ACTIVE, and TxRxMode is set to 1T2R, the total numbers of both TX and RX antennas specified in the SECTOREQM MO associated with the EuPrbSectorEqm MO in the Cell MO must be 1 and 1, or 1 and 2. The preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER.
    • If FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, MultiRruCellMode, TxRxMode, CrsPortNum, and CellActiveState in a Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD, BOOLEAN_TRUE, SFN, 4T4R, CRS_PORT_2, and CELL_ACTIVE, respectively, sector equipment specified in the SECTOREQM MO associated with the EuPrbSectorEqm of the Cell MO must be configured with four TX antennas and four RX antennas, two TX antennas and four RX antennas, two TX antennas and two RX antennas, one TX antennas and two RX antennas, or one TX antenna and one RX antenna.
    • In a Cell MO, if CellActiveState is CELL_ACTIVE and MultiRruCellMode is not set to CELL_COMBINATION, and MultiRruCellMode is not set to SFN, the total number of TX antennas and the total number of RX antennas are calculated as follows: If TxRxMode is set to 1T1R or 2T2R and WorkMode is set to UL_DL, the total number of TX antennas (The preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER) and that of RX antennas associated with the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the EuPrbSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be equal to the number of TX antennas and that of RX antennas specified by the value of TxRxMode, respectively. If TxRxMode is set to 1T1R or 2T2R and WorkMode is set to DL_ONLY, the total number of TX antennas (The preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER) associated with the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the EuPrbSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be equal to 0 or the number of TX antennas specified by the value of TxRxMode, and the total number of RX antennas associated with this SECTOREQM MO must be equal to the number of RX antennas specified by the value of TxRxMode.
    • If one EuPrbSectorEqm MO instance is configured for a Cell MO instance in which the MultiRruCellFlag parameter is not configured and the CellActiveState, FddTddInd, and TxRxMode parameters are set to CELL_ACTIVE, CELL_FDD, and 4T4R, respectively, and the ANTCFGMODE parameter in the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the EuPrbSectorEqm MO is not set to BEAM, the numbers of TX antennas and RX antennas in the sector equipment configured using the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to this EuPrbSectorEqm MO instance must be both 4 (the TX antennas refer to those whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER). If two EuPrbSectorEqm MO instances are configured for the Cell MO instance and the ANTCFGMODE parameter in the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the EuPrbSectorEqm MO is not set to BEAM, the numbers of TX antennas and RX antennas in the sector equipment configured using the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each EuPrbSectorEqm MO instance must be both 2 and the antennas must correspond to one RRU or RFU.
    • If CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, each antenna associated with the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the EuPrbSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO must be unique.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch and MDU MOs:
    • If the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and the TYPE parameter in the MDU MO is set to MDUC, the TurboReceiverSwitch parameter in the corresponding CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and the TYPE parameter in the MDU MO is set to MDUC, the OutOfBandRelaySwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the corresponding CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the SymbolPwrSaving and MDU MOs:
    • If the value of the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE and the TYPE parameter in the MDU MO is set to MDUC, the TrigBndlSchDlAvgPrbThld parameter in the SymbolPwrSaving MO cannot be set to a value greater than 0.
  • Relation with the CaGroupCell and eNodeBFunction MOs:
    • If eNodeBId in a CaGroupCell MO is equal to eNodeBId in the eNodeBFunction MO, the Cell MO corresponding to the LocalCellId in the CaGroupCell MO must have been configured.
    • If eNodeBId in a CaGroupCell MO is equal to eNodeBId in the eNodeBFunction MO, the NbCellFlag in the Cell MO corresponding to the LocalCellId in the CaGroupCell MO must be set to FALSE.
  • Relation with the CellUlschAlgo and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • The sum of UlVoipRsvRbStart in CellUlschAlgo and UlVoipRsvRbNum in CellUlschAlgo cannot be larger than 24 when the UlVoipRbRsvSwitch option of UlSchExtSwitch is selected and DlBandWidth is equal to CELL_BW_N25.
    • The sum of the values of UlVoipRsvRbStart in CellUlschAlgo and UlVoipRsvRbNum in CellUlschAlgo cannot be larger than 49 when the UlVoipRbRsvSwitch option UlSchExtSwitch is selected and DlBandWidth is equal to CELL_BW_N50.
    • The sum of the values of UlVoipRsvRbStart in CellUlschAlgo and UlVoipRsvRbNum in CellUlschAlgo cannot be larger than 74 when the UlVoipRbRsvSwitch option of UlSchExtSwitch is selected and DlBandWidth is equal to CELL_BW_N75.
    • The sum of the values of UlVoipRsvRbStart in CellUlschAlgo and UlVoipRsvRbNum in CellUlschAlgo cannot be larger than 99 when the UlVoipRbRsvSwitch option of UlSchExtSwitch is selected and DlBandWidth is equal to CELL_BW_N100.
    • In the Cell MO, if the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD and the UlVoIPLoadBasedSchSwitch option of the UlEnhencedVoipSchSw parameter is selected, the SpsSchSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter must be selected.
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE and the NbUlSubcarrierSpacingType parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO is set to 3dot75K or HYBRID_MODE, the UlJointReceptionSwitch option of the UplinkCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE and the NbUlSubcarrierSpacingType parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO is set to 3dot75K or HYBRID_MODE, the IntraBaseBandSwitch option of the SfnUplinkCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to TRUE and the NbUlSubcarrierSpacingType parameter in the associated CellUlschAlgo MO is set to 3dot75K or HYBRID_MODE, the MrcIrcAdptSwitch option of the PuschIrcAlgoSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the PrbRachCeConfig and Prb MOs:
    • If the AnchorCarrierFlag parameter in a Prb MO is TRUE and the EDT_RANDOM_ACCESS_SW option of the PrachAlgoSw parameter in a PrbRachCeConfig MO with that coverage level under this Prb MO is also selected, or if an option (indicating a coverage level) of the PrachCoverageLevelType parameter in a Prb MO is selected and the AnchorCarrierFlag parameter in the Prb MO is FALSE and the EDT_RANDOM_ACCESS_SW option of the PrachAlgoSw parameter in a PrbRachCeConfig MO with that coverage level under this Prb MO is also selected, the following parameter setting requirements must be met for this Prb at the corresponding coverage level: (1) The PrachSubcarrierNumber parameter cannot be set to SC_NUM_36 or SC_NUM_48. (2) The sum of the subcarrier index indicated in the value of the PrachSubcarrierOffset parameter and the subcarrier quantity indicated in the value of the PrachSubcarrierNumber parameter must be equal to the subcarrier index indicated in the value of the EdtPrachSubcarrierOffset parameter, or the sum of the subcarrier index indicated in the value of the EdtPrachSubcarrierOffset parameter and 12 must be equal to the subcarrier index indicated in the value of the PrachSubcarrierOffset parameter.
  • Relation with the PrbRachCeConfig MO:
    • If the value of the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO for a cell is CELL_ACTIVE and the NbCellFlag parameter in this Cell MO is set to TRUE, the EDT_RANDOM_ACCESS_SW option of the PrachAlgoSw parameter in the PrbRachCeConfig MO with a specific coverage level under any Prb MO of the cell can be selected only when the option indicating that coverage level is selected in the CoverageLevelType parameter in this Cell MO.
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO associated by the LocalCellId parameter in the PrbRachCeConfig MO is set to TRUE(TRUE) and the CellActiveState parameter in this Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the PrachRepetitionCount parameter in the PrbRachCeConfig MO under this Cell MO must meet the following condition: When COVERAGE_LEVEL_1 in the CoverageLevelType parameter in the Cell MO is set to 1, the PrachRepetitionCount parameter value for CoverageLevel of 0 must be less than that for CoverageLevel of 1.
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO associated by the LocalCellId parameter in the PrbRachCeConfig MO is set to TRUE(TRUE) and the CellActiveState parameter in this Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the PrachRepetitionCount parameter in the PrbRachCeConfig MO under this Cell MO must meet the following condition: When COVERAGE_LEVEL_2 in the CoverageLevelType parameter in the Cell MO is set to 1, the PrachRepetitionCount parameter value for CoverageLevel of 1 must be less than that for CoverageLevel of 2.
    • If the COVERAGE_LEVEL_2 option of the CoverageLevelType parameter in the Cell MO is selected, the PrachSubcarrierNumber parameter in the PrbRachCeConfig MO with any coverage level corresponding to all carriers cannot be set to SC_NUM_48.
  • Relation with the PrbRachCeConfig, CellAlgoSwitch, and Prb MOs:
    • If the 100KM_CELL_RADIUS_SWITCH option of the NbCellAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected for a cell, any option (indicating a coverage level) of the PrachCoverageLevelType parameter in any Prb MO of this cell and the AnchorCarrierFlag parameter in the Prb MO is FALSE and the EDT_RANDOM_ACCESS_SW option of the PrachAlgoSw parameter in the PrbRachCeConfig MO with that coverage level under that Prb MO cannot be both selected, and the AnchorCarrierFlag parameter in the Prb MO is TRUE and the option in the PrachAlgoSw parameter in the PrbRachCeConfig MO under that Prb cannot be selected.
  • Relation with the PrbUlSchCeAlgo and CellEdtConfig MOs:
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE and the EdtMtSchToneNum parameter in the CellEdtConfig MO under this Cell MO is set to TONE_1, the following requirements must be met: (1) If the EdtMsg3Mcs parameter in a PrbUlSchCeAlgo MO for the cell is set to EDT_MSG3_MCS_3, the EdtMaxTbs parameter in this MO must be set to 328_BITS, 408_BITS, or 504_BITS. (2) If the EdtMsg3Mcs parameter in a PrbUlSchCeAlgo MO for the cell is set to EDT_MSG3_MCS_4, the EdtMaxTbs parameter in this MO cannot be set to 936_BITS or 1000_BITS.
  • Relation with the Prb and PrbRachCeConfig MOs:
    • If the value of CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, the EDT_RANDOM_ACCESS_SW option of PrachAlgoSw cannot be selected in the PrbRachCeConfig MOs set for a specific coverage level under the Prb MOs in which AnchorCarrierFlag is set to FALSE when the following conditions are met: (1) The option of PrachCoverageLevelType indicating that specific coverage level is deselected in the Prb MOs in which AnchorCarrierFlag is set to FALSE. Alternatively, the option of PrachCoverageLevelType indicating that specific coverage level is selected in a Prb MO in which AnchorCarrierFlag is set to FALSE but PrachProbabilityForAnchor is set to ONE in the PrbRachCeConfig MO set for that specific coverage level under the Prb MO in which AnchorCarrierFlag is set to TRUE. (2) The EDT_RANDOM_ACCESS_SW option of the PrachAlgoSw parameter is selected in the PrbRachCeConfig MO under the Prb MO in which AnchorCarrierFlag is set to TRUE.
    • If the value of CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, the EDT_RANDOM_ACCESS_SW option of PrachAlgoSw cannot be selected in the PrbRachCeConfig MOs set for a specific coverage level under the Prb MO in which AnchorCarrierFlag is set to TRUE when the following conditions are met: (1) The option of PrachCoverageLevelType indicating that specific coverage level is selected in a Prb MO in which AnchorCarrierFlag is set to FALSE and PrachProbabilityForAnchor is set to a value other than ONE in the PrbRachCeConfig MO set for that specific coverage level under the Prb MO in which AnchorCarrierFlag is set to TRUE. (2) The EDT_RANDOM_ACCESS_SW option of the PrachAlgoSw parameter is deselected in the PrbRachCeConfig MOs under the Prb MOs in which AnchorCarrierFlag is set to FALSE.
    • If the AnchorCarrierFlag parameter in a PRB MO is set to TRUE, the MtNprachRangeMsg3InitTone parameter in the PrbRachCeConfig MO with the highest coverage level under this Prb MO cannot be set to ZERO_12TONE, ZERO_6TONE, ZERO_3TONE, or ZERO_1TONE.
    • If the MtNprachRangeMsg3InitTone parameter is set to ZERO_1TONE, ZERO_3TONE, ZERO_6TONE, or ZERO_12TONE in a PrbRachCeConfig MO under a PRB MO in which the AnchorCarrierFlag parameter is set to FALSE, the PrachProbabilityForAnchor parameter in the PrbRachCeConfig MO with the highest coverage level under the associated PRB MO in which the AnchorCarrierFlag parameter is set to TRUE cannot be set to ZERO.
  • Relation with the UtranNCell and UtranExternalCell MOs:
    • In all UtranNCell MOs of a local Cell MO, the combination of UtranDlArfcn and PScrambCode in the UtranExternalCell MO corresponding to each UtranNCell MO must be unique.
  • Relation with the CellFrameOffset MO:
    • If FddTddInd in the Cell MO identified by LocalCellId in a CellFrameOffset MO is set to CELL_FDD(FDD), FrameOffsetMode in the CellFrameOffset MO must be set to CustomFrameOffset.
    • When the following conditions are met, the FrameOffsetMode and FrameOffset parameters in a CellFrameOffset MO under a Cell MO must be set to CustomFrameOffset and 0, respectively: The CellActiveState, FddTddInd, and WorkMode parameters in the associated Cell MO are set to CELL_ACTIVE, CELL_TDD, and LAA, respectively, and the CellFrameOffset MO under this Cell MO has been configured.
  • Relation with the EutranBlkNCell, EutranExternalCell, and EutranInterNFreq MOs:
    • In inter-eNodeB scenarios, the value of DlEarfcn in the EutranExternalCell MO associated with an EutranBlkNCell MO must be the same as the value of either DlEarfcn in the corresponding Cell MO or DlEarfcn in the EutranInterNFreq MO.
  • Relation with the CellPrsCfg and CellMBMSCfg MOs:
    • If FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the MBMSSwitch option of MBMSSwitch is selected, the last digit of the value of PrsConfigurationIndex must be 0, 4, 5, or 9.
  • Relation with the CellMBMSCfg and CellPrsCfg MOs:
    • If FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the MBMSSwitch option of MBMSSwitch is selected, NumberofDlFramesforPrs must be set to SF1 or SF2 when the last digit of the value of PrsConfigurationIndex is 4 or 9, or NumberofDlFramesforPrs must be set to SF1 when the last digit of the value of PrsConfigurationIndex is 0 or 5.
  • Relation with the CellCspcPara and ENodeBAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • If the CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and the CelleCspcSwitch in the CellCspcPara MO is ON, DlIcicSwitch in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be set to DlIcicSwitch_OFF_ENUM.
    • If the CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and the IntraEnbCspcSw in the CellCspcPara MO is ON, the DlIcicSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be set to DlIcicSwitch_OFF_ENUM.
  • Relation with the EutranInterNFreq and UtranNFreq MOs:
    • MlbFreqUlPriority in the EutranInterNFreq MO associated with this Cell MO cannot be set to the same value as MlbFreqPriority in the UtranNFreq MO associated with this Cell MO.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch and MPT MOs:
    • If TYPE in the MPT MO is set to LMPT, MtcSwitch in the associated CellAlgoSwitch cannot be set to ON.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, SectorEqm, CellCsiRsParaCfg, and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the same MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, and the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_COMBINATION or SFN, if TM9Switch(TM9Switch) under the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter is selected in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the CsiRsSwitch parameter in the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO is not set to NOT_CFG, and the CsiRsPortNum parameter in the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO is set to 8, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to 8T8R, and the number of TX antennas (whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER) specified by the SECTOREQMANTENNA parameter in the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO must not be 4.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch, eUCellSectorEqm, SECTOREQM, RRU, and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • When the MultiRruCellMode parameter in a Cell MO is not set to MPRU_AGGREGATION and the TP parameter is set to BRANCH(BRANCH) in the RRU MO mapping the SECTOREQM MO indicated by the SectorEqmId parameter specified in the eUCellSectorEqm MO in the same Cell MO, the DMIMOJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When the MultiRruCellMode parameter in a Cell MO is not set to MPRU_AGGREGATION and the TP parameter is set to BRANCH(BRANCH) in the RRU MO mapping the SECTOREQM MO indicated by the SectorEqmId parameter specified in the eUCellSectorEqm MO in the same Cell MO, the DMIMOJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the EuSectorEqmGroup and CellLaaCoexistPara MOs:
    • When the WorkMode parameter in a Cell MO is set to LAA, and the EuSectorEqmGroup MO under the Cell MO consists of only one piece of sector equipment, and CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the LsbSwitch parameter in the CellLaaCoexistPara MO under the Cell MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the ENodeBAlgoSwitch, eUCellSectorEqm, SECTOREQM, and RRU MOs:
    • When the AntCalibrationTimeSwitch parameter in an ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is set to ON, FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, MultiRruCellMode, FreqBand, SubframeAssignment, and SpecialSubframePatterns parameters in a Cell MO are set to TDD, BOOLEAN_TRUE(True), CELL_COMBINATION, 34 or 39, SA2, and SSP6 or SSP7, respectively, the values of the RS parameters in the RRU MO under the Cell MO must be the same.
  • Relation with the EuPrbSectorEqm, BASEBANDEQM, and BBP MOs:
    • When CellActiveState in an FDD Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, BasebandEqmId in the EuPrbSectorEqm MO under the Cell MO has been set, and TYPE is set to UBBP in at least one BBP MO corresponding to the EuPrbSectorEqm MO, BBWS in the BBP MO must be set to NBIOT if NbCellFlag is set to TRUE.
  • Relation with the EuCellPriBBEqm, BASEBANDEQM, and BBP MOs:
    • In the Cell MO, if CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, and NbCellFlag is set to TRUE, if all the baseband processing units corresponding to the PriBaseBandEqmId parameters correlated to the EuCellPriBBEqm in the BASEBANDEQM MO are UBBPs, then the related BBWS parameters must be set to NBIOT, or the EuCellPriBBEqm in the BASEBANDEQM MO are LBBPs and the related WM parameters must be set to NBIOT, FDD_NBIOT, FDD_NBIOT_ENHANCE or FDD_NBIOT_CUSTOM.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, SectorSplitCell, and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • When the CellActiveState in a Cell is set to CELL_ACTIVE and the CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm of the Cell is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, and the number of the SectorSplitCell belonging to the SectorSplitGroup to which the Cell belongs equals 1 or 2, UlCompForVideoSwitch under the UplinkCompSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch must be turned off.
    • When the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, and the number of SectorSplitCell MO instances in the SectorSplitGroup MO associated with the Cell MO is less than or equal to two, the New4TcodebookSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
    • When the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, and the number of SectorSplitCell MO instances in the SectorSplitGroup MO associated with the Cell MO is less than or equal to two, the Pusch32Switchoption of the DlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the SRSCfg, eUCellSectorEqm, BASEBANDEQM, and BBP MOs:
    • When the SrsResOptSwitch parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to ON(On), and the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the BaseBandEqmId parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO must not be set to 255, the AutoCfgFlag parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO must not be set to AUTO_MODE_CFG, and the associated BASEBANDEQM MO can be configured with only one BBP board.
    • When the SrsResOptSwitch parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to ON(On), and the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, TYPE in an BBP MO corresponding to BaseBandEqmId in the eUCellSectorEqm MO cannot be set to LBBP.
  • Relation with the SRSCfg, EuSectorEqmGroup, BASEBANDEQM, and BBP MOs:
    • When the SrsResOptSwitch parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to ON(On), and the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the BaseBandEqmId parameter in the EuSectorEqmGroup MO must not be set to 255, the AutoCfgFlag parameter in the EuSectorEqmGroup MO must not be set to AUTO_MODE_CFG, and the associated BASEBANDEQM MO can be configured with only one BBP board.
    • When the SrsResOptSwitch parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to ON(On), and the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, TYPE in an BBP MO corresponding to BaseBandEqmId in the EuSectorEqmGroup MO cannot be set to LBBP.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, SECTOREQM, RRU, and RFU MOs:
    • If one eUCellSectorEqm MO instance is configured for a Cell MO instance in which the MultiRruCellFlag parameter is not configured and the CellActiveState, FddTddInd, and TxRxMode parameters are set to CELL_ACTIVE, CELL_FDD, and 4T4R, and the WorkMode is not set DL_ONLY, respectively, and the ANTCFGMODE parameter in the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO is not set to BEAM, the numbers of TX antennas and RX antennas in the sector equipment configured using the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to this eUCellSectorEqm MO instance must be both 4 (the TX antennas refer to those whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER). If two eUCellSectorEqm MO instances are configured for the Cell MO instance and the ANTCFGMODE parameter in the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO is not set to BEAM, the numbers of TX antennas and RX antennas in the sector equipment configured using the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each eUCellSectorEqm MO instance must be both 2 and the antennas must correspond to one RRU or RFU.
    • If one eUCellSectorEqm MO instance is configured for a Cell MO instance in which the MultiRruCellFlag parameter is not configured and the CellActiveState, FddTddInd TxRxMode, and WorkModeparameters are set to CELL_ACTIVE, CELL_FDD, 4T4R, and DL_ONLY, respectively, and the ANTCFGMODE parameter in the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO is not set to BEAM, the numbers of TX antennas and RX antennas in the sector equipment configured using the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to this eUCellSectorEqm MO instance must be 4 (the TX antennas refer to those whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER) and 4 or 0. If two eUCellSectorEqm MO instances are configured for the Cell MO instance and the ANTCFGMODE parameter in the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO is not set to BEAM, the numbers of TX antennas and RX antennas in the sector equipment configured using the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each eUCellSectorEqm MO instance must be 2 and 2 or 0, and the antennas must correspond to one RRU or RFU.
  • Relation with the EuPrbSectorEqm, SECTOREQM, RFU, RRU, and PrbSectorEqmGrpItem MOs:
    • In a Cell MO, if NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the EuPrbSectorEqm MO or PrbSectorEqmGrpItem MO under this Cell MO must be associated with an RRU or RFU MO supporting NBIOT.
  • Relation with the PDSCHCfg and eUCellSectorEqm MOs:
    • The sector represented by a SECTOREQM MO associated with a Cell MO must be configured with four TX antennas and four RX antennas when the following conditions are true: The SINGLE_CA_PWR_SHARE_SW_FOR_4T option of the TxChnPowerCfgSw parameter in a PDSCHCfg MO is selected, the MultiRruCellFlag, MultiRruCellMode, and CellActiveState parameters in a Cell MO are set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, SFN, and CELL_ACTIVE, respectively, and the ANTCFGMODE parameter in this SECTOREQM MO corresponding to eUCellSectorEqm MO under the Cell MO is set to ANTENNAPORT. The preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER.
  • Relation with the EuPrbSectorEqm, SECTOREQM, BASEBANDEQM, EuPrbSectorEqmGroup, and PrbSectorEqmGrpItem MOs:
    • In a Cell MO, if CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the EuPrbSectorEqm MO or the EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MO under this Cell MO must be configured, and if BasebandEqmId in the EuPrbSectorEqm MO or BasebandEqmId in the EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MO is not set to 255, BASEBANDEQMTYPE in all BASEBANDEQM MOs corresponding to EuPrbSectorEqm or EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MOs under this Cell MO must be set to ULDL.
  • Relation with the Prb and PrbSectorEqmGrpItem MOs:
    • If CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, SectorEqmId in all PrbSectorEqmGrpItem MO instances in the Prb MO under this Cell MO must be unique.
    • If NbCellFlag and CellActiveState in a Cell MO are set to TRUE and CELL_ACTIVE, respectively, the number of PrbSectorEqmGrpItem instances with the same SectorEqmGroupId and SectorEqmId parameter settings must be equal to the number of Prb MO instances under this Cell MO.
  • Relation with the EuPrbSectorEqmGroup, PrbSectorEqmGrpItem, and SECTOREQM MOs:
    • If CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, and MultiRruCellMode in this Cell MO is set to MPRU_AGGREGATION or SFN, EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MOs cannot be configured under this Cell MO after the cell is activated. If the EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MO has been configured under this Cell MO before the cell is activated, the PrbSectorEqmGrpItem MO associated with this EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MO must be configure, and the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the PrbSectorEqmGrpItem MO must be configured.
  • Relation with the PrbSectorEqmGrpItem, SECTOREQM, RRU, RRUCHAIN, and BBP MOs:
    • When the CellActiveState and CPRICompression parameters in a Cell MO are set to CELL_ACTIVE and NO_COMPRESSION, respectively, the CPRIEX parameter in the BBP MO connected to the RRU MO used by the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the PrbSectorEqmGrpItem MO under the Cell MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the PrbSectorEqmGrpItem and SECTOREQM MOs:
    • The sector represented by a SECTOREQM MO under the PrbSectorEqmGrpItem MO associated with a Cell MO can be configured with one TX antenna and one RX antenna if the following conditions are met in the Cell MO: FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, TxRxMode is set to 1T1R, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, and MultiRruCellMode is set to SFN or MPRU_AGGREGATION. The sector represented by the SECTOREQM MO under the PrbSectorEqmGrpItem MO associated with the Cell MO can be configured with two TX antennas and two RX antennas if the following condition is met in the Cell MO: TxRxMode, MultiRruCellFlag, and MultiRruCellMode are set to 2T2R, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and MPRU_AGGREGATION, respectively. The sector represented by the SECTOREQM MO under the PrbSectorEqmGrpItem MO associated with the Cell MO can be configured with one TX antenna and one RX antenna or two TX antennas and two RX antennas if the following condition is met in the Cell MO: TxRxMode, MultiRruCellFlag, and MultiRruCellMode are set to 2T2R, 2T4R, or 4T4R, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and SFN, respectively.
  • Relation with the EuPrbSectorEqmGroup, BASEBANDEQM, and BBP MOs:
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, and the BasebandEqmId parameter in the EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MO under this Cell MO is configured, the TYPE parameters in all associated BBP MOs cannot be set to LBBP.
  • Relation with the EuPrbSectorEqmGroup, BBP, and BASEBANDEQM MOs:
    • When the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE: If the value of the BasebandEqmId parameter in the EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MO under this Cell MO is not 255, the TYPE parameters in all BBP MO instances corresponding to BasebandEqmId must be set to UBBP and the BBWS parameter has a value of NBIOT. If the value of the BasebandEqmId parameter in the EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MO under this Cell MO is 255, there must be at least one BBP MO instance with TYPE being UBBP and BBWS having a value of NBIOT.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud, SectorSplitCell, and SectorSplitGroup MOs:
    • If the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under a Cell MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, the UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW option of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is selected, and the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 32T32R, then the number of SectorSplitCell MOs under the SectorSplitGroup MO that includes the cell specified by the Cell MO must be less than or equal to 4.
    • If the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under a Cell MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, and the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 32T32R, then the number of SectorSplitCell MOs under the SectorSplitGroup MO that includes the cell specified by the Cell MO must be less than or equal to 6.
    • If the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under a Cell MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, the CellActiveState in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, then all cells specified in the SectorSplitCell MOs under the SectorSplitGroup MO that includes the cell specified by the Cell MO must meet the following requirements: The SpectrumCloud MOs of these cells must exist, the GsmArfcnGroupId parameter in these SpectrumCloud MOs is set to the same value, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in these SpectrumCloud MOs is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • If the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under a Cell MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, the UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW option of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is selected, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 32T32R, and the CellActiveState in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, then all cells specified in the SectorSplitCell MOs under the SectorSplitGroup MO that includes the cell specified by the Cell MO must meet the following requirements: The SpectrumCloud MOs of these cells must exist, the ULSpectrumSharingConfigId parameter in these SpectrumCloud MOs is set to the same value, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in these SpectrumCloud MOs is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, and the UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW option of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in these SpectrumCloud MOs is selected.
    • For all cells with the same value of the SectorSplitGroupId parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MOs, if the CellActiveState parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO associated with the Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, then the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MOs associated with other Cell MOs must be set to the same value as that associated with this Cell MO.
    • For all Cell MOs with the same LteNrSpectrumShrCellGrpId parameter value, if the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO associated with this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR or LTE_NR_COORD_SPECTRUM_SHR, then the SectorSplitCell MOs associated with the LocalCellId parameter in all of these Cell MOs cannot be configured.
    • Among the Cell MOs with the same value of the SectorSplitGroupId parameter in the associated SectorSplitGroup MO, if the FddTddInd parameter in one Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO associated with the Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR or LTE_NR_COORD_SPECTRUM_SHR, then the LteNrSpectrumShrCellGrpId parameter in the LteNrSpctShrCellGrp MO associated with the other Cell MOs must be set to the same value as that associated with this Cell MO.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud, SECTOREQM, and eUCellSectorEqm MOs:
    • The SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in a SpectrumCloud MO must be set to OFF or LTE_NR_COORD_SPECTRUM_SHR if the following conditions are true: The FddTddInd parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD; the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1; the ANTCFGMODE parameter in the SECTOREQM MO is set to ANTENNAPORT.
  • Relation with the SECTOREQM and CellShortTtiAlgo MOs:
    • When the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 4T4R, the ANTCFGMODE parameter in the SECTOREQM MO is set to BEAM, and the BEAMLAYERSPLIT parameter in the SECTOREQM MO is set to INNER_LAYER or OUTER_LAYER, the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, SECTOREQM, and PDSCHCfg MOs:
    • In the SECTOREQM MO, if the ANTCFGMODE parameter is set to BEAM and the BEAMAZIMUTHOFFSET parameter is set to None, the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to NORMAL, and the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 4T4R, then the CrsPowerBoostingAmplitude parameter in the PDSCHCfg MO can only be set to DB_0.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell, CellMMAlgo, and CellUlschAlgo MOs:
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 32T32R, and no SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, the EnhancedVmimoSwitch parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO can be set to ON only if the PUSCH_RECEIVE_ENH_SWITCH option of the MMAlgoOptSwitch parameter in the CellMMAlgo MO is selected.
  • Relation with the CellCsiRsParaCfg, eUCellSectorEqm, and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • When the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO associated with the Cell MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, and there is only one SectorSplitCell MO in the SectorSplitGroup MO to which the Cell MO belongs, the CsiRsPeriod parameter in the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO associated with the Cell MO must not be set to ms80.
  • Relation with the CellMMAlgo, CellAlgoSwitch, and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • The PRECISE_PAIRING_OPT_SW option of the PreciseMubfOptSwitch parameter in the CellMMAlgo MO can be selected only when the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is not configured and the MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
  • Relation with the MDU and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • If the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and the TYPE parameter in the MDU MO is set to MDUC, the AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch option of AvoidInterfSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and the TYPE parameter in the MDU MO is set to MDUC, other than Tm9JtSwitch, the options of the DlCompSwitch parameter in the corresponding CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch, NCellSrsMeasPara, PUSCHCfg, CellUlIcAlgo, and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and no SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, the MASSIVE_MIMO_SRS_IC_SW option of the SrsIcSwitch parameter in the CellUlIcAlgo MO can be selected only if the SrsTimeMeasSwitch option of the SrsAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the SrsAutoNCellMeasSwitch parameter in the NCellSrsMeasPara MO is set to ON, the NCellSrsTimeMeasSwitch option of the NCellMeasSwitch parameter in the NCellSrsMeasPara MO is selected, the GroupHoppingEnabled in the PUSCHCfg MO is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE, and the SeqHoppingEnabled parameter in the PUSCHCfg MO is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE.
  • Relation with the CellCspcPara and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • The CelleCspcSwitch parameter in the CellCspcPara MO can be set to ON only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the BFModeUserPwrSwitch, BFModeUserAdptPwrSwitch, and BfEnhChnPwrFormSwitch options of the DlPcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are deselected.
    • The CelleCspcSwitch parameter in the CellCspcPara MO can be set to ON only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the InterDlCompDcsSwitch, and InterDlCompCbfSwitch options of the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch are deselected.
  • Relation with the CellMMAlgo and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • When the FddTddInd and TxRxMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and 64T64R or 32T32R respectively and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is not configured, the PUSCH_RECEIVE_ENH_SWITCH option of the MMAlgoOptSwitch parameter in the CellMMAlgo MO can be selected.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and GsmInterfArfcnCfg MOs:
    • When SpectrumCloudSwitch in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM and the cell bandwidth is 3 MHz, 5 MHz, 10 MHz, 15 MHz, or 20 MHz, the downlink GSM frequency determined by GsmArfcn in the GsmInterfArfcnCfg MO corresponding to GsmArfcnGroupId in the SpectrumCloud must meet the following requirements:
    • 1. If the cell bandwidth is 3 MHz, the GSM frequency is not within the RB range of [0, 14].
    • 2. If the cell bandwidth is 5 MHz, the GSM frequency is not within the RB range of [0, 24].
    • 3. If the cell bandwidth is 10 MHz, the GSM frequency is not within the RB range of [0, 49].
    • 4. If the cell bandwidth is 15 MHz, the GSM frequency is not within the RB range of [0, 74].
    • 5. If the cell bandwidth is 20 MHz, the GSM frequency is not within the RB range of [0, 99].
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud, ULSpectrumShrConfig, and CellEmtcAlgo MOs:
    • The EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO under an Cell MO cannot be selected when the following conditions are met: (a) The DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50. (b) The SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR or the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING and the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in this MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW. (c) The PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO is set to LTE_DL_HIGH_PRIORITY_PRB. (d) The LtePrbStartIndex parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO is set to a value greater than 19 or the LtePrbEndIndex parameter in this MO is set to a value less than 30.
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR or UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO is set to LTE_UL_AVAILABLE_PRB, and the value of the LtePrbStartIndex parameter is greater than 19 or the value of the LtePrbEndIndex parameter is smaller than 30, the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO must be deselected.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR or UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO is set to LTE_DL_AVAILABLE_PRB, and the value of the LtePrbStartIndex parameter is greater than 19 or the value of the LtePrbEndIndex parameter is smaller than 30, the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and ULSpectrumShrConfig MOs:
    • In a ULSpectrumShrConfig, the LtePrbStartIndex parameter must be set to 0 or 1 and the LtePrbEndIndex parameter must be set to 13 or 14 when the following conditions are met: (a) The DlBandWidth parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15. (b) The SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR or UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING and the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW. (c) The PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO is set to LTE_DL_AVAILABLE_PRB.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR or UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, and the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO under the Cell MO is set to LTE_DL_AVAILABLE_PRB, the value ranges of the LtePrbStartIndex and LtePrbEndIndex parameters must be 0 to 33 and 41 to 74, respectively.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR or UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, and the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO under the Cell MO is set to LTE_DL_AVAILABLE_PRB, the value ranges of the LtePrbStartIndex and LtePrbEndIndex parameters must be 0 to 46 and 53 to 99, respectively.
    • If the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, the UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW option of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch in the SpectrumCloud MO is not selected, and the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO is set to LTE_UL_AVAILABLE_PRB, then the value of the LtePrbEndIndex parameter must be greater than or equal to the value calculated using the following formula: Value of the LtePrbStartIndex parameter + Number of RBs indicated by the value of the UlBandWidth parameter * 0.75 - 1.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR or UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, and the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO under the Cell MO is set to LTE_DL_AVAILABLE_PRB, the value of the LtePrbEndIndex parameter must be greater than or equal to the value calculated by the following formula: LtePrbStartIndex + Number of RBs indicated by DlBandWidth * 0.76 - 1.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR or UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, and the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO under the Cell MO is set to LTE_DL_AVAILABLE_PRB, the value of the LtePrbEndIndex parameter must be greater than or equal to the value calculated by the following formula: LtePrbStartIndex + Number of RBs indicated by DlBandWidth * 0.75 - 1.
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR or UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, and the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO under the Cell MO is set to LTE_UL_AVAILABLE_PRB, the value ranges of the LtePrbStartIndex and LtePrbEndIndex parameters in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO must be 0 to 49.
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR or UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, and the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO under the Cell MO is set to LTE_UL_AVAILABLE_PRB, the value ranges of the LtePrbStartIndex and LtePrbEndIndex parameters in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO must be 0 to 24.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR or UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, and the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO under the Cell MO is set to LTE_DL_AVAILABLE_PRB, the value of the LtePrbEndIndex parameter must be greater than or equal to the value calculated by the following formula: LtePrbStartIndex + Number of RBs indicated by DlBandWidth x 0.56 - 1.
    • If the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, the UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW option of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch in the SpectrumCloud MO is not selected, and the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO is set to LTE_UL_AVAILABLE_PRB, then the value of the LtePrbEndIndex parameter must be greater than or equal to the value calculated using the following formula: Value of the LtePrbStartIndex parameter + Number of RBs indicated by the value of the UlBandWidth parameter * 0.56 - 1.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR or UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, and the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO under the Cell MO is set to LTE_DL_AVAILABLE_PRB, the value ranges of the LtePrbStartIndex and LtePrbEndIndex parameters must be 0 to 21 and 28 to 49, respectively.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR or UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, and the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO under the Cell MO is set to LTE_DL_AVAILABLE_PRB, the value of the LtePrbEndIndex parameter must be greater than or equal to the value calculated by the following formula: LtePrbStartIndex + Number of RBs indicated by DlBandWidth x 0.66 - 1.
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR or UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, and the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO under the Cell MO is set to LTE_UL_AVAILABLE_PRB, the value ranges of the LtePrbStartIndex and LtePrbEndIndex parameters in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO must be 0 to 99.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR or UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, and the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO under the Cell MO is set to LTE_DL_AVAILABLE_PRB, the value ranges of the LtePrbStartIndex and LtePrbEndIndex parameters must be 0 to 8 and 16 to 24, respectively.
    • In a ULSpectrumShrConfig MO, the value of LtePrbEndIndex must be greater than or equal to (Value of LtePrbStartIndex + Number of RBs indicated in the value of DlBandWidth in the Cell MO x 0.56 - 1) when the following conditions are met: (a) The SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING. (b) The option UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is deselected. (c) The PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO is set to LTE_DL_HIGH_PRIORITY_PRB.
    • If the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, the UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW option of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch in the SpectrumCloud MO is not selected, and the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO is set to LTE_UL_AVAILABLE_PRB, then the value of the LtePrbEndIndex parameter must be greater than or equal to the value calculated using the following formula: Value of the LtePrbStartIndex parameter + Number of RBs indicated by the value of the UlBandWidth parameter * 0.76 - 1.
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR or UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, and the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO under the Cell MO is set to LTE_UL_AVAILABLE_PRB, the value ranges of the LtePrbStartIndex and LtePrbEndIndex parameters in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO must be 0 to 74.
    • If the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, the UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW option of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch in the SpectrumCloud MO is not selected, and the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO is set to LTE_UL_AVAILABLE_PRB, then the value of the LtePrbEndIndex parameter must be greater than or equal to the value calculated using the following formula: Value of the LtePrbStartIndex parameter + Number of RBs indicated by the value of the UlBandWidth parameter x 0.66 - 1.
    • In a ULSpectrumShrConfig, the LtePrbStartIndex parameter and the LtePrbEndIndex parameter must be set to a value less than 15 when the following conditions are met: (a) The UlBandWidth parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15. (b) The SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM or UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING and the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW. (c) The PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO is set to LTE_PUCCH_AVAILABLE_PRB.
    • In a ULSpectrumShrConfig, the LtePrbStartIndex parameter and the LtePrbEndIndex parameter must be set to a value less than 25 when the following conditions are met: (a) The UlBandWidth parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25. (b) The SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM or UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING and the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW. (c) The PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO is set to LTE_PUCCH_AVAILABLE_PRB.
    • In a ULSpectrumShrConfig, the LtePrbStartIndex parameter and the LtePrbEndIndex parameter must be set to a value less than 50 when the following conditions are met: (a) The UlBandWidth parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50. (b) The SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM or UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING and the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW. (c) The PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO is set to LTE_PUCCH_AVAILABLE_PRB.
    • In a ULSpectrumShrConfig, the LtePrbStartIndex parameter and the LtePrbEndIndex parameter must be set to a value less than 75 when the following conditions are met: (a) The UlBandWidth parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75. (b) The SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM or UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING and the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW. (c) The PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO is set to LTE_PUCCH_AVAILABLE_PRB.
    • In a ULSpectrumShrConfig, the LtePrbStartIndex parameter and the LtePrbEndIndex parameter must be set to a value less than 100 when the following conditions are met: (a) The UlBandWidth parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100. (b) The SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM or UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING and the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW. (c) The PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO is set to LTE_PUCCH_AVAILABLE_PRB.
    • If the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR (or the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING and the UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW option of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch in the SpectrumCloud MO is selected), and the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO is set to LTE_UL_AVAILABLE_PRB, then the value of the LtePrbEndIndex parameter must be greater than or equal to the value calculated using the following formula: Value of the LtePrbStartIndex parameter + Value obtained by rounding down the result of the number of RBs indicated by the value of the UlBandWidth parameter multiplied by 0.5.
    • If the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR (or the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING and the UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW option of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch in the SpectrumCloud MO is selected), and the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO is set to LTE_UL_AVAILABLE_PRB, then the value of the LtePrbEndIndex parameter must be greater than or equal to the value calculated using the following formula: Value of the LtePrbStartIndex parameter + Value obtained by rounding up the result of the number of RBs indicated by the value of the UlBandWidth parameter multiplied by 0.5 + 1.
