    • If the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR (or the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING and the UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW option of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch in the SpectrumCloud MO is selected), and the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO is set to LTE_UL_AVAILABLE_PRB, then the value of the LtePrbEndIndex parameter must be greater than or equal to the value calculated using the following formula: Value of the LtePrbStartIndex parameter + Value obtained by rounding up the result of the number of RBs indicated by the value of the UlBandWidth parameter multiplied by 0.5.
    • If the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR (or the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING and the UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW option of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch in the SpectrumCloud MO is selected), and the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO is set to LTE_UL_AVAILABLE_PRB, then the value of the LtePrbEndIndex parameter must be greater than or equal to the value calculated using the following formula: Value of the LtePrbStartIndex parameter + Value obtained by rounding up the result of the number of RBs indicated by the value of the UlBandWidth parameter multiplied by 0.5 + 1.
    • If the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR (or the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING and the UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW option of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch in the SpectrumCloud MO is selected), and the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO is set to LTE_UL_AVAILABLE_PRB, then the value of the LtePrbEndIndex parameter must be greater than or equal to the value calculated using the following formula: Value of the LtePrbStartIndex parameter + Value obtained by rounding up the result of the number of RBs indicated by the value of the UlBandWidth parameter multiplied by 0.5 + 1.
    • When the UlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR or UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, and the PrbRsvType parameter in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO under the Cell MO is set to LTE_UL_AVAILABLE_PRB, the value ranges of the LtePrbStartIndex and LtePrbEndIndex parameters in the ULSpectrumShrConfig MO must be 0 to 14.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch, SRSCfg, RACHCfg, SpectrumCloud, CellLteFlexBw, and ContigIntraBandCarr MOs:
    • The PUCCH_SRS_ADAPT_SWITCH option of the NonStandardBwAlgoSw parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected when all the following five conditions are true: The PucchSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected; the PrachFreqOffsetStrategy parameter in the RACHCfg MO is set to AUTOMATIC or FULLY_AUTOMATIC; the AnSrsSimuTrans parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE; the UlVmimoSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected; the CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CFG, and the CompactBwCtrlIntrfMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to a value other than NORMAL, or the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR or UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, or the LTE_FB_CFG_SWITCH option of the LteFlexBwSwitch parameter in the CellLteFlexBw MO is selected, or the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw parameter in the ContigIntraBandCarr MO is selected.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and DdCellGroup MOs:
    • When the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR, UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR, or LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, and the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, the cell corresponding to the SpectrumCloud MO must not be a cell in the DdCellGroup MO.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch, RACHCfg, PUCCHCfg, CellPdcchAlgo, SpectrumCloud, and ULZeroBufferZone MOs:
    • The PrachFreqOffset parameter in the RACHCfg MO must be properly set so that the PRACH RBs and PUCCH RBs do not overlap in the frequency domain under the following conditions: (a) The SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is not set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, or DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR, the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in the ULZEROBUFFERZONE MO is deselected, or the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in the ULZEROBUFFERZONE MO and the PucchFlexCfgSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are selected. (b) The NbCellFlag, FddTddInd, and UlBandWidth parameters in the Cell MO are set to FALSE, CELL_FDD, and CELL_BW_N15, respectively. (c) The PucchSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. (d) The PrachFreqOffsetStrategy parameter in the RACHCfg MO is not set to FULLY_AUTOMATIC.
    • The PrachFreqOffset parameter in the RACHCfg MO must be properly set so that the PRACH RBs and PUCCH RBs do not overlap in the frequency domain under the following conditions: (a) The SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is not set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, or DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR, the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in the ULZEROBUFFERZONE MO is deselected, or the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in the ULZEROBUFFERZONE MO and the PucchFlexCfgSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are selected. (b) The NbCellFlag, FddTddInd, and UlBandWidth parameters in the Cell MO are set to FALSE, CELL_FDD, and CELL_BW_N25, respectively. (c) The PucchSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected (d) The PrachFreqOffsetStrategy parameter in the RACHCfg MO is not set to FULLY_AUTOMATIC.
    • The PrachFreqOffset parameter in the RACHCfg MO must be properly set so that the PRACH RBs and PUCCH RBs do not overlap in the frequency domain under the following conditions: (a) The SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is not set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, or DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR, the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in the ULZEROBUFFERZONE MO is deselected, or the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in the ULZEROBUFFERZONE MO and the PucchFlexCfgSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are selected. (b) The NbCellFlag, FddTddInd, and UlBandWidth parameters in the Cell MO are set to FALSE, CELL_FDD, and CELL_BW_N50, respectively. (c) The PucchSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. (d) The PrachFreqOffsetStrategy parameter in the RACHCfg MO is not set to FULLY_AUTOMATIC.
    • The PrachFreqOffset parameter in the RACHCfg MO must be properly set so that the PRACH RBs and PUCCH RBs do not overlap in the frequency domain under the following conditions: (a) The SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is not set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, or DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR, the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in the ULZEROBUFFERZONE MO is deselected, or the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in the ULZEROBUFFERZONE MO and the PucchFlexCfgSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are selected. (b) The NbCellFlag, FddTddInd, and UlBandWidth parameters in the Cell MO are set to FALSE, CELL_FDD, and CELL_BW_N75, respectively. (c) The PucchSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. (d) The PrachFreqOffsetStrategy parameter in the RACHCfg MO is not set to FULLY_AUTOMATIC.
    • The PrachFreqOffset parameter in the RACHCfg MO must be properly set so that the PRACH RBs and PUCCH RBs do not overlap in the frequency domain under the following conditions: (a) The SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is not set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, or DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR, the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in the ULZEROBUFFERZONE MO is deselected, or the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in the ULZEROBUFFERZONE MO and the PucchFlexCfgSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are selected. (b) The NbCellFlag, FddTddInd, and UlBandWidth parameters in the Cell MO are set to FALSE, CELL_FDD, and CELL_BW_N100, respectively. (c) The PucchSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. (d) The PrachFreqOffsetStrategy parameter in the RACHCfg MO is not set to FULLY_AUTOMATIC.
  • Relation with the Prb and EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MOs:
    • If NbCellFlag and CellActiveState in a Cell MO are set to TRUE and CELL_ACTIVE, respectively, and the number of Prb MO instances under this Cell MO is greater than 1, then the number of EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MO instances (under this Cell MO) with the same SectorEqmGroupId parameter setting must be equal to the number of Prb MO instances under this Cell MO, and AutoConfigFlag must be set to the same value in these EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MO instances.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoExtSwitch and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • The ANT_SEL_SCHEDULING_OPT_SWITCH option of the AntennaSelectionAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO can be selected only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and no SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO:
    • If UlCoverageEnhancementSw in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO is set to ON, FddTddInd in the Cell MO must be set to CELL_FDD.
    • If UlCoverageEnhancementSw in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO is set to ON, HighSpeedFlag in the Cell MO must be set to LOW_SPEED.
    • If the UlCoverageEnhancementSw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO is set to ON, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO must be set to BOOLEAN_FALSE.
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to FALSE and the CellRadius parameter in the Cell MO is set to a value greater than 100000, the UlCoverageEnhancementSw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the UlCoverageEnhancementSw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO is set to ON, the TxRxModeparameter in the Cell MO must be set to 2T2R, 2T4R, or 4T4R.
    • If the FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the DlBandWidth parameter in this Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N6 or CELL_BW_N15, the LteInBandInterFreqDeploySw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO under this Cell MO cannot be set to ON.
    • If the INTRF_BASED_INTER_FREQ_HO_SW option of the HeavyLoadPrfmOptSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO is selected, the MultiRruCellFlag and MultiRruCellMode parameters in the Cell MO must be set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and MPRU_AGGREGATION, respectively.
  • Relation with the EuSectorEqmGroup, eUCellSectorEqm, and SECTOREQM MOs:
    • If the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to MPRU_AGGREGATION, SFN, or CELL_COMBINATION, a maximum of 96 RRU MOs can be used for the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the EuSectorEqmGroup associated with the Cell MO and for the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud, eUCellSectorEqm, and SECTOREQM MOs:
    • If the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM or DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR, or if the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING and the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in the same MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW, then the SECTORID parameters must be set to different values in the SECTOREQM MOs corresponding to the SectorEqmId parameters in the eUCellSectorEqm MOs.
  • Relation with the EnodebMpruEs and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • The TDOA_SWITCH option of the LbsSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected for cells with the DlEarfcn parameter in the Cell MO set to the same value as the MpruDormancyDlEarfcn parameter in the EnodebMpruEs MO.
  • Relation with the EnodebMpruEs, EuSectorEqmGroup, and SECTOREQM MOs:
    • If the Cell MO is associated with at least one EuSectorEqmGroup MO which is configured with more than 16 SECTOREQM MOs, the DlEarfcn parameter in the Cell MO cannot be set to the same value as the MpruDormancyDlEarfcn parameter in the EnodebMpruEs MO.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoExtSwitch and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • If the PUSCH_INTRF_RSRP_HIGH_THLD_SW option of the HeavyLoadPrfmOptSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO is selected, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO must be set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to MPRU_AGGREGATION, and the PuschRsrpHighThdSwitch option of the UlPcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
    • The CELL_CAPB_BASED_GBR_OPT_SW option of the WTTxAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO can be selected only when the BEST_EFFORT_GBR_SW option of the DlSchExtSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected and the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell, CellAlgoSwitch, and CellAlgoExtSwitch MOs:
    • The SPACE_DOMAIN_MEAS_SW option of the MeasOptAlgoExtSw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO can be selected only if all of the following conditions are met:
    • 1. The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD.
    • 2. The TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R.
    • 3. The SubframeAssignment parameter in the Cell MO is set to SA2.
    • 4. The MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE.
    • 5. The BfSwitch option of the BfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • 6. The SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is not configured.
    • 7. The MultiCellShareMode parameter in the Cell MO is not set to SPLIT.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and InterRatCellShutdown MOs:
    • When the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, ForceShutdownSwitch in the InterRatCellShutdown must be set OFF.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell, CellMMAlgo, CellAlgoSwitch, SRSCfg, and CellBf MOs:
    • The MaxBfRankPara parameter in the CellBf MO can be set to FOUR_LAYER_BF only when all of the following conditions are met: 1. The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD. 2. The TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R. 3. The SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is not configured. 4. The CrsPortNum parameter in the Cell MO is set to CRS_PORT_2. 5. The MM_ANT_SEL_ENH_SWITCH option of the MMAlgoOptSwitch parameter in the CellMMAlgo MO is selected. 6. The TM9Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected. 7. The SrsResExpansionSwitch parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to OFF.
  • Relation with the CellDlschAlgo, CellAuxEnbBind, and CellAuxDuBind MOs:
    • If the AdaptPilotOffPrbThld parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO for a cell is set to a value greater than 0, the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO for this cell must be set to CELL_FDD and the CellAuxEnbBind and CellAuxDuBind MOs cannot be added for this cell.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell, SectorSplitGroup, and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO is set to MODE_3, and the SectorSplitCell MO corresponding to the Cell MO exists, then the TM9Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO is set to NOT_CFG, then the BfSwitch option of the BfAlgoSwitch parameter, the MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter, and the TM9Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO is set to MODE_3, then the ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch option of the UlSuMimoAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the CellLaaCoexistPara MO:
    • When the FddTddInd and WorkMode parameters in a Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and LAA, the CoexistMode parameter in the CellLaaCoexistPara MO under this Cell MO must be set to ALWAYS_ON, LBT_TYPEA or LBT_TYPEB.
    • When the FddTddInd and WorkMode parameters in a Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and LAA, respectively, if CellActiveState in this Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the CellLaaCoexistPara MO related to this Cell must be configured in advance.
  • Relation with the CellRbReserve and CellMBMSCfg MOs:
    • If RbRsvMode in a CellRbReserve MO under a Cell MO is set to NB_DEPLOYMENT and the value of CellActiveState in this Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, the MBMSSwitch option of MBMSSwitch in the CellMBMSCfg MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the CellRbReserve and CellDlschAlgo MOs:
    • When the RbRsvMode parameter in a CellRbReserve MO is set to NB_DEPLOYMENT(NB_DEPLOYMENT), and the CellActiveState in the Cell MO associated with the CellRbReserve MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, all the options of the MbsfnSfCfg parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO under the same cell as the CellRbReserve MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the PCCHCfg and BcchCfg MOs:
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the value of the ModifyPeriodCoeffForNb parameter in the BcchCfg MO multiplied by the value of the DefaultPagingCycleForNb parameter in the PCCHCfg MO must be an integer multiple of 40.96 seconds.
  • Relation with the CellOp and CnOperatorTa MOs:
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the NbIotTaFlag parameter in the CnOperatorTa MO for the TrackingAreaId parameter in the CellOp MO must be BOOLEAN_TRUE(BOOLEAN_TRUE).
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, the NbIotTaFlag parameter in the CnOperatorTa MO for the TrackingAreaId parameter in the CellOp MO must be BOOLEAN_FALSE(BOOLEAN_FALSE).
  • Relation with the CellResel MO:
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the values of the SIntraSearchCfgInd and SNonIntraSearchCfgInd parameters in the CellResel MO must be CFG(Configure).
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO that corresponds to LocalCellId in a CellResel MO is set to FALSE, the value range of QRxLevMin parameter in the CellResel MO is from -70 to -22.
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO that corresponds to LocalCellId in a CellResel MO is set to FALSE, the value range of SIntraSearch parameter in the CellResel MO is from 0 to 31.
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the SpeedDepReselCfgInd, MeasBandWidthCfgInd, SpeedStateSfCfgInd, and ThrshServLowQCfgInd parameters in the CellResel MO must be set to NOT_CFG.
  • Relation with the CellSel MO:
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the QQualMin parameter in the CellSel MO must not be set to 0.
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO that corresponds to LocalCellId in a CellSel MO is set to FALSE, the value range of QRxLevMin parameter in the CellSel MO is from -70 to -22.
  • Relation with the CellCqiAdjAlgo MO:
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the PUSCH_CQI_FALSE_DETECTION_SW option of the CqiOptSwitch in the CellCqiAdjAlgo MO must be deselected.
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the PUCCH_CQI_FALSE_DETECTION_SW option of the CqiOptSwitch in the CellCqiAdjAlgo MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the CellMBMSCfg MO:
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the MBMSSwitch option of the MBMSSwitch in the CellMBMSCfg MO must be deselected.
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the SERVICECONTINUITYSWITCH option of the MBMSServiceSwitch in the CellMBMSCfg MO must be deselected.
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the NEIGHCELLCONFIGADAPTIVESWITCH option of the NCellMbmsCfgSwitch in the CellMBMSCfg MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the CellPucchAlgo MO:
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the SriAdaptiveHoldForVoIPSW option of the SriAlgoSwitch in the CellPucchAlgo MO must be deselected.
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the SriOffsetReConfigForDrxSW option of the SriAlgoSwitch in the CellPucchAlgo MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the CellEmtcAlgo and CellMBMSCfg MOs:
    • Under the same cell, when the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to FALSE and the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellEmtcAlgo MO is selected, the MBMSSwitch parameter in a CellMBMSCfg MO must not be set to MBMSSwitch-1.
  • Relation with the CellLowPower MO:
    • If NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, LowPwrSwitch in the CellLowPower must be set OFF.
  • Relation with the CellRbReserve and CellLowPower MOs:
    • When the RbRsvMode parameter in a CellRbReserve MO is set to NB_DEPLOYMENT(NB_DEPLOYMENT), the LowPwrSwitch parameter in the CellLowPower MO under the same cell as the CellRbReserve MO must be set to Off.
  • Relation with the EutranNFreqRanShare MO:
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the CellReselPriorityCfgInd parameter in an EutranNFreqRanShare MO must be set to NOT_CFG.
  • Relation with the CaGroupSCellCfg MO:
    • The NbCellFlag in the Cell MO corresponding to the LocalCellId in the CaGroupSCellCfg MO must be set to FALSE.
  • Relation with the CaGroupSCellCfg and eNodeBFunction MOs:
    • If SCelleNodeBId in a CaGroupSCellCfg MO is equal to eNodeBId in the eNodeBFunction MO, the NbCellFlag in the Cell MO corresponding to the SCellLocalCellId in the CaGroupSCellCfg MO must be set to FALSE.
  • Relation with the GlobalProcSwitch and CellEmtcAlgo MOs:
    • Under the same eNodeB, when the NbCellFlag parameter in any Cell MO is set to FALSE and the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellEmtcAlgo MO is selected, the QciParaEffectFlag parameter in the GlobalProcSwitch MO must be set to ON.
  • Relation with the CellRbReserve, CellEmtcAlgo, and CellAlgoExtSwitch MOs:
    • EMTC_SWITCH of EmtcAlgoSwitch in CellEmtcAlgo under a Cell MO cannot be selected when all conditions in any of the following condition sets are met: (1) FddTddInd and DlBandWidth in the Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and CELL_BW_N25 respectively, RbRsvType, RbRsvMode, and RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex in a CellRbReserve MO are set to DOWNLINK_MODE, NB_RESERVED or NB_DEPLOYMENT, and a value in the range of 0 to 5 or 19 to 24, respectively. (2) FddTddInd and DlBandWidth are set to CELL_FDD and CELL_BW_N50, respectively, LteInBandInterFreqDeploySw in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO is set to OFF, RbRsvType, RbRsvMode, and RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex in a CellRbReserve MO are set to DOWNLINK_MODE, NB_RESERVED or NB_DEPLOYMENT, and a value in the range of 1 to 18 or 31 to 48, respectively. (3) FddTddInd and DlBandWidth are set to CELL_FDD and CELL_BW_N75, respectively, LteInBandInterFreqDeploySw in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO is set to OFF, RbRsvType, RbRsvMode, and RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex in a CellRbReserve MO are set to DOWNLINK_MODE, NB_RESERVED or NB_DEPLOYMENT, and a value in the range of 1 to 30 or 44 to 73, respectively. (4) FddTddInd and DlBandWidth are set to CELL_FDD and CELL_BW_N100, respectively, LteInBandInterFreqDeploySw in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO is set to OFF, RbRsvType, RbRsvMode, and RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex in a CellRbReserve MO are set to DOWNLINK_MODE, NB_RESERVED or NB_DEPLOYMENT, and a value in the range of 2 to 43 or 56 to 97, respectively.
  • Relation with the CellFrameOffset and ENodeBFrameOffset MOs:
    • The TddFrameOffset parameter in an ENodeBFrameOffset MO must be set to 0 when the following conditions are met: The CellActiveState, FddTddInd, and WorkMode parameters in the associated Cell MO are set to CELL_ACTIVE, CELL_TDD, and LAA, respectively, and the CellFrameOffset MO under this Cell MO has not been configured.
  • Relation with the CellQciPara MO:
    • For extended QCI sets of 10 to 64, 67 to 68, and 71 to 254, the sum of QCIs with QciAmbrLimitFlag in a CellQciPara MO under a Cell MO not set to NORMAL must be less than or equal to 5.
    • A maximum of 75 CellQciPara MOs can be configured for each Cell MO.
    • The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO must be set to CELL_TDD when the ADMISSION_FORBIDDEN option of the QciAlgoSwitch parameter in the CELLQCIPARA MO is selected.
  • Relation with the CellEmtcAlgo, CellCeSchCfg, and CellCeCfg MOs:
    • Under the same cell, the SIB1RepNum parameter in the CellCeSchCfg MO corresponding to the CoverageLevel parameter in the CellCeCfg MO must be set to SIB1_NUM_R4(SIB1_NUM_R4) when the following conditions are true: The FddTddInd and UlBandWidth or DlBandWidth parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD(FDD) and CELL_BW_N6(1.4M) or CELL_BW_N15(3M), respectively; the EMTC_ONLY_CELL_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO is selected.
  • Relation with the PrbSchConfig, PrbPdcchCeConfig, PrbDlGapConfig, and CellRachCECfg MOs:
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and the DL_GAP_SWITCH option of the DlSchedulingSwitch parameter in the PrbSchConfig MO is selected, parameter adjustment is allowed for the coverage level for which the PdcchMaxRepetitionCnt parameter value is greater than or equal to the DlGapThreshold parameter value only when the following condition is met: The rounded-up result of (DlGapPeriod x DlGapDurationCoeff/(PdcchMaxRepetitionCnt x PdcchPeriodFactor) + 3) is less than the value of ContentionResolutionTimer and RaResponseWindowSize.
  • Relation with the RACHCfg and PrbRachCeConfig MOs:
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE and the PrachStartTimeCfgInd parameter in the RACHCfg MO is set to CFG, the value of the PrachTransmissionPeriod parameter with a specific coverage level must not be lower than the value of the PrachStartTime parameter with the same coverage level in the PrbRachCeConfig MO associated with the same LocalCellId.
  • Relation with the CellNprsCfg MO:
    • A maximum of two CellNprsCfg MO instances can be configured for a Cell MO.
  • Relation with the PrbSchConfig, PrbPdcchCeConfig, and PrbDlGapConfig MOs:
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell is set to TRUE, the DL_GAP_SWITCH option of the DlSchedulingSwitch parameter in the PrbSchConfig MO can be selected for a PRB in the cell only if the PRB meets the following condition: The PdcchPeriodFactor parameter in the PrbPdcchCeConfig MO is set to a value greater than G_4 for the coverage level for which the PdcchMaxRepetitionCnt parameter in the PrbPdcchCeConfig MO is set to a value greater than or equal to the value of the DlGapThreshold parameter in the PrbDlGapConfig MO or the PdcchMaxRepetitionCnt parameter less than the DlGapThreshold parameter corresponding to all the coverage levels.
  • Relation with the CaMgtCfg MO:
    • If the CaDataAllocEnhancementSwitch option of the CellCaAlgoSwitch parameter in the CaMgtCfg MO under a Cell MO is selected, the WorkMode parameter in this Cell MO must be set to LAA.
    • The CaUlSmartSelectionSwitch option of the CellCaAlgoSwitch parameter in the CaMgtCfg MO can be selected only if the following conditions are met: 1. The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD. 2. The TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R.
    • When the DlMassiveCaSwitch option of the CellCaAlgoSwitch parameter is selected or the DisCloudBBCaMaxCcNum, FddTddCaDlMaxCcNum, RelaxedBackhaulCaMaxCcNum, or FTRelaxedBHCaDLMaxCcNum parameter is set to DL_MASSIVE_CA in a CaMgtCfg MO, the FddTddInd parameter in the associated Cell MO must be set to CELL_FDD.
    • LocalCellId in a CaMgtCfg MO must have been set in the associated Cell MO.
  • Relation with the NbCellMultiPrbConfig and CellUlschAlgo MOs:
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to TRUE, and the NbUlSubcarrierSpacingType parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO is set to 3dot75K or HYBRID_MODE, the NbRfCarrierStrategy parameter in the NbCellMultiPrbConfig MO cannot be set to SHARE.
  • Relation with the NbCellMultiPrbConfig and Prb MOs:
    • When the number of Prb instances in Cell is greater than 2, the NbRfCarrierStrategy parameter of the NbCellMultiPrbConfig in the same cell cannot be set to SEPARATE.
  • Relation with the CellRbReserve and CellAlgoExtSwitch MOs:
    • The LteInBandInterFreqDeploySw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO under a Cell MO cannot be set to ON when all of the following conditions are met: (a) The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD. (b) The DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50. (c) The RbRsvType parameter in a CellRbReserve MO under the Cell MO is set to DOWNLINK_MODE. (d) The RbRsvMode parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to NB_RESERVED or NB_DEPLOYMENT. (e) The RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to a value in the range of 28 to 48.
    • The LteInBandInterFreqDeploySw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO under a Cell MO cannot be set to ON when all of the following conditions are met: (a) The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD. (b) The DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75. (c) The RbRsvType parameter in a CellRbReserve MO under the Cell MO is set to DOWNLINK_MODE. (d) The RbRsvMode parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to NB_RESERVED or NB_DEPLOYMENT. (e) The RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to a value in the range of 50 to 73.
    • The LteInBandInterFreqDeploySw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO under a Cell MO cannot be set to ON when all of the following conditions are met: (a) The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD. (b) The DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100. (c) The RbRsvType parameter in a CellRbReserve MO under the Cell MO is set to DOWNLINK_MODE. (d) The RbRsvMode parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to NB_RESERVED or NB_DEPLOYMENT. (e) The RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to a value in the range of 74 to 97.
    • The LteInBandInterFreqDeploySw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO under a Cell MO cannot be set to ON when all of the following conditions are met: (a) The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD. (b) The DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50. (c) The RbRsvType parameter in a CellRbReserve MO under the Cell MO is set to DOWNLINK_MODE. (d) The RbRsvMode parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to NB_RESERVED or NB_DEPLOYMENT. (e) The value of the PhyCellId parameter in the Cell MO modulo 6 is 0 or 3 and the RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to a value in the range of 1 to 6, the value of the PhyCellId parameter in the Cell MO modulo 6 is 1 or 4 and the RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to a value in the range of 7 to 12, or the value of the PhyCellId parameter in the Cell MO modulo 6 is 2 or 5 and the RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to a value in the range of 13 to 18.
    • The LteInBandInterFreqDeploySw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO under a Cell MO cannot be set to ON when all of the following conditions are met: (a) The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD. (b) The DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75. (c) The RbRsvType parameter in a CellRbReserve MO under the Cell MO is set to DOWNLINK_MODE. (d) The RbRsvMode parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to NB_RESERVED or NB_DEPLOYMENT. (e) The value of the PhyCellId parameter in the Cell MO modulo 10 is 0, 4, 6, or 8 and the RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to a value in the range of 1 to 6, the value of the PhyCellId parameter in the Cell MO modulo 10 is 1, 5, 7, or 9 and the RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to a value in the range of 7 to 12, the value of the PhyCellId parameter in the Cell MO modulo 10 is 0, 2, 6, or 8 and the RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to a value in the range of 13 to 18, the value of the PhyCellId parameter in the Cell MO modulo 10 is 1, 3, 7, or 9 and the RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to a value in the range of 19 to 24, the value of the PhyCellId parameter in the Cell MO modulo 10 is 0, 2, 4, or 8 and the RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to a value in the range of 25 to 30, or the value of the PhyCellId parameter in the Cell MO modulo 10 is 1, 3, 5, or 9 and the RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to a value in the range of 44 to 49.
    • LteInBandInterFreqDeploySw in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO under a Cell MO cannot be set to ON when all of the following conditions are met: (a) FddTddInd in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD. (b) DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100. (c) RbRsvType in a CellRbReserve MO under the Cell MO is set to DOWNLINK_MODE. (d) RbRsvMode in the CellRbReserve MO is set to NB_RESERVED or NB_DEPLOYMENT. (e) The value of PhyCellId in the Cell MO modulo 14 is 0, 5, 8, or 11 and RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex in the CellRbReserve MO is 2~7, the value of PhyCellId modulo 14 is 1, 6, 9, or 12 and RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex is 8~13, the value of PhyCellId modulo 14 is 2, 7, 10, or 13 and RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex is 14~19, the value of PhyCellId modulo 14 is 0, 3, 8, or 11 and the RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex is 20~25, the value of PhyCellId modulo 14 is 1, 4, 9, or 12 and RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex is 26~31, the value of PhyCellId modulo 14 is 2, 5, 10, or 13 and RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex is 32~37, the value of PhyCellId modulo 14 is 0, 3, 6, or 11 and RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex is 38~43, the value of PhyCellId modulo 14 is 1, 4, 7, or 12 and RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex is 56~61, the value of PhyCellId modulo 14 is 2, 5, 8, or 13 and RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex is 62~67, or the value of PhyCellId modulo 14 is 0, 3, 6, or 9 and RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex is 68~73.
    • The LteInBandInterFreqDeploySw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO under a Cell MO cannot be set to ON when all of the following conditions are met: (a) The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD. (b) The DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50. (c) The RbRsvType parameter in some CellRbReserve MOs under the Cell MO is set to DOWNLINK_MODE. (d) The RbRsvMode parameter in these CellRbReserve MOs is set to NB_RESERVED or NB_DEPLOYMENT. (e) The values of the RbRsvStartIndex and RbRsvEndIndex parameters in these CellRbReserve MOs fall into two or more ranges of 1 to 6, 7 to 12, and 13 to 18.
    • The LteInBandInterFreqDeploySw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO under a Cell MO cannot be set to ON when all of the following conditions are met: (a) The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD. (b) The DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N75. (c) The RbRsvType parameter in some CellRbReserve MOs under the Cell MO is set to DOWNLINK_MODE. (d) The RbRsvMode parameter in these CellRbReserve MOs is set to NB_RESERVED or NB_DEPLOYMENT. (e) The values of the RbRsvStartIndex and RbRsvEndIndex parameters in these CellRbReserve MOs fall into two or more ranges of 1 to 6, 7 to 12, 13 to 18, 19 to 24, 25 to 30, and 44 to 49.
    • The LteInBandInterFreqDeploySw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO under a Cell MO cannot be set to ON when all of the following conditions are met: (a) The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD. (b) The DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N100. (c) The RbRsvType parameter in some CellRbReserve MOs under the Cell MO is set to DOWNLINK_MODE. (d) The RbRsvMode parameter in these CellRbReserve MOs is set to NB_RESERVED or NB_DEPLOYMENT. (e) The values of the RbRsvStartIndex and RbRsvEndIndex parameters in these CellRbReserve MOs fall into two or more ranges of 2 to 7, 8 to 13, 14 to 19, 20 to 25, 26 to 31, 32 to 37, 38 to 43, 56 to 61, 62 to 67, and 68 to 73.
    • The LteInBandInterFreqDeploySw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO under a Cell MO cannot be set to ON when all the following conditions are met: (a) The FddTddInd and DlBandWidth parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and CELL_BW_N75, respectively. (b) The RbRsvType and RbRsvMode parameters in a CellRbReserve MO under the Cell MO are set to DOWNLINK_MODE and NB_RESERVED (or NB_DEPLOYMENT), respectively. (c) The value of the PhyCellId parameter in the Cell MO modulo 10 is 0, 2, 4, 6, 7, or 8 and the RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to a value in the range of 44 to 49, or the value of the PhyCellId parameter in the Cell MO modulo 10 is 1, 3, 5, or 9 and the RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to a value in the range of 25 to 30.
    • The LteInBandInterFreqDeploySw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO under a Cell MO cannot be set to ON when all the following conditions are met: (a) The FddTddInd and DlBandWidth parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and CELL_BW_N100, respectively. (b) The RbRsvType and RbRsvMode parameters in a CellRbReserve MO under the Cell MO are set to DOWNLINK_MODE and NB_RESERVED (or NB_DEPLOYMENT), respectively. (c) The value of the PhyCellId parameter in the Cell MO modulo 14 is 0, 1, 3, 6, 7, 9, 10, 11, or 12 and the RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to a value in the range of 62 to 67, or the value of the PhyCellId parameter in the Cell MO modulo 14 is 2, 4, 5, 8, or 13 and the RbRsvStartIndex or RbRsvEndIndex parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to a value in the range of 56 to 61.
  • Relation with the CellEdtConfig and PrbRachCeConfig MOs:
    • The EDT_RANDOM_ACCESS_SW option of the PrachAlgoSw parameter in the PrbRachCeConfig MO can be selected for a cell only when the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to TRUE and the EDT_SWITCH option of the EdtAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEdtConfig MO is selected for the cell.
  • Relation with the CellEdtConfig and PrbUlSchCeAlgo MOs:
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the EdtMtSchToneNum parameter in the CellEdtConfig MO under this Cell MO is not set to TONE_1, and the CoverageLevel parameter in a corresponding PrbUlSchCeAlgo MO is set to COVERAGE_LEVEL_0, then the EdtUlTransRptCount parameter in this PrbUlSchCeAlgo MO must be set to REP_1, REP_2 or REP_4.
  • Relation with the CellEdtConfig and CellRachCECfg MOs:
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to TRUE and the EdtEarlyContentionResMode parameter in the CellEdtConfig MO is set to OFF for a cell, the EdtContentionResTimer parameter in the CellRachCECfg MO must be set to PP_64 for the cell.
  • Relation with the CellEdtConfig MO:
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to TRUE and the EdtEarlyContentionResMode parameter in the CellEdtConfig MO is set to OFF for a cell, the EdtDlDataWaitingTimer parameter in the CellEdtConfig MO must be set to a value less than or equal to 5 for the cell.
  • Relation with the CellUSParaCfg and CellPdcchAlgo MOs:
    • If UsPdcchOptSwitch of UsAlgoSwitch in CellUSParaCfg is set on and FddTddInd in Cell is set to CELL_TDD, PdcchSparePowerAllocStrage in CellPdcchAlgo must be set to NONE.
  • Relation with the TimeAlignmentTimer MO:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is not set to CELL_FDD, the TaEnhancePucch option of the TaEnhance parameter in the TimeAlignmentTimer MO must be deselected.
    • If the HighSpeedFlag parameter in a Cell MO is not set to LOW_SPEED, the TaEnhancePucch option of the TaEnhance parameter in the TimeAlignmentTimer MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the TA_SEARCH_WINDOW_SIZE_OPT_SW and TA_DETECT_OPT_SW options of the TaMeasOptSwitch parameter in the TimeAlignmentTimer MO under this Cell MO cannot be both selected.
  • Relation with the CellEmergencyArea MO:
    • A maximum of 16 CellEmergencyArea MOs can be configured for each cell.
  • Relation with the PDSCHCfg and CellCspcPara MOs:
    • The CelleCspcSwitch parameter in the CellCspcPara MO can be set to ON only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the EmfPowerLimitSwitch parameter in the PDSCHCfg MO is deselected.
  • Relation with the SRSCfg and NsaDcMgmtConfig MOs:
    • The NSA_DC_UL_PATH_SELECTION_SW option of the NsaDcAlgoSwitch parameter in the NsaDcMgmtConfig MO can be selected only when the SrsCfgInd parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to ON and the HighSpeedFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to LOW_SPEED.
  • Relation with the UeCooperationPara MO:
    • In TDD, the Tm9Port5Switch option of the SpecUserCooperationSwitch parameter in the UeCooperationPara MO can be selected only when the SpecUeIdentifySwitch option of the same parameter is selected.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch and UeCooperationPara MOs:
    • The Tm9Port5Switch option of the SpecUserCooperationSwitch parameter in the UeCooperationPara MO can be selected only when all of the following conditions are met: 1. The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD. 2. The TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R. 3. The TM9Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected. 4. The SpecUeIdentifySwitch option of the SpecUserCooperationSwitch parameter in the UeCooperationPara MO is selected.
  • Relation with the CellMMAlgo, CellAlgoSwitch, ENodeBAlgoSwitch, and UeCooperationPara MOs:
    • The MM_ANT_SEL_ENH_SWITCH option of the MMAlgoOptSwitch parameter in the CellMMAlgo MO can be selected only if the FddTddInd and TxRxMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and 64T64R or 32T32R respectively, the BfSwitch option of the BfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the UeSRSAntSelectCtrlSwitch option of the CompatibilityCtrlSwitch parameter in the ENODEBALGOSWITCH MO is deselected, the SpecUeIdentifySwitch option of the SpecUserCooperationSwitch parameter in the UECOOPERATIONPARA MO is selected.
  • Relation with the RACHCfg and CellEmtcAlgo MOs:
    • If the DlBandWidth or UlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N6(1.4M) and the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO is selected, the EmtcPrachPeriod parameter in the RACHCfg MO must be set to MS20.
  • Relation with the Prb and CellRbReserve MOs:
    • When the DeployMode and WorkMode parameters in a Prb MO are set to IN_BAND and UL_DL, respectively, the following conditions must be met when you activate the NB-IoT cell identified by the LocalCellId parameter in the Prb MO:
    • The cell identified by the LteCellId parameter must be configured with a CellRbReserve MO with RbRsvMode set to NB_DEPLOYMENT and RbRsvType set to DOWNLINK_MODE and a CellRbReserve MO with RbRsvMode set to NB_DEPLOYMENT and RbRsvType set to UPLINK_MODE.
    • If the LteDlRbIndex is set to an invalid value, the frequency value calculated based on the DlEarfcn and DlFreqOffset parameter settings must be within the frequency value set calculated based on the value of the RbRsvStartIndex parameter in a CellRbReserve MO instance of the cell identified by the LteCellId parameter. In addition, the RbRsvType and RbRsvMode parameters in the CellRbReserve MO instance must be set to DOWNLINK_MODE and NB_DEPLOYMENT, respectively. The frequency value calculation must comply with 3GPP TS 36.101. Moreover:
    • If the UlEarfcnCfgInd parameter is set to CFG, the LteUlRbIndex is set to an invalid value, the frequency value calculated based on the UlEarfcn and UlFreqOffset parameter settings must be within the frequency value set calculated based on the value of the RbRsvStartIndex parameter in a CellRbReserve MO instance of the cell identified by the LteCellId parameter. In addition, the RbRsvType and RbRsvMode parameters in the CellRbReserve MO instance must be set to UPLINK_MODE and NB_DEPLOYMENT, respectively. The frequency value calculation must comply with 3GPP TS 36.101.
    • When the DeployMode and WorkMode parameters in a Prb MO are set to IN_BAND and UL_DL, respectively, the following conditions must be met when you activate the NB-IoT cell identified by the LocalCellId parameter in the Prb MO:
    • The cell identified by the LteCellId parameter must be configured with a CellRbReserve MO with RbRsvMode set to NB_DEPLOYMENT and RbRsvType set to DOWNLINK_MODE and a CellRbReserve MO with RbRsvMode set to NB_DEPLOYMENT and RbRsvType set to UPLINK_MODE.
    • If the LteDlRbIndex is set to an invalid value, the frequency value calculated based on the DlEarfcn and DlFreqOffset parameter settings must be within the frequency value set calculated based on the value of the RbRsvStartIndex parameter in a CellRbReserve MO instance of the cell identified by the LteCellId parameter. In addition, the RbRsvType and RbRsvMode parameters in the CellRbReserve MO instance must be set to DOWNLINK_MODE and NB_DEPLOYMENT, respectively. The frequency value calculation must comply with 3GPP TS 36.101. Moreover:
    • If the UlEarfcnCfgInd parameter is set to CFG, the LteUlRbIndex is set to an invalid value, the frequency value calculated based on the UlEarfcn and UlFreqOffset parameter settings must be within the frequency value set calculated based on the value of the RbRsvStartIndex parameter in a CellRbReserve MO instance of the cell identified by the LteCellId parameter. In addition, the RbRsvType and RbRsvMode parameters in the CellRbReserve MO instance must be set to UPLINK_MODE and NB_DEPLOYMENT, respectively. The frequency value calculation must comply with 3GPP TS 36.101.
    • If the DeployMode and WorkMode parameters in a Prb MO are set to IN_BAND and UL_ONLY, respectively, the following conditions must be met when you activate the NB-IoT cell identified by the LocalCellId parameter in the Prb MO:
    • The cell identified by the LteCellId parameter must be configured with a CellRbReserve MO with RbRsvMode set to NB_DEPLOYMENT and RbRsvType set to UPLINK_MODE.
    • In addition, if the UlEarfcnCfgInd parameter is set to CFG and the LteUlRbIndex parameter is set to an invalid value, the frequency value calculated based on the values of the UlEarfcn and UlFreqOffset parameters in the Prb MO must be within the frequency value set calculated based on the value of the RbRsvStartIndex parameter in a CellRbReserve MO of the cell identified by the LteCellId parameter. In addition, the RbRsvType and RbRsvMode parameters in the CellRbReserve MO must be set to UPLINK_MODE and NB_DEPLOYMENT, respectively. The frequency value calculation must comply with 3GPP TS 36.101.
    • When the DeployMode and WorkMode parameters in a Prb MO are set to IN_BAND and DL_ONLY, respectively, the following conditions must be met when you activate the NB-IoT cell identified by the LocalCellId parameter in the Prb MO:
    • The cell identified by the LteCellId parameter must be configured with a CellRbReserve MO with RbRsvMode set to NB_DEPLOYMENT and RbRsvType set to DOWNLINK_MODE.
    • If the LteDlRbIndex is set to an invalid value, the frequency value calculated based on the DlEarfcn and DlFreqOffset parameter settings must be within the frequency value set calculated based on the value of the RbRsvStartIndex parameter in a CellRbReserve MO instance of the cell identified by the LteCellId parameter. In addition, the RbRsvType and RbRsvMode parameters in the CellRbReserve MO instance must be set to DOWNLINK_MODE and NB_DEPLOYMENT, respectively. The frequency value calculation must comply with 3GPP TS 36.101.
  • Relation with the Prb and CellEmtcAlgo MOs:
    • If DlBandWidth in the LTE Cell MO associated with LteCellId in the Prb MO is set to CELL_BW_N25, the EMTC_SWITCH option of EmtcAlgoSwitch in the CellEmtcAlgo MO is selected, and CellActiveState in the corresponding NB-IoT Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, then the number of Prb MO instances (under the NB-IoT Cell MO) with DeployMode set to IN_BAND and WorkMode set to UL_DL or DL_ONLY cannot exceed three.
    • If CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd in the LTE Cell MO associated with LteCellId in the Prb MO is set to CFG, DlBandWidth in the LTE Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50, the EMTC_SWITCH option of EmtcAlgoSwitch in the CellEmtcAlgo MO is selected, and CellActiveState in the corresponding NB-IoT Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, then the number of Prb MO instances (under the NB-IoT Cell MO) with DeployMode set to IN_BAND and WorkMode set to UL_DL or DL_ONLY cannot exceed three.
  • Relation with the Prb and CellLteFlexBw MOs:
    • If LTE_FB_CFG_SWITCH of LteFlexBwSwitch in the CellLteFlexBw MO under the LTE Cell MO associated with LteCellId in the Prb MO is selected, the absolute value of the difference between DlCustStartPrbIndex and DlCustEndPrbIndex is greater than or equal to 25 and less than 50, and CellActiveState in the corresponding NB-IoT Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, then the number of Prb MO instances (under the NB-IoT Cell MO) with DeployMode set to IN_BAND and WorkMode set to UL_DL or DL_ONLY cannot exceed four.
    • If LTE_FB_CFG_SWITCH of LteFlexBwSwitch in the CellLteFlexBw MO under the LTE Cell MO associated with LteCellId in the Prb MO is selected, the absolute value of the difference between UlCustStartPrbIndex and UlCustEndPrbIndex is greater than or equal to 25 and less than 50, and CellActiveState in the corresponding NB-IoT Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, then the number of Prb MO instances with DeployMode set to IN_BAND and WorkMode set to UL_DL or UL_ONLY cannot exceed four.
  • Relation with the Prb, CellLteFlexBw, and CellEmtcAlgo MOs:
    • If LTE_FB_CFG_SWITCH of LteFlexBwSwitch in the CellLteFlexBw MO under the LTE Cell MO associated with LteCellId in the Prb MO is selected, the absolute value of the difference between DlCustStartPrbIndex and DlCustEndPrbIndex is greater than or equal to 25 and less than 50, EMTC_SWITCH of EmtcAlgoSwitch in the CellEmtcAlgo MO is selected, and CellActiveState in the corresponding NB-IoT Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, then the number of Prb MO instances (under the NB-IoT Cell MO) with DeployMode set to IN_BAND and WorkMode set to UL_DL or DL_ONLY cannot exceed three.
  • Relation with the Prb and SpectrumCloud MOs:
    • If SpectrumCloudSwitch in the SpectrumCloud MO under the LTE Cell MO associated with LteCellId in the Prb MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, DlBandWidth in the LTE Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50, and CellActiveState in the corresponding NB-IoT Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, then the number of Prb MO instances (under the NB-IoT Cell MO) with DeployMode set to IN_BAND and WorkMode set to UL_DL or DL_ONLY cannot exceed four.
    • If SpectrumCloudSwitch in the SpectrumCloud MO under the LTE Cell MO associated with LteCellId in the Prb MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, UlBandWidth in the LTE Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50, and CellActiveState in the corresponding NB-IoT Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, then the number of Prb MO instances (under the NB-IoT Cell MO) with DeployMode set to IN_BAND and WorkMode set to UL_DL or UL_ONLY cannot exceed four.
    • If SpectrumCloudSwitch in the SpectrumCloud MO under the LTE Cell MO associated with LteCellId in the Prb MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, DlBandWidth in the LTE Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50, and CellActiveState in the corresponding NB-IoT Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, then the number of Prb MO instances (under the NB-IoT Cell MO) with DeployMode set to IN_BAND and WorkMode set to UL_DL or DL_ONLY cannot exceed four.
    • If SpectrumCloudSwitch in the SpectrumCloud MO under the LTE Cell MO associated with LteCellId in the Prb MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, UlBandWidth in the LTE Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50, and CellActiveState in the corresponding NB-IoT Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, then the number of Prb MO instances (under the NB-IoT Cell MO) with DeployMode set to IN_BAND and WorkMode set to UL_DL or UL_ONLY cannot exceed four.
  • Relation with the Prb, SpectrumCloud, and CellEmtcAlgo MOs:
    • If SpectrumCloudSwitch in the SpectrumCloud MO under the LTE Cell MO associated with LteCellId in the Prb MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, DlBandWidth in the LTE Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50, EMTC_SWITCH of EmtcAlgoSwitch in the CellEmtcAlgo MO is selected, and CellActiveState in the corresponding NB-IoT Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, then the number of Prb MO instances (under the NB-IoT Cell MO) with DeployMode set to IN_BAND and WorkMode set to UL_DL or DL_ONLY cannot exceed three.
    • If SpectrumCloudSwitch in the SpectrumCloud MO under the LTE Cell MO associated with LteCellId in the Prb MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, DlBandWidth in the LTE Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50, the EMTC_SWITCH option of EmtcAlgoSwitch in the CellEmtcAlgo MO is selected, and CellActiveState in the corresponding NB-IoT Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, then the number of Prb MO instances (under the NB-IoT Cell MO) with DeployMode set to IN_BAND and WorkMode set to UL_DL or DL_ONLY cannot exceed three.
  • Relation with the Prb, CellLteFlexBw, and SpectrumCloud MOs:
    • If DlBandWidth in the LTE Cell MO associated with LteCellId in the Prb MO is set to CELL_BW_N25 and CellActiveState in the corresponding NB-IoT Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, the number of Prb MO instances (under the NB-IoT Cell MO) with DeployMode set to IN_BAND cannot exceed two when any of the following conditions is met:
    • The LTE_FB_CFG_SWITCH option of the LteFlexBwSwitch parameter in the CellLteFlexBw MO under the LTE Cell MO is selected.
    • The SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY.
    • The SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING.
    • The CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CFG.
  • Relation with the SRSCfg, CellAlgoSwitch, SectorSplitGroup, and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • When the PATTERN_ADJ_SWITCH option of the SectorSplitSwitch parameter in a SectorSplitGroup MO is selected, and the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MOs corresponding to all SectorSplitCell MOs under this SectorSplitGroup MO is set to CELL_FDD, all the following requirements must be met:
    • (a) The SrsCfgInd parameter in the SRSCfg MOs corresponding to all SectorSplitCell MOs under this SectorSplitGroup MO must be set to BOOLEAN_TRUE.
    • (b) The FddSrsCfgMode parameter in the SRSCfg MOs corresponding to all SectorSplitCell MOs under this SectorSplitGroup MO must be set to Default Mode.
    • (c) The SrsSubframeCfg parameter in the SRSCfg MOs corresponding to all SectorSplitCell MOs under this SectorSplitGroup MO must be set to the same value.
    • (d) The SrsSubframeRecfSwitch option of the SrsAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MOs corresponding to all SectorSplitCell MOs under this SectorSplitGroup MO must be set to the same value.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch and CellMMAlgo MOs:
    • The MM_ACCESS_CONTROL_OPT_SWITCH option of the MMAlgoOptSwitch parameter in the CellMMAlgo MO can be selected only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and the AcBarAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ACBAR_SWITCH_DYNAMIC or the EABAlgoSwitch option of the MTCCongControlSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell and CellMimoParaCfg MOs:
    • The MuMimoPairingStrategy parameter in the CellMimoParaCfg MOs under the Cell MOs corresponding to the SectorSplitCell MOs under a SectorSplitGroup MO can be set to a value other than DEFAULT only if the following conditions are met: The TxRxMode and FddTddInd parameters in these Cell MOs are set to 32T32R and CELL_FDD, respectively, and the number of SectorSplitCell MOs under the SectorSplitGroup MO is less than 3.
    • If the number of SectorSplitCell MOs under a SectorSplitGroup MO is greater than or equal to 3 and the value of the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MOs corresponding to these SectorSplitCell MOs is CELL_ACTIVE, then the setting of each of the MASSIVE_MIMO_SUBBAND_BF_SW, PRECISE_DL_CSI_SW, and MASSIVE_MIMO_MU_RANK_OPT_SW options of the MimoSwitch parameter must be the same in the CellMimoParaCfg MOs under these Cell MOs.
    • If the number of SectorSplitCell MOs under a SectorSplitGroup MO is greater than or equal to 3 and the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MOs corresponding to these SectorSplitCell MOs is CELL_ACTIVE, the FREQ_DIFF_AMEND_SW option of the MimoSwitch parameter must be set the same in the CellMimoParaCfg MOs under all these Cell MOs.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, and the cell specified by this Cell MO is added in a SectorSplitCell MO, then the McsIndexThldForAdaptTm9 parameter in the CellMimoParaCfg MO must be set to 255.
  • Relation with the CellMimoParaCfg and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • If the MASSIVE_MIMO_SUBBAND_BF_SW option of the MimoSwitch parameter in the CellMimoParaCfg MO under a Cell MO is selected, the following parameter setting requirements must be met: The FddTddInd parameter in this Cell MO must be set to CELL_FDD, the New4TCodebookSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected, the TxRxMode parameter in this Cell MO must be set to 32T32R, and the ChannelRecSwitch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell, CellMimoParaCfg, and SectorSplitGroup MOs:
    • If the PRECISE_DL_CSI_SW option of the MimoSwitch parameter in the CellMimoParaCfg MO under a Cell MO corresponding to a SectorSplitCell is selected and the value of the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, then the following parameter setting requirements must be met: (a) The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO must be set to CELL_FDD. (b) The TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to 32T32R. (c) The number of SectorSplitCell MOs under the SectorSplitGroup MO that includes the cell specified by the Cell MO must be less than 3; alternatively, if the number is greater than or equal to 3, the JOINT_SCHEDULING_SWITCH option of the IntelligentBeamSchSwitch parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO must be selected.
    • If the MASSIVE_MIMO_MU_RANK_OPT_SW option of the MimoSwitch parameter in the CellMimoParaCfg MO under a Cell MO corresponding to a SectorSplitCell is selected and the value of the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, then the following parameter setting requirements must be met: (a) The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO must be set to CELL_FDD. (b) The TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to 32T32R. (c) The number of SectorSplitCell MOs under the SectorSplitGroup MO that includes the cell specified by the Cell MO must be less than 3; alternatively, if the number is greater than or equal to 3, the JOINT_SCHEDULING_SWITCH option of the IntelligentBeamSchSwitch parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO must be selected.
    • If the FREQ_DIFF_AMEND_SW option of the MimoSwitch parameter in the CellMimoParaCfg MO under a Cell MO corresponding to a SectorSplitCell MO is selected and the value of the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, then the following parameter setting requirements must be met: (a) The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO must be set to CELL_FDD. (b) The TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to 32T32R. (c) The number of SectorSplitCell MOs under the SectorSplitGroup MO that includes the cell specified by the Cell MO must be less than 3; alternatively, if the number is greater than or equal to 3, the JOINT_SCHEDULING_SWITCH option of the IntelligentBeamSchSwitch parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO must be selected.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch, CellCsiRsParaCfg, and CellBf MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the cell is activated, the TM9HybridPrecodingSwitch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the CsiRsPortNum parameter in the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO is set to CSI_RS_PORT_8, then the PdschSplitBeamConfigIndex parameter in the CellBf MO must be set to CONFIG_INDEX4.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the cell is activated, the TM9HybridPrecodingSwitch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the PdschSplitBeamConfigIndex parameter in the CellBf MO is set to CONFIG_INDEX4, then the CsiRsPortNum parameter in the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO must be set to CSI_RS_PORT_8.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoExtSwitch and ENodeBAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • In the Cell command, if the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD and the UeSRSAntSelectCtrlSwitch option of the CompatibilityCtrlSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, the CARRIER_BASED_ANT_SEL_SWITCH option of the AntennaSelectionAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO can be selected.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell and CellRfShutdown MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R, 32T32R, or 8T8R, and the SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, the RfShutdownSwitch parameter in the CellRfShutdown MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell and CellShutdown MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and the SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, the CellShutdownSwitch parameter in the CellShutdown MO must not be set to ON_CO_COV_AUTO_IDENT.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in this Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the cell specified by this Cell MO is added in a SectorSplitCell MO under a SectorSplitGroup MO, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in this SectorSplitGroup MO is set to NOT_CFG, then the CellShutdownSwitch parameter in the CellShutDown MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell, ENodeBAlgoSwitch, and CellDynPowerSharing MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, and the SsrdDynDlPwrSharingSwitch option of the SSRDAlgoSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is selected, then the DynamicPowerSharingSwitch parameter in the CellDynPowerSharing MO cannot be set to ON.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell and CellLowPower MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in this Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the cell specified by this Cell MO is added in a SectorSplitCell MO under a SectorSplitGroup MO, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in this SectorSplitGroup MO is set to NOT_CFG, then the LowPwrSwitch parameter in the CellLowPower MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 32T32R or 64T64R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, then the LowPwrSwitch parameter in the CellLowPower MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and ContigIntraBandCarr MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR or LTE_NR_COORD_SPECTRUM_SHR, the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw parameter in the ContigIntraBandCarr MO must not be selected for this cell.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw parameter in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs under all Cell MOs with the same LteNrSpectrumShrCellGrpId parameter value as this Cell MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and CellUSParaCfg MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the UsUeGuaranteeSwitch option of the UsAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellUSParaCfg MO must not be selected.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and CellDlschAlgo MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR or LTE_NR_COORD_SPECTRUM_SHR, the ADAPTIVE_FREQ_PRIORITY_SW option of the MultiCarrierCoSchAlgoSw parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO must not be selected for this cell.
  • Relation with the ContigIntraBandCarr MO:
    • If the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw parameter in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs corresponding to two cells having the same FreqBand parameter value but different DlEarfcn parameter values is selected and the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD for both cells, then the bandwidth overlap between the two cells must be smaller than or equal to 14% of the smaller DlBandWidth parameter value between the two cells or the bandwidth occupied by 25 RBs, whichever is smaller.
    • When FddTddInd in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and NbCellFlag is set to FALSE, the GUARDBAND_COMPACT_SW and POWER_RAISE_SW options of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw parameter in a ContigIntraBandCarr MO can be selected and FixedExclusiveResRate can be set to a value other than 255.
    • If CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd in the Cell MO is set to CFG, the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of ContigIntraBandCarrSw in the ContigIntraBandCarr MO must be deselected.
    • For cells that have the same setting of FreqBand, if the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of ContigIntraBandCarrSw is selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs corresponding to the these cells, UlEarfcnCfgInd must be set to an identical value for all these cells: either CFG or NOT_CFG.
    • The difference between the values of DlEarfcn of two cells meeting the following conditions must be an integer multiple of 15 kHz: The two cells have the same setting of FreqBand and different settings of DlEarfcn, the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of ContigIntraBandCarrSw is selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs corresponding to the two cells, and the difference between the values of DlEarfcn of the two cells is less than half of the sum of the bandwidths specified by DlBandWidth of the two cells.
    • The difference between the values of UlEarfcn of two cells meeting the following conditions must be an integer multiple of 15 kHz: UlEarfcnCfgInd is set to CFG for the two cells and the two cells have the same setting of FreqBand and different settings of UlEarfcn, the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of ContigIntraBandCarrSw is selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs corresponding to the two cells, and the difference between the values of UlEarfcn of the two cells is less than half of the sum of the bandwidths specified by UlBandWidth of the two cells.
    • The difference between the values of DlEarfcn of two cells meeting the following conditions must be greater than or equal to half of the sum of the bandwidths occupied by the RBs of the quantities indicated in the values of DlBandWidth of the two cells: The two cells have the same setting of FreqBand and different settings of DlEarfcn, the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of ContigIntraBandCarrSw is selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs corresponding to the two cells, FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD for the two cells, and the values of DlBandWidth of the two cells are CELL_BW_N6(1.4M) and CELL_BW_N6(1.4M), or CELL_BW_N6(1.4M) and CELL_BW_N15(3M), or CELL_BW_N15(3M) and CELL_BW_N15(3M).
    • The difference between the values of UlEarfcn of two cells meeting the following conditions must be greater than or equal to half of the sum of the bandwidths occupied by the RBs of the quantities indicated in the values of UlBandWidth of the two cells: UlEarfcnCfgInd is set to CFG for the two cells and the two cells have the same setting of FreqBand and different settings of UlEarfcn, the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of ContigIntraBandCarrSw is selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs corresponding to the two cells, FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD for the two cells, and the values of UlBandWidth of the two cells are CELL_BW_N6(1.4M) and CELL_BW_N6(1.4M), or CELL_BW_N6(1.4M) and CELL_BW_N15(3M), or CELL_BW_N15(3M) and CELL_BW_N15(3M).
    • If the [OPTION]CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW[/OPTION] option of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw parameter is selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs corresponding to two cells having the same FreqBand parameter value but different DlEarfcn parameter values and the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_FDD for both cells, then the bandwidth overlap between the two cells must be less than or equal to either 40% of the bandwidth occupied by the RBs of the quantity indicated in the smaller DlBandWidth parameter value between the two cells or the bandwidth occupied by 25 RBs, whichever is smaller.
    • If the FreqBand parameter is set to the same value for two cells, the UlEarfcnCfgInd parameter is set to CFG for both cells, the UlEarfcn parameter is set to different values for the two cells, the [OPTION]CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW[/OPTION] option of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw parameter is selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs corresponding to the two cells, and the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_FDD for both cells, then the bandwidth overlap between the two cells must be less than or equal to either 40% of the bandwidth occupied by the RBs of the quantity indicated in the smaller UlBandWidth parameter value between the two cells or the bandwidth occupied by 25 RBs, whichever is smaller.
    • If NbCellFlag in the Cell MO is set to TRUE, the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of ContigIntraBandCarrSw in the ContigIntraBandCarr MO cannot be selected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw parameter in the ContigIntraBandCarr MO under this Cell MO is selected, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO can only be set to 1T1R, 1T2R, 2T2R, 2T4R, or 4T4R.
  • Relation with the SymbolPwrSaving and ENodeBAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the MultiRatSymbolShutdownSw parameter in the SymbolPwrSaving MO corresponding to the LocalCellId parameter in the Cell MO is set to ON, the TddSymbolShutdownSwitch option of the PowerSaveSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the MultiRatSymbolShutdownSw parameter in the SymbolPwrSaving MO corresponding to the Cell is set to ON, the SymbolShutdownSwitch option of the PowerSaveSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
    • If the TrigBndlSchDlAvgPrbThld parameter in the SymbolPwrSaving MO is not set to 0 for a cell and the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD for the cell, the SymbolShutdownSwitch option of the PowerSaveSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
    • If the TrigBndlSchDlAvgPrbThld parameter in the SymbolPwrSaving MO is not set to 0 for a cell and the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD for the cell, the TddSymbolShutdownSwitch option of the PowerSaveSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
    • If the TrigBndlSchDlAvgPrbThld parameter in the SymbolPwrSaving MO is not set to 0 for a cell and the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD for the cell, the DlIcicSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be set to DlIcicSwitch_OFF_ENUM.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the DEEP_SYMBOL_SHUTDOWN_SW option of the SymbolShutdownEnhancedSw parameter in the SymbolPwrSaving MO under this Cell MO is selected, the TddSymbolShutdownSwitch option of the PowerSaveSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the DEEP_SYMBOL_SHUTDOWN_SW option of the SymbolShutdownEnhancedSw parameter in the SymbolPwrSaving MO under this Cell MO is selected, the SymbolShutdownSwitch option of the PowerSaveSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
  • Relation with the SRSCfg, CellAlgoSwitch, CellRicAlgo, CellBf, and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • The SrsResExpansionSwitch parameter in the SRSCfg MO can be set to SRS_RES_EXPANSION_MODE only when all of the following conditions are met: 1. The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD. 2. The TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R. 3. The SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is not configured. 4. The WTTxSRSIntrfAvoidanceSw option of the SrsCfgPolicySwitch parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to OFF. 5. The SplitSdmaSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. 6. The MuteUpPTSSymNum parameter in the CellRicAlgo MO is set to 0. 7. The PucchFlexCfgSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. 8. The LARGE_DELAY_SPREAD_UE_IDENT_SW option of the UeIdentificationSwitch parameter in the CellBf MO is selected.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch, SectorSplitCell, and SectorSplitGroup MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in this Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the cell specified by this Cell MO is added in a SectorSplitCell MO under a SectorSplitGroup MO, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in this SectorSplitGroup MO is set to MODE_3, then in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the following options must be deselected: QUICK_CHN_CAL_SWITCH option of the EnhChnCalSwitch parameter, IntraDlCompSwitch, InterDlCompDcsSwitch, and InterDlCompCbfSwitch options of the DlCompSwitch parameter, TurboBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter, AntSelEnhanceBfSwitch option of the BfAlgoSwitch parameter, InterCellDmimoJTSwitch and DmimoJTSwitch options of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter, and UlCamcSw option of the CamcSwitch parameter.
  • Relation with the CellLowPower, SectorSplitGroup, and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in this Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the cell specified by this Cell MO is added in a SectorSplitCell MO under a SectorSplitGroup MO, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in this SectorSplitGroup MO is set to MODE_3, then the LowPwrSwitch parameter in the CellLowPower MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the CellShutdown, SectorSplitGroup, and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in this Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the cell specified by this Cell MO is added in a SectorSplitCell MO under a SectorSplitGroup MO, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in this SectorSplitGroup MO is set to MODE_3, then the CellShutdownSwitch parameter in the CellShutDown MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the CellDynPowerSharing, ENodeBAlgoSwitch, SectorSplitGroup, and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in this Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the cell specified by this Cell MO is added in a SectorSplitCell MO under a SectorSplitGroup MO, the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in this SectorSplitGroup MO is set to MODE_3, and the SsrdDynDlPwrSharingSwitch option of the SSRDAlgoSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is selected, then the LTE_DYN_POWER_SHARING_SW option of the DynamicPowerSharingSwitch parameter in the CellDynPowerSharing MO cannot be selected.
  • Relation with the CellRfShutdown, SectorSplitGroup, and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in this Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the cell specified by this Cell MO is added in a SectorSplitCell MO under a SectorSplitGroup MO, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in this SectorSplitGroup MO is set to MODE_3, then the RfShutdownSwitch parameter in the CellRfShutdown MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the CellShutdown, SectorSplitCell, and SectorSplitGroup MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the cell specified by this Cell MO is added in a SectorSplitCell MO under a SectorSplitGroup MO, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in this SectorSplitGroup MO is set to MODE_3, then the CellShutdownSwitch parameter in the CellShutDown MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the InterRatCellShutdown, SectorSplitCell, and SectorSplitGroup MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the cell specified by this Cell MO is added in a SectorSplitCell MO under a SectorSplitGroup MO, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in this SectorSplitGroup MO is set to MODE_3, then the ForceShutdownSwitch parameter in the InterRatCellShutdown MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the CellDynPowerSharing, SectorSplitCell, and SectorSplitGroup MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the cell specified by this Cell MO is added in a SectorSplitCell MO under a SectorSplitGroup MO, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in this SectorSplitGroup MO is set to MODE_3, then the LTE_DYN_POWER_SHARING_SW option of the DynamicPowerSharingSwitch parameter in the CellDynPowerSharing MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the SRSCfg, SectorSplitGroup, and SectorSplitCell MOs:
