    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in this Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the cell specified by this Cell MO is added in a SectorSplitCell MO under a SectorSplitGroup MO, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in this SectorSplitGroup MO is set to MODE_3, then the TddSrsCfgMode parameter in the SRSCfg MO must be set to ACCESS_ENHANCED.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and CellMbsfnSfEnhConfig MOs:
    • The RadioFrameAllocOfs in the CellMbsfnSfEnhConfig must be set to 0 when the FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR and the SubframeAllocationMode is not set to NOT_CONFIG.
  • Relation with the ContigIntraBandCarr, PDSCHCfg, eUCellSectorEqm, and SECTOREQM MOs:
    • Assume that two cells use the same sector equipment and have the same setting of the FreqBand parameter but different settings of the DlEarfcn parameter in their Cell MOs, and the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw parameter in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs of the two cells is selected. If the difference in the DlEarfcn parameter value between the two cells is less than or equal to half of the sum of the RB quantities indicated by the DlBandWidth parameter for the two cells, and the PWR_RAISE_SW option of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw parameter in the ContigIntraBandCarr MO of either of the two cells is selected, then the EmfPowerLimitSwitch parameter must be set to OFF in the PDSCHCfg MOs of both cells.
    • Assume that two cells use the same sector equipment and have the same setting of the FreqBand parameter but different settings of the DlEarfcn parameter in their Cell MOs, and the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw parameter in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs of the two cells is selected. If the difference in the DlEarfcn parameter value between the two cells is less than or equal to half of the sum of the RB quantities indicated by the DlBandWidth parameter for the two cells, and the PWR_RAISE_SW option of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw parameter in the ContigIntraBandCarr MO of either of the two cells is selected, then the EmfPowerLimitSwitch parameter must be set to OFF in the PDSCHCfg MOs of both cells.
  • Relation with the CellDynPowerSharing and CellPdcchAlgo MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the LTE_NR_DYN_POWER_SHARING_SW option of the DynamicPowerSharingSwitch parameter in the CellDynPowerSharing MO is selected, then the PdcchSparePowerAllocStrage parameter in the CellPdcchAlgo MO must be set to NONE and the PdcchSdmaWeightType parameter in the CellPdcchAlgo MO cannot be set to POWER_SHARING_AMONG_3_BEAMS, POWER_SHARING_AMONG_4_BEAMS, or POWER_SHARING_AMONG_5_BEAMS.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the LTE_NR_DYN_POWER_SHARING_SW option of the DynamicPowerSharingSwitch parameter in the CellDynPowerSharing MO is selected, then the PdcchPowerCheckSwitch parameter in the CellPdcchAlgo MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the MDU and CellRfShutdown MOs:
    • If the value of the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE and the TYPE parameter in the MDU MO is set to MDUC, the MultiRatJointChnShutdownSw parameter in the corresponding CellRfShutdown MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell, CellBf, and CellMMAlgo MOs:
    • The MUBF_PWR_ENH_SW option of the PreciseMubfOptSwitch parameter in the CellMMAlgo MO can be selected only when all of the following conditions are met: 1. The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD. 2. The TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R. 3. The SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is not configured. 4. The MubfPowerNormAdptFactor parameter in the CellBf MO is set to 0. 5. The MovingUeMuBfScheme parameter in the CellBf MO is not set to HEAVY_LOAD_SCHEME, LIGHT_LOAD_SCHEME, or SPLIT_SDMA_SCHEME.
  • Relation with the UlInterfSuppressCfg and RACHCfg MOs:
    • If the CHN_CALIB_SHIFT_SW option of the RemoteInfULEnhanceSw parameter in the UlInterfSuppressCfg MO is selected, then the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO must be set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to 32T32R or 64T64R, the PrachConfigIndex parameter in the RACHCfg MO must be set to 4, and the ChnCalOptSwitch parameter in the UlInterfSuppressCfg MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the CellMMAlgo and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • The DL_CLUSTER_PAIRING_SW option of the PreciseMubfOptSwitch parameter in the CellMMAlgo MO under a Cell MO can be selected only when all the following conditions are met: The TxRxMode parameter in this Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, the MuBfSwitch and SplitSDMASwitch options of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO are selected and deselected, respectively, and the PRECISE_PAIRING_OPT_SW option of the PreciseMubfOptSwitch parameter in the CellMMAlgo MO is deselected.
    • The MubfTbsForecastPairLyrThld parameter in the CellMMAlgo MO under a Cell MO is not equal to zero only when all the following conditions are met: (1) The FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_TDD; (2) The TxRxMode parameter in this Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R; (3) The MuBfSwitch options of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO is selected.
  • Relation with the ContigIntraBandCarr and CellRbReserve MOs:
    • The spectrum ranges specified by RbRsvStartIndex and RbRsvEndIndex for two cells meeting the following conditions must not overlap: The two cells have the same setting of FreqBand and different settings of DlEarfcn, the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of ContigIntraBandCarrSw is selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs corresponding to the two cells, the difference between the values of DlEarfcn of the two cells is less than or equal to half of the sum of the bandwidths occupied by the RBs of the quantities indicated in the values of DlBandWidth of the two cells, RbRsvMode is set to NB_DEPLOYMENT or NB_RESERVED for the two cells, and RbRsvType is set to DOWNLINK_MODE for the two cells.
    • The spectrum ranges specified by RbRsvStartIndex and RbRsvEndIndex for two cells meeting the following conditions must not overlap: UlEarfcnCfgInd is set to CFG for the two cells and the two cells have the same setting of FreqBand and different settings of UlEarfcn, the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of ContigIntraBandCarrSw is selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs corresponding to the two cells, the difference between the values of UlEarfcn of the two cells is less than half of the sum of the bandwidths occupied by the RBs of the quantities indicated in the values of UlBandWidth of the two cells, RbRsvMode is set to NB_DEPLOYMENT or NB_RESERVED for the two cells, and RbRsvType is set to UPLINK_MODE for the two cells.
    • The spectrum ranges specified by RbRsvStartIndex and RbRsvEndIndex for two cells meeting the following conditions must not overlap: UlEarfcnCfgInd is set to NOT_CFG for the two cells and the two cells have the same setting of FreqBand and different settings of DlEarfcn, the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of ContigIntraBandCarrSw is selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs corresponding to the two cells, the difference between the values of DlEarfcn of the two cells is less than half of the sum of the bandwidths occupied by the RBs of the quantities indicated in the values of UlBandWidth of the two cells, RbRsvMode is set to NB_DEPLOYMENT or NB_RESERVED for the two cells, and RbRsvType is set to UPLINK_MODE for the two cells.
  • Relation with the ContigIntraBandCarr and Prb MOs:
    • Assume that two cells have the same setting of FreqBand and different settings of DlEarfcn, the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of ContigIntraBandCarrSw is selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs corresponding to the two cells, the difference between the values of DlEarfcn of the two cells is less than or equal to half of the sum of the bandwidths occupied by the RBs of the quantities indicated in the values of DlBandWidth of the two cells, and DeployMode is set to GUARD_BAND for the two cells. The difference between the value of DlEarfcn of any of the two cells and the value of DlEarfcn of any other cell except the cells where these NB-IoT cells are deployed must be greater than half of the bandwidth specified by DlBandWidth corresponding to DlEarfcn of this other cell.
  • Relation with the ContigIntraBandCarr, eUCellSectorEqm, and SECTOREQM MOs:
    • If the FreqBand parameter is set to the same value in different Cell MOs, the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw parameter is selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs under these Cell MOs, and the ANTCFGMODE parameter is set to BEAM in the SECTOREQM MOs associated with these Cell MOs, then the SECTORID parameter must be set to either the same value or different values in these SECTOREQM MOs. If the SECTORID parameter is set to the same value in these SECTOREQM MOs, each of the BEAMSHAPE, BEAMLAYERSPLIT, and BEAMAZIMUTHOFFSET parameters must be set to the same value in these SECTOREQM MOs.
    • If the FreqBand parameter is set to the same value in different Cell MOs, the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw parameter is selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs under these Cell MOs, and the ANTCFGMODE parameter is set to BEAM in the SECTOREQM MOs associated with these Cell MOs, then the SECTORID parameter must be set to either the same value or different values in these SECTOREQM MOs. If the SECTORID parameter is set to the same value in these SECTOREQM MOs, each of the BEAMSHAPE, BEAMLAYERSPLIT, and BEAMAZIMUTHOFFSET parameters must be set to the same value in these SECTOREQM MOs.
    • If the FreqBand parameter is set to the same value in different Cell MOs, the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw parameter is selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs under these Cell MOs, and the ANTCFGMODE parameter is set to ANTENNAPORT in the SECTOREQM MOs associated with these Cell MOs, then each of the ANTCN, ANTSRN, ANTSN, and ANTN parameters must be set to either the same value or different values in these SECTOREQM MOs.
    • If two cells have the same setting of FreqBand and different settings of DlEarfcn, the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of ContigIntraBandCarrSw is selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs corresponding to the two cells, and the settings of SectorEqmId in the eUCellSectorEqm MOs for the two cells are the same, then the settings of the combination of MultiRruCellFlag, MultiRruCellMode, SectorEqmNum, and TxRxMode must be the same for the two cells.
    • If two cells have the same setting of FreqBand and different settings of DlEarfcn, the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of ContigIntraBandCarrSw is selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs corresponding to the two cells, and the settings of SectorEqmId in the eUCellSectorEqm MOs for the two cells are the same, then the settings of the combination of MultiRruCellFlag, MultiRruCellMode, SectorEqmNum, and TxRxMode must be the same for the two cells.
    • Assume that two cells use the same sector equipment and antenna channels and have the same setting of FreqBand but different settings of DlEarfcn. If the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw parameter is selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs for the two cells, and the difference between the values of DlEarfcn of the two cells is less than or equal to half of the sum of the bandwidths occupied by the RBs of the quantities indicated in the values of DlBandWidth of the two cells, the ReferenceSignalPwr parameter must be set to the same value for the two cells.
    • Assume that two cells use the same sector equipment and antenna channels and have the same setting of FreqBand but different settings of DlEarfcn. If the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw parameter is selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs for the two cells, and the difference between the values of DlEarfcn of the two cells is less than or equal to half of the sum of the bandwidths occupied by the RBs of the quantities indicated in the values of DlBandWidth of the two cells, the ReferenceSignalPwr parameter must be set to the same value for the two cells.
    • Among the cells that are served by the same sector equipment, have the same setting of FreqBand and different settings of DlEarfcn, and have the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of ContigIntraBandCarrSw selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs corresponding to these cells, any given cell can have the following relationship with only one of the other cells: The difference between the values of DlEarfcn of the two cells is less than half of the sum of the bandwidths occupied by the RBs of the quantities indicated in the values of DlBandWidth of the two cells.
    • Among the cells that are served by the same sector equipment, have the same setting of FreqBand and different settings of DlEarfcn, and have the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of ContigIntraBandCarrSw selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs corresponding to these cells, any given cell can have the following relationship with only one of the other cells: The difference between the values of DlEarfcn of the two cells is less than half of the sum of the bandwidths occupied by the RBs of the quantities indicated in the values of DlBandWidth of the two cells.
    • Among the cells that are served by the same sector equipment, have UlEarfcnCfgInd set to CFG, have the same setting of FreqBand and different settings of UlEarfcn, and have the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of ContigIntraBandCarrSw selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs corresponding to these cells, any given cell can have the following relationship with only one of the other cells: The difference between the values of UlEarfcn of the two cells is less than half of the sum of the bandwidths occupied by the RBs of the quantities indicated in the values of UlBandWidth of the two cells.
    • Among the cells that are served by the same sector equipment, have UlEarfcnCfgInd set to CFG, have the same setting of FreqBand and different settings of UlEarfcn, and have the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of ContigIntraBandCarrSw selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs corresponding to these cells, any given cell can have the following relationship with only one of the other cells: The difference between the values of UlEarfcn of the two cells is less than half of the sum of the bandwidths occupied by the RBs of the quantities indicated in the values of UlBandWidth of the two cells.
    • If two cells are served by the same sector equipment, have the same setting of FreqBand, and have the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of ContigIntraBandCarrSw selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs corresponding to the two cells, then DlEarfcn must be set to different values for the two cells.
    • If two cells are served by the same sector equipment, have the same setting of FreqBand, and have the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of ContigIntraBandCarrSw selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs corresponding to the two cells, then DlEarfcn must be set to different values for the two cells.
  • Relation with the ContigIntraBandCarr, PDSCHCfg, CellDlpcPdschPa, eUCellSectorEqm, SECTOREQM, and CellDlpcPdsch MOs:
    • If two cells served by the same sector equipment have the same setting of FreqBand and different settings of DlEarfcn, the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of ContigIntraBandCarrSw is selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs corresponding to the two cells, and the difference between the values of DlEarfcn of the two cells is less than or equal to half of the sum of the RB quantities indicated in the values of DlBandWidth of the two cells, then the setting of each of ReferenceSignalPwr, Pb, PaPcOff, and SINGLE_CA_PWR_SHARE_SW_FOR_4T option of TxChnPowerCfgSw must be the same for the two cells, and RemainingPwrUseStrategy in CellDlpcPdsch must be configured the same.
    • If two cells served by the same sector equipment have the same setting of FreqBand and different settings of DlEarfcn, the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of ContigIntraBandCarrSw is selected in the ContigIntraBandCarr MOs corresponding to the two cells, and the difference between the values of DlEarfcn of the two cells is less than or equal to half of the sum of the RB quantities indicated in the values of DlBandWidth of the two cells, then the setting of each of ReferenceSignalPwr, Pb, PaPcOff, and SINGLE_CA_PWR_SHARE_SW_FOR_4T option of TxChnPowerCfgSw must be the same for the two cells, and RemainingPwrUseStrategy in CellDlpcPdsch must be configured the same.
  • Relation with the SymbolPwrSaving MO:
    • If the TrigBndlSchDlAvgPrbThld parameter in the SymbolPwrSaving MO is not set to 0 for a cell, the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO must be set to FALSE for the cell.
    • If the TrigBndlSchDlAvgPrbThld parameter in the SymbolPwrSaving MO is not set to 0 for a cell and the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD for the cell, the MultiCellShareMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to NORMAL.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, then the TrigBndlSchDlAvgPrbThld parameter in the SymbolPwrSaving MO in the Cell MO MO must be set to 0.
    • If the DEEP_SYMBOL_SHUTDOWN_SW option of the SymbolShutdownEnhancedSw parameter in the SymbolPwrSaving MO under a Cell MO is selected, the MultiRruCellMode parameter in this Cell MO cannot be set to MPRU_AGGREGATION.
    • If the LNR_BUNDLED_SCHEDULING_SW option of the SymbolShutdownEnhancedSw parameter in the SymbolPwrSaving MO under a Cell MO is selected, the MultiRruCellMode parameter in this Cell MO cannot be set to MPRU_AGGREGATION.
  • Relation with the SymbolPwrSaving and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • If the TrigBndlSchDlAvgPrbThld parameter in the SymbolPwrSaving MO under a Cell MO is set to a value other than 0, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in this Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, and the MultiRruCellMode parameter in this Cell MO is set to SFN or MPRU_AGGREGATION, then the SfnDlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be set to JOINT.
    • If the TrigBndlSchDlAvgPrbThld parameter in the SymbolPwrSaving MO is not set to 0 for a cell and the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD for the cell, the DmimoJTSwitch and InterCellDmimoJTSwitch options of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected for the cell.
    • If the TrigBndlSchDlAvgPrbThld parameter in the SymbolPwrSaving MO is not set to 0 for a cell and the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD for the cell, the GLPwrShare parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF for the same cell.
  • Relation with the SymbolPwrSaving and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • The TrigBndlSchDlAvgPrbThld parameter in the SymbolPwrSaving MO must be set to 0 for a cell if the following conditions are met for the cell: 1. The CellActiveState parameter in the Cell is set to CELL_ACTIVE. 2. The CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO in the associated Cell MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2. 3. The number of SectorSplitCell MO instances in a SectorSplitGroup MO to which the Cell MO belongs is greater than 1.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, then the TrigBndlSchDlAvgPrbThld parameter in the SymbolPwrSaving MO in the Cell MO MO must be set to 0.
  • Relation with the SymbolPwrSaving and CspcAlgoPara MOs:
    • If the TrigBndlSchDlAvgPrbThld parameter in the SymbolPwrSaving MO is not set to 0 for a cell and the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD for the same cell, the CspcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CspcAlgoPara MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the SymbolPwrSaving, CspcAlgoPara, and CellCspcPara MOs:
    • If the TrigBndlSchDlAvgPrbThld parameter in the SymbolPwrSaving MO is not set to 0 for a cell and the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD for the same cell, then the TddCspcMacroMicroSwitch option of the TddCspcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CspcAlgoPara MO must be deselected and the CellCspcSwitch parameter in the CellCspcPara MO must be set to OFF for the cell.
  • Relation with the SymbolPwrSaving and CellDlschAlgo MOs:
    • If the TrigBndlSchDlAvgPrbThld parameter in the SymbolPwrSaving MO is not set to 0 for a cell and the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD for the cell, the MULTI_CARRIER_COSCH_SW option of the MultiCarrierCoSchAlgoSw parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO must be deselected for the cell.
  • Relation with the SymbolPwrSaving and CellDynPowerSharing MOs:
    • If the TrigBndlSchDlAvgPrbThld parameter in the SymbolPwrSaving MO is not set to 0 for a cell and the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD for the same cell, the UMTS_LTE_DYN_POWER_SHARING_SW option of the DynamicPowerSharingSwitch parameter in the CellDynPowerSharing MO must be deselected for the cell.
  • Relation with the SymbolPwrSaving and CellCspcPara MOs:
    • If the TrigBndlSchDlAvgPrbThld parameter in the SymbolPwrSaving MO is not set to 0 for a cell and the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD for the cell, the CelleCspcSwitch parameter in the CellCspcPara MO must be set to OFF for the cell.
  • Relation with the ContigIntraBandCarr and CellEmtcAlgo MOs:
    • If the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw parameter in the ContigIntraBandCarr MO under a Cell MO is selected and the DlBandWidth parameter in this Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N6, CELL_BW_N15 or CELL_BW_N25, the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO under this Cell MO cannot be selected.
  • Relation with the ContigIntraBandCarr and PDSCHCfg MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw parameter in the ContigIntraBandCarr MO under this Cell MO is selected, the CrsPowerBoostingAmplitude parameter in the PDSCHCfg MO under this Cell MO cannot be set to DB_1DOT77 or DB_3.
  • Relation with the ContigIntraBandCarr, CellAuxEnbBind, and CellAuxDuBind MOs:
    • If the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw parameter in the ContigIntraBandCarr MO under a Cell MO is selected, the CellAuxEnbBind, and CellAuxDuBind MOS cannot be configured under this Cell MO.
  • Relation with the CellSmartPwrLock MO:
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the SmartPowerLockMode parameter in the associated CellSmartPwrLock MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N6 or CELL_BW_N15, the SmartPowerLockMode parameter in the associated CellSmartPwrLock MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the SmartPowerLockMode parameter in the associated CellSmartPwrLock MO is set to AVERAGE_POWER_CONTROL, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO can only be set to 2T2R, 2T4R, 2T8R, 4T4R, or 4T8R.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and SmartPowerLockMode in the associated CellSmartPwrLock MO is set to AVERAGE_POWER_CONTROL, the TxRxMode parameter in this Cell MO can only be set to 2T2R, or 4T4R.
    • If the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, the SmartPowerLockMode parameter in the associated CellSmartPwrLock MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the EmtcCeSchCfg MO:
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to FALSE and the CoverageLevel parameter in the EmtcCeSchCfg MO is set to COVERAGE_LEVEL_0 or COVERAGE_LEVEL_1, the PdschInitRepNum parameter in the EmtcCeSchCfg MO can be set to INVALID, PDSCH_INIT_REP_INDEX_1, PDSCH_INIT_REP_INDEX_2, PDSCH_INIT_REP_INDEX_3, or PDSCH_INIT_REP_INDEX_4.
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to FALSE and the CoverageLevel parameter in the EmtcCeSchCfg MO is set to COVERAGE_LEVEL_0 or COVERAGE_LEVEL_1, the PuschInitRepNum parameter in the EmtcCeSchCfg MO can be set to INVALID, PUSCH_INIT_REP_INDEX_1, PUSCH_INIT_REP_INDEX_2, PUSCH_INIT_REP_INDEX_3, or PUSCH_INIT_REP_INDEX_4.
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to FALSE and the MpdcchInitRepNumType1Css parameter in the EmtcCeSchCfg MO is set to INVALID, the PdschInitRepNumType1Css parameter in the EmtcCeSchCfg MO must be set to INVALID. If the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to FALSE and the MpdcchInitRepNumType1Css parameter in the EmtcCeSchCfg MO is set to a value other than INVALID, the PdschInitRepNumType1Css parameter in the EmtcCeSchCfg MO must be set to a value other than INVALID.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitGroup, eUCellSectorEqm, SectorSplitCell, and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • If the CellActiveState parameter in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, and the TM4WeightScheme parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO (under which a SectorSplitCell MO identifies the cell defined by this Cell MO) is set to COVERAGE_MODE_2, then one of the following configuration requirements must be met: (a) The BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO must be set to MODE_1, and the number of SectorSplitCell MO instances under the SectorSplitGroup MO must be 2. (b) The BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO must be set to MODE_1, the number of SectorSplitCell MO instances under the SectorSplitGroup MO must be 3, and the New4TCodebookSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected. (c) The BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO must be set to MODE_2, the number of SectorSplitCell MO instances under the SectorSplitGroup MO must be 4 or 5, and the New4TCodebookSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell and PDSCHCfg MOs:
    • If the value of the CellActiveState parameter in a Cell MO identified in a SectorSplitCell MO under a SectorSplitGroup MO is CELL_ACTIVE, the LogicalPortSwapSwitch parameter must be set to the same value in the PDSCHCfg MOs under the Cell MOs identified in all the SectorSplitCell MOs under this SectorSplitGroup MO.
  • Relation with the CellBf and CellMMAlgo MOs:
    • The UE_STATUS_BASED_MLB_SWITCH option of the MMAlgoOptSwitch parameter in the CellMMAlgo MO can be selected only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the SRS_LMT_AND_ABN_UE_IDENT_SW option of the UeIdentificationSwitch parameter in the CellBf MO is selected.
  • Relation with the CellBf, CellMMAlgo, SectorSplitCell, ENodeBAlgoSwitch, CellAlgoSwitch, and CellBfMimoParaCfg MOs:
    • The TM8_PORT_ALLOC_OPT_SWITCH option of the MMAlgoOptSwitch parameter in the CellMMAlgo MO can be selected only when all of the following conditions are met: 1. The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD. 2. The TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R. 3. The SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is not configured. 4. The Tm8PairingOptSwitch parameter in the CellBf MO is set to ON. 5. The UeSRSAntSelectCtrlSwitch option of the CompatibilityCtrlSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. 6. The FakeAntSelIdentificationSwitch option of the BfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected. 7. The NON_AS_UE_MAX_BF_RANK_CTRL_SW option of the BfMimoAlgoOptSwitch parameter in the CellBfMimoParaCfg MO is selected.
  • Relation with the UeCooperationPara and ENodeBAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • The UlClosedloopIntelAntSelSwitch option of the SpecUserCooperationSwitch parameter in the UeCooperationPara MO can be selected only if all of the following conditions are met: 1. The FddTddInd parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD. 2. The UeSRSAntSelectCtrlSwitch option of the CompatibilityCtrlSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. 3. The TxRxMode parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to 64T64R, 32T32R, 8T8R, or 4T4R. 4. The SpecUeIdentifySwitch option of the SpecUserCooperationSwitch parameter in the UeCooperationPara MO is selected.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell, ENodeBAlgoSwitch, CellUSParaCfg, CellPdcchAlgo, CellAlgoSwitch, and PDSCHCfg MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R, 32T32R, or 8T8R, the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, and the SsrdDynDlPwrSharingSwitch option of the SSRDAlgoSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is selected, then: 1. The UsUeDlAdaptPwrBoostThld parameter in the CellUSParaCfg MO must be set to 0; 2. The USPwrSharePaSwitch option of the UsAlgoExSwitch parameter in the CellUSParaCfg MO must be deselected; 3. The PdcchSparePowerAllocStrage parameter in the CellPdcchAlgo MO must be set to NONE; 4. The PdcchSdmaWeightType parameter in the CellPdcchAlgo MO cannot be set to POWER_SHARING_AMONG_3_BEAMS, POWER_SHARING_AMONG_4_BEAMS, or POWER_SHARING_AMONG_5_BEAMS; 5. The BfEnhChnPwrFormSwitch option of the DlPcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected; 6. The MimoUePaAdaptSw parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF; 7. The EmfPowerLimitSwitch parameter in the PDSCHCfg MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the CellDlschAlgo and ENodeBAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the DL_INTRA_JAMC_ALGO_SW option of the DlJointAMCAlgoSw parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO is selected, the DlIcicSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be set to DlIcicSwitch_OFF_ENUM.
  • Relation with the CellDlschAlgo and CellCspcPara MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the DL_INTRA_JAMC_ALGO_SW option of the DlJointAMCAlgoSw parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO is selected, the CelleCspcSwitch parameter in the CellCspcPara MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the DL_INTRA_JAMC_ALGO_SW option of the DlJointAMCAlgoSw parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO is selected, the CellCspcSwitch parameter in the CellCspcPara MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the CellDlschAlgo and CspcAlgoPara MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the DL_INTRA_JAMC_ALGO_SW option of the DlJointAMCAlgoSw parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO is selected, the TddCspcMacroMicroSwitch option of the TddCspcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CspcAlgoPara MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell and CellDlschAlgo MOs:
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the DL_INTRA_JAMC_ALGO_SW option of the DlJointAMCAlgoSw parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO can be selected only if the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is not configured.
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, the DL_INTRA_JAMC_ALGO_SW option of the DlJointAMCAlgoSw parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO can be selected only if the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is not configured.
  • Relation with the CellShutdown, eUCellSectorEqm, SectorSplitGroup, and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • If CellShutdownSwitch in the CellShutdown MO under a Cell MO is not set to OFF, CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, and the number of SectorSplitCell MOs under the SectorSplitGroup MO in which the cell specified by this Cell MO is included is greater than or equal to 2, then neither of DlPrbThd and UlPrbThd in the CellShutdown MO can be set to 0.
    • If CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under a Cell MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2 and the number of SectorSplitCell MOs under the SectorSplitGroup MO in which the cell specified by this Cell MO is included is greater than or equal to 2, CellShutdownSwitch in the CellShutdown MO under this Cell MO cannot be set to ON_CO_COV_AUTO_IDENT.
  • Relation with the CellShutdown and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, a SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, and the CellShutdownSwitch parameter in the CellShutdown MO is set to ON, the DlPrbThd and UlPrbThd parameters in the CellShutdown MO must not be set to 0.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell, SectorSplitGroup, and CellShutdown MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameters in the CELL MO are set to CELL_FDD, the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, and the number of SectorSplitCell MOs under the SectorSplitGroup MO in which the cell specified by this Cell MO is included is greater than or equal to 2, the CellShutdownSwitch parameter in the CellShutdown MO cannot be set to ON_MULTI_CARRIER_HIER_SHUTDOWN.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the CELL MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R, and the number of SectorSplitCell MOs under the SectorSplitGroup MO in which the cell specified by this Cell MO is included is greater than or equal to 2, the CellShutdownSwitch parameter in the CellShutdown MO cannot be set to ON_MULTI_CARRIER_HIER_SHUTDOWN.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud, SpctCloudAuxEnodeb, and LteNrSpctShrCellGrp MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the maximum number of cells with the same value of the LteNrSpectrumShrCellGrpId parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO as this cell, including cells with the same value of the LteNrSpectrumShrCellGrpId parameter in the SpctCloudAuxEnodeb MO, is 15.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and ENodeBAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the DlIcicSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be set to DlIcicSwitch_OFF_ENUM.
    • When the parameter FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_TDD, if the parameter SpectrumCloudSwitch in the SpectrumCloud MO corresponding to this cell is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the SsrdDlPwrShareSwitch and SsrdDynDlPwrSharingSwitch options of the SSRDAlgoSwitch in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch must be set to OFF.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the TDD_RRU_LOCATION_SWITCH option of the RruLocationSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and CellCspcPara MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the CelleCspcSwitch parameter in the CellCspcPara MO must not be set to ON.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and eNodeBUSParaCfg MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the BigPackageIdentify parameter in the eNodeBUSParaCfg MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell and NCellSrsMeasPara MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, the SrsAutoNCellMeasSwitch parameter in the NCellSrsMeasPara MO must not be set to ON.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell, SectorSplitGroup, and CellDynPowerSharing MOs:
    • For all Cell MOs associated with the SectorSplitCell MOs in a SectorSplitGroup MO, if the FddTddInd, TxRxMode, and CellActiveState parameters in one of the Cell MOs are set to CELL_TDD, 8T8R, and CELL_ACTIVE, respectively, and the cell specified by this Cell MO is added in a SectorSplitCell MO under a SectorSplitGroup MO, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in this SectorSplitGroup MO is set to NOT_CFG, then the setting of the LTE_DYN_POWER_SHARING_SW option of the DynamicPowerSharingSwitch parameter in the CellDynPowerSharing MO for all these Cell MOs must be the same.
  • Relation with the MDU and CellShutdown MOs:
    • If the value of the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE and the TYPE parameter in the MDU MO is set to MDUC, the CellShutdownSwitch parameter in the CellShutdown MO cannot be set to ON_MULTI_CARRIER_HIER_SHUTDOWN.
  • Relation with the CellMbsfnSfEnhConfig and CellDlschAlgo MOs:
    • If the SubframeAllocationMode parameter in the CellMbsfnSfEnhConfig MO under a Cell MO is set to ONE_FRAME or FOUR_FRAME, none of the options of the MbsfnSfCfg parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO under this Cell MO can be selected, the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO must be set to CELL_FDD.
  • Relation with the CellSmartPwrLock and eUCellSectorEqm MOs:
    • If the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE and the SmartPowerLockMode parameter in the corresponding CellSmartPwrLock MO is set to AVERAGE_POWER_CONTROL, the total number of eUCellSectorEqm MOs corresponding to the Cell MO must not be greater than 1.
  • Relation with the BASEBANDEQM, BBP, eUCellSectorEqm, and EuSectorEqmGroup MOs:
    • If there are BBP MOs on a base station in which WM is set to SPEC_MODE1 and only the LTE_FDD option or only the LTE_FDD and NBIOT options of BBWS are selected, the eUCellSectorEqm or EuSectorEqmGroup MOs corresponding to any Cell MO on this base station in which the values of CellActiveState, FddTddInd, and NbCellFlag are CELL_ACTIVE, CELL_FDD, and FALSE, respectively, must be bound to BASEBANDEQM MOs. In addition, each of the BASEBANDEQM MOs must correspond to a unique BBP MO.
    • If there are BBP MOs on a base station in which WM is set to SPEC_MODE1 and the LTE_TDD option of BBWS is selected, the eUCellSectorEqm or EuSectorEqmGroup MOs corresponding to the Cell MOs (for this base station) with the CellActiveState and FddTddInd parameters set to CELL_ACTIVE and CELL_TDD, respectively, must be all bound to a BASEBANDEQM MO. In addition, this BASEBANDEQM MO must correspond to a unique BBP MO.
  • Relation with the BASEBANDEQM and BBP MOs:
    • If there are BBP MOs on a base station in which WM is set to SPEC_MODE1 and only the LTE_FDD option or only the LTE_FDD and NBIOT options of BBWS are selected, the eUCellSectorEqm or EuSectorEqmGroup MOs corresponding to any Cell MO on this base station in which the values of CellActiveState, FddTddInd, and NbCellFlag are CELL_ACTIVE, CELL_FDD, and FALSE, respectively, must be bound to BASEBANDEQM MOs.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, BASEBANDEQM, BBP, EuSectorEqmGroup, CellAlgoSwitch, CellLcsCfg, and ENodeBAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • For a Cell MO in which the values of CellActiveState, FddTddInd, and NbCellFlag are CELL_ACTIVE, CELL_FDD, and FALSE, respectively, if the eUCellSectorEqm or EuSectorEqmGroup MOs corresponding to this Cell MO are bound to BASEBANDEQM MOs, and WM is set to SPEC_MODE1 and only the LTE_FDD option or only the LTE_FDD and NBIOT options of BBWS are selected in the BBP MOs corresponding to these BASEBANDEQM MOs, then: (1) UlCyclicPrefix and DlCyclicPrefix in the Cell MO cannot be set to EXTENDED_CP. (2) CellRadius cannot be set to a value larger than 100000. (3) MultiRruCellMode cannot be set to TWO_RRU_COMBINATION. (4) The SuperCombCellSwitch option of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected. (5) The NB_OTDOA_SWITCH option of CellLcsSwitch in the CellLcsCfg MO and the OtdoaSwitch option of LcsSwitch in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected. (6) TxRxMode in the Cell MO cannot be set to a value larger than 4T4R. (7) CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm MOs cannot be set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 or MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2. (8) ANTCFGMODE in the SECTOREQM MOs corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm or EuSectorEqmGroup MOs cannot be set to BEAM.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, BASEBANDEQM, BBP, EuSectorEqmGroup, and CellEmtcAlgo MOs:
    • For a Cell MO in which the values of CellActiveState, FddTddInd, and NbCellFlag are CELL_ACTIVE, CELL_FDD, and FALSE, respectively, if the eUCellSectorEqm or EuSectorEqmGroup MOs corresponding to this Cell MO are bound to BASEBANDEQM MOs, and WM is set to SPEC_MODE1 and only the LTE_FDD and NBIOT options of BBWS are selected in the BBP MOs corresponding to these BASEBANDEQM MOs, then the EMTC_SWITCH and EMTC_ONLY_CELL_SWITCH options of EmtcAlgoSwitch in the CellEmtcAlgo MO cannot be selected.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, SECTOREQM, BASEBANDEQM, and BBP MOs:
    • For a Cell MO in which the values of CellActiveState, FddTddInd, NbCellFlag, and MultiRruCellFlag are CELL_ACTIVE, CELL_FDD, FALSE, and BOOLEAN_TRUE, respectively, UlBandWidth or DlBandWidth is set to a value larger than or equal to CELL_BW_N75, and MultiRruCellMode is set to SFN or MPRU_AGGREGATION, if the eUCellSectorEqm MOs corresponding to this Cell MO are bound to BASEBANDEQM MOs, WM is set to SPEC_MODE1 and only the LTE_FDD option or only the LTE_FDD and NBIOT options of BBWS are selected in the BBP MOs corresponding to these BASEBANDEQM MOs, and the number of transmit or receive antennas configured in the SECTOREQM MOs for the corresponding cell is larger than two, then the CPRI compression mode of the cell cannot be "no compression".
  • Relation with the EuSectorEqmGroup, SECTOREQM, BASEBANDEQM, and BBP MOs:
    • For a Cell MO in which the values of CellActiveState, FddTddInd, NbCellFlag, and MultiRruCellFlag are CELL_ACTIVE, CELL_FDD, FALSE, and BOOLEAN_TRUE, respectively, UlBandWidth or DlBandWidth is set to a value larger than or equal to CELL_BW_N75, and MultiRruCellMode is set to SFN or MPRU_AGGREGATION, if the EuSectorEqmGroup MOs corresponding to this Cell MO are bound to BASEBANDEQM MOs, WM is set to SPEC_MODE1 and only the LTE_FDD option or only the LTE_FDD and NBIOT options of BBWS are selected in the BBP MOs corresponding to these BASEBANDEQM MOs, and the number of transmit or receive antennas configured in the SECTOREQM MOs for the corresponding cell is larger than two, then the CPRI compression mode of the cell cannot be "no compression".
  • Relation with the eNodeBFunction, eUCellSectorEqm, SfnAuxResBind, EuSectorEqmGroup, SfnAuxResGrpBind, EuPrbSectorEqm, MPT, Node, EuPrbSectorEqmGroup, and eNodeBResModeAlgo MOs:
    • When UMPT is configured in an MPT MO and WM in the corresponding NODE MO is set to CONCURRENT, the sum of the numbers of the following MO instances must meet a certain requirement: Cell MO instances in which MultiRruCellFlag and EuCellStandbyMode are set to BOOLEAN_FALSE and ACTIVE, respectively; Cell MO instances in which MultiRruCellFlag and MultiRruCellMode are set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and TWO_RRU_COMBINATION (or DIGITAL_COMBINATION), respectively; EuSectorEqmGroup and EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MO instances corresponding to the Cell MO instances in which MultiRruCellFlag and MultiRruCellMode are set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and MPRU_AGGREGATION, respectively; eUCellSectorEqm and EuPrbSectorEqm MO instances of other cells; SfnAuxResBind and SfnAuxResGrpBind MO instances. The requirement varies as follows: (1) The sum must be less than or equal to 36 if the value of BRDSPEC for the UMPT configured in the MPT MO is UMPTa, UMPTb, or UMPTa/b. (2) The sum must be less than or equal to 72 in other scenarios.
  • Relation with the eNodeBFunction, SfnAuxResGrpBind, SfnAuxResBind, Node, MPT, and eNodeBResModeAlgo MOs:
    • When UMPT is configured in an MPT MO and WM in the corresponding NODE MO is set to CONCURRENT, the sum of the number of Cell MO instances in which EuCellStandbyMode is set to ACTIVE and the number of SfnAuxResBind and SfnAuxResGrpBind MO instances in which both Main eNodeB ID and Main eNodeB Local cell ID are set to different values must meet one of the following requirements: (1) The sum must be less than or equal to 36 if the value of BRDSPEC for the UMPT configured in the MPT MO is UMPTa, UMPTb, or UMPTa/b. (2) The sum must be less than or equal to 72 in other scenarios.
  • Relation with the BBP and BASEBANDEQM MOs:
    • If there are BBP MOs on a base station in which WM is set to SPEC_MODE1 and the LTE_TDD option of BBWS is selected, the eUCellSectorEqm or EuSectorEqmGroup MOs corresponding to the Cell MOs (for this base station) with the CellActiveState and FddTddInd parameters set to CELL_ACTIVE and CELL_TDD, respectively, must be all bound to a BASEBANDEQM MO.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoSwitch, CellAlgoExtSwitch, and UlInterfSuppressCfg MOs:
    • The UlIntrfDetectionTrigThld parameter in the UlInterfSuppressCfg must be set to 127 when the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to TRUE, the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the UlCoverageEnhancementSw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO is set to ON, or the UlCyclicPrefix parameter in the Cell MO is set to EXTENDED_CP.
  • Relation with the CellDlpcPdsch, eUCellSectorEqm, and SimuloadPmiTemplate MOs:
    • When FddTddInd in Cell is configured as CELL_TDD, or NbCellFlag in Cell is configured as TRUE, or CrsPortNum in Cell is not configured as CRS_PORT_4, or TxRxMode in Cell is not configured as 4T4R, or CellBeamMode in eUCellSectorEqm is not NORMAL, or SimuloadPmiTemplate in CellSimuloadPmi is configured as 4, 5, 6, or 7, RemainingPwrUseStrategy in CellDlpcPdsch must be configured as OFF.
  • Relation with the CellDlpcPdsch and EuSectorEqmGroup MOs:
    • When RemainingPwrUseStrategy in CellDlpcPdsch is configured as PORT_PWR_BALANCING, and MultiRruCellFlag is BOOLEAN_TRUE and MultiRruCellMode is SFN and CellActiveState is CELL_ACTIVE, EuSectorEqmGroup cannot be configured.
  • Relation with the CellDlpcPdsch, eUCellSectorEqm, and SECTOREQM MOs:
    • When RemainingPwrUseStrategy in CellDlpcPdsch is configured as PORT_PWR_BALANCING, and MultiRruCellFlag is BOOLEAN_TRUE and MultiRruCellMode is SFN and CellActiveState is CELL_ACTIVE, and the ANTCFGMODE in SECTOREQM corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm of the cell configuration is ANTENNAPORT, the total number of transmitting antennas (the transmitting antenna means "transmitting antenna active/standby mode" is configured as MASTER) and the number of receive antennas of the corresponding cell must be both 4.
  • Relation with the CellSiMap and CellIntelAmcConfig MOs:
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, and the ForbidCellSiSwitch option of the SiSwitch parameter in a CellSiMap MO is set to ON, the DL_INTEL_AMC_SELECTION_SW option of the IntelligentAmcSwitch parameter in a CellIntelAmcConfig MO must be deselected under the same cell.
  • Relation with the CellIntelAmcConfig MO:
    • When the HighSpeedFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to HIGH_SPEED, ULTRA_HIGH_SPEED or EXTRA_HIGH_SPEED, the DL_INTEL_AMC_SELECTION_SW option of the IntelligentAmcSwitch parameter in a CellIntelAmcConfig MO must be deselected under the same cell.
    • When the FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, and the DL_INTEL_AMC_SELECTION_SW option of the IntelligentAmcSwitch parameter in the CellIntelAmcConfig MO is selected, the CrsPortNum in the Cell MO must be set to CRS_PORT_4.
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the DL_INTEL_AMC_SELECTION_SW option of the IntelligentAmcSwitch parameter in a CellIntelAmcConfig MO must be deselected under the same cell.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitGroup, SectorSplitCell, PDSCHCfg, and ENodeBAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • If TxRxMode is set to 8T8R and FddTddInd is set to CELL_TDD in the Cell MOs corresponding to all the SectorSplitCell MOs under a SectorSplitGroup MO in which BeamDeploymentMode is set to MODE_3, and the SsrdDlPwrShareSwitch option of the SSRDAlgoSwitch in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be set to ON, then ReferenceSignalPwr must be set to the same value in the PDSCHCfg MOs under these Cell MOs.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, BASEBANDEQM, BBP, EuSectorEqmGroup, and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • For a Cell MO in which the values of CellActiveState, FddTddInd, and NbCellFlag are CELL_ACTIVE, CELL_FDD, and FALSE, respectively, if the eUCellSectorEqm or EuSectorEqmGroup MOs corresponding to this Cell MO are bound to BASEBANDEQM MOs, and WM is set to SPEC_MODE1 and only the LTE_FDD option or only the LTE_FDD and NBIOT options of BBWS are selected in the BBP MOs corresponding to these BASEBANDEQM MOs, then:The InBandRelayDeNbSwitch, InBandRelayReNbSwitch and InBandRelayDistantDeploySw option of RelaySwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell, eUCellSectorEqm, BASEBANDEQM, and BBP MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the SectorSplitCell MO associated with Cell MO is configured, the BaseBandEqmId parameter in an eUCellSectorEqm MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, and the LTE_TDD option of BBWS is selected in the BBP under the corresponding BASEBANDEQM MO, then the WM parameter cannot be set to SPEC_MODE1.
  • Relation with the SsrdCellGroup, eUCellSectorEqm, BASEBANDEQM, and BBP MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the SsrdCellGroup MO with the PrimaryLocalCellID or SsrdLocalCellID parameter set to the same value as the LocalCellId parameter of the Cell MO is configured, the BaseBandEqmId parameter in an eUCellSectorEqm MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, and the LTE_TDD option of BBWS is selected in the BBP under the corresponding BASEBANDEQM MO, then the WM parameter cannot be set to SPEC_MODE1.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell, SectorSplitGroup, and ENodeBAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO is set to NOT_CFG, then the UlIcicFreqSwitch and DlIcicSwitch parameters in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF and DlIcicSwitch_OFF_ENUM, respectively.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO is set to MODE_3, then the UlIcicFreqSwitch parameter in the EnodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF and the DlIcicSwitch parameter in the EnodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be set to DlIcicSwitch_OFF_ENUM.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell, SectorSplitGroup, CellCspcPara, and CspcAlgoPara MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO is set to NOT_CFG, then the CelleCspcSwitch parameter in the CellCspcPara MO must be set to OFF and the TddCspcMacroMicroSwitch option of the TddCspcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CspcAlgoPara MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO is set to MODE_3, then the CelleCspcSwitch parameter in the CellCspcPara MO must be set to OFF and the TddCspcMacroMicroSwitch option of the TddCspcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CspcAlgoPara MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell and CellRicAlgo MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, then the RicAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellRicAlgo MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, then the DuctDLSubfrmShutoffSwitch parameter in the CellRicAlgo MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, then the RemoteInfAdpAvoidSwitch parameter in the CellRicAlgo MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, then the DUCT_DL_SUBFRM_SHUTOFF_OPT_SW option of the DuctDLSubfrmShutoffOptSw parameter in the CellRicAlgo MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, then the DUCT_DL_SF_SCHED_SHUTOFF_SW option of the DuctDLSubfrmShutoffOptSw parameter in the CellRicAlgo MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm and CellRfShutdown MOs:
    • The RsPwrAdjOffset parameter in a CellRfShutdown MO must be set to 0 if all the following conditions are met: The value of the CellActiveState parameter in this Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, the TxRxMode, FddTddInd, and CrsPortNum parameters in this Cell MO are set to 32T32R, CELL_FDD, and CRS_PORT_4, respectively, the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, and the RfShutdownSwitch parameter in the CellRfShutdown MO under this Cell MO is set to ON.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell, SectorSplitGroup, ENodeBAlgoSwitch, CellUSParaCfg, CellPdcchAlgo, CellAlgoSwitch, and PDSCHCfg MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, there is a SectorSplitCell MO that corresponds to this Cell MO, the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO that includes this SectorSplitCell MO is set to MODE_3, the SsrdDynDlPwrSharingSwitch option of the SSRDAlgoSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is selected, then the following parameter setting requirements must be met: The UsUeDlAdaptPwrBoostThld parameter in the CellUSParaCfg MO must be set to 0, the USPwrSharePaSwitch option of the UsAlgoExSwitch parameter in the CellUSParaCfg MO must be deselected, the PdcchSparePowerAllocStrage parameter in the CellPdcchAlgo MO must be set to NONE, the MASSIVE_MIMO_PDCCH_SDMA_SW option of the SplitBeamPdcchSdmaSw parameter in the CellPdcchAlgo MO must be deselected, the BfEnhChnPwrFormSwitch option of the DlPcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected, the MimoUePaAdaptSw parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF, and the EmfPowerLimitSwitch parameter in the PDSCHCfg MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitGroup, SectorSplitCell, and CellLteFlexBw MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, there is a SectorSplitCell MO that corresponds to this Cell MO, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO that includes this SectorSplitCell MO is set to MODE_3, then the LTE_FB_CFG_SWITCH option of the LteFlexBwSwitch parameter in the CellLteFlexBw MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitGroup, SectorSplitCell, and CellPdcchAlgo MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, there is a SectorSplitCell MO that corresponds to this Cell MO, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO that includes this SectorSplitCell MO is set to MODE_3, then the EpdcchFunctionSwitch option of the EpdcchAlgoSwitch parameter in the associated CellPdcchAlgo MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm and DdCellGroup MOs:
    • If the value of CellActiveState in the Cell MO for a cell is CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd and TxRxMode in this Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and 8T8R, respectively, and CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to NORMAL, then the cell cannot be added to any DdCellGroup MO.
  • Relation with the CellShutdown and CellRbReserve MOs:
    • If the RbRsvMode parameter in a CellRbReserve MO under a Cell MO is set to NB_DEPLOYMENT, the value of the CellActiveState parameter in this Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, and the CellShutdownSwitch parameter in the CellShutdown MO under this Cell MO is set to a value other than OFF and ON, then either the UlPrbThd or DlPrbThd parameter in this CellShutdown MO must be set to 0.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell and CellUSParaCfg MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, the UeMobilIdentifyStrategy parameter in the CellUSParaCfg MO can only be set to NONE.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and CellCsiRsParaCfg MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, and the CsiRsSwitch parameter in the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO under this Cell MO is set to a value other than NOT_CFG, the CsiRsPeriod parameter in the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO cannot be set to ms5.
  • Relation with the CellShutdown and CellSiMap MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a CELL MO is set to CELL_FDD, the CellShutdownSwitch parameter in the CellShutdown MO under this Cell MO is set to a value other than OFF, and at least one of the UlPrbThd and DlPrbThd parameters in this CellShutdown MO is set to 0, then the ForbidCellSiSwitch option of the SiSwitch parameter in the CellSiMap MO under the Cell MO cannot be selected.
  • Relation with the MDU and CellAlgoExtSwitch MOs:
    • If the value of the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE and the TYPE parameter in the MDU MO is set to MDUC, the LnrSmartCarrierShutdownSw parameter in the corresponding CellAlgoExtSwitch MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell, SectorSplitGroup, ENodeBAlgoSwitch, and CellRicAlgo MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO is set to MODE_3, and the SsrdSchOptSwitch option of SSRDAlgoSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is selected, then the MuteUpPTSSymNum parameter in the CellRicAlgo MO must be 0, and the PreambleFmt parameter in the Cell MO must not be set to 4.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, CellAlgoSwitch, CellLowPower, and CellRfShutdown MOs:
    • If the value of the CellActiveState parameter in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, the FddTddInd and TxRxMode parameters in this Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and 8T8R, respectively, and the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to NORMAL, then the DynAdjVoltSwitch option of the DynAdjVoltSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be set deselected, the LowPwrSwitch parameter in the CellLowPower MO under this Cell MO must be set to OFF, and the RfShutdownSwitch parameter in the CellRfShutdown MO under this Cell MO must also be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the CellAutoShutdown and eNodeBAutoPowerOff MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD or the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, and the AutoPowerOffSwitch parameter in the eNodeBAutoPowerOff MO is set to ON, then the CellAutoShutdownSwitch parameter in the CellAutoShutdown MO under this Cell MO can only be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the CellEdtConfig and CellUlschAlgo MOs:
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to TRUE, if the NbUlSubcarrierSpacingType parameter in the associated CellUlschAlgo MO is set to 3dot75K or HYBRID_MODE, the EDT_SWITCH option of the EdtAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEdtConfig MO cannot be selected for the cell.
  • Relation with the CellBfMimoParaCfg MO:
    • The BfMimoAdapWithTm4Switch parameter in the CellBfMimoParaCfg MO can be set to ON only if the HighSpeedFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to LOW_SPEED.
  • Relation with the CellDlCompAlgo MO:
    • LocalCellId in a CellDlCompAlgo MO must have been set in the associated Cell MO.
  • Relation with the CellUlCompAlgo MO:
    • LocalCellId in a CellUlCompAlgo MO must have been set in the associated Cell MO.
  • Relation with the DistBasedHO MO:
    • If a Cell MO is configured, the DistBasedHO MO that references LocalCellId in the Cell MO must exist.
  • Relation with the CellBfMimoParaCfg and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • The BfMimoAdapWithTm4Switch parameter in the CellBfMimoParaCfg MO can be set to ON only if all of the following conditions are met: The CrsPortNum parameter in the Cell MO is set to CRS_PORT_4. The BfSwitch check box is selected under the BfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO. The BfMimoAdaptiveSwitch parameter in the CellBfMimoParaCfg MO is set to MIMO_BF_ADAPTIVE.
    • The BfMimoAdapWithTm4Switch parameter in the CellBfMimoParaCfg MO can be set to ON only if the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE or all of the following conditions are met: The MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE. The MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to SFN. The SfnDlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to JOINT, or the SfnDlSchSwitch parameter is set to ADAPTIVE and the SfnDl2LoadLevelAdptSwitch check box is selected under the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO.
  • Relation with the CellMcPara MO:
    • LocalCellId in a CellMcPara MO must have been set in the associated Cell MO.
  • Relation with the NsaDcMgmtConfig, RACHCfg, ENodeBAlgoSwitch, and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the TDM_SWITCH option of the NsaDcAlgoSwitch parameter in the NsaDcMgmtConfig MO associated with the cell can be selected when either of the following conditions is met: 1. The PrachConfigIndexCfgInd parameter in the RACHCfg MO is set to CFG and the PrachConfigIndex parameter in the same MO is set to 0, 3, 6, 9, 13, 14, 16, 19, 22, 25, 29, 32, 35, 38, 41, 45, 48, 51, 54, or 57. 2. The PrachConfigIndexCfgInd parameter in the RACHCfg MO is set to NOT_CFG, the PrachTimeStagSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is set to OFF, and the RachAdjSwitch option of the RachAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected.
