    • If the value of the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is CELL_TDD and SA5 is used, the relationship between the values of OnDurationTimerSpecial and LongDrxCycleSpecial must meet the following requirements; If the value of the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is CELL_TDD, SA5 is used, and the value of ShortDrxCycleSwitchSpecial is UU_ENABLE, the relationship between the values of OnDurationTimerSpecial and ShortDrxCycleSpecial also must meet the following requirements: In the following formula, x indicates the value of the LongDrxCycleSpecial or ShortDrxCycleSpecial parameter divided by 10 and rounded down, and y indicates the remainder of the value of the LongDrxCycleSpecial or ShortDrxCycleSpecial parameter divided by 10. The value of LongDrxCycleSpecial or ShortDrxCycleSpecial equals to x*9 + temp (y) where: if y = 0, temp = 0; y = 2, temp = 2; y = 4, temp = 3; y = 5, temp = 4; y = 6, temp = 5; y = 8, temp = 7. The value of LongDrxCycleSpecial calculated based on the preceding formula must be greater than or equal to the value of OnDurationTimerSpecial. If the value of ShortDrxCycleSwitchSpecial is UU_ENABLE, the value of the ShortDrxCycleSpecial calculated based on the preceding formula must be greater than or equal to the value of OnDurationTimerSpecial.
    • If the value of the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is CELL_TDD and SA6 is used, the relationship between the values of OnDurationTimerSpecial and LongDrxCycleSpecial must meet the following requirements; If the value of the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is CELL_TDD, SA6 is used, and the value of ShortDrxCycleSwitchSpecial is UU_ENABLE, the relationship between the values of OnDurationTimerSpecial and ShortDrxCycleSpecial also must meet the following requirements: In the following formula, x indicates the value of the LongDrxCycleSpecial or ShortDrxCycleSpecial parameter divided by 10 and rounded down, and y indicates the remainder of the value of the LongDrxCycleSpecial or ShortDrxCycleSpecial parameter divided by 10. The value of LongDrxCycleSpecial or ShortDrxCycleSpecial equals to x*5 + temp (y) where: if y = 0, temp = 0; y = 2, temp = 2; y = 4, temp = 2; y = 5, temp = 2; y = 6, temp = 3; y = 8, temp = 4. The value of LongDrxCycleSpecial calculated based on the preceding formula must be greater than or equal to the value of OnDurationTimerSpecial. If the value of ShortDrxCycleSwitchSpecial is UU_ENABLE, the value of the ShortDrxCycleSpecial calculated based on the preceding formula must be greater than or equal to the value of OnDurationTimerSpecial.
    • If the value of the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is CELL_TDD and SA0 is used, the relationship between the values of OnDurationTimerSpecial and LongDrxCycleSpecial must meet the following requirements; If the value of the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is CELL_TDD, SA0 is used, and the value of ShortDrxCycleSwitchSpecial is UU_ENABLE, the relationship between the values of OnDurationTimerSpecial and ShortDrxCycleSpecial also must meet the following requirements: In the following formula, x indicates the value of the LongDrxCycleSpecial or ShortDrxCycleSpecial parameter divided by 10 and rounded down, and y indicates the remainder of the value of the LongDrxCycleSpecial or ShortDrxCycleSpecial parameter divided by 10. The value of LongDrxCycleSpecial or ShortDrxCycleSpecial equals to x*4 + temp (y) where: if y = 0, temp = 0; y = 2, temp = 2; y = 4, temp = 2; y = 5, temp = 2; y = 6, temp = 3; y = 8, temp = 4. The value of LongDrxCycleSpecial calculated based on the preceding formula must be greater than or equal to the value of OnDurationTimerSpecial. If the value of ShortDrxCycleSwitchSpecial is UU_ENABLE, the value of the ShortDrxCycleSpecial calculated based on the preceding formula must be greater than or equal to the value of OnDurationTimerSpecial.
  • Relation with the NsaDcMgmtConfig MO:
    • If ImdIntrfFreqAvoidanceType in the NsaDcMgMtConfig MO is set to PRIOR_ALLOC_NO_INTRF_RB, FddTddInd in the CELL MO must be set to CELL_FDD.
  • Relation with the CellHoParaCfg MO:
    • LocalCellId in a CellHoParaCfg MO must have been set in the associated Cell MO.
  • Relation with the EuSubscriber and CellAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • If the SubscriberType parameter in the EuSubscriber MO is set to RRN, the Cell MO referenced by the CellId parameter in the EuSubscriber MO must be configured, and the OutOfBandRelaySwitch or InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in at least one Cell MO must be selected.
  • Relation with the GeranInterfArfcn MO:
    • LocalCellId in a GeranInterfArfcn MO must have been set in the associated Cell MO.
    • A maximum of 20 GeranInterfArfcn MOs can be configured for each Cell MO.
  • Relation with the InterRatHoNrParamGrp MO:
    • LocalCellId in an InterRatHoNrParamGrp MO must have been set in the associated Cell MO.

Related MML Commands

Table 1 describes the MML commands related to this MO.

Table 1 Related MML commands

MML Command

Function
ACT CELL

Use this command to activate a cell.
ADD CELL

Use this command to add a cell.
ADD CELLBAND

Use this command to add a cell slave band.
BLK CELL

Use this command to block a cell.
DEA CELL

Use this command to deactivate a cell. When a cell is deactivated, all the user-related services are disrupted.
DSP CELL

Use this command to list the dynamic information about a cell.
DSP CELLBYLOCATION

Use this command to query a cell by its location.
DSP CELLCALIBRATION

Use this command to query the channel calibration result of a cell. The command output includes historical data and some details of the calibration results. Pay attention to the calibration time and cell activation time.
DSP CELLCAMCSTATUS

Use this command to query the UL CAMC enablement status of UL CAMC-enabled cells. In the Dsp Local Cluster Cell Info area, information about intra-eNodeB CAMC cluster cells is displayed. In the Dsp Cluster Cell Info area, information about inter-eNodeB CAMC cluster cells in centralized Cloud BB is displayed. This command applies only to LTE TDD networks.
DSP CELLCPRIINFO

Use this command to query the CPRI information of a cell.
DSP CELLFRAGPREADJUST

Use this command to:

(1) Query the cells for which baseband resources need to be adjusted (which will cause cell reestablishment) for the establishment of a target cell if the target cell is specified. (The target cell establishment previously failed due to baseband resource fragmentation.)

(2) Query the cells for which baseband resources need to be adjusted (which will cause cell reestablishment) to eliminate resource fragments on the eNodeB if no target cell is specified.
DSP CELLHLHCHK

Use this command to query the uplink synchronization check results of a cell.
DSP CELLLAA

Use this command to query whether the FCS and coexistence parameters of the LAA cell take effect based on the customer interface configurations.
DSP CELLPCICONFLICT

Use this command to query the PCI conflict records of a cell.
DSP CELLPHYTOPO

Use this command to query the physical topology of cells. The physical topology of auxiliary cells on an auxiliary eNodeB for a primary eNodeB can be queried by specifying the primary eNodeB's eNodeBId on the auxiliary eNodeB. On the primary eNodeB, eNodeBId is invalid.
DSP CELLULCOMPCLUSTER

Use this command to query the uplink (UL) coordinated multi-point transmission/reception technology (CoMP) list of a cell.

.
DSP CELLULICCLUSTER

Use this command to query the cooperating cells in uplink IC.
DSP DDCELLGROUP

Use this command to query the status of cells in a double deck cell group.
DSP EUCELLRPCURRURELATION

Use this command to query the pairing relationship between radio phase calibration unit (RPCU) and RRU.
DSP LRTWPRTTST

Use this command to query results of the latest RTWP tests and the status of the ongoing RTWP tests.
DSP PRIBBPADJUST

Use this command to query whether the eNodeB has sufficient resources and how to configure a cell on the primary BBP by adjusting the configuration of other cells after the cell allocation to the primary BBP failed.
DSP PRIBBPRESINFO

Use this command to query the used and remaining resources on the primary baseband processing board.
DSP ULINTRFDETECTNCELLINFO

Use this command to query information about neighboring cells involved in uplink interference detection for a cell. This command applies only to LTE FDD.
LST CELL

Use this command to list the static information about a cell.
LST CELLBAND

Use this command to list a cell slave band.
MOD CELL

Use this command to modify a cell.
RMV CELL

Use this command to remove a cell. The execution of this command deactivates the cell, interrupts the services provided by the cell, and removes all configurations related to the cell from the data base.
RMV CELLBAND

Use this command to remove a cell slave band.
RMV CELLNRT

Use this command to remove all data related to neighboring cells of the local cell, including blacklisted intra-frequency neighboring cells, blacklisted inter-frequency neighboring cells, inter-frequency neighbor relationships, neighboring E-UTRAN frequencies, intra-frequency neighbor relationships, neighbor relationships with UTRAN cells, neighboring UTRAN frequencies, neighbor relationships with GERAN cells, neighbor GERAN frequency group, neighbor GERAN BCCH frequencies, neighbor relationships with CDMA2000 HRPD cells, neighbor relationships with CDMA2000 1xRTT cells, neighboring CDMA2000 frequencies, neighboring CDMA2000 frequency bands, blacklisted neighboring E-UTRAN cells, blacklisted neighboring UTRAN cells, neighbor relationships with NR cells, neighboring NR frequencies, EutranNFreqSCellOp, EutranNFreqRanShare, QciEutranRelation, QciUtranRelation, UtranNFreqSCellOp, UtranRanShare, QciGeranRelation, GeranNFGroupSCellOp, GeranRanShare, Cdma2000BcSCellOp and NrNFreqSCellOp.
RST EUCELLRES

Use this command to reset cell equipment resources.
RST EXCLUSIVERES

Use this command to re-establish cells on a piece of baseband equipment in the following scenario: Other cells are bound to this baseband equipment but these cells are set up on other baseband equipment to which they are not bound.
STP INTERCELLCROSSFEEDERTST

Use this command to stop a cable sequence check between cells.
STP LRTWPRTTST

Use this command to stop RTWP tests in all cells under the eNodeB.
STR CELLFRAGPREADJUST

Use this command to:

(1) Adjust the cells indicated by baseband resource pre-adjustment information to eliminate baseband resource fragments, and then reestablish the target cell, if the target cell is specified. (The target cell establishment previously failed due to baseband resource fragmentation.)

(2) Eliminate baseband resource fragments on the eNodeB if no target cell is specified.
STR CELLHLHCHK

Use this command to start an uplink synchronization check for a cell.
STR CELLRFLOOPBACK

Use this command to start a cell RF loopback test to check whether the connection from the BBP to the RRU works properly.
STR CELLSELFTEST

Use this command to start a self-test for virtual services. Currently, this command does not apply to cells established on an LBBPc.
STR INTERCELLCROSSFEEDERTST

Use this command to start a cable sequence check between cells.
STR LRTWPRTTST

Use this command to start RTWP tests.
STR PRIBBPADJUST

Use this command to adjust the primary BBP for a cell. Some cells are automatically reestablished in sequence.
UBL CELL

Use this command to unblock a cell.

Related Parameters

Table 2 and Table 3 describes the parameters related to this MO.

Table 2 Configuration parameters

Parameter

Description
Local Cell ID

Indicates the local ID of the cell. It uniquely identifies a cell within a BS.
Cell Name

Indicates the name of the cell.
Csg indicator

Indicates whether the cell is a closed subscriber group (CSG) cell. A CSG cell has restrictions on UE access. It broadcasts a specific CSG ID in the SIB and only those UEs of the group can access the CSG cell. BOOLEAN_FALSE indicates that the cell is not a CSG cell, and BOOLEAN_TRUE indicates that the cell is a CSG cell. Currently, Huawei eNodeBs do not support CSG cells. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Uplink cyclic prefix length

Indicates the UL cyclic prefix length of a cell. A cyclic prefix can be a common or extended cyclic prefix. An extended cyclic prefix is generally used in a complex environment with a strong multi-path effect and long delay. In a cell, the UL cyclic prefix length can be different from the DL one. In addition, the UL or DL cyclic prefix length of a cell must be the same as that of the cell using the same BBP. For details, see 3GPP TS 36.211.
Downlink cyclic prefix length

Indicates the length of the DL cyclic prefix of a cell. A DL cyclic prefix can be a common or extended cyclic prefix. An extended cyclic prefix is generally used in a complex environment with a strong multi-path effect and long delay. In a cell, the UL cyclic prefix length can be different from the DL one. In addition, the UL or DL cyclic prefix length of a cell must be the same as that of the cell using the same BBP. For details, see 3GPP TS 36.211.
Frequency band

Indicates the frequency band in which a cell operates. For details about this parameter, see 3GPP TS 36.104.

The LTE-U function has been removed, and the values in the range of 252 to 255 are invalid. Although the configuration interface in this version supports synchronization and delivery of the values in this range, the system does not use these values. Therefore, avoid setting this parameter to these values.
Uplink EARFCN indication

Indicates whether to configure the uplink EARFCN of the cell. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Uplink EARFCN

Indicates the UL EARFCN of the cell. It is optional, depending on the setting of the UL EARFCN configuration indicator (UlEarfcnCfgInd). If the value of this parameter is not specified, the default UL EARFCN is used, which is calculated based on the DL EARFCN. For details, see 3GPP TS 36.104. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Downlink EARFCN

Indicates the DL EARFCN of a cell. For details about this parameter, see the 3GPP TS 36.104.

The LTE-U function has been removed, and the values in the ranges of 255144 to 256143 and 260894 to 262143 are invalid. Although the configuration interface in this version supports synchronization and delivery of the values in these ranges, the system does not use these values. Therefore, avoid setting this parameter to these values.
Uplink bandwidth

Indicates the uplink bandwidth of the cell. The bandwidth is expressed in the number of RBs. For example, the value CELL_BW_N25 indicates a cell bandwidth of 25 RBs and the value CELL_BW_N50 indicates a cell bandwidth of 50 RBs. For details, see 3GPP TS 36.104. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Downlink bandwidth

Indicates the downlink bandwidth of the cell. The bandwidth is expressed in the number of RBs. For example, the value CELL_BW_N25 indicates a cell bandwidth of 25 RBs and the value CELL_BW_N50 indicates a cell bandwidth of 50 RBs. For details, see 3GPP TS 36.104. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Cell ID

Indicates the ID of the E-UTRAN cell. The combination of this parameter and the eNodeB ID forms the E-UTRAN cell identity. The combination of the E-UTRAN cell identity and the PLMN identity forms the ECGI. For details, see 3GPP TS 36.413.
Physical cell ID

Indicates the physical cell ID. For details, see 3GPP TS 36.331.
Standby Cell SFN Recovery Time

Indicates the automatic SFN recovery time of the inter-BBU cell on the secondary BBU after inter-BBU SFN faults are rectified. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Additional spectrum emission

Indicates the additional spectrum emission, which restricts the emission power of the UEs in the cell. For details, see 3GPP TS 36.101.
Cell active state

Indicates the activation state of the cell. It consists of the activated and deactivated states.
Cell admin state

Indicates the administrative state of the cell. If this parameter is set to CELL_HIGH_BLOCK, the cell is deactivated immediately. If this parameter is set to CELL_MIDDLE_BLOCK, new UEs are not allowed to access the cell, and the cell is immediately deactivated when it has no user to serve within the period specified by the CellMidBlkTimer parameter or is deactivated when the timer expires irrespective of whether it has UEs to serve. If this parameter is set to CELL_LOW_BLOCK, new UEs are not allowed to access the cell and the cell is deactivated only when the cell has no user to serve. If this parameter is set to CELL_LOSSLESS_BLOCK, new UEs are not allowed to access the cell, UEs in the cell are proactively handed over to neighboring cells, UEs in other cells are not allowed to be handed over to this cell, and the cell is deactivated when it has no user to serve within the period specified by the CellMidBlkTimer parameter or when the timer expires.
Cell Blocking Duration

Indicates the cell blocking duration. During the specified duration, new UEs are not allowed to access the cell. If there are no UEs in the cell, the cell is deactivated immediately. If there are UEs in the cell, the cell is deactivated after the cell blocking duration ends. In hitless cell blocking, UEs are not allowed to be handed over to the cell either within the specified cell blocking duration.
Cell FDD TDD indication

Indicates the duplex mode of the cell. CELL_FDD indicates the FDD mode, and CELL_TDD indicates the TDD mode.
Subframe assignment

Indicates the ratio of UL subframes to DL subframes for an LTE TDD cell. For details, see 3GPP TS 36.211. This parameter applies only to LTE TDD.
Special subframe patterns

Indicates the ratio of special UL subframes to special DL subframes for an LTE TDD cell. For details, see 3GPP TS 36.211. This parameter applies only to LTE TDD.
Cell specific offset

Indicates the cell specific offset for the serving cell. It affects the probability of triggering handovers from the serving cell to its neighboring cells. A smaller value of this parameter leads to a higher probability. For details, see 3GPP TS 36.331. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Frequency offset

Indicates the specific frequency offset of the serving cell. This parameter is contained in the measurement configuration and is related to the handover difficulty between the serving cell and the neighboring cell. For details, see 3GPP TS 36.331. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Root sequence index

Indicates the index of the start logical root sequence used to generate preamble sequences for PRACH configuration set type A in a low-speed, high-speed, or ultra-high speed cell. Each logical root sequence maps one physical root sequence. For details about the mapping relationships, see 3GPP TS 36.211. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
High speed flag

Indicates the speed flag of the cell. Set this parameter to HIGH_SPEED or ULTRA_HIGH_SPEED if the cell is used to provide coverage for high-speed railways. Set this parameter to LOW_SPEED in other scenarios. This parameter can be set only to LOW_SPEED for TDD 5 MHz cells. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Preamble format

Indicates the preamble format used in the cell. For details, see 3GPP TS 36.211. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Cell radius

Indicates the radius of the cell, that is, the farthest distance that a cell can cover under the condition of keeping certain network performance. If this parameter is set to a value greater than 100 km, the Extended Cell Access Radius Beyond 100km feature takes effect. Only FDD cells can be configured with a radius more than 100 km. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Customized bandwidth configure indicator

Indicates whether to configure a compact bandwidth for a cell. A compact bandwidth can be configured only when a standard bandwidth 1.4 MHz, 5 MHz, 10 MHz, 15 MHz, or 20 MHz has been configured. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD.
Customized uplink bandwidth

Indicates a customized uplink bandwidth configured for a cell. A compact bandwidth can be configured only when a standard bandwidth 1.4 MHz, 5 MHz, 10 MHz, 15 MHz, or 20 MHz has been configured. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD.
Customized downlink bandwidth

Indicates a customized downlink bandwidth configured for a cell. A compact bandwidth can be configured only when a standard bandwidth 1.4 MHz, 5 MHz, 10 MHz, 15 MHz, or 20 MHz has been configured. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD.
Ue max power allowed configure indicator

Indicates whether to configure the maximum TX power of the UE in the cell. If the value of this parameter is not specified, the maximum power is subject to the UE capability.
Max transmit power allowed

Indicates the maximum transmit power that a UE can apply to uplink transmission in the cell. It is used in cell selection criterion S to calculate the compensated power. If the value of this parameter is not specified, the maximum power is subject to the UE capability. For details, see 3GPP TS 36.304.
Flag of Multi-RRU Cell

Indicates whether to enable or disable the multi-RRU cell feature.
Mode of Multi-RRU Cell

Indicates the type of the multi-RRU cell.
CPRI Compression

Indicates the CPRI compression type of the cell. CPRI compression is used in RRU cascading scenarios to enable more RRUs to be cascaded without changing the CPRI data rate. The parameter can be set to NO_COMPRESSION, NORMAL_COMPRESSION, ENHANCED_COMPRESSION, QUADPLEX_COMPRESSION, TRIPLEX_COMPRESSION_10M, or 3_2_COMPRESSION. The parameter value NO_COMPRESSION indicates that CPRI compression is not used. The parameter value NORMAL_COMPRESSION indicates that normal CPRI compression is used. The parameter value ENHANCED_COMPRESSION indicates that enhanced CPRI compression is used. The parameter value QUADPLEX_COMPRESSION indicates that quadruple CPRI compression is used. The parameter value TRIPLEX_COMPRESSION_10M indicates triple CPRI compression for a 10 MHz cell, which applies only to LTE FDD. The value 3_2_COMPRESSION indicates that the compression ratio of 3.2:1 is used. This compression ratio applies only to TDD cells and FDD massive MIMO cells. If the eNodeB is equipped with LBBPd boards and the CPRI data rate is 9.8 Gbit/s for LTE FDD cells or the CPRI data rate is greater than 4.9 Gbit/s for LTE TDD cells, the CPRI compression type of cells served by RRUs in the same RRU chain or ring must be the same. The LBBPc does not support CPRI compression. All types of LBBP boards do not support enhanced or quadruple CPRI compression. Only the UBBPe and UBBPg support triple CPRI compression for 10 MHz cells. Before changing the value of this parameter for a cell served by an RRU in an RRU chain or ring, ensure that all cells served by the RRUs in the chain or ring are deactivated. After the parameter value is changed, activate the cells. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Physical Cell Number of SFN Cell

Indicates the number of physical cells constituting an SFN cell. In macro eNodeBs, this parameter value equals the number of sector equipment sets serving an SFN cell. In LampSite eNodeBs, this parameter value equals the number of sector equipment groups serving an SFN cell. In macro + LampSite eNodeBs, the parameter value equals the total number of sector equipment sets and sector equipment groups serving an SFN cell. For FDD, a maximum of six physical cells can be combined to form an SFN cell. For TDD, a maximum of seven physical cells can be combined to form an SFN cell.
CRS Port Number

Indicates the number of ports for transmitting cell-specific reference signal (CRS). As defined in 3GPP specifications, this parameter can be set to CRS_PORT_1, CRS_PORT_2, or CRS_PORT_4. The value CRS_PORT_1 indicates that one CRS port (port 0) is configured. The value CRS_PORT_2 indicates that two CRS ports (ports 0 and 1) are configured. The value CRS_PORT_4 indicates that four CRS ports (ports 0, 1, 2, and 3) are configured.
Cell transmission and reception mode

Indicates the transmission and reception mode of the cell.
User label

Indicates the user label of the cell.
Work mode

Indicates the work mode of the cell. The DL_ONLY mode applies only to the carrier aggregation scenario.
Cell Standby Mode

Indicates the active or standby mode of the cell. A cell is in active mode by default. If this parameter is set to STANDBY, an inter-BBU SFN auxiliary cell can switch back to the standby mode to provide services if inter-BBU faults occur. Only common cells, SFN cells or MPRU_AGGREGATION cells support the standby mode.
CRS Antenna Port Mapping

Indicates the mapping between a cell-specific reference signal (CRS) port and a transmit (TX) channel in an RRU. In cells with one or two TX channels, this parameter cannot be set. In cells with four or eight TX channels, this parameter can be set. As defined in 3GPP specifications, the CrsPortNum parameter can be set to CRS_PORT_1, CRS_PORT_2, or CRS_PORT_4. The value CRS_PORT_1 indicates that one CRS port (port 0) is configured. The value CRS_PORT_2 indicates that two CRS ports (ports 0 and 1) are configured. The value CRS_PORT_4 indicates that four CRS ports (ports 0, 1, 2 and 3) are configured. In values of this parameter, mTnP indicates that the number of TX channels configured for a cell is "m" and the number of CRS ports is "n". The parameter value 4TnP_abcd indicates that reference signals transmitted over CRS ports a, b, c, and d are mapped to TX channels 1, 2, 3, and 4 in an RRU, respectively (The TX channel number here is for reference only and may be R0A to R0D, or others). The parameter value 8TnP_abcdefgh indicates that reference signals transmitted over CRS ports a to h are mapped to TX channels 1 to 8 in an RRU, respectively. Duplicate numbers of abcd or abcdefgh indicate that virtual antenna mapping (VAM) is applied, that is, the reference signals transmitted over the CRS ports are mapped to corresponding TX channels. The parameter value NOT_CFG indicates that the mapping between a CRS port and a TX channel in an RRU is not configured, and CRS ports are mapped to TX channels in an RRU in a default manner. In TDD cells, "n" in mTnP must be equal to the CrsPortNum parameter value. For cells established on LBBPc boards, the parameter value NOT_CFG takes effect regardless of the actual parameter setting. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
CPRI Ethernet Compression Ratio

Indicates the compression ratio of data transmitted on the CPRI port over Ethernet for an MPRU aggregation cell. Data compression on the CPRI port over Ethernet reduces the bandwidth of LTE data on the Ethernet cable between the RHUB and MPRUs, and therefore the Ethernet cable supports more carriers of different RATs. The parameter values NO_COMPRESSION, 2_TO_1, 3_TO_1, and 4_TO_1 indicate non-compression and ratios of 2:1, 3:1, and 4:1, respectively. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Downlink Punctured RB Number

Indicates the number of downlink RBs blocked in a cell. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD.
Coverage Level Type

Indicates the NB-IoT cell coverage level. A maximum of three coverage levels are supported, corresponding to 0 dB, 10 dB, and 20 dB coverage enhancement. This parameter is valid only when the NbCellFlag parameter is set to TRUE.

COVERAGE_LEVEL_0: The NB-IoT cell supports 0 dB coverage enhancement only when this option is selected. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

COVERAGE_LEVEL_1: The NB-IoT cell supports 10 dB coverage enhancement only when this option is selected. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

COVERAGE_LEVEL_2: The NB-IoT cell supports 20 dB coverage enhancement only when this option is selected. This parameter applies only to NB-IoT.

Parameter Relationship: The COVERAGE_LEVEL_1 and COVERAGE_LEVEL_2 options take effect only when the COVERAGE_EXTENSION_SWITCH option is selected. If the COVERAGE_LEVEL_1 and COVERAGE_LEVEL_2 options are selected but the COVERAGE_EXTENSION_SWITCH option is deselected, the eNodeB broadcasts the coverage level thresholds in SIB2 and allocates NPRACH resources for coverage levels 1 and 2. However, the eNodeB does not respond to the random access requests initiated by UEs at coverage levels 1 and 2.
NB-IoT Cell Flag

Indicates whether a local cell is an NB-IoT cell. When this parameter is set to TRUE, the local cell is an NB-IoT cell. When this parameter is set to FALSE, the local cell is not an NB-IoT cell. This parameter applies only to NB-IoT.
Compact Bandwidth Control Interference Mode

Indicates the interference control mode for scenarios where compact bandwidth is used. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD.
Uplink Punctured RB Number Offset

Indicates the offset for the number of RBs blocked in a cell in the uplink. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD.
SFN Master Cell Label

Used to maintain and query the information about the primary cell to which SFN secondary cells belong. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD.
Cell Slave Band

Indicates slave frequency bands of a cell. If the operating frequency of a cell belongs to multiple frequency bands, the frequency band with the highest priority is configured by the FreqBand parameter, and frequency bands with other priorities are configured by the CellSlaveBand parameter and added in descending order based on frequency band priorities. The frequency band priorities of a cell are determined in network planning. It is recommended that the frequency bands supported by UEs that comply with 3GPP TS 36.331 V8.16.0, 3GPP TS 36.331 V9.1.0, 3GPP TS 36.331 V10.5.0, or a later vision be configured as slave frequency bands.
CN Operator Sharing Group ID

Indicates the ID of the eNodeB operator group that shares the cell. This parameter must be set to a value within the range of 0 to 5 if the eNodeB works in hybrid RAN sharing mode. This parameter must be set to 255 (an invalid value) in independent RAN sharing, RAN sharing with dedicated carrier, or RAN sharing with common carrier mode.
Intra Frequency RAN Sharing Indication

Indicates whether an intra-frequency neighboring cell is a shared neighboring cell. If this parameter is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, the serving frequency is a shared frequency, and the shared operator information must be checked on the OSS before all intra-frequency neighboring cells are added. If this parameter is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE, the serving frequency is not a shared frequency, and the shared operator information does not need to be checked on the OSS before all intra-frequency neighboring cells are added. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
IntraFreq ANR Indication

Indicates whether to enable ANR on intra-frequency neighboring cells. The value ALLOWED indicates that this type of ANR is allowed. The value NOT_ALLOWED indicates that this type of ANR is not allowed. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
SSP6 DwPTS Mode

Indicates whether the DwPTS is truncated for transmission in LTE TDD cells when SSP6 is used. This parameter applies only to LTE TDD cells that work on band 39 or band 34 and use SA2+SSP6. This parameter applies only to LTE TDD.
Cell Radius Start Location

Indicates the start location of the cell coverage. This parameter is used to calculate the hysteresis in uplink timing when the Extended Cell Access Radius Beyond 100km feature takes effect. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Specified Cell Flag

Indicates whether the local cell is a specified-service cell. This parameter is configured based on the operator's policies. If this parameter is set to SPECSERCELL, the local cell is a WBB service-dedicated cell and preferentially serves WBB UEs. In this case, UEs identified as non-WBB UEs will be transferred out from the cell. This function is under license control. If this parameter is set to MBBSERCELL, the local cell is an MBB-preferred cell and preferentially serves MBB UEs. In this case, UEs identified as non-MBB UEs will be transferred out from the cell. If this parameter is set to NONE, the local cell is a common cell. In this case, WBB and MBB UEs can be transferred to this cell. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Multi Cell Share Mode

Indicates the co-RRU mode for multiple cells. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Ultra High-Speed Cell Root Sequence Index

Indicates the index of the start logic root sequence of the preamble sequences generated by class-2 PRACH configuration sets in an ultra-high speed cell. Each logic root sequence maps one physical root sequence. For details about the mapping relationships, see 3GPP TS 36.211. This parameter is valid only when the HighSpeedFlag parameter is set to ULTRA_HIGH_SPEED and the HighSpeedR14Switch option is selected. PRACH enhancement in high mobility scenarios added in 3GPP Release 14 takes effect only when the HsCellRootSequenceIndex parameter is set to a value other than 65535. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Table 3 Dynamic parameters

Parameter

Description
LAA Configuration Reference Cell ID

Indicates the local cell ID of the reference cell whose FCS and coexistence parameter configurations are referenced by the LAA cell. This parameter applies only to LTE TDD.
MPT information

Indicates the location information of the MPT used by a cell. The value of this parameter is expressed as cabinet number - subrack number - slot number. The value "-" (hyphen) indicates an invalid value.
Cell instance state

Indicates the state of the cell instance.

In the case of access barring, the state can be CELL_INST_BARRED(Barred), CELL_INST_EMTC_BARRED(eMTC Barred), or CELL_INST_LTE_EMTC_BARRED(LTE and eMTC Barred). If NB-IoT or LTE UEs are barred from accessing the cell, CELL_INST_BARRED(Barred) is displayed as the state of the cell instance. If eMTC UEs are barred from accessing the cell, CELL_INST_EMTC_BARRED(eMTC Barred) is displayed as the state of the cell instance. If both LTE and eMTC UEs are barred from accessing the cell, CELL_INST_LTE_EMTC_BARRED(LTE and eMTC Barred) is displayed as the state of the cell instance.
Reason for latest state change

Indicates the reason for the latest state change of the cell, which can be a cell activation success or a cell setup failure.
Cell latest setup time

Indicates the latest setup time of the cell.
Cell latest setup operate type

Indicates the latest setup type of the cell.
Cell latest remove time

Indicates the latest removal time of the cell.
Cell latest remove operate type

Indicates the latest removal type of the cell.
Cell power save state

Indicates the energy saving status of the cell.
Primary BBP information

Indicates the cabinet number, subrack number, and slot number of the primary BBP that serves the cell.
Cell topology type

Indicates the topology of the cell.
RF module cabinet number

Indicates the cabinet number of the RF module that serves the cell.
RF module subrack number

Indicates the subrack number of the RF module that serves the cell.
RF module slot number

Indicates the slot number of the RF module that serves the cell.
Symbol shutdown state

Indicates whether the cell is in the symbol power saving state.
Maximum transmit power

Indicates the theoretical maximum transmit power of a single antenna channel of the cell. The value 65535 indicates that this parameter is invalid, and the maximum transmit power can be adjusted by modifying the PDSCH configuration or the PA-related parameters for PDSCH power control. The maximum transmit power of LTE and NB-IoT physical cells in the extension information is the same as that in the basic information. The maximum transmit power of LampSite and 32T32R cells is not calculated, and the invalid value 65535 is used for these cells. The maximum transmit power of auxiliary eNodeBs for SFN cells may be different from that in the basic information. For FDD 8T8R cells, this parameter indicates the cell max power of each logical ant.
eNodeB ID

Indicates the ID of the eNodeB.
BBP Cabinet No.

Indicates the cabinet number of the BBP that serves the cell.
BBP Subrack No.

Indicates the subrack number of the BBP that serves the cell.
BBP Slot No.

Indicates the slot number of the BBP that serves the cell.
Work Status

Indicates the work status of the RF module and BBP that serves the cell.
Coordinating Local Cell ID

Indicates the local cell ID of the cell that functions as a cooperating cell in UL CoMP (DSP CELLULCOMPCLUSTER is used to query the ID), the local cell ID of the cell that functions as a cooperating cell in uplink IC (DSP CELLULICCLUSTER is used to query the ID), or the local cell ID of the cell that functions as a cooperating cell in uplink interference detection (DSP ULINTRFDETECTNCELLINFO is used to query the ID).
RF Module Information

Indicates the cabinet, subrack, and slot where the RF module serves the cell is located.
Served BBP Information

Indicates the cabinet, subrack, and slot of the baseband processing unit on which the cell is set up is located.
1 RRU Cell Number

Indicates the number of cells (each cell is served by one RRU) supported by the remaining resources on the primary baseband processing board.
2 RRU Cell Number

Indicates the number of cells (each cell is served by two RRUs) supported by the remaining resources on the primary baseband processing board.
3 RRU Cell Number

Indicates the number of cells (each cell is served by three RRUs) supported by the remaining resources on the primary baseband processing board.
4 RRU Cell Number

Indicates the number of cells (each cell is served by four RRUs) supported by the remaining resources on the primary baseband processing board.
5 RRU Cell Number

Indicates the number of cells (each cell is served by five RRUs) supported by the remaining resources on the primary baseband processing board.
6 RRU Cell Number

Indicates the number of cells (each cell is served by six RRUs) supported by the remaining resources on the primary baseband processing board.
7 RRU Cell Number

Indicates the number of cells (each cell is served by seven RRUs) supported by the remaining resources on the primary baseband processing board.
SectorEqm(SectorEqmGroup) Number

Indicates the quantity of resources occupied by the cell on the primary baseband processing board. The resource quantity is indicated by the number of RRUs.
Number of Cells Where Adjustment Fails

Indicates the number of cells for which the adjustment fails in the one-click manner.
Number of Cells Where Adjustment Succeeds

Indicates the number of cells for which the adjustment is successful in the one-click manner.
Connected BBP Port Information

Indicates the information about the CPRI port on the baseband processing board that is directly connected to the RF unit serving a cell. The information includes the cabinet, subrack, slot, and CPRI port numbers. A hyphen (-) represents an invalid value.
TX Channel No.

Indicates information about the TX channel of a cell.
RX Channel No.

Indicates information about the RX channel of a cell.
Served Bbp Primary Secondary Mode

Indicates whether a BBP serving the cell is a primary or secondary BBP. If the value of this parameter is PRIMARY for a slot, the BBP in this slot is the primary BBP, which carries the MAC layer resources of the cell. If the value of this parameter is SECONDARY for a slot, the BBP in this slot is a secondary BBP, which carries the physical layer resources of the cell.
Coordinating Cell Route Type

Indicates the type of route between the serving BBPs of the serving and cooperating cells in UL CoMP (DSP CELLULCOMPCLUSTER is used to query the type), the type of route between the primary BBPs of the benefited and interfering cells in uplink IC (DSP CELLULICCLUSTER is used to query the type), the type of route between the serving and cooperating cells in uplink interference detection (DSP ULINTRFDETECTNCELLINFO is used to query the type).
Coordinating Cell eNodeB ID

Indicates the ID of the eNodeB serving the coordinating cell.
Coordinating Sector Equipment(group) ID

Indicates the ID of the coordinating sector equipment (group).
Coordinating Sector Equipment(group) eNB ID

Indicates the ID of the eNodeB equipped with the coordinating sector equipment (group).
Anti Interfere Status in High Speed Scenario

Indicates the status of interference coordination in high-speed railway scenarios. The value of this parameter can be InterfHighSpeedStatus_NO_OP~0(Disable), InterfHighSpeedStatus_ACT~1(Run), or InterfHighSpeedStatus_RENEW~2(Restore).

When interference coordination in high-speed railway scenarios is disabled for a cell, the value of this parameter is InterfHighSpeedStatus_NO_OP~0(Disable) in the command output. When this function is being performed in the cell, the value of this parameter is InterfHighSpeedStatus_ACT~1(Run). When resources are restored to normal status, the value of this parameter is InterfHighSpeedStatus_RENEW~2(Restore). This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Serving Sector Equipment(group) ID

Indicates the ID of the serving sector equipment(group).
Serving Sector Equipment(Group) eNB ID

Indicates the ID of the eNodeB equipped with the serving sector equipment(group).
Coordinating Sector Equipment Type

Indicates the type of sector equipment in the coordinating cell.
Serving Sector Equipment Type

Indicates the type of sector equipment in the serving cell.
Calibration Result

Indicates the cell-level channel calibration result. If channel calibration fails (for example, due to a low SINR) in cells served by combined RRUs, the antenna system must be adjusted.
Calibration Time

Indicates the end time of a cell-level channel calibration.
Calibration Type

Indicates the cell-level channel calibration type.
Calibration Index

Indicates the cell-level channel calibration index. Results of a maximum of 10 channel calibrations can be displayed in the DSP CELLCALIBRATION command output.
Cell Check Time

Indicates the health check time of the cell. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Fault Unit Info

Indicates the cabinet, subrack, slot, and port of the faulty unit. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Fault Unit Type

Indicates the type of the faulty unit. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
RF Loop Result

Indicates the RF loopback verification result of each channel of the cell.
eNodeB Du ID

Indicates the ID of the eNodeB DU. A hyphen (-) indicates an invalid value.
Probe End Time

Indicates the end time of detecting the pairing relationship between RRU and radio phase calibration unit (RPCU).
Probe Index

Indicates the index for detection of the pairing relationship between RRU and radio phase calibration unit (RPCU). The DSP EUCELLRPCURRURELATION command output includes a maximum of 10 latest detection results.
Probe Result

Indicates the detection result of the pairing relationship between RRU and radio phase calibration unit (RPCU).
RPCU Information

Indicates information about the cabinet, subrack, and slot of the radio phase calibration unit (RPCU) used by the cell.
RRU Information

Indicates information about cabinet, subrack, and slot of the RF module serving the cell.
Cell Tx Power Offset

Indicates the offset to the maximum transmit power of a single antenna channel of the cell. This parameter is related to the power allocation algorithm for each control channel and traffic channel of the eNodeB. The value 65535 indicates that this parameter is invalid. When the actually used power is less than or equal to the configured power, the value 65535 is used. If the cell is a LampSite cell, the value 65535 is used.
